rīśīlavṛttaphalaṃ śrutaṃ

kaccidvaḥ sa phalaṃtapūṃtapaścāṃdrāpaṇādikaṃ

yathāmati pravakṣyāmi vadaṃ tu munipuṃgavāḥ

kṛtāṃjali purā ūrcurnattvā sāṣṭāṃagamādarāt

kathaṃ brahmavijijñāsyaṃ kīdṛśairniyata vrataiḥ

kālasvarūpaṃ dādibhirdvijaiḥ vibhāṃḍakasutaḥ prāhanāmṛṣīnśaṃsitavratān

śrotavyaṃ yogināṃ śreṣṭheḥ sāvadhānaiḥ samāhitaiḥ

nimeṣakāla ityuktaḥ kāṣṭhātetriṃśadīditāḥ

gaḍīrūpaṃ muhūrtaḥ syādyāmasteṣāṃ catuṣṭayaṃ

ṣaṣṭināḍyo dinaṃ prokta mahorātraṃ tadevahi

pakṣe pakṣe paurṇamāsī puṇyātithiramāpi vā

māsairdvādaśabhirviprāḥ saṃvatsara iti smṛtaḥ

catvāryāhuḥ sahasrāṇi vatsarāṇāṃ kṛtaṃ yuge

** (?) varṣa sahasrāṇi tretāyugamiti smṛtaṃ

sahasradvayamandānāṃ dvāparaṃ yugamīritaṃ

tasya tāvacchatī saṃ***(?) mānena gaṇyate

itthaṃ dvādaśasāhasrama**(?)devayugaṃ smṛtaṃ

caturyugānāṃ sāhasraṃ brahmaṇo hatadīritaṃ

caturyugānāṃ sāhasraṃ dvayaṃ brāhmaṃ dinaṃ smṛtaṃ

caturvaktasya tasyāmasargakṛtparameṣṭinaḥ

brahmaṇaḥ paramāyuḥ syāddivasaṃ viṣṇurudravoḥ

naivāhastasya na niśā nityasya paramātmanaḥ

kālaḥ saṃharate viśvaṃ kālādhītamidaṃ jagat

layaṃgatā asaṃkhyātā rudrā api tapodhanāḥ

narāṇāmapi tiryaścākā kathāpipra puṃgavāḥ

bhakṣayaṃtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ sṛjaṃtamakhilaṃ jagat

brahmatatvamavijñāya prajayā karmaṇāpi vā

aśrutvācārya śuśrūṣāmavijñāya cidātmakaṃ

kālasyāpi pitāsākṣādbrahmarūpī śriyaḥ patiḥ

parasya brahmatatvaṃ sākṣānnārāyaṇātmanaḥ

ācaryasya kṛpā mūrteḥ sākṣānnārāyaṇātmanaḥ

tasnānairna tapobhirvā na bhasmasnānatopi vā

gaṃgādi sarvatīrteṣu matsyāśca makarādayaḥ

vakāmaunena tiṣṭhaṃti mīnā udakapāyinaḥ

rāsabhātryādhicaścāno bhasmāhaṣitavigrahāḥ

saṃcaraṃto mṛgānityamahiṃsādharma tatparāḥ

tasmādācārya śuśrūṣāṃ kṛtvā nityaṃ ciraṃ dvijāḥ

bhītyāvānāṃ vāti hasaṃ tataṃ

tadājñaṣaivakālopi viśvaṃ sṛjati haṃti ca

vijñāya vaṃcayitvā saukālaṃ sarvabhayāsūrāṃ

saiṣānaṃdasya mīmāṃsā vakṣyate vipra puṃgavāḥ

arogī pakvabhugnityaṃ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ

sārvabhaumatvamāsādyamānuṣānaṃdamaśnute

ekomat**(?) gaṃdharvānaṃdamuktaḥ śrutau smṛtau

śataṃ manuṣyagaṃdharvānaṃdānāṃsataṃ dvijapta

tadānaṃdaśataṃ divyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ munipuṃgavāḥ

ānaṃdānāṃśataṃ prokta **(?) ājānajāmare

ānaṃda ekaḥ saṃproktastato mukhyasureṣvapi

tadānaṃdaśataṃ śakrasyaika ānaṃda ucyate

gurvānaṃda*****(?)vasyānaṃda ucyate

hiraṇyagarbhānaṃdānāṃ śataṃ viṣṇoḥ śivasya ca

tadānaṃdaśataṃ sākṣānnityasya paramātmanaḥ

nārāyaṇasya nityasya brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ

ācāryapādaśuśrūṣā niratasya yatātmanaḥ

jīvanmuktasya tasyāpi brahmānaṃda udāhṛtaḥ

koṭijanma tapobhirvā mahādānāyutairapi

tasmādbrahmavijijñāsā kāryācārya mukhāṃvujān

iti kāla japopāyaḥ kathito brahmavittamāḥ

iti dāśarathīye smin sarvavedārthasaṃgrahe

brahmatatvaṃ kathaṃ jñeyaṃ śrotavyaṃ kathamādarāt

sarvametatsamāsenavadāsmākaṃdayānidhe

śāśvataṃ śuddhamavyaktaṃ sadānaṃdamayaṃ hi tat

cidānaṃdamayaṃ nityaṃ manovācāmagocaraṃ

na strī na ṣaṃṭho na yumānnadvāmaparamaṃ mahat

hṛdguhāsusthitaṃ sarvaprāṇināṃcitapaṃkaje

manubhāti sarvaṃ tasya bhāsāmidaṃ vibhāti

sargāsthiti vināśānāṃ hetu bhūtasanātanaṃ

tasmānnādātmakaḥ sākṣādakārātmāsanātanaḥ

viṃdussamabhavatasmājjīvātmāṃ tassanātanaḥ

tayormadhye samudabhūtkalā cidrūpiṇī parā

tata āvirabhūt sākṣātpaṃcāśadvarṇamātṛkā

sākṣānnārāyaṇamayī brahmābhinnā cidātmakā

athāṃtasthā athoṣmāṇa ityevaṃ mātṛkāsmṛtā

aca ityevamākhyātāḥ kādamohala īritāḥ

kādayohala ityuktā vyaṃjanāni vidurbudhāḥ

tasmādi yamakārādi kṣakārāṃtākṣarātmikā

mātṛkā paramāśaktirabhinnā brahmaṇo dvijāḥ

ca anayai samudbhūtā vedāḥ sāṃ*(?)sanātanāḥ

catuṣṣaṣṭi kalāvidyā vidhā aṣṭādaśāpi ca

parāsisṛkṣā bhagavadbrahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ

asṛjatadbrahmarūpī puru****(?)dvijāḥ

arddhena nārī tasyāṃ tu virājamasṛjadvibhuḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarātmāsāveka eva parocyutaḥ

sa evādyaḥ pumānnityaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ

aṣṭākṣara **(?) vedavedāṃtairapyagocaraḥ

parāsya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā ca

sā nārīśrīrmahālakṣmīḥ khyātāparakalāmayī

strīpuṃrūpamidaṃ tasya brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ

cidrūpiṇīṃ śriyaṃ guptāṃ vibhrannārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

śrūyate vedavedāṃtairapi vigrahadhāribhiḥ

sargakāle yadā vibhrat strīpuṃrūpamakalmaṣaṃ

tadvādaśārṇamaṃtreṇavācyaṃ brahmaparātparaṃ

virājaṃ puruṣaṃ sargādikālesṛjadīśvaraḥ

puruṣaṇavirāduktaḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇaṃ

yamāha sarvajagatāṃ kāraṇaṃ parameśvaraṃ

tasya vaktrādāvirabhūtpraṇavaḥ śāśvatovyayaḥ

bhūriti vyāhṛtiḥ paścādbhuṣaḥ suvariti dvayaṃ

etadeva mahadviśvaṃ vairājaṃ rūpamuttamaṃ

savitāviśvarūpo sau virāṭpuruṣa īśvaraḥ

ayameva jagatsarvaṃ sṛjyatyavatihaṃti ca

ayaṃ savitṛrūpiṇyagyāyatryākālarūpayā

kālātmako vedamayo yenedamakhilaṃ jagat

amāduha tato vedānbhūrādivyāhṛti trayaṃ

utpannāḥ sarvalokānāmihāmutragatipradāḥ

nārāyaṇamayonityomahāpuruṣa īśvaraḥ

aya eva sargādautāsuvīryamavāsṛjat

tasminjajñe vidhirbrahmāsvayaṃ bhūritiḥ śrutiḥ

ayanaṃ tasyatāḥ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ

aṃdhakā****(?) saṃtatāya sumahattayaḥ

nārāyaṇotha brāhmāṇaṃ puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇapiṃgalaḥ

lajjayāvanataṃ sargakarmāśaktaṃ svayaṃ bhuvaṃ

śrutyuktena vidhānena sargaṃ kurvititi ***(?)

bhagavatejasaḥ prāptyai bhūyopi sumahattayaḥ

mahāpuruṣo bhūtvā vidhūyāṃ dhanamokhilaṃ

śrībhagavānuvāca bho bho vra**(?)pitāṃhaṃ yoyakastava

ahameva paraṃbrahma paramātmā sanātanaḥ

vāsudevosmyahaṃ viṣṇurvirādrūpī hiraṇmayaḥ

cinmayohamanaṃtohamādi madhyāṃtavarjitaḥ

vedāṃtavedyoviśvātmā viśvarūpohmyahaṃ vibhuḥ

yosāvaṣṭākṣaro maṃtraḥ svabhāva praṇavādikaḥ

nārāyaṇastadātmāhaṃ cidrūpiṇyāśrimānnitaḥ

asyaivāṣṭārṇamaṃtrasyaśrīmatvaṃ trijagatsvapi

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīvidhā tasmāt vyātā jagattraye

vedeṣu sārabhutayaṃ śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarī mama

iti madvadanodbhūta śrutibhissamudīritā

nārāyaṇetimannāma paraṃbrahma śriyānvitaḥ

anyāstu sakalāvidhāḥ prakṣipraśrī samanvitāḥ

paratejomayīṃ saṃvinmahāsāmrājyanāyikāṃ

dadāmyetāṃ mahāvidyāṃmatejaḥ prāptaye tava

** chaṃdaḥ syāgāyatraṃ nānyaduktaṃ jāyate

madaṣṭārṇamayī jñeyā mama tejaḥ kalānvitā

madardhatejasāyuktānārīlakṣmīritīritā

dvādaśārṇamahāvidhārūpo brahmamayomyahaṃ

ahaṃ jaganmayatvenacchaṃdasāṃ jāgataṃ varaṃ

brahmavidyeti vikhyātā pradānādittastava

imāmapi mahāvidyāṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇabhidhāṃ

yosau virāṅmayaḥ sākṣāt puruṣohaṃ purātanaḥ

sa eva vedapuruṣo vedanārāyaṇomyahaṃ

sahāyaṃte kariṣyaṃti sargakārye prajāpate

tadvācyaḥ savitā brahman devo nārāyaṇosmyahaṃ

janmamṛtyujarāvyādhi saṃsārabhayanāśanaḥ

mātṛkāyā samākhyātā sāmadrūpaiva vāvayate

lakṣmīrūpaṃ ca madrūpaṃ tatatsāraṃ na saṃśayaḥ

brahmavidyopāsakomevirāṭpuruṣarūpadhṛk

mamatāmupāsmanaraḥ sākṣān mamasāyujya mahyute

madātmakaṃ **(?) maṃtrayītatasmātmadātmikā

gāyatrīṃ samupāsyātha hutvāṃmāṃjātavedasi

yāvaccha samupāsyātha śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ yata

upāsyamatkālāmuko bhūtvā sṛsṭikarobhava

tvayā sṛṣṭamidaṃ viśvakāle kāle harāmyahaṃ

nārāyaṇosmyahaṃ tena sarvalokeśvareśvaraḥ

grahāṇatvatsahāyena sṛjatya vahaṃti ca

etadācārya vadanādvijñāyā ***(?)rthanaḥ

ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ śaucamiṃdriyanigrahaḥ

mama tādā**(?) siddhiṃ pratyete saptahetavaḥ

aṃtarddhānaṃ jagāmāśu nityamuktairamiṣṭutaḥ

sṛṣṭvādau sanakādibhyo vaśiṣṭāddibhya uktavān

brahmabhūtāstaponiṣṭhā adyāpi sukhi***(?)

aciṃtyaśaktitā sarvamaṃtrāṇāṃ dvijapuṃgavāḥ

śraddhayācārya vākyeṣu sarvasiddhistapodhanāḥ

kālasya vaṃcanopāyakāraṇaṃ nānyadīṣyate

jñātvācāryamukhāṃbhojāt saṃsevyācāryamanvahaṃ

parasya brahmaṇastatvasvarūpaṃ kiṃcidīritaṃ

suguptamapita tatvaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ

ācāryaḥ kīdṛśo lokeśiṣyo vāpi kathaṃ mune

tatpāda padmabhaktānāsmākaṃ tadvadāśubho

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojaśuṣaṇavidhiparaṃ

satataṃ vaidikācāranirataḥ śāstrapāragaḥ

śāṃtodāṃtojitāmitrojiteṃdriyaḥ vedādhyayanakṛnnityaṃ vedārthasya vicārakṛt

aharniśaṃ śruterjāmī śrautācāraratasyadā

śivaviṣṇorabhedena nityopāhitarato dvijāḥ

sahasrakṛtto gāyatrī japakartā dine dine

dadāmyetāṃ mahāvidyāṃmatejaḥ prāptaye tava

chaṃdaḥsyādasya gāyatraṃ nānyaduktaṃ prajāpate

maddaṣṭārṇamayī jñeyā mamattejaḥ kalānvitā

madardhatejasāyukānārīlakṣmīritīritā

dvādaśārṇa mahāvidyārūpo brahmabhayosmyahaṃ

ahaṃ jaganmayatvena chaṃdasāṃ jāgataṃ varaṃ

brahmavidyeti vikhyātā pradānādādittastava

imāmapi mahāvidyāṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇābhidhāṃ

yosau virāṅmayaḥ sākṣātpuruṣohaṃ purātanaḥ

sa eva vedapuruṣo vedanārāyaṇohmyahaṃ *(?)jño gururukto mahānayaṃ

tatsānnidhyenavidhānāṃ kartavyaṃ grahaṇaṃbhuvi

nyāsānāma parijñātā pūjāyaṃtrādikasya ca

sarvāgamarahasyārthapāragaṃ śrūti tatparaṃ

a****(?)to nityaṃ daṃbhalobhādi saṃyutaḥ

tamoguṇapradhānaśca paranārīratassadā

jihmaḥ kaṭhinavittau sau satataṃ krodha tatparaḥ

vadavāhyodurālāpī***(?) grahakārakaḥ

vedādhyayana kṛnnityaṃ daṃbhalobha vivarjitaḥ

satataṃ vaidikācāra tatparaḥ śrutipāragaḥ

piturmātuśca śuśrū*****(?) vivarjitaḥ

anvahaṃ gurupādābja śuśrūṣurvītamatsaraḥ

sakṛdvā guruniṃdāyāṃ samāsaktamanāyadi

ākruṣṭastāḍito vāpikaśābhiḥ pādatāḍanaiḥ

avaśyaṃ tasya dātavyā vidyāsvargāpavargadā

catvāro guravaḥ proktāḥ pūjyāḥ pratyahamādarāt

sevyamanvahaṃ bhaktyāpāda śuśrūṣaṇādibhiḥ

akṣapādakṛtaṃ śāstraṃ mīmāṃsā dvitayaṃ tathā

śāstraṣaṭkamiti proktamapavargādikāraṇaṃ

upādhyāya iti proktaḥ pūjyo guruvadanvahaṃ

sāṃganyāsādi sahitāṃ yaṃtrapūjādisaṃyutāṃ

ācāraṃ śikṣayennityaṃ rahasyamapi vodhayan

sākṣānnārāyaṇātmāsau vijñeyaḥ puruṣo namaḥ

yaṃtraṃnyāsavidhiṃ vāpi tadrahasya mathāpi vā

vidyāllokavacakaṃ

gṛhītvā prajapedvidhāṃ sa jñeyo naragarddamaḥ

vidmāmāśramadhītyānyaṃ sarahasyajñamuttamaṃ

vahuyojana sāhasradūraṃ gatvāpi vā dvijāḥ

ekena janmanā sākṣādbrahmānaṃdābhidhaṃ sukhaṃ

ācāryamāśrayennityaṃ prāṇairarthairdhipāpi ca

bhagavadraśmi vidyābhissahitāṃ sarahasyakāṃ

ācāryarūpiṇaṃ vidyātasevaśrīpatiṃ vibhuṃ

ācāryamagrato vastrālaṃkārādyairghanairapi

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādipurogyācāryamagnataḥ

sarvasvamapi datvā vā prāṇānapi kalevaraṃ

athekākṣaradātāraṃ guruṃdhikṛtyavāsakṛt

śvānajanmasamāsādhacaṃḍāleṣvabhijāyate

ācāryonāva saṃtavyaḥ prāṇaiḥ kaṃṭhagataiḥrapi

mahāpātakinaṃ viṃdyātameva varakālayaṃ

nahi dhanyattarastasmādadhikovidyate bhuvi

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarī vidyā **(?) vidyā tathaiva ca

tṛtīyaṃ raśmimālākhya sūktamatyaṃtapāvanaṃ

labhyate yadi dhanyosau brahmānaṃdayuto bhavet

kurvaṃti bhagavadbudhyāprāsādāṃvarabhūṣaṇaiḥ

mūlaṃ sakaladharmāṇāṃ hetuṃ svargāpavargayoḥ

nārcayaṃti vimūḍhāste pratimāsu yathā tathā

ācārya eva bhagavānsākṣānnārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

narākṛtirasausākṣādbhagavāniṃdirāpatiḥ

saṃsārasāgaraṃ tīrthāyāti brahmapadaṃ mahat

mahādānāyutairvāip vājimedhāyutairapi

dhikṛtyavāpi huṃ kṛtyaniṃdāṃ kṛtvāpi vāsakṛt

majjaṃti narakaudyeṣutyatkādehamataḥ paraṃ

śrutvāpātakamāsādyanimajjaṃtyapi raurave

ācārya evastottavyorājavidvatsabhāsu ca

ārcā evayoṣmo hi dhanavāsonnabhūṣaṇaiḥ

ācāryasadguṇā eva śrotavyāḥ pratyahaṃ budhaiḥ

ācāryāyaiva dātavyamapi sarvasvamādarāt

itthamācārya śuśrūṣāṃ kurvannapyekavatsaraṃ

itthamācārya śuśrūṣāṃ kṛtvā varṣatrayaṃ bhuvi

kurvannācārya suśrūṣāyāṃvajjīvamataṃdritaḥ

itthāmācārya śuśrūdhā kuryan pratyahamādarāt

brahmavidyānusaṃdhātāraśmimālānuciṃtakaḥ

kalevarāṃte sāyujyaṃ māpnotyeva śriyaḥ yateḥ

vidhyarthavādamaṃtrātmarūpiṇī sarvasiddhidā

dharmaśāstrāṇi divyāni kṛtavaṃtastapodhanāḥ

kṛtaṣaṃtodarśanāni ṣaḍete paramarṣayaḥ

upanīyatuyaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedamadhyāyayedvijaḥ

dvitīyo brahmāpidyāyāḥ pradātācārpaṭhacyate

nyāsayaṃtrarahasyādisahitāmidirāpateḥ

tameva bhagavadrūpaṃ prāhurācāryamuttamaṃ

nārāṇo jagatsvāmī bhagavāniṃdirāpatiḥ

śrīrāma iti lokesminvyāto dāśarathi vibhuḥ

guptaṃ rākṣasakorībhirbhinnāṃjanacayopamaṃ

vaśiṣṭhādimaharṣīsammitaṃ ******

kathayāmāsa rudrāya pṛcchate munipuṃgavāḥ

**(?) bhūtamidaṃ vedavedāṃtārtha rahasyakaṃ

patraiva brahmaṇastatvarahasyaṃ śrutisaṃgrahaṃ

rahasyamapi yatroktaṃ bhogasvargāparvargadaṃ

bhagavadvadanāṃbhojādāvirbhūtamidaṃ śubhaṃ

ācāryavadanāṃbhojādvijñeyaṃ nānyathā dvijāḥ

ślokamekamathātrāpi japtvā jīvan vimucyate

śrutvā caturmukhaḥ paścāt sanakādibhpaṭhaktavān

śrutvā vibhāṃ ukāyātha svasuttāyoktavānidaṃ

adhītavān piturvaktrādvibhāṃḍakatunedvijāḥ

brahmadarśana sidhyarthaṃ mokṣalakṣmīsamṛddhaye

idaṃ dāśarathīyākhyaṃ taṃtraṃ dṛṣṭvā maharṣayaḥ

sanatkumāro bhagavāndattāteyo jagatpatiḥ

bhārgavo jāmadagnyopiyosau bhagavadaṃ śataḥ

paṃcaite saṃhitāśca krurvedavedāṃtasaṃmmitāḥ

tat tadācārya vadanād jñātavyaṃ tatvamuttamaṃ

sevitavyaṃ bhagavataḥ sākṣātkārasyasiddhaye

kṣīyaṃte cāsyakarmāṇi tasmindaṣṭe parātmani

jarāmaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ malamūtraughasaṃkulaṃ

sevitavyaṃ sadācārya pādāṃvujayuṃgādvījāḥ

punarjarāpunarvālyamādhayovyādhayaḥ punaḥ

stotavyamarcanīyaṃ ca sevitavyaṃ dvijottamāḥ

jānakīpatināsākaṃrāmeṇa paramātmanā

kimarthamuktavānetta taṃtraṃ vedārthasaṃgrahaṃ

śṛṇudhvamṛṣayaḥ sarve rahasyamapi vakṣyate

pulastyavaṃśasaṃbhūtadurātmānaṃ mahābalaṃ

sākṣātparabrahmamayaḥ śrīrāmojagatīpatiḥ

raṇehatvādurādharṣaṃ vīraṃ trailokyarāvaṇaṃ

bhrātṛbhiḥ sahitorāyo vaśiṣṭhādhairmaharṣibhiḥ

ayodhyānagare ramye pālayāmāsamedinīṃ

sarvadevagaṇairyuktāssiddhavidyā dharānvitāḥ

tadājñayā samāsīnāḥ suśubheṣāsaneṣu*(?)

narākṛtidharaṃ sākṣānnārāyaṇamajaṃ hariṃ

draṣṭumabhyāyayau devaṃ brahmāpi caturānanaḥ

dattātreyopi bhagavānagastyaḥ ka eva eva ca

ekanaścadvitaścaivayavakrītothagālavaḥ

jāmadagnyastatorāmyedevarāto mahātapāḥ

yogācāryotha vālmīkiramamanyustaponidhiḥ

śrīrāmaṃ paramātmānaṃ nārāyaṇamajaṃ vibhuṃ

abhyāyayustayoniṣṭhā ṛṣayodīptatejasaḥ

taṃdunaṃdīśabhṛṃgīśavīrabhadragaṇeśvaraiḥ

draṣṭumiṃdīparaśyāmaṃ prasannaṃ jānakīpatiṃ

śivasya brahmaṇonyeṣāmāsanāni samādiśat

idamūcurvacotyaṃtarāmaṃ rājīvalocanaṃ

yatadakṣayamavyaktaṃ paśyāmastvāṃ jagatpate

aghanaḥ saphalā yajñā vahuśo dakṣiṇānvitāḥ

vedā api ca vedāṃtā svāmeva brahmarūpiṇaṃ

yato vāco nivartatepyaprāpyamanasāsaha

trailokyakaṃṭakaṃ vīraṃ durdharṣa mamarairapi

pālayitvā gajadidaṃ punarāgaccha tatpadaṃ

rājīvalocanaṃ rāmaṃ paśyaṃtassamupāsate

suprasannasukhaṃ śātaṃ gaurīsahita ādarāt

rāmacaṃdrakṛpāsiṃdhośaraṇāgatavatsala

ime brāhmādayo devāḥ śakrādyāścadigīśvarāḥ

kapilādyā mahāsiddhā dattātre pādayopi ca

kiṃcidvijaptumicchāmidedānujñāṃ mama prabho

ājñāyayāmāsa tathā brūhīti girijāpatiṃ

hitāya sarvalokānāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ ca nityaśaḥ

nārāyaṇātmanaḥ sākṣāt parabrahmamayaspate

saṃsārasāgaremagnāḥ kathaṃ tvāṃ brahmarūpiṇaṃ

kathaṃ tuṣṭaḥ paraṃdhāma prayacchasi jagatpate

vedā api ca vedāṃtāstavarū***(?) mahat

māṃ prerayaṃti brahmādyā amī ca paramarṣayaḥ

*(?)tyukto bhagavāṃstatra śrīrāmojagatīpatiḥ

prasannavadanaḥ prāhaśaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaraṃ

bhagavato mama rahasyamapi vedāṃtasārabhūtārthasaṃgrahaṃ

tadeva me parabrahmarūpaṃ nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ

dhyeyosmi yogeśvara siddhasaṃghaiḥ pūjyosmi nityaṃ bhuvaneṣu sarvaiḥ

nārāyaṇohaṃ nararūpadhārī rudrosmi kālokādhipatiśca śakraḥ

satyānaṃdacidākāraṃ nityaṃ śāśvatamakṣayaṃ

nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ sākṣāttamaḥpāre pratiṣṭitaṃ

na vratairnamahādānairnayāgairbahuvakṣiṇaiḥ

aśakyaṃ mama tadrūpaṃ prāptuṃ gaurīpate vibho

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyājapahomādi tatparaiḥ

raśmimālālāpakaistadrūpaṃ prāptuṃ hi śakyate

bhinnevabhāti lokesminmamāyāduratyayā

śrutirmadvadanādāvirbhūtāsāṃgāsanātanī

vāmavāhurmadīpastvaṃ viṣṇurhṛdayamevame

tatrāpi dahūṃ gaganaṃ viśokastasminpadaṃ tasvadupāsitavyaṃ

tena vācyosmi satyātmā paramabrahma śabdabhāk

nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ brahmarūpaṃ yattsaccidātmakaṃ

viśvataḥ paramaṃ nityaṃ viśvanārāyaṇātmakaṃ

akṣayaṃ śāśvataṃ divyaṃ vedāṃtairapyagocaraṃ

tadeva me mahadguptaṃ rūpaṃ jyotirathākṣayaṃ

patiṃ viśvasyātmeśvaraṃ śāśvataṃ śivamacyutaṃ

nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ jyotistatvaṃ nārāyaṇātmakaṃ śivaṃ

nārāyaṇa paraṃbrahmatatvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ parā

yacca kiṃcijjagatyasmindadṛśyate śrūyatepi vā

nārāyaṇopaniṣadāyadetetpratipādyate

nārāyaṇeti mannāmayesmaraṃ tyapi saṃtataṃ

matsvarūpa parijñāne yadi vāṃchā prajāpate

śuśrūṣaṇa parobhūtvā prāṇairarthairdhiyāpi ca

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānaṃ kṛtvā mama dine dine

madraśmi vidyāsahitāṃ samastanyā sa saṃyutāṃ

prajaptvā satataṃ bhaktyā mama sāyujya maśnute

mama vidyānusaṃdheyāsvācāryānugrahaṃvinā

madvidyā japahomādi karmānuṣṭhānamuttamaṃ

tatrāpyācārya pādābja śuśrūṣaṇamanuttamaṃ

ekena janmanovāsau madrūpaṃ soṣṇute dhruvaṃ

matsvarūparijñāne kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchāsi

bhūyopi jānakīnāthaṃ prāha lokahitaṃ vacaḥ

ete brahmarṣayaḥ siddhāstvatprasādāptavaibhavāḥ

manvādi munibhiproktamapyācāraṃ śrutīritaṃ

lakṣaṇaṃ gurubhaktasparaśmisūktaṃ śriyaḥ pateḥ

māhātmyaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ śrotuṃ kautūhalānvitāḥ

bhavatīhadvijātīnāṃ sarvametadvadasvanaḥ

brahmarṣayopiśṛ evaṃ tu siddhāye ca tapodhanāḥ

yacchrutvācārya vadanā brahmabhūto bhavediha

sārajñena mayā guhyaṃ brahmadarśanamuddhṛtaṃ

medinīṃ pālayāmāsaḥ vedādhyayana tatparaḥ

tulāhiraṇyagarbhādyairyajan devaṃ śriyaḥ patiṃ

ārādhayāmāsamuniṃgautamaṃ brahmatatparaṃ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ nityaṃ dadātyācārya karṇayoḥ

pādukādvitayaṃ kṛtvā muktālavibhūṣitaṃ

ācāyaṃ pādukāvidyāmuccaranpratyahaṃ śuciḥ

pādukādvitayasyātha kṛtvā śuśrūṣaṇaṃ namaḥ

ācāryadupādukāyugmamarcayitvānvahaṃ śuciḥ

itthaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi pādukādvayapūjanaṃ

siddhaye brahmavidyāmavāpyādau bhagavadraśmi saṃyutāṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ prāptavān gautamādguroḥ

kṛtavānniyamaṃ ghoraṃ brahmadarśanasiddhaye

sauvarṇapuṣpasāhasrairbhokṣyāmyahamataḥ paraṃ

prātaḥ samutthāya samabhyarcyopacārakaiḥ

jāti smarattvaṃ prāpto saupādukā dvitayārcanāt

anaśnanneva bhūposau japannācārya pādukāṃ

prārthitaḥ pitṛmātṛbhyāmapi brahmarṣi puṃgavaiḥ

anaśnanneva bhūpālo bhagavanyarpitāśayaḥ

prādādaṣṭākṣarīṃ siddhiṃ **(?) darśanasiddhidāṃ

athājagāmataṃ saṃbhūyaṃ draṣṭumunmattaveṣabhṛt

kṛṣṇājinottarīyaśca śiṣyeṇaikena saṃyutaḥ

bhūtvā paramahaṃ soyaṃ sākṣānnārāyaṇāvibhuḥ

pādukā dvitayārūḍhaṃ śiṣyeṇaikena saṃyutaṃ

dadarśapādukāyugmaṃ tenārūḍhaṃ mahātmanā

bhūyo bhūyonamaskṛtyasvarṇapuṣpairapūjayat

tanmastaketha dṛṣṭvāsau vismayaṃ prāpasādaraṃ

dṛṣṭvā na māmasāṣṭāṃgaṃdhanyosmīti muhurvadan

dṛṣṭvā nimīlitākṣausau pādayugme yamāhataha

athāyaśmaccharīresya sarvametaccarācaraṃ

śakrādīniṣṭa dikpālānsādhyāṃśca samarudgaṇān

viśvametadaśeṣeṇa dṛṣṭvā macchaśuronyāḥ

dṛṣṭvā vismayamāpannaṣyunastatpādukādvayaṃ

sāṣṭāṃgaṃ daṃḍavannatvā śruti sūktairanekaśaḥ

apaśyat pūrvavadvipraṃ jaṭāvalkyalasaṃyutaṃ

ācārya pādukāyugmapūjanādgurutoṣaṇāt

sākṣānnārāyaṇāsyedaṃ darśanaṃ paramātmanaḥ

āsanaṃ kalpayāmāsayogiveṣadharasyahi

arghyapādyādibhirdivyagaṃdhaiśca kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ

ācāryapādukāyugmamagnebhyarcyaiva śaṃkara

uvāca vacanaṃ bhūyaṃ kṛtāṃjalipuraṃ sthitaṃ

tattaddāsyāmi bhūyopi prārthitādadhikaṃ phalaṃ

kimanyadvarayebhīṣṭaṃ sakalaṃ prāptamadya me

ācāryapādukāyugmapūjāpratyahamastume

tvadvidyābhajanemestudṛḍhābhaktissadā mama

prāhanārāyaṇoyogīnareṇasahito vibhuḥ

tatadastu mahīpāla nityamācāryasevayā

tvatayaḥ phalataḥ sarvetvadanyapa sa muktvāḥ

ahaṃ dāśarathirbhūtvālakṣmaṇānucaro bhuvi

śrīryācidrūpiṇī śaktirmamavedeṣu viśrutā

avāpta sarvabhāgyastvaṃ bhūtvā vigatakalmaṣaḥ

ityuktvā bhagavāṃstūṣṇīmāsīnaḥ samyagāsane

prasādābhimukhaṃ vīkṣya ****(?) sanātanaṃ

uvāca janakorājā kṛtāṃjaliridaṃ vacaḥ

hitāya sarvalokānāṃ kiṃcidvijñaptumudyataḥ

gurupādābja śuśrūṣā kartavyā kathamādarāt

vidhinā kena kartavyaṃ tvadvidyābhajanaṃ vibho

pṛṣṭametattrijagatāmupakārakamādarāt

yatatsatyaciānaṃdamakhaṃḍaṃ brahmanirguṇaṃ

saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ ceti rūpadvitayamuttamaṃ

saccidānaṃdamadvaitaṃ jyotirūpaṃ parātparaṃ

saguṇaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhāra sthitikāraṇamadbhutaṃ

saguṇasya mamodbhūtāvadanācchutirāśayaḥ

ajñāchedī mamadrohīdehāṃte narakālayaḥ

nārāyaṇapadaṃ nityaṃ dehāṃte praviśatyasau

ācāryapādāṃvujasevamāthanārāyaṇānugrahabhāgasau bhavet

teṣuniṣṭhassadācārayutomadbhajanerataḥ

ācāryapādukāsevāpurassaramathādarāt

samastanyāsasahitaśrīmadṛṣṭāsarīṃ japan

brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprasthopi saṃtataṃ

devatāṃ maṃtrarūpiṇyo maṃtrāvedeṣu saṃsthitāḥ

sarvavedamayā viprāḥ sarvadevamayo hariḥ

pṛṣṭo vadarikāvāsīprāhanārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

sadācāraṃ parabrahmāparokṣajñānasiddhidaṃ

ācārāvyāpyane saukhyamācārāt prāpyate yaśaḥ

adhītyavedānvedau vā vedaṃ vā śaktitodvijaḥ

rajanī prāṃtayāmasya pūrvārddhaṃ brāhmyanāmakaḥ

saṃdhyāparārdhakālaḥ syāccaturnāḍī mahīpate

jananīcaraṇau smṛtvā sarvatīrthottamottamau

caraṇau paṃcadhā smṛtva śriyaḥ patimathācyutaṃ

gajāsyamatha saṃsmṛtya viśveśaṃ pārvatī vibhuṃ

aruṃdhatyādi sādhvīśca devarṣīnnāradādikān

kavīvālmīkimukhyāṃścatvadādīndvayatinapi

kiṃ karānyāyuputrādīnmahādidṛḍhān

vasuṃdharāṃ namaskṛtya daṃkṣaṃ pādaṃ mahītale

prāṃjaliḥ svagurūnsarvānnatvā brahmaviletanaḥ

dhyātvā tatpadakamalagalatpīyūṣa viṃdubhiḥ

mūlādi brahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ jvalaṃtīṃ saṃvidaṃ parāṃ

āsaneramyagāsīnaḥ prāṇānāyasya vāgpataiḥ

jptvā dvādaśadhāvadhinatvācārya padāṃvujaṃ

ācārya caraṇāṃbhojārūḍhaṃ śrīpādukādvayaṃ

dāruṇā nirmitaṃ vāpi śubhalagneśubhedine

prātaḥ prātaḥ samutthāya śreya icchannanuttamaṃ

ācāryapādukāvidyāmuccaranpraṇamenṛpa

pāpakaṃ bhūpatiṃ vipravaryaṃ vedāṃtapāragaṃ

prātarutthāyayo nityaṃ pādukā dvitayaṃ guroḥ

jāhnavyādīni tīrthāni saptako pṛṣayopi ca

ācāryapādukābhāvepyācārya caraṇadvayaṃ

upacāraiḥ samabhyarcya manasā kalpitaiḥ śubhaiḥ

brahmavidyāpradātāyasmarayācārya ucyate

bhagacadraśmi vidyābhiḥ sahitāṃ sarvasiddhidāṃ

sarāvabhagavadrūpī mahānācārya ucyate

tameva brahma tādātmya siddhimūlaṃ vidurbudhāḥ

ācāryākṛtimannityaṃ saṃpūjyoghaṃghodinedine

bhagavadraśmi vidyānāṃ kramasūktamathojape

saṃsmṛtya bhagavatsūktaṃ japennārāyaṇaṃ sakṛn

vāsasāśira āvṛtyaṣṭīvanocchāsavarjitaḥ

udaṅmukho malaṃ mūtramaharbhāge parityajet

śuddhāṃ mṛdaṃ samādāyadadyālliṃge mṛdaṃ sakṛt

daśavāmakare dadyādubhayossaptamṛtikāḥ || 38 |||

etacchau ca gṛhasthasya dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ

śaucācāravihīnasya niṣphalāḥ saphalāḥ kriyāḥ

pratipaddarśa ṣaṣṭhīṣu navamyāṃ ravivāre

tṛṇaparṇaissadā kuryādayādamāmekādaśīṃ vinā

snānaṃ kuryānmahānadyāṃ taḍāge vā hṛdepi ca

tasmāt snānamṃ prakartavyaṃ vāhyāṃtarmalanāśanaṃ

prājāpatya samaṃ prāhustanmahādyavighātanaṃ

dhyātvā snānaṃ prakurvīta sarvapāpa viśuddhaye

keśavādi caturviṃśannāmāni manasāsmaran

ekasmin divase vāpi snānaṃ kṛvattha mādarāt

snātvātha vāsasī śuddhe paridhāya samāhitaḥ

udayāt prāktaṃnī saṃdhyāṃ ghaṭikā tritayāṃ viduḥ

saṃdhyākarma paṭityāgī sarvakarma vahiṣkṛtaḥ

tyaktā saṃdhyābhidhaṃ karmayonyatra kurute pratiṃ

prātasāṃdhyāṃ sa nakṣatrāmupāsīta yathāvidhi

arddhāmita nakṣatramaṃtarikṣaṃ yadā bhavet

prāṇānāyamma saṃkalpa saṃdhyākarma samāret

ayamevahi dṛṣṭāṃto vaṃdhyāstrīsaṃgamo yathā

gāyatrī maṃtratoyādyaṃ dattaṃ yenājalitrayaṃ

tena vajrodakenāśumaṃdehānāmarākṣasāḥ

saṃdhyākarmaparityāgī maṃdehorākṣaso bhavet

pradakṣiṇī kṛtā tena saptādvīpavanīmahī

āsanastho japenmaunī pratyagātārakodayāt

savinābhagavānsākṣādbrahmarūpīśriyaḥ patiḥ

tanmaṃḍalagataṃ dhyātvā devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ vibhuṃ

sahasra paramāṃ devīśatamadhyaṃ daśāparaṃ

gāyatrīṃ prajapennityaṃ pātakodyaiḥ pramucyate

japetyaṃ ca śataṃ vāpi catuśatamathāpi vā

aṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ japtvā brāhmaṇaḥ syāddinedine

sahasra kṛtvo gāyatrīṃ japaṃtaṃ pratpadaṃ śuci

na vedapāṭhādbrāhmaṇyaṃ na śāstrapaṭhanādapi

aśaktaḥ prajapennityaṃ tiṣṭhannāsī na eva vā

udite tu sahasrāṃśau tanmaṃḍala lagataṃ vibhuṃ

dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ śriyāsahitamacyutaṃ

diśāmapi namaskuryāddi devebhyastataḥ paraṃ

punarācamaśuddhātmā ravimaṃḍalamadhyagaṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ saṃdhyāṃ kṛtvā sidhyarthamādarāt

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ nyāsādi rahitāmapi

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā vāpi noce*(?)rddharna jāyate

kṛtāṃjali puṭo bhūtvā sūktaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ japet

gāyatryā vāhya tanmadhye yajñeśaṃ śrīpatiṃ vibhuṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣareṇāthagavyamājyaṃ tadnahīpate

hutvā dvādaśadhā vahnāvaddhaṃga śrīpateratha

ṛksrāmayajuṣāṃ pāṭhaṃ brahmayajñārthamācaret

ravau śukre trayodaśyāṃ saptamyāṃ niśisaṃdhyayoḥ

kāmya vrateṣu juhuyādraśmividyābhirekadhā

ācāryacaraṇāṃ bhojadvayaṃ brahmavile śubhe

prāṇā nārāyamya saṃkalpya samastanyāsasaṃyutāṃ

japenpaṃcaśataṃ vāpi catuḥśatamathāpi vā

nārāyaṇena sūktena stutvā devaṃ śriyaḥ patiḥ

upeyādatharājānaṃ yogakṣemārthasiddhaye

saṃdhyāṃ māhnikīṃ kuryādādityābhimukhaḥ śuciḥ

prātarmadhyāhnavelāyāṃ snāśaktopi satataṃ

madhyāhnepi yathā śaktyā gāyatrīṃ samupāsya ca

ācāryapādukāyugmaṃ yadi tiṣṭhati maṃdire

pādukā sannidhau nityaṃ kiṃcidārpadakṣiṇāṃ

vatsaratritarpanaiva pādukārcanato guṇaiḥ

prāpnoti mahatīṃ siddhiṃ maṃtrāṇāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

liṃge vā pūjayennityaṃ pārvatīparameśvarau

hutvā dvādaśadhā vahnāvannaṃgavyaghṛtaplutaṃ

ācāryaṃ bhojayetasya patnīmapi sutānapi

yadvā varṣāśanamitaṃ dhanamācāryapādayoḥ

tathāpyaharahaḥ kiṃciddeyamācāryapādayoḥ

rudreṇa bhuṃktaṃ nirmālāmabhojyamiti kathyate

ācāryabhuktaśiṣṭaṃ syādamṛtaṃ pāpanāśanaṃ

pavitraṃ kathyate vedeno cedaśuci tatsmṛtaṃ

ahutvāśī kṛmīnbhuṃkte adatvā viṣabhugbhavet

atithiṃtaṃ vijānīyādbhojayetaṃ prayatnataḥ

ṣaḍeneharaharbhojyā annairbahuvidhairapi

aparāmṛśyayobhuṃkte sabhuṃkte narakaṃ dhruvaṃ

aparāmṛśyate bhuṃkte sabhuṃkte kilviṣaṃ dhruvaṃ

bhojayedaśanādyaiśca coṣyalehyādibhistathā

bhuktvā yatheṣṭaṃ hastau ca prakṣālyacaraṇau mukhaṃ

saṃsārayogakṣemāṃtamathagacchedvahiḥ pumān

kṛtvā sāyaṃtanaṃ homamarddhāṃgaṃ śrīpatermanuṃ

cakrarvyūhātmakasyātha maṃtrāmānuṣṭumaṃ paraṃ

tāṃbūlāmodivadano gurupādāṃvujadvayaṃ

smṛtvā śriyaḥ patiṃ devamāṃjaneyaṃ tataḥ paraṃ

kurvannitya mahorātraṃ karmavaidikamādarāt

yatkṛtvā manujoloke śreyo mahadavāpnuyāt

vaidikācārahīnoyojīvanneva sa paṃcamaḥ

hiraṇyābhyudayaśrāddhaṃ kuryādācamya yatnataḥ

tāṃvūlagaṃdhamālyānibhūṣaṇānyapi suvrataḥ

jātake mṛtake cāpi saṃdhyākarma na saṃtyajet

ekādaśehni kurvītanāma karma tataḥparaṃ

garbhāṣṭamemāvdevābrāhmaṇasyoyanāthanaṃ

brahmacārī tadāramya vedādhyana siddhaye

ājñāṃnollaṃghaye tasya samitpuṣpakuśādikān

kṛtvā bhikṣādanaṃ nityaṃ gurave vinivedayet

tatpūśūnyālayennityaṃ tadgṛhaṃ mārjayet sadā

vedānadhītyavedau vā vedaṃ vā śaktito dvijaḥ

ananyapūrvikāṃ suptāṃ niṣkrāṃtāṃrudatīmapi

raktakeśaśmaśumatīṃpiṃgākṣīṃ vikṛtastavīṃ

varjayedvaraṇe kanyārātārājanmahāmate

śrotriyānvaya saṃbhūtāṃ gṛhārcanaratāṃ sadā

gṛhasaukhyasamṛdhyarthaṃ lakṣaṇyāṃ striyamudvahet

pratikūlaṃ kalatraṃ cetkiṃduḥkhaṃ narakobhavet

dharmeratiḥ sajjanasaṃgatiścaduḥsvasya mūloddharaṇāni paṃca

ca tasro rātrayaḥ styājyā viṣamāsuna saṃviśet

ekādaśīparvavarjaṃ strīṣu saṃgamamācaret

anṛtaṃ madyagaṃdha ca divāhyā paṃcamaithunaṃ

devāścapitaronityaṃ gṛhastha mṛṣayopi ca

sarvāśrama variṣṭhoyaṃ gṛsthāśrama ucyate

śrāddhakṛtsadyavādī ca gṛhasthomuktamucyate

vedapārāyaṇaṃ kurvanvedasyārthaṃ vicāra*(?)

śrotriyānvayasaṃbhūtāṃ gṛhārcanaratāṃ sadā

gṛhasaukhyasamṛdhyarthaṃ lakṣaṇyāṃ striyamudvahet

pratikūlaṃ kalatraṃ cetkiṃduḥkhaṃ narake bhavet

dharmeratiḥ sajjanasaṃgatiśca duḥkhasya mūloddharaṇāni paṃca

catasrorātrayaḥ styājyā viṣamāsunasaṃviśet

ekādaśīparvavarjaṃ strīṣu saṃgamamācaret

anṛtaṃ madyagaṃdhaṃ ca divāsvāyaṃ paṃcamaithunaṃ

devāścapitaronityaṃ gṛhasthamṛṣayopi ca

sarvāśramavariṣṭhoyaṃ gṛsthāśrama ucyate

śrāddhakṛtsadyavādī ca gṛhastho mukta ucyate

viprajanmaśatenāsau brahmaloke mahīyate

kalyāṃtaṃ satyalokepi bhuktvā bhogānmanoharān

sadācāraratonityaṃ vedapārāyaṇerataḥ

kṣayaṃ mahatsaukhyaṃ kalpasahasramādarāt

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja śuśrūṣaṇarataḥ śuciḥ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ samastanyā saṃyutāṃ

punarāratirahitaṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

na tena prāpyate vidyā śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarī śubhā

maheṃdralokemādaṃte kalpāṃtaṃ divyabhoginaḥ

kṛtvā sukṛtakarmāṇi punaḥsvargasukhānvitāḥ

punarjanma punarmṛtyuḥ punarduḥkhaṃ punassukhaṃ

pāpe narakeduḥkhaṃ puṇye na tridive sukhaṃ

svargepi duḥkhasaṃmitaṃśraṃ viṣayuktānnavatsukhaṃ

brahmānaṃdābhidhaṃ prāhurbrahmatatvavidomalāḥ

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānācchīmadaṣṭākṣarī japāt

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojadakṣiṇārahitaṃ dinaṃ

yadyekajanmanā brahmatādātmyapadamicchati

brahmavidyā samāyuktāṃ raśmividyāsamanvitāṃ

gṛhastha eva sarveṣāmāśramāṇāṃvara smṛtaḥ

gṛhasthāśramamāsādya sadācāreṇa saṃyutaṃ

devarṣi pitṛsaṃtoṣātatprāsādāpta vaibhavaḥ

modate apramedhāmni daśapūrvairdaśāparaiḥ

satyapūtaṃ vadedvākyaṃ nyāyapūtaṃ dhanaṃ grahet

aṃtyajānapibhikṣitvā dadyādācārya dakṣiṇāṃ

suvarṇadakṣiṇāṃ datvā yāgasya phalamaśnute

vahuśo dakṣiṇādeyātasmādyāgasamṛddhaye

datvāśvamedhasāhasradakṣiṇāphalāmaśru*(?)

rocate caṃdrabhaumādyā ahāpi divisthitāḥ

satyena lokān jayati muktiḥ satyena pratiṣṭhitā

yoginaḥ sanakādyāśca vaśiṣṭhādyā maharṣayaḥ

maheṃdrādyāśca dikpālāḥ pūrvabhūtapatayopi ca

bhagavadvacanaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ vedoditaṃ tathā

brahmatatvavidonityaṃ vedavedāṃtapāraṇāḥ

etannārāyaṇīyākhyaṃ satyamevocyate mayā

ahaṃdāśarathirbhūtvā rudrāya paripṛcchate

taṃtraṃ dāśarathīyākhyaṃ tadārabhya maharṣayaḥ

asmindāśarathīyākhye taṃtre yadyadudīritaṃ

tasmādgṛhasthaḥ satataṃ devānāṃ tṛptiḥmācaran

brahmavidyānusaṃdhātā śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīrataḥ

nārāyaṇamayo bhūtvā modate paramepade

prāpteṣva labhyayogeṣu navarātrāpratepi ca

dātavyaṃ ca mahīdānaṃ godānaṃ cāvaśeṣataḥ

ācāryakarṇayorddevamavaśyaṃ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

vāsare bhārgavevaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ śrīsamṛddhaye

tryācāryapatnīsatataṃ vahulaṃ karaṇādibhiḥ

mahālakṣmīdhiyā śiṣyaiścaturvargaphalāptaye

ācārya eva pātrāṇāmuttamaṃ pātramucyate

pātramityucyate vedeśāstreṣu ca mahīpate

ṣaṇmāse vatsare vāpi dehasaṃśuddhi hetave

ācāryadakṣiṇādravyaṃ yācayennāpi tatkule

datvā yāgadhanaṃ sākṣāttaddarṣe brahma paśyati

paṭhanaṃ dharmaśāstrāṇāmitihāsapurāṇayoḥ

ācāryadakṣiṇāhīnaṃ vaṃdhyamityucyate dinaṃ

samastavidyā sidhyarthaṃ brahmajñā phalāptaye

purāṇapustakaṃ vāpi dadhānmuktiphalāptaye

aviyogavatīmṛddhiṃpatnīputrāditissadā

paṃcayajñāḥ sadākāryā brāhmaṇaiḥ śaṃsitavrataiḥ

tato manuṣyayajñaḥ syādbrahmayajñastataḥ paraṃ

devayajña iti proktaḥ svargaḥ sādhana īritaḥ

pitṛyajña iti proktaḥ kālavādhānivārakaḥ

yadvijebhyonnadānaṃ syānnarayajñaṃ sa ucyate

yodhīte brahmayajñoyaṃ sa ukto brahmavittamaiḥ

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ kiṃcidyathā śakta*(?)marpayet

sadguṇaiśvaryasaṃpatiḥ patiḥ padbhiryāgaiḥ prājāyate

devarṣipitṛpītyarthaṃ pacayajñāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

madhyāhnakāle saṃprāpte datvā bhūtavaliṃ bhuvi

atithiṃ tatra saṃprāptaṃ pūjayet svagatādinā

śraddhayānna pradānena priyamaśrotareṇa ca

ajñātakulanāmācadūradeśāddumāgataḥ

svādhyāyaṃgotracaraṇamapṛṣṭāṃ ca tathākulaṃ

atithināṃ sahasrāṇi yasya bhuktānimaṃdire

ārcācaraṇāṃbhojadvayaprakṣālitaṃ jalaṃ

samastatīrthasnānasya phalasiddhan mahīpate

yatiśca brahmacārī ca pakvānnasvāmināvubhau

yatihaste jalaṃ dadyāt bhaikṣyaṃ dadyātyubharjalaṃ

ācāryo vimukho gṛhṇāngṛhānpratinivartate

ācāryapatniputrāṇāmavaśyaṃ poṣaṇaṃ vudhaiḥ

pratijñātaṃ manovāgbhyāṃ daridrāya kuṭuṃvine

śīghrameva pradātavyaṃ tadṛṇaṃ svargasaukhyadaṃ

api merusamājñeyāmāsādūrdhvamṛṇaṃ mahat

māsābhyaṃtarattodeyaṃ dvimāsābhyaṃtarepivā

yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti sadācāryasya pādayoḥ

trīṇyāhuratidānāni kanyāpṛthvīsarasvatī

brahmalokaṃ vrajatyaṃte pitaraḥ svargabhāginaḥ

vipraṃ vivāhapitvaikaṃ daridraṃ vedapāragaṃ

kachṛtyupuṣpakaṃ divyamāruhya pitṛbhissaha

gaṇyaṃ tepāṃśavobhūmergaṇyaṃte divitārakāḥ

viprāya vedaviduṣe daridrāya mahīpateḥ

āṃcaṃdratārakaṃ prāpya brahmalokevasatyasau

śrotriyātha daridrāya caturvṛṣayutāṃ dadan

pakvasasyayutāṃ pṛthvī catuḥpuṃgavasaṃyutāṃ

pitṛṇāmapi sāhasraṃ nimagnaṃ narakārṇave

bhagavatsadṛśākāromodate bhagavatpade

brāhmaṇaḥ pūjyatāṃ prāpya brahmaṇā sahamodate

pradattvā pustakaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ pārago bhavet

śālagrāmaśilādānavamīvamahaṃdaṃtaraṃ

śālagrāma śilāmekāmapi dakṣidāyānvitāṃ

brahmāṃḍakoṭidānena yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ

annaṃ nityaṃ pradātavyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo viśeṣataḥ

payasvinīṃ savatsāṃgāṃkāṃsyarohasamanvitāṃ

alaṃkṛtya daridrā yayo dadāti kuṭuṃvine

patrakṣīravahānadyo yatra pāpa sakarddamāḥ

ācāryāya viśeṣeṇa savatsāṃgāṃ payasvinīṃ

samarpya bhagavaddhāmni pitṛbhiḥ sahamodate

taddātāpitṛsāhasrasahito bhagavatyade

gavāṃvikrayakṛdviprāḥ gṛhītānāṃ mahīpate

tryalaṃkāreṣu sarveṣu padmarāga samanvitaṃ

divyāṃ laṃkaraṇaṃ proktaṃ tadatvācāryakarṇayoḥ

bhagavatsadṛśākāro vaikuṃṭheva sati dhruvaṃ

aśvadānākuvereṇa sāmyaṃ prāpnoti tatpade

āṃdolikāṃ pradadyādya ācāryāya viśeṣataḥ

garuḍadhvajamāruhyavimānaṃ divyabhogayuk

prāpnoti pitṛbhiḥ sākaṃ nārāyaṇasasākṛtiḥ

nityasaṃpūjito brahmapade saramate dhruvaṃ

brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samarpyārtha svācāryāya viśeṣataḥ

śākarūpa ghṛtādīniyodadyādguravenvahaṃ

annaṃ niveditaṃ viṣṇorhaviḥ śeṣeṇa saṃyutaṃ

bhuktvā sakṛtaiḥ pāpaistatkṣaṇādevamucyate

paṃcagrāsaṃmahāmaunaṃ prāṇādyāpyāyanaṃ yataḥ

noparaktaṃ navāristhaṃ na nagnāṃ ca parastriyaṃ

noccāsane ca nivasennaśamyāyāṃ guroḥ svapet

divāpādukamāruhya na codgrāma madhyamai

pradāṃ puṃsāṃ divāpāpapradāṃ puṃsāṃ tadyoniṃ na nirīkṣapet

upatiṣṭhedyathānyāyaṃ samyagā campakpārthiva

anyatra sūtakāśau ca vibhramāturabhītitaḥ

prāyaścitāyate bhūmāvapi viprottayo nṛpa

upatiṣṭhennaraḥ saṃdhyāya svayaṃścadināṃtyajāṃ

vrajamtite durātmānastāmisraṃ narakaṃ dhruvaṃ

avaśyaṃ muktimāpnoti daśapūrvairdaśatparaiḥ

jñānaṃ ca vimalīkurvannabhyāsenanuyācayet

anāśramī na tiṣṭhetakṣaṇamekamapi kvacit

catvāra āśramā ete dvijānāmavanīpate

putreṣu bhāryāṃ nikṣipya kalatrasahitopi vā

hutvā sāyaṃ tathā prātaḥ kṛtvā cāṃdrāyaṇādikaṃ

devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca pitṛṇāṃ tṛptimācaran

smarannārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ padaṃ viṣṇoravāpnuyāt

viśeṣaṇa sadācārya śuśrūṣāmeva saṃtataṃ

vibhūte sannamusalevyaṃgorebhuktavartite

sadānārāyaṇecittaṃ kṛtvācāryasya vaṃdanaṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāmavaśyaṃ prajapan śuciḥ

ekavastra dhanodaṃḍa kamaṃḍalu dhanastathā

adhomukhaḥ sadāstrīṣu vedāṃtaśrayaṇerajaḥ

ācārya eva sarveṣāṃ gatirnānyo vidhīyate

raviḥ somo mahendraśca pitāmātāguruḥsakhā

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoje dvayameva satāṃgatiḥ

ataḥ paraṃ mahīpāla kiṃbhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi

vedoditaḥ sadācāraḥ śruttastavamukhāṃvujāt

brāhmaṃ samayamārabhya kiṃ vā kartavyamādarāt

raśmimālā prajapyeti tvayoktaṃ bhagavanmama

śṛṇu rājanpravakṣyāmi yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi

utthāya samaye brāhme sāvadhānamanādvijaḥ

praṇavaṃ citkalāvījamuccāryādau samāhitaḥ

rūpācāryapadaṃ paścāchrī pūrvācārya nāma ca

ācāryapādukāvidyāguptatarāśubhā

mahāpātakaṃ saṃhartrīvidyānāṃ siddhidāyinī

chaṃdogāyatramityaktamācāryo bhagavanmayaḥ

ācāryakaruṇāsiddhai viniyoga udāhṛtaḥ

tadabhidhānapūrvaṃ guruṃ

karuṇāpūrṇanetrāṃtaṃ prasannamukhapaṃkajaṃ

śuklavastraparidhānaṃ divyabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaṃ

dātāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalaṃ

dhyātvetthaṃ samaye brāhme japedācāryapādukāṃ

japahomārhatā sarvavidyānāmasya jāyate

paṃcopacārairabhyarcya puṣpairdvādaśadhārcayan

samarpyācāryapādābje pradatvātha dine dine

apiśākaghṛtādīni samarpya pratyahaṃ bhuvi

japādāvadhikārīsyādrupadeśaṃ vināpi ca

arcayitvā puraścaryāṃ vinā siddho bhavatyasau

abhyarcyagaṃ puṣpādyairvarṣāśanamitaṃ dhanaṃ

pradatvā tadabhāve tu tasya putrāya vārpayet

datvā yatphalamāpnoti tatodhikaphalaṃ labhet

mahatīṃ siddhimāpnoti tasmin varṣena saṃśayaḥ

japtvādau mahatīṃ siddhiṃ vidyānāṃ labhate nṛpa

icchatā mahatīṃ lakṣmīṃ japtavyācāryapādukā

koṭi sahasraṃ japtvāpi vidyā siddhi na viṃdati

yasya smaraṇamātreṇa nityaṃ pāpairvimucyate

hyāvitryaṣṭākṣarī vidyā sarvādhivyādhibhaṃjanī

caturdaśākṣarī vidyādvaipāya nama mahāmuneḥ

brahmavidyā trijagatāṃ smartavyā sarvasiddhidā

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarī vidyā smaraṇī patitaḥ paraṃ

samastasaṃpatsāmrājya mahāsiṃhāsaneśvarī

tato vasuṃdharā vidyāmokṣātkalpalatābhidhā

dvādaśārṇā mahāvidyā tato nārāyaṇīśubhā

haṃsanārāyaṇī vidyāsmaraṇīyā tataḥ paraṃ

aṣṭākṣara mahālakṣmīsahitāpi tataḥ paraṃ

ānuṣṭubhākhyavidyāthahayagrīvaya bhūpate

tataḥ kūrma mahāvidyāvarāhasya tataḥ paraṃ

tato bhārgavarāmasya vidyādāśarathestataḥ

vidyāthakalkirūpasya śrīpateḥ paramātmanaḥ

sudarśanamahāvidyā samastabhayanāśinī

bhagavadraśmi mālepaṃ proktā sakalasiddhidā

ādau mayaiva kathita brahmaṇe parameṣṭine

vakṣyate sarvadevānāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ ca sannidhau

arddhāṃga śrīpatebhirvighāṣoḍaśārṇā taponidhe

vinā vasuṃdharā vidyāṃ japyate kaiścidapyathaḥ

vidyāmetāṃ vijānī hi saṃpūrṇāṃ raśmimālikāṃ

ekaikasyāśca vidyāyāvarṇituṃ phalamuttamaṃ

raśmisrajamimāṃ vidyāṃ samastaphalasiddhidāṃ

ācāryakaruṇāpūrṇā yasya viprasya jāyate

vahusaṃvatsaraṃ vāpi svācāryaṃ karuṇānidhiṃ

raśmimālāpradaḥ svāmīraśmimālāpradaḥ pitā

ayameva sadācāryo vijñeyaḥ śāstravittamaiḥ

ātharvaṇa mahāvedesārabhūtā manūttamāḥ

imāmātharvaṇīṃ vidyāmācāryaṃ bahuvārṣikaṃ

saṃprāpya satataṃ brāhme samayeśaṃsitavrataḥ

prajñaptvā sarvavidyānāṃ puraścaryā phalaṃ labhet

ācāryakaruṇāpūrṇāḥ prajapedraśmimālikāṃ

sarvavedemayastasmādbhagavadraśmimālikāṃ

yena prajapyate nityaṃ bhagavadraśmimalikā

samastadevatā asya varadāḥ syurnasaṃśayaḥ

raśmimālā prajaptavyā paṃcadhā vā tridhāpi vā

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīvidyāmapinyāsa samanvitāṃ

ekasyā api vidyāyāssaptadvīpavatī mahī

tasmādavaśyamācāryaṃ gatvāpi bahuyojanaṃ

saṃsarasāgaraṃ tartumicchanne kena janmanā

brahmavidyā japāṃte vā tatpūrvamatha vā sadā

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japatvā brahmānaṃdasukhī bhavet

idaṃ dāsarathīyākhyaṃ taṃtraṃ vedārthasammitaṃ

saṃprāpya bahudūraṃ vātamevācāryamāśrayet

ta eva mahatīṃ siddhiṃ vrajaṃtīha paratra ca

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dvayameva dine dine

ihāpi mahatīṃ siddhiṃ prāpyaṃte muktimāpnuyāt

itaḥ paraṃ mahārāja kiṃ punaḥ śrotusicchasi

bhagavadraśmimāleyaṃśutā tavamukhāṃvujāt

ekaikasyāśca vidyāyā māhātmyaṃ kramaśo vada

tasya śravaṇamātreṇa mahatīṃ siddhimaśnute

śriyaḥpaterbhagavattoraśmimālāttataḥ paraṃ

tatrādau saṃpravakṣyāmi gāyatrīṃ vedamātaraṃ

praṇavo bhūrbhuvaḥ paścātsuvarītyakṣaradvayaṃ

bhargodevasya śabdaṃ ca dhīmahīti tataḥ paraṃ

vyāhṛtitrayasaṃyuktāgāyatrī triṃśadakṣarā

mahāpāpopapāpaugha saṃhatrī siddhidāyinī

tulitā brahmaṇā pūrvamadhikābhūdiyaṃ parā

brāhmaṇyaṃ jāyate rājan sāvitrya dhyayanaṃ vinā

śriyaḥpaterbhagavato devasya jagatāṃ pateḥ

vayaṃ dhīmahi hṛtpadme sohameveti sattataṃ

asmākaṃ nodhiyo nityamaṃtarpyātmyānāsthitaḥ

vedamātturayaṃ proktaḥ śrutyukto yo mahāmate

nārāyaṇo vedamayo mahāpuruṣa īśvaraḥ

na gāyatrī samosaṃtronāyodhyā sadṛśī purī

nācārya caraṇāṃbhojadvayena sadṛśī gatiḥ

sāvitrīmanupāsyāddauyonyatra kurute matiṃ

anupāsyānvahaṃ vedamātaraṃ vahuśo nṛpa

tasmādvaśyaṃ gāyatrīṃ prajaptvā vahuśotvahaṃ

sahasraṇe japedyastu sāvitrīmanvahaṃ śuciḥ

bahuyojana sāhasramapi saṃprāpyataṃ dvijaṃ

ekataḥ sakalā vedāyāgāstīrthavratādayaḥ

sāhasrakṛtvo gāyatrīmavaśyaṃ pratyahaṃ japet

vā triśataṃ dviśataṃ cāpi japedaṣṭottaraṃśataṃ

gāyatryeva paraṃbrahmagāyatryevaparohariḥ

gāyatryevatrayī sākṣācchabdabrahmasvarūpiṇī

rājarṣitvaṃ parityajya brahmarṣi padamāptavān

chaṃdo gāyatrame tasyāḥ savitā parameśvaraḥ

mukhamagniḥ śirobrahmāśikhārudroṃvikāpatiḥ

viniyogaścaturvargāphalasiddhyarthamīritaḥ

prāṇāyāme prajaptavyākulaṃbhakena tridhānvahaṃ

gāyatrī vācamādyaṃtarahitaṃ sarvakāraṇaṃ

dhyātvā samastavedānāṃmātaraṃ pūjayetparāṃ

puraścaryāsya kathitā bhagavadrūpasiddhidā

mahāpāpopapāpaudyairapimāsena mucyate

prajaptvā bhagavadrūpīsiddhānāmadhiyo bhavet

brahmaghnaśca surāyaśca svarṇastepī ca mucyate

sahasraṃ sahaṇe hutvā tilaistatphalamaśnute

bhānumaṃḍala madhyasthaṃ bhagavaṃtaṃ prapaśyati

mātaraṃ sarvavedānāmanvahaṃ bahuśo japet

puraścaraṇā koṭyāpi maṃtrasiddhiṃ na viṃdati

hutvādau sarvavidyānāṃ homasya phalamaśnute

sarveṣāṃ mahasāṃ rāśiḥ kalātmākālanāyakaḥ

bhagavānsavitetktaḥ śrīśonārāyaṇocyutaḥ

ayameva parorudrastrayīrūpoyamacyutaḥ

taṃ vijānī hi hṛtpadmamadhyagaṃ sarvadehināṃ

nārāyaṇaṃ jagannāthaṃ stuvaṃti satataṃ vibhuṃ

yajūṃṣi trijagadvaṃdyaṃ viśvarūpaṃ parātparaṃ

sāmāni saṃstuvaṃtyenaṃ śriyaḥ patimanīśvaraṃ

savitābhagavānsākṣāttriguṇostrijagatpatiḥ

trayāṇāmapi vedānāṃ phalamadhyayanāllabhet

jāyakaḥ sarvavidyānāmapi nityamasau bhavet

sāvitrāśaṣṭākṣaraṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣvā vahito nṛpa

praṇavaṃ ghṛṇirityuktvā sūrya ityakṣaradvayaṃ

sarvādhivyādhisaṃhartrī sarvaroganivarhiṇī

devabhāgosya saṃprokto munirbrahmavidāṃvaraḥ

bhagavatprītaye prokto viniyogo maharṣibhiḥ

tejojvālāmaṇipadaṃ huṃ phaṭ vāhnipriyāmatha

dhṛtapadmadvayaṃ bhānuṃ tejomaṃḍalamadhyagaṃ

dhyātvetthaṃ prajapeddāstu sāvitrāṣṭākṣarīṃ śubhāṃ

pratyekaṃ sarvaraśmīnāṃ japahomādikarmasu

bhagavadraśmividyānāṃ mālikādhyayanenvahaṃ

bhagavadraśmi vidyānāṃ samudāyasya bhūpate

gāyatryādīnicchaṃdāṃsi viśvāmitrādayodvijāḥ

tatrāpi nānāchaṃdāṃsi sarvatejomayo vibhuḥ

harirnārāyaṇaḥsvāmīdevatāsamudīritaḥ

asaṃkhyātamaḥ puṃjapijarīkṛtadiṅmukhaṃ

raśmimālāṃ japitvaivamārṣachaṃdādikaṃ nyaset

pratyekamārṣaṃ chaṃdaśca vinyasyātha ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ

sāvitrāṣṭākṣarī vidyāmahāsiddhipradāyinī

aṣṭalakṣaṃ puraścaryāddaśāṃśaṃtaparyayetpayaḥ

athavāravivāreṇasahiteraviparvaṇi

tilairgavyājyasahitairhutvāgnau saṃskṛte śubhe

vahusasyayutāṃ pṛthvīṃ datvā dakṣiṇayānvitāṃ

japtvā dvādaśalakṣāṇi tarpayitvā gavāṃ payaḥ

kuṣṭharogāt pramucyate tadurvicitrādanekaśaḥ

pāyasānnamatho hutvā vātarogādvimucyate

bhagaṃdarānmeharogādagnimāṃdyācca mucyate

sahasrayāddhuvaṃ

ajīrṇarogānmucyete netrarogāccadāruṇāt

śirorogādvimucyetamūlarogāccaduḥsahāt

nānāvidhānnalarogānmaṃḍalena vimucyate

prakṣālyamukharogebhyo vimukto bhavati dhruvaṃ

asādhyairapi mukto saurogajālaiḥ suduḥsahaiḥ

saṃprāpyamahatīṃ lakṣmīmaṇimādi guṇānvitām

aṣṭottara sahasraṃyaḥ prātaḥ snātvānvahaṃ japet

dehāṃte samavāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ

idamuktamathātreyavyāsayorapi vakṣyate

māhātmyaṃ yasyavijñānaranmahatīṃ siddhimaśnute

aiṃdrasvaraṃ vyomavījaṃ tṛtīyasvarasaṃyutaṃ

ityekoktāmahāvidyādattātreyasyayoginaḥ

dattātreyapadaṃ paścāccaturthāṃtasamanvitaṃ

pratyekaṃ vājapedvidyāṃmilitaṃ vā dvayaṃ japet

samastavidyāsāmrājyasiddhidāmuktidāyinī

dattātreya mahāvidyā japtavyāttairmahīyate

aṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ yogaṃ brahmajñānaṃ sanātanaṃ

pādukāṃjana khaḍgādi siddhāṣṭakamamīpsatā

corāriduṣṭasaṃdhebhyomahābhayanivāraṇaṃ

icchratādattatavidyeyaṃ prajaptavyā mahīpate

jāgataṃ chaṃdaṃ ityuktaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakaḥ

śaktirmāyāpāśavījamaṃkuśaṃkīlakaṃ smṛtaṃ

nattiṃ śaktimiti prāhurbrahmatādātmyasiddhaye

ṣaḍdīrghayutamāyārṇāṃ kuśārṇābhyāṃ ṣaḍaṃkaṃ

nīlajīmūtasaṃkāśaṃ jarāmaṃḍalaśomitaṃ

pārijātatarormūledivyasiṃhāsanāśritaṃ

mudrāṃ jñānamaryāṃ dakṣahaste vāme'bhayaṃ tathā

prasannavadanaṃ śāṃtaṃ susmitāna na paṃkajaṃ

siddhavidyādharagaṇaiḥ sevitaṃ ca maharṣibhiḥ

sahitaṃ nāradādyaiśca sevitaṃ trijagatpatiṃ

dattātreya mahāvidyāmiti dhyātvā japat śuciḥ

lakṣatrayaṃ puraścaryādekaikasya manornṛpa

guravedakṣiṇāṃ datvā sarvasvamapi sādhakaḥ

upoṣaṇaparoyadvāvyatīpāte mahīpate

daśāṃśaṃ jvalite vahnau gurave kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

yajñopavīta triayaṃ datvā vāso yugānvitaṃ

ekāmapi mahāvidyāṃ dattātreyasya yoginaḥ

ṣaṇmāsādaṇimāghaṣṭaguṇaiśvaryayuto bhavet

pāyasenājyamuktena hutvā vidyānidhirbhavet

japtvā sahasramekāṃ vā vidyāṃ vidyādvayaṃ tu vā

sākṣādevasasva cakṣurbhāṃpaśyatyakhilasiddhidaṃ

trisahasraṃ japedrātrau nityaṃ paścimadiṅmukhaṃ

śasyāsamīpe japtve vidyāmaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā mārgaṃ gacchatiyaḥ pumān

sarvarogādipīḍāsu japanyaṃ ca sahasrakaṃ

aṣṭāṃgayogamanvicchanphalāśīniyatavrataḥ

aṣṭāṃgayogamāsādhakhecarojāyate dhruvaṃ

sahasratritayaṃ nityaṃ japtvāśaṃśitavrataḥ

atrisūno māṃ vidyāmekaikāmanvahaṃ śuciḥ

japtvāsaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ maṃtrīsahasraṃ vijiteṃdriyaḥ

aṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ yogaṃ vāksiddhimatulāṃ śubhāṃ

bhogānaṣṭavidhāṃścāpi labdhāṃte muktimāpnuyāt

atrisūnoridaṃ yaṃtraṃ mahābhāgyasamṛddhidaṃ

ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ṣaḍaṃgāni pitaraṃ cānasūyayā

sanatsujānaṃ prahlādaṃ kārtavīryamataḥ paraṃ

itthamatrisuttaṃ sākṣādbhagavaṃtaṃ śriyānvitaṃ

ataḥparaṃ pravakṣyāmi dvaipāyanamanornṛpa

praṇavaṃ namasāyuktaṃ vadan bhagavate tataḥ

ekatriṃśārṇasahitaṃ dvaipāyanapadaṃ tataḥ

parāśarasutasyeyaṃ vidyāmanvakṣarīśubhā

mamaiva mūrtiraparākṛṣṇadvaipāyanābhidhā

vibhāgakartrīvedānāṃ jagatāṃ hitakāriṇī

dvaipāyanābhidhaḥ sākṣādbhagavān hariracyutaḥ

brāhmesamaya utthāya saṃsmṛtyācāryapādukāṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā vāpi prajaptvādau samāhitaḥ

ācāryaṃ bhāvayennityaṃ vedavyāsa svarūpiṇaṃ

ācārya paramātmānaṃ sākṣādvaipāyanākṛtiṃ

vādarāyaṇirasyokto munirbrahmavidāṃvaraḥ

vedavyāso mahāyogī deva ukto mahīpate

ekenākṣara yugmena caturbhiratha saptabhiḥ

divyasiṃhāsanāsīnaṃ kālanīlāṃvudaprabhaṃ

vibhrāṇaṃ cinmayīmudrāṃvīrāsana samāśritaṃ

kuṃḍaladvayaṃ saṃśobhimukhāṃbhojaṃ jagadguruṃ

sevitaṃ siddhasaṃdhaiśca brahmavidbhiḥ samāvṛtaṃ

dvaipāyanamuniṃ sākṣādbhagavaṃtamahaṃbhaje

arcayet ṣaḍareyaṃtre paṃcakoṇasamanvite

ṣaṭkoṇa madhye saṃpūjya vedavyāsaṃ jagadguruṃ

jaiminiṃ ca sumaṃtuṃ ca ṣaṭkoṇeṣu prapūjayet

atharvaṇaṃ tato vedamitihāsamatatparaṃ

romaharṣaṇanāmānaṃ yājñavalkyaṃ mahāmuniṃ

vaudhāyanāyastaṃvau ca caturasredigīśvarān

ākaraḥ sarvavidyānāmāśrayaḥ sarvasaṃpadāṃ

lakṣatrayaṃ japecchuddho vedavyāsa mahāmanuṃ

ācāryapādayordatvā niṣkadvādaśakaṃ nṛpa

athavaikādaśī śuddhākārtike māghamāsi vā

sahasraṃ juhuyādagnau dvādaśyāṃ kāpilaṃ ghṛtaṃ

samastaparvasahitaṃ bhāratākhyānapustakaṃ

triniṣkadakṣiṇāmuktamācāryāya samarpya ca

aśvatthamūlamāśrinityaṃ niyamataḥ śuciḥ

tribhirmāsairnasaṃdehovidyānāṃ pārago bhavet

vedavyāsa mahāvidyāṃ japtvā lakṣaṃ yata vrataḥ

japtvā lākṣamimaṃtraṃ gurumārādhya yatnataḥ

śriyaṃ cāpnoti vipulāṃ brahmavidbhavati dhruvaṃ

mūkasyāpyaparairjaptā mahāvāksiddhidāyinī

japtvā sahasraṃ śuddhātmā varṣamekaṃ jiteṃdriyaḥ

saṃprāpya paramaṃbrahmasākṣādevaprapaśyati

ācāryamarcayeddhīmān vastubhiḥ sakalairapi

vṛṣaparvādayo daityā udaṃkādyādvijottamāḥ

śarīraṃ naśvaraṃ matvā saṃsāraṃ duḥkhasaṃkulaṃ

ācāryacaraṇāveva samabhyarcyānvahaṃ nṛpa

ekena janmanaivaitesiddhiṃ saṃprāpya durlabhāṃ

tasmādbhaumaṃ samāsādya śarīraṃ duḥkhasaṃkulaṃ

ācāryānugrahaḥ pūrṇoyasya nāsti mahītale

saṃsārasāgaraṃ tartuṃya icchatyeka janmanā

ācāryapādukāvidyā pūrvikāṃ raśmimālikāṃ

samastanyāsasahitāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japet

bhagavansadṛśākāro bhagavat kiṃkaro bhavet

uktātubhyaṃ mahārāja kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi

daśamodhyāya uktoyaṃ brahmajñānibhirāvṛtaḥ

śrutā tava mukhāṃbhojādbhagavadraśmimālikā

dattātreyasya māhātmyaṃ śrutaṃ dvaipāyanasya ca

iti dāśarathiḥ pṛṣṭo bhagavānjānakīpatiḥ

śṛṇu rājanpravakṣyāmi śrīpateḥ paramātmanaḥ

vedeṣu sarvavedāṃteṣvapi sārārthasaṃgrahe

yadakṣaraṃ paraṃbrahma manovācāmagocaraṃ

tacchaktiścitkalānāmaśrīḥ sarvajagadaṃvikā

cidrūpiṇyātayāśaktyāsahitaḥ paramaḥ pumān

ajaṃ svayaṃbhuvaṃ nāmnā viśvasṛṣṭividhāyakaṃ

parabrahmamayaḥ sākṣādahaṃ nārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

dakṣavānasmimadvidyāṃyayāmatphalayānvitaḥ

yasminkāle mahāvidyāṃ śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarīṃ

vidyāyā brahmarūpatvādbrahmavidyetitāṃ jaguḥ

yasmātadbrāhmasamayedateyaṃ brahmaṇe mayā

brahmatādātmya hetutvādbrahmavidyeyamīritā

brahmavidyāmimāṃ prāhuḥ śrīśrīśabdādaśākṣarīṃ

parabrahmamayobhūtvā sasarjanikhilaṃ jagat

śarīre saṃpraviṣṭābhūdbrahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭinaḥ

kartṛtvaṃ sarvajagatāṃmaṇimādi guṇānapi

śrīśrīśadvādaśārṇeyaṃ brahmavidyā tataḥ smṛtā

brāhmesamaya utthāya saṃsmṛtyācāryapādukāṃ

labhate nityakalyāṇamakṣayāḥ saṃpadopi ca

rājñī sakalavidyānāmadhikābhūcchutiṣvapi

siddhyaṃti saiva sarvāsāṃ vidyānāmadhikā smṛtā

ārabhya tadinaṃ nityaṃ yataḥ śreyaḥ paraṃparā

vijñeyaḥ paramācāryastameva satataṃ vibhuṃ

nācarenmanasādrohamapi tasya guroḥ kvacit

na tasya niṃdāśravaṇaṃ yatra kutrāpi vā caret

putramitrakalatrādisaṃpabhyaḥ pracyavaṃtite

grāmasūkaratāṃprāpya caṃḍāleṣvapi jāyate

tyaktvānyasmaidhanaṃdhānyamalaṃkārādikaṃ śubhaṃ

śrutyuktaṃ vihitaṃ karmatyaktvānyatraratodvijaḥ

ācāryapatnīputrādipoṣaṇaṃ nityamādarāt

tasmādavaśyamācāryacaraṇāṃvuruhadvayaṃ

api brahmavidenyasmaidadātu na dadātu vā

satpatnīpūjanaṃ sākṣānmahālakṣmyārcanaṃ viduḥ

ācāryapatnīputrāṇāmasāṃnidhye śriyaḥpateḥ

tatsarvamarpitaṃ vastu gatvāpi bahuyojanaṃ

pautrāya vātha tadbhātre gurudravyaṃ samarpayet

aṃdhaṃ tamaḥ pravitrāti yāvadābhūta saṃplavaṃ

kartavyamanvahaṃ nityaprayatnenāpi sādhakaiḥ

pitṛsaṃpoṣaṇaṃ paścāt patnīputrādipoṣaṇaṃ

ācāryamāṃvijānīyātatpatnīmapi ca śriyaṃ

tadvaṃśasaṃbhavānviddhisanakārīnmahāmate

patnyādipoṣaṇenehasaukhyamakṣapyamaśnutte

etatkālevarāṃtesya matsvarūpaṃ dadāmyahaṃ

tyajet svapatnīputrādīn tyajedbhrātṛṃścavāṃdhavān

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vāpi bhrātaraṃ vāṃdhavaṃtu vā

ācāryaniṃdākartāraṃ hanyādyadvā parityajet

janāhasaṃtumāṃ dṛṣṭvā rājānodaṃḍayaṃ tu vā

eva māpadgatasyāpimatiryasya bhave dṛḍhā

janāḥ stuvaṃtu kutsaṃtu lakṣmīstiṣṭhatu gacchatu

brahmavidyāpradaḥ svāmībrahmavidyāpradaḥ pitā

brahmavidyāpradaḥsākṣādācāryonānya ucyate

saṃsevya bhagavadrūpamācāryaṃ karuṇānidhiṃ

praṇavaṃ pramoṣmāṇaṃ vāmanetravibhūṣitaṃ

tadeva viṃdurahitaṃ tṛtīyāṃtasthyamapyatha

sa dīrghaṃ vāmapādānagravījaṃ vahnimataḥ paraṃ

ekatriṃśākṣaraṃ paścāddīrghasvaradibhūṣitaṃ

hṛdayena samāyuktaṃ śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarī

sarvayogamayī sarvayāga tīrthasvarūpiṇī

sarvapātavāsaṃhartrī sarvavyādhinināśinī

āyurārogyaputrārtharājyalakṣmīsamṛddhidā

mayāpi varṇitu naiva śakyeyaṃ dvādaśākṣarī

ekena janmanaivāṃtebhagavatpadamakṣayaṃ

avaśyaṃ tena japtavyā śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarī

maṃdirenivasaṃtyevayāvadācandratārakaṃ

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānamahimnātraiva labhyate

kṛttādyathatenaivalābhyate dvādaśākṣarī

nālye na tapasālabhyamihalokemahīpate

ācāryānugrahādbrāhmekāle japtvānvahaṃ nṛpa

triśataṃ vā śucirdhīmān smṛtvācāryasya pādukaṃ

anenajanmanaiyāsomatkalāsahito bhavet

adyāpi brahmarudrādyāḥ sanakādyāścayoginaḥ

āyurārogmamaiśvaryaṃ paśavaścavasūni ca

ācāryāpādukāyugmapūjakasya dine dine

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānāṃnaṃtaraṃ raśmijāpinaḥ

dhanadhanyākalāvājimuktāratnaughasaṃkulā

āvirbhavati vidyābhiḥ saṃkulāsatataṃ nṛpa

na dṛśyate mahāvidyādvidaivatyāśi mahāmate

cūḍāmaṇyātmikaikaiva brahmavidyāparānahi

śacīndraurohiṇīcaṃdrau svāhā vahnī prabhāravī

strīṣuṃ rūpāṇi sarvāṇi śrīśrīpatyaṃśaṃjānihi

sarvavidyāmayīṃ tasmāchrī śrīśadvādaśākṣarīṃ

carācarasametāni bhuvanāni caturdaśa

asau viśvaṃbharaḥ sākṣādbrahmavidhāprajāpakaḥ

ācāryapādukāyugmamabhyarcyānvahamādarāt

gṛhe svargasukhaṃ teṣāmakṣayaṃsyāddinedine

śatravomitratāṃ yāṃtimāyaṃ ca sukṛttāyate

dhaneśaḥ śakramukhyāśca surāḥkaikaryamanvahaṃ

yoginaḥ sanakādyāśca vaśiṣṭhādyāmaharṣayaḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojadvayārcanaratonvahaṃ

siddhakṣetraṃ tadevoktaṃ samaṃtādyojanāyutaṃ

asādhyamapi devānāmapi dīpāṃtarasthitaṃ

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānādapyasyānvayasaṃbhavāḥ

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānaṃ kriyate yena nityaśaḥ

tadgahadvāritiṣṭhaṃti siddhayoṣṭāṇimādayaḥ

tasmādiyaṃ mahāvidyā śrīśrīdvādaśākṣarī

upāsitavyā yatnena pratyahaṃ niyatavrataiḥ

iti dāśarathīyesmin taṃtre vedārthasaṃgrahe

yasya vijñānamātreṇa brahmavidyāśu siddhyati

brahmavidyādvidaivatyośrīśadvādaśākṣarī

sādhanaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ śṛṇu vakṣyāpi nṛpate

śirasyaṃ jalimādhāya daśadaṣṭvādvādaśadhāpirā

ānanyadarśanau dṛṣṭvā saṃpūjyāpi svaśaktitaḥ

brahmavidyāṃjapaṃtyaśvābhaśrīśrīśadvādaśakṣarīṃ

ācāryapādukāvidyābhāvepi svasya mūrddhani

mānasaiḥ svarṇapuṣpādyaiḥ samabhyarcyopacārakaiḥ

ṛṣirasyamahāyogīmādhyonārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇo sarvajagataḥ pitarau vibhū

svāpinau sarvagaṃ jagatāṃ puvānāvatimuṃdarau

trayī vījaṃ ca vidvājaṃ vījamatranṛponama

prītyarthaṃ viniyogotra samudāhṛta iṣṭadaḥ

cinyasya panamānmānaṃ dhyāyeccitkalayānvitaṃ

kalpavāṭī samāyuktesaṃtānārāmaśobhite

maṃrāravāṭī mahite pārijātavanāhṛte

padmagagākhyamāṇikyaprākāreṇātha śobhite

vaiḍūryaratnaprākāraśobhitenaramevite

muktāphalamahāratnaprākāreṇāthaveṣṭite

hrasva vidgumaralodyapākāreṇa ca veṣṭite

tasminmaṇimayatnaṃbha sāhasrāyutaśāṃbhive

tatra cādhāraśaktyādi dhṛtehīrahiraṇyayaṃ

tasminnaṣṭadaleyagne maṃtrākṣarapayeśubhe

tasminvālārkasāhasrakoṭinulyamabhānvite

īśvaryā sahaviśve śā vāsudevaḥ manānanaḥ

srigdhaviṃvaphalābhoṣṭaḥ musmitānana paṃkajaḥ

haricaṃdana liptāṃgaḥ kastūrītilakāṃcitaḥ

japākusupasaṃkāśakarapallavaśobhinaḥ

śrībhatsakaustubhābhyāṃ ca śobhitaḥ pṛthukkṣamā

vālārkamadaśajyotsrapītāṃvarapugāhṛtaḥ

akalaṃkitacaṃdrābhananakhapaṃktivibhūṣitaḥ

pāṃcatanparathāṃgābhyāṃ vigajitakaradvayaḥ

sphuradvidyullatākrāṃtāsitāsibhra ivarājane

gade ca paramekrī utpanna sākṣājjagatpatiḥ

divyacedaṃnalīsāṃgīkanakāṃvarabhūṣaṇā

maṃrārapārijātādi divyapuṣpasraganvitā

ratnāvataṃ sa śobhādyācikurāṃtelimannibhe

keyūrāṃgadahārādyairbhūṣaṇai ete śobhitā

anyonyā śliṣṭahṛdvāhūdivyābharaṇaśobhinau

śaraṇyo sarvalokyanāṃśaraṇāmanavatmalau

kartāraumarvajagatāṃ viśvaraśvarakṣaṇa tatparau

nīlādevyā ca bhūrevyāvāmadakṣiṇaśobhitau

brahmarudra maheṃdrādiśigemāṇikyakāṃtibhiḥ

ralartihāsanāsīnaurājitāvilabhūṣaṇau

yogihṛtpadmasaṃpūjyau nityadaivanasevitau

lakṣmīnārāyaṇau nityaṃbhajāmi pratyahaṃ vibhū

prajapet samaye brahme śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarīṃ

dhyātvā nārāyaṇāṃ sākṣāt sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣite

ācāryapalīmāyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇai vibhū

ācāryanāṃ vijānīhi tat patnīmapidhaściyaṃ

athavā saccidānaṃdasvarūpasthamavyayaṃ

niṣkalaṃke nirupamaṃtejorūpeparātparaṃ

ekaiveyaṃ dvidaivatyā lakṣmīnārāyaṇīśubhā

yāṃ vidyāṃjapatonityaṃkalyāṇaṃ sakalabhavat

śubhemāsi śubhelagne divase ca śubhe nṛpa

sadguruḥ śubhadaḥ proktaḥ sanmāsaḥ śubhadaḥ smṛtaḥ

madhumāse mahaduḥkhaṃ sādhakasya prajāyate

jyeṣṭhamaraṇamāprotividyomaprapivaṃdhubhiḥ

śrāvaṇe mahato lakṣmīṃ saṃprāptotya ca lośubhāṃ

muktāmāṇikyasaṃpūrṇāmāśvine labhatethiyaṃ

mārgaśīrṣe bhavellakṣmīrdhanahānyādisaṃkulā

sākṣādbhagavatā lakṣmyā api saṃdarśanaṃ nṛpa

phālgunemāsi siddhiḥ syāt tridaśairapi durlabhā

daśamyekādaśī śuklādvādaśī ca trayodaśī

siddhidāṃ kecidapyahuḥ śuklapakṣāṣṭamīṃ śubhāṃ

śubhapradājñeyā hānidau bhaumabhānujau

śubhagnahāṇāṃ daṣṭvānye muhūrttā api siddhidāḥ

sarvāgamarahasyatamācāryaṃ vṛṇuyā dvibhuṃ

rahasyamapi vidyānāmavijñāya mahīpate

tāvubhāvapiduḥkhauyairihāpi sahitau śṛpa

bhūyopyavaśyaṃ vedeṣu dharmaśāstreṣu pāragaṃ

bahuyojana sāhasramapi gatvā mahītale

datvā rahasyaṃ vidyānāṃ sāṃgamācāryavaktranaḥ

tameva toṣayennityamācāryaṃ bhavāvanmayaṃ

yathāktyārccaye tatra niyamonāsti bhūpate

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ samastanyāsa saṃyutāṃ

samadvīyavatīṃ pṛthvīṃ dhanadhānyauya saṃkulāṃ

tasmādyegaṃ ca kālaṃ ca sarahasyānmanūnapi

sarvajñasyaiva sānnidhye gṛhṇīyāt sakalānyanūn

avaśyamapi gṛhṇīyādbhūyaḥ sarvajña sannidhau

arpayeddakṣiṇāṃ sāṃgā japahomādikarmasu

āvāryadakṣiramādeyāyatkiṃcidapi bhūpate

datvāyatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ dharmaśāstra śrutiṣvapi

aśvamedhādi yāgeṣu datvā koṭi sahasraśaḥ

naṣṭe puṇye martyalokaṃ bhūyopi prāpnuvaṃti hi

punarāvṛttirahitaṃ śāśvataṃ padamaśrute

brahmavidyājayāsaktāraśmimālānuciṃtakaḥ

samānarūpāḥ śrīviṣṇordivyālaṃkārabhūṣitāḥ

ayodhyānagare sākṣātyade bhagavataḥ śubhe

gatvātrananivarjaṃte viṣṇunāsahasasthitāḥ

tatsamānasukhaṃ nityaṃ prāpnuvaṃti manīṣiṇaḥ

nāmuvaṃ nipunaḥ sargaṃ sargasthā iva jaṃtavaḥ

tathā tenaiva jāyaṃte murtyalokeyathecchayā

na karpavaṃdhanaṃ janmabrahmavidyāratātmanāṃ

nadāsyaṃ vai pareśasyavaṃdhanaṃ parikīrtitaṃ

ābrahmabhuvanāllokāḥ punarāvṛttilakṣaṇāḥ

vahvāyāsaphalāduḥkhajanmakāṇūhetavaḥ

svargamaṃsthānnarān dṛṣṭvā kṣīṇe karmaṇi devatāḥ

tasmāt svargasukhaṃ bhūpavahuyatnaikasādhanaṃ

yajña na taporūpaṃ karmavedesu codita

ācāryapatnīputrādīnabhyarcya pratyahaṃ tataḥ

raśmimālāmanuspṛtya śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japan

ācāryācaraṇāṃbhoja dvayameva madāsmaret

ādyaṃ vījadvayaṃtyatkā śrīśrīpatidaśākṣarīṃ

araṇye vānaraṇye vāsvagṛhevānadītaṭe

āsīnovāśayānośrivātiṣṭhan gacchanathāpi vā

ācāryānugrahaṃ prāpya sabhataṃ masmarannapa

vratakoṭi tapastītha mahāyāgaphalānvitaḥ

daśārṇavidyāmātraṃ vā sadācāryā kṛpānvitaḥ

samāpya sarvavidyānāmādhipatyaṃ ca durlabhaṃ

dehāṃte tatparaṃdhāma vrajatyeva śriyaḥ pateḥ

tanniṣṭassatataṃ bhūtvā bhagavaṃtamanussaret

śrīśājñollaṃghakaḥ sarvajagadohī mahīpate

dātavyānyapirānāti satpātrebhyoviśeṣataḥ

yatravṛtamimevobhe tatpātraṃ pracakṣyate

kurvan vaidikamācārepātrabhūtobhaveddvijaḥ

jīvanneva sa śūdraḥ syātanmukhaṃ nāvalokayet

apātradānānnarakaṃ pātradānāt paraṃ padaṃ

dhanaṃ yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tadācāryatya pādayoḥ

rahasya manyadvakṣyāmi śṛṇu rājanmahīpate

bhagavadraśmimālākhyāvidyāpeyemayeritā

brahmavidyājayāsa sakto vedoktācāra tatparaḥ

pātrāṇāmutamaṃ pātra mayamevocyate budhe

uktācārarato nityamācāryacaraṇārccaka

avāpya sarvayāgānāṃ phalaṃ pratyahamaśrutaṃ

brahmaṇaḥ padamādyanodatekālamakṣeyaṃ

brahmavidyājapedyastu śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarī

bhagavānapittaṃ nityaṃ pitṛvadkṣati dhruvaṃ

samarcayati hastābhyā taddhastaṃ śaṃkhakravat

ananyadarśanaḥ prātaḥ prātarutthāya paśyati

kalayānitaṃ

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānādbrahmarṣitvamupāgatāḥ

punaḥ prācetaso bhūtvā brahmavidyānu ciṃtanāt

pulahādyā mahābhāgā brahmaputrā mahīpate

brahmavidyānusaṃdhānamācāryacaraṇārcanaṃ

tasmācchubheṣumāseṣusuśrubheṣvapi

navāramāsadoṣaḥ syādgadurbrahmaṇe caṃdrasūryayoḥ

akṣayaṃ japahomādiphalaṃ syāttatra saṃtataṃ

meruśailasamaṃ viddhitatsvarṇaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ

dātavyānimayatena vittalobhavivarjinīṃ

ācāryasannidhau kṛtvā mahālakṣmī samanvitaṃ

pūrvopadiṣṭā vidyāścaravīdugrahaṇe śubhe

sākṣādayaṃ parvakālo mahānukto maharṣibhiḥ

yahaṇe sarvavidyānāmuktamāmeṣu saṃtataṃ

srvāsāmapi viyānāmācāryavadanāṃdujāt

pādyamāsepradeyāni sarvadānāni bhūpate

yasyopanayanārthaṃ vā mādyamāsi prayacchati

māghamāse daridrāya vivāhadhanamuttamaṃ

narakasthān samuddhṛtya brahmaloke mahīpate

kṣayona nasyapurāyasya tadvisyoḥ parame pade

tāvadvaṃśābhivṛddhiḥ syādiha loke mahīpate

padyānakṣetra saṃyuktā paurṇimā tatra pāsmṛtā

vaiśākhaśrāvaṇādīni māghamāsa samānihi

aśubheṣvapimaraseṣu ravīṃdugrahaṇaṃ nṛpa

alabhyā api yogāsyuḥ śubhātaśubhamāsyapi

vidyopadeśaṃ kurvīta nānyathā siddhi rucyate

nadadyātra ca gṛhṇīyādvidyājāta mahīpate

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvabhajanīyaṃ dine dine

ādau kṛtvā prayatnena samastanyāsa saṃyutāṃ

etadeva paraṃtatvaṃ vedāṃteṣu śrutiṣvapi

iti dāśarathīyesminnaṃ vedārthasaṃgrahe

iktoyaṃ dvādaśodhyāyo brahmatatvārthasādhanaḥ

sādhanaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ kathayāmāsa śabhave

nārāyaṇo mahāyogī kathayāmāsa yatparaṃ

sādhanaṃ brahmavidyāyānavācyamapi kutracit

nahomastarpaṇaṃ vāpi siddhavidyeyamiṣṭadā

parasya brahmaṇaḥ sākṣādāśudarśanasiddhidā

avaśyaṃ savaye brāhmeja savyāsiddhimicchatā

pūjayet samaye brāhme brahmavidyāmimāṃ śuciḥ

catuḥśataṃ japedyadvātriśateśatameva vā

maṃsiddhiṃ mahatīmiṣṭāṃ prāpnotyāśrunasaṃśayaḥ

eva bhaktāśanonityaṃ tatpūrvadivasatrayaṃ

saṃkalpyakeśavādīnimataśoccaran

mahāpātakakoṭibhyassadya eva vimutyate

pītvāṃtarapi śuddhaḥ syāt sadyo niṣkalmaṣo bhavet

trirācampa sadācārya sannidhauśaṃsita vrataḥ

ācāryapatnīmācīryāṃtrātvā śrīśrīśarūpiṇau

padmarāga samāyukte taptahema vinirmitaṃ

muktāratnamayaṃ vāpitasatyā api bhūṣaṇaṃ

maṃhīdakṣiṇayuktāṃ godvayaṃ ca tamarpayet

sarvāvaraṇasaṃyuktau lakṣmīnārāyaṇau vibhu

sparśakālaṃ samārabhyamokṣakālāṃtamādarāt

suvarṇapuṣpalakṣeṇavarṣakodyarcanātphalaṃ

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt pūrvamuktāṃ tataḥ paraṃ

tasminkāle gurordatvā māsātryāgevasādhakaḥ

viṃśāḍhakamitānmudrānatha vā dvādaśāṭhakān

prasthaṃ cātha tadarthaṃ vā ghṛtaṃ dhenu samudbhavaṃ

palīputra sametāyasvācāryāya samarpayet

dvādaśārṇāgahātidyāmadyaḥ siddhyati bhūpate

śrīśrīśayoḥ prasādena tasmicarye mahīpate

dhanadhānya samāyuktāṃ saṃtati vyāpinīṃ dhruvaṃ

mahadārogyammasādyadīrghamāyuranuttamaṃ

śuklā tṛtīyo vaiśākhe bhṛgutvāsaraṃ saṃyutā

tretādyā sā tithirjanmatithivālamahātmanaḥ

umāśaṃkarayogaśca kathyataitaddine vudhaiḥ

atra dattaṃ japtanityā kṣayaphalapradaṃ

ācāryājñāṃ samāsādya prātasnātvā viśuddhadhīḥ

anyarcya kusumairyāvatphalaṃ prāpte bhūtale

prāpyayusmyaṃ viśuddhātmā

phalacittayamādāyapānasaṃsumanoharaṃ

niṣkadvayayutaṃ vāpi dadyādācāryapādayoḥ

dadyādācāryayanmaiva vahvālaṃkaraṇādikaṃ

gavyaṃ dadyadhyaryayetpaścādāḍhakaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāmitthaṃ kṛtvā dvijottamaḥ

asādhyamaṃtrasaṃsiddhimakṣayāṃ prāpya bhūtale

maṃdabhāgyopi saṃmāpyaśriyamaṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

sākṣātkaśyapavanputrapautrasaṃpatisaṃyutaḥ

aṃte vaikuṃḍamāpnoti daśapūrverdaśāparaiḥ

vastvarpayati śuddhātmātrasareṇumitaṃ tu vā

pradadāti na saṃdehasmasminneva hi vatsare

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāmitthaṃ kuryānmahīpate

navarātrabhidhaṃ parvaratnaṃ sakalasiddhidaṃ

bhāgyavahamabhāpānāṃ sākṣādbrahmatvahetukaṃ

yā mahānavamī tatra samastaphalasiddhidā

mahānavamyāsadṛśī na puṇyatithirucyate

yadyatkerāti karmeṣṭaṃtanmahānavamīdine

nakṣatramutarāṣāḍābhārgavovāsaro yai

svāyaṃbhu vākhyamanvādisahitā sā mahātithiḥ

alabhyonāmayogoyaṃ durlabhastridaśairapi

navarātravrataṃ kuryādavaśyaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

koṭiṇovi vratairanyaiṃ kṛtairnāproti tatphalaṃ

datamiṣṭaṃ hutaṃ japtamanaṃta phalasiddhidaṃ

kṛtaṃ kamarmātra sāmrājya śriyamevadadātphalaṃ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇau nityamarcayedbrahmavidyayā

ekabhaktāśanastatrasahasraṃ vārcayeddhīmān

sahasraṃ triśatāyikaṃ

tata ācāryapādābjaṃ natvā sāṣṭāṃgamādarāt

kuṃḍaladvitayāṃramyaṃ madatvācāryakarṇayoḥ

dadyādācārya pādābja caraṇāya vasuṃdharā

alaṃkārān vahūnanyānapitasmai samarpayet

dadyādvāsoyugaṃ kṣaumaṃ kāryā saṃvāsusūkṣmīkaṃ

sahānavamyāṃ vasudhācāryāya samarpayet

datvā sahasvaśaḥ puṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ

brahmāṃḍakoṭidānasya phalamatyaṃta maśrute

pitṛṇāmapi sāhasraṃtārayettisthitaṃ

divyabhogasamāyuktā jīvanyuktā bhavetyapi

sāṃgadakṣiṇayāyuktagosahasarapradānaja

karṇālaṃkaraṇaṃ hematāṭaṃka dvitayaṃ śubhaṃ

muktājāle samāyuktavālīyugalameva vā

sākṣālakṣmīnivāsosmaibhagavatpatra na saṃśayaḥ

divyāṃ varadharo divyaratnābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ

bhūdānadakṣiṇoṃ proktā sarvastaṃ aṃdirasthitaṃ

caturviśati niṣkaṃvānadabhāvetadardhakaṃ

mahīpratinidhi dravyamathavā dakṣiṇānvitaṃ

vatvāronaḍuhodeyā bhūdānerājavallabha

caturṇāmapyanaḍuhāṃ caturniṣkamathāpicā

prasīdati hariḥ sākṣānmahālakṣmīsamanvitaḥ

śuklātṛtīyāvaiśākhīṃ yā proktā kṣayatijñitā

asmin dinadvayenityaṃ sarvamakṣayasiddhidaṃ

labhyate vahubhirvarṣairdurlabhaṃ stridaśairapi

pare ghurvijayānāmadaśamī śravaṇānvitā

śrīśrīśāvarcayedvīmānsahasraṃ sādhakonṛpaḥ

nighadvayasuvarṇāṃ vā niṣkaṃmekamathāpi vā

samāśrurāja sānnidhye vivodepi mahādraṇe

itthamācarataḥ puṃso bhāgyārāśinyadepade

anakurddhaṃ pravakṣyāmi patrapuṣpārcanāt phalaṃ

vilvapatraissamabhyarcyatya sahasrakaṃ

acalāṃ mahatīṃ lakṣmīsaṃtativyāpinīṃ bhavet

sahasraṃ māsamātreṇa śatajanmasamudbhavaiḥ

mahākavirmahāvāgmī bhavatyeva natyena na saṃśayaḥ

mahāpāpopavāpodyairapi māsādvimucyate

ketakī kusumairnityamabhyarcya brahmavidyamā

madadāyuṣyamārogyamaiścaryamacalaṃ labhet

mahāpātakodyāpi māsamātreṇa mucyate

saṃprāpya putrapautrādi samṛddhiṃ mahatīṃ labhet

saṃbhayatyarisaṃghātānacalāṃ ca śriyaṃ labhet

abhyabhyarcya lakṣmī lakṣmīśau māsamātreṇa pātakaiḥ

jakṛtahasamudbhūteḥ komalaiḥ pallavaiḥ śubhaiḥ

anekakoṭijanmotthairapi pāpairvimucyate

ālayassarvabhogānāṃ bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

abhyarcya māsamātreṇa mahāpāpaiśca dāruṇaiḥ

putrapautrādisaṃpatimapyasau labhate nṛpa

abhyarcyamāsamātreṇa vimuktaḥ pāparāśibhiḥ

divyabhogayuto bhūtvā putrapautravaṃtī śriyaṃ

vrajatpatena saṃdeho bhagavansannidhiṃ nṛpa

sauvarṇaṃ puṣpamekavā datvā bhagavāte nṛpa

ekaṃ suvarṇapuṣpaṃ vā daśacihukanirmitaṃ

maheṃrasadṛśo lakṣmyāvidyayādhiṣaṇaprabhaḥ

sau varṇapuṣpamekaṃ vā puṣpamanyansugaṃdhi vā

lakṣmīlakṣmīśayoddetvā pratyahaṃ dvādaśārṇayā

ghṛtaṃ haviḥ phalaṃ vāpi madhuratritayaṃtu vā

agniṣṭomaphalaṃ nityaṃ protyatra na saṃśayaḥ

raśmimālāmanusmṛtya kalyāṇānidinedine

anenaiva pramāṇebha saptakoṭimanuṣvapi

anenaiva pramāṇena brahmavidyābhadaṃguru

brahmavidyāpradācāryasadaśaṃvajagattraye

tasmādavaśyamācāryapādukāyugamādarāt

namākṛtyāpi saṃpūjyaśreyasāṃrāśimaśnute

mahānadīṃ śrotriyaṃ kamaṇi ratnamathāpi vā

sarvāpadmovimuktaḥ syācchreyānnityamavāpnuyāt

samāpya śrīmadaṣṭārṇajapādikamapyārabhet

kiṃciduktaṃ yayā bhūpapunaḥ kiṃ śrotumicchasi

māhātmyaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ śrutamasmābhirādarāt

vaśiṣṭādi maharṣīṇāṃ sannidhau jānakīpatiḥ

yathā nārāyaṇenādau janakāyoktamādarāt

anaṃtaviṃdusahitaṃ śaktirvidudvayānvitā

punastrayodaśayutāśaktirvyomasaviṃdukaṃ

dehāṃtarvarti jīvātmā parabrahmāṃśasaṃbhavaḥ

niśvāsollāsarūpeṇa japatyanudinaṃ manuṃ

haṃsarūpīhariḥ sākṣātparamātmaivedevatā

paramātmānusaṃdhāne viniyoga udāhṛtaḥ

haṃsamityādi ṣaṭvīryavarṇairaṃguliṣunyaset

sūryātmane hṛdinyasya pūrvadirghādimaṃ tathā

haṃsī somātmane mūdhinyasya haṃsamathoccaret

kavacaṃ nyasya haṃsai ca padaṃ brahmānmanetasaḥ

śabdabrahmatanuṃ hṛdaṃnujakuṭīnīḍesahāsaṃsmaret

māheśvaraṃ paraṃdhāmasmarāmimanasāhṛdi

devatābhyo japāvidyā japaṃ nityaṃ samarpayet

vyomavījena jīvātmā somena parameguraḥ

jīvojapatyanudinaṃ parabrahmaibhyasiddhaye

japatyanudinaṃ haṃsahaṃsahaṃseti dvijapuṃgavāḥ

japatyanukṣaṇasohaṃ sohamityevasaṃtataṃ

anaṃtatvāddhakārasya sakāreṇacidīritā

lakṣmīnārāyaṇīmetāṃ vidyāṃ tasmānmaharṣayaḥ

cinevedananvidhiṃ vakṣye japasyai tanmanordvijāḥ

mūlādhārapardamaṃḍape caturdalaśabdataḥ

gaṇeśāyamayāpaścātkṛtamityajapāpadaṃ

vādilāṃta vījaṣaṭkaṃ samuccārya tataḥ paraṃ

padmesthitāya hemādyavarṇāya brahmaṇe padaṃ

gadiphāṃtān samuccārya maṇipūrakamaṃḍape

viṣṇavetha mayetpādi ṣaṭvahasraṃ nivedayet

anāhatapadaṃ maṃḍape dvādaśadale tataḥ

varṇāpātha sadāpūrvaśivāyeti padātparaṃ

svaraṣoḍaśakaṃ proktvā viśuddhābhidhamaṃḍale

gopaketyathavarṇāya tato jīvātmane padaṃ

haṃ kṣaṃ bhrūmadhya ityuktvā ājñāmaṃḍapa ityatha

vicitravarṇāya padaṃ paramātmana ityatha

akārādi kṣakārāṃtānvarṇānuccārya yatnataḥ

sahasradalaśabdaṃnna tataḥ padmesthitādya ca

nayetpādyeka sāhasraṃ japaṃ tasmai nivedayet

tithi vādādi saṃkalpyārabhyārkohyamapyatha

ṣaṭśatāṣaṭśatādhikamutkaikaviṃśatiṃ proccaret padaṃ

ucchvā sanniśvāsāmyāmevātha jāgrat svapnapadaṃ tataḥ

ajapādimagāyatrīṃ maṃtravākyamataḥ paraṃ

eva mucchvā saniśvāsa japaṃ śrīparamātmane

na vidyate japo yasyā vidyāpāparamātmanaḥ

ahomaṃdadhiyo viprāḥ kāmamohavaśaṃgatāḥ

ajayā maṃtrasārajñoyaṃ kiṃcidapi vā manuṃ

haṃsavidyāmavijñāyayomaṃtrānna parā bhavet

vedā api purāṇāni dharmaśāstrāṇi saṃtataṃ

japtvā dvādaśasāhasraṃ caturvarṇamimaṃ manuṃ

niyamoyaṃtīnāṃ syānnānyeṣāṃ samudīhṛtaḥ

ajayā manumuccārya gṛhasthassahpalau bhavet

caturarṇamimaṃ maṃtraṃ saṃsmṛtya satataṃ śuciḥ

paśyatyevasva cakṣurbhyāṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitaṃ

yasyāmanupalabdhāyāṃ maṃtrāḥ sarvevṛyaivahi

śrīmātṛkā samākhyātā devatādeśikottamaṃ

ṛṣirbrahmāsya maṃtrasya chaṃdogāyatramīritaṃ

kramāt ṣaṭgānyabhidheyānihvaṃgulīvapi saṃtataṃ

ṣaḍaṃgamātramapi vā kṛtvā vaśyaṃ japedimāṃ

viśadaprabhāṃ trinayanāṃ vāgdevatāmāśraye

yādikṣāṃtādaśoktāścarāka paṃcāśadakṣarā

japtavyā viṃdurahitā viṃduyuktāpi vā sasa

saptakoṭi mahāmaṃtrādharmaśāstrāṇyathāpi ca

ajaptvā mātṛkā vidyāṃ yo manūna parān japet

kṣakārāṃte japāyaṃtraṃhaṃsatyuccaransadā

prajaptvānudinaṃ maṃtrān puraścaraṇapuṇyabhāk

yojayennityamekāgraḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ

haṃsarūpī hariḥ sākṣātparamātmāśriyaḥ patiḥ

samarpya pūrvamajayāṃ saṃkalpyāpi tataḥ paraṃ

phalaṃ sakalavidyānāṃpārago jīvavadbhavet

sakṛcchravaṇamātreṇagnahedveda catuṣṭayaṃ

itihāsapurāṇānāṃ sārajño bhavati dhruvaṃ

dṛśyaṃte vatsarādūrdhvaṃ prasannāvaradā api

tasya hastastu na sparśāt sākṣātmūkopi vatsarān

tadṛṣṭi kṛṣṭo bhavati nidhissahalasaṃpadāṃ

divyaṃ vimānamāruptya vaikuṃṭho nivasatyasau

sarvajñatvaṃ manojñatvaṃ brahmarṣitvamanuttamaṃ

varṣādvarṣadvayādvāpi labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ

anulomavilomasthaiḥ kḷptayā varṇamālayā

vairimaṃtrā api nṛṇāmanyemaṃtrāśca kiṃ punaḥ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhānityaṃdaśadvādaśadhāpi vā

avaśyaṃ mahatī siddhiṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

nyāsajāla samāyukta śrīmaddaṣṭārṇaciṃtanaṃ

śrīmātṛkānusaṃdhānālasthatapasaḥ phalaṃ

tyajet sakaladharmāṃśca na tyajen mātṛkā parāṃ

labhyaṃte yasya vaktrābjāt prāṇāṃtepinataṃ tyajet

ācāryavadanāṃbhojāllabdhasti saphalāḥ smṛtāḥ

gururyadittayaḥ siddhastasya vidyāśusiddhyati

dehatyādāvatāṃ kāśyāṃ gurorīśasya vaktrataḥ

vālmīkirvipramātro saukiṇāṃtastrī samāgamāt

anugrahānmaharṣīṇāṃ dhanyastaṃ rāmanāma ca

prāptavānagragaṇyattvaṃ brahmādyairapi pūjyatāṃ

sadyaḥ siddho bhavecchiṣyaḥ sākṣādbrahmāpi paśyati

sahasrakṛtvo gāyatrī japakartā dine dine

īdṛśaḥ sadgururyeṣāṃ bhavatyatra dvijanmanāṃ

niṃdaṃ tu vāṃdhavāssarvetyajyaṃ tu strīsutādayaḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojadvayameva gatirmama

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dvayānnānyagatirnama

sasaṃtarati saṃsāraṃ muktimuktiṃ ca viṃdati

mṛtiradyayugāṃtesyādācāryaṃ parityajet

putrānphaleṃnnaṃ bhrātṛṃśca vedāṃtāṃśca śrutīstathā

mātṛkāṃ yo nagṛhṇati mātṛkāṃ na dadātiyaḥ

mātṛkājapataḥ puṃsaḥ pratyahaṃ niyatātmanaḥ

tasya vaktrāṃvujādbhāṃti sakalyāssarahasyakāḥ

tasmādācāryakāruṇyānmātṛkāṃ prāpya sādhakaḥ

anāvāsena saṃsiddhiṃ labhate mahatīṃ parāṃ

jānakīpatinā proktaṃ mayāpi samudīritaṃ

iti dāśarathīye yesmin taṃtre dhyāyaścaturdaśaḥ

asmābhirbrahmavidyāyāmāhātmyaṃ tvanmukhācchrutaṃ

vedavedāṃtaśāstraughasārabhūtārthasaṃgrahāt

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī vidyāmāhātmyaṃ saṃgamādarāt

śṛṇudhvaṃ brāhmaṇāśreṣṭhāssarvajñādīrghadarśinaḥ

bhagavandevadeveśa jānakīvallabhaprabho

śrīmaṣṭākṣarīvidyāmūlahe turitīritā

saṃsārābhodhitaraṇaṃ brahmajñānamathājñayaṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃvānyāṃ vidyāṃ kathayame prabhe

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ kathayāmāsatatvataḥ

purā vaidehanagare janakāya mahātmane

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇa māhātmyaṃ yaduktaṃ tacchṛṇu prabho

śrīmaddaṣṭarṇā māhātmyaṃ tvayāpṛṣṭaṃ pravakṣyate

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ brahmadarśanasiddhāṃ

nirguṇaṃ nirvikāraṃvattamaḥ pārepratiṣṭhitaṃ

tadeva puruṣākāramabhūnnārāyaṇātmakaṃ

svāminnacitsamāyukto jagatsargemanodadhe

bhavāndraṣṭuṃ kathaṃ śakya āvābhyāṃ jagatīpate

brahmāṇamimaṃ vācāṃ patiṃ prabhuṃ anaṃtaraṃ

mahārudraṃṣṭaṣṭavānparameśvaraṃ

aśaktautau vibhuṃ draṣṭaṃ brahmarudrānatho catu

sarvajñakaruṇā kaṇāpūrṇanetrastvaṃ vadāvābhyāṃ maheśvaraḥ

paramātmāparabrahmasacidānaṃdalakṣaṇaḥ

etadgṛhītvā satataṃ kurutāṃ sargamuttamaṃ

acyutohamānaṃ tohaṃ śivohaṃ śaṃkarosmyahaṃ

rahasyaṃ paramaṃ guptaṃ navācyamapi kutracit

kṛtvā tadanusaṃdhānaṃ sadātmānau viṣpattaḥ

jarāmṛtyuvihīnatvaṃ yuvayossidhyati dhruvaṃ

nārāyaṇo mahāyogīvabhūvāṃtarhito nṛpa

bhūtānāmaṃtarānmāhaṃ paramātmāsanātanaḥ

aṇoraṇīyānmahatomahīpānasmyahaṃ nṛpa

yadādityagataṃ jyotissarvalokaprakāśakaṃ

ahaṃ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā śarīre prāṇināṃ sthitaḥ

prajāpatīnāṃ brahmāhaṃ rudrāṇāṃ śaṃkarosmyahaṃ

amarāṇāṃ maheṃdrosmivitteśo yakṣarakṣasāṃ

sṛjāminikhilaṃ viśvaṃ saṃharāmi punaḥ punaḥ

yajñeśoṃyajñabhoktāsmivedanārāyaṇosmyahaṃ

yajñanārāyaṇaścāsmi sarvayajña phalapradaḥ

samastanyāsasahitāmaṣṭārṇāṃviddhimāṃ nṛpa

akṣarāṇāmakārātmāvaruṇoyāṃyatistathā

jīvātmā sarvabhutānāmahameva mahīpate

digīśo lokapālātmā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarosmyahaṃ

vedasāra mahāmaṃtraḥ śrīmānaṣṭākṣaraḥ smṛtaḥ

ācāryavadanāllabdhāye japaṃti dine dine

ācāryaṃ māṃ vijānīhi maṃtramūrtidharāṃ vibhuṃ

bhagavadraśmi vidyābhissahitāṃ sarahasyakāṃ

vedasāramahāvidyātrayameva mahīpate

anyesaṃpuṭitā maṃtrāvetthaṃ sarvaphalapradāḥ

iṣṭasiddhipradāḥ proktā na cānye saṃpuṭī kṛtāḥ

asaṃpuṭitanevedaṃyatīnāṃ muktisiddhidaṃ

gāyatrī sarvavedānīmātā sakalasiddhidā

svabhāva praṇavāghaiva mahāsiddhipradā smṛtā

tāvanmātraphalaṃdātuṃśaknoti na mahatphalaṃ

miśritāṣṭākṣarī vidyā svasāmarthyaṃ nayacchati

śuddhaivāṣṭākṣarī vidyāsvācāryavadanāṃvujāt

sarvavedamayī sarvavedāṃtārthasvarūpiṇī

sarvayogamayī sarvasāṃkhyavedāṃtarūpiṇī

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarī vidyānānyāvidyedaśīnṛpa

samastabhogadā sarvavidyāsiddhipradāyinī

samastamuktāratnaughavarṣiṇī bhaktamaṃdire

jatṛṇāṃ rūpasaubhāgya samastaiśvaryasiddhidā

samastarogasaṃhartrī sarvaduḥkhaprabhaṃjanī

sarvopadravasaṃhartrī mahādādivyabhaṃjanī

samastaśreyasāṃrāśirmahāsalakṣmīracaṃlā

aśvamedhādiyāgānāṃ phalamicchan dine dine

tulāpuruṣadānādiphalamicchan dine dine

caturṇāmapi vedānāṃ pārāyaṇa phaleptunā

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīviyā japakarturathānvahaṃ

yaśetiṣṭhaṃti śakrādyādikpālāḥ kiṃ karā iva

saṃcaransakalānlokānbhāskarāyutasannibhaḥ

mūrtimadbhiścaturvedaiḥścaturddikṣusamanvitaṃ

svāhākārasvadhākāra vaṣaṭkāraissamūrtibhiḥ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃvidyāṃ jihvāyāṃ pradadarśaha

āpādamastake tasya dṛṣṭvāsau vismito bhavat

dadarśa caturovedān dīvyamānāt svatejasā

uvāca yajña puruṣaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayaṃ

tvāṃ matye sarvalokānāṃ kartāraṃ sthitikārakaṃ

tathāpīyaṃ mahāmāyāmāṃ yohayati satataṃ

kemīsvaṃtejasādīptā vidyāmahātmyasūcakāḥ

kasyopāsamayānityaṃ tvāṃ paśyāmyagrataḥ sthitaṃ

saṃkhyāya na mahābhāgaṃ yatvaṃ māṃ pṛṣṭavānasi

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇamaṃtrātmālokānāmīśvaromyahaṃ

nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃbrahmaparamātmāhamīśvaraḥ

mamavāca ime vedājagatsargādihetavaḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojadvaṃdvārcanarattāssadā

divyakanyā sahasraśca kāmadhenubhiranvitāḥ

ciṃtāmaṇiḥ kalpataruḥ kāmadhenuḥ sudhārasaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyānyāsa pūrvādinedine

nyāsahīnāmimāṃ vidyāṃ yo dadāti vimūrḍhyāḥ

pūrvaṃ nyāsairvirahitāṃ gṛhītvāpi mahāmate

bhagavadraśmi vidyānāṃ pāraṃgatamakalmaṣaṃ

bahuyojanasāhasramapi gatvā śrayedguruṃ

pitāmātā tathā bhrātā caturveda pradopi vā

yattadbrahmaparaṃ jyotiśrīṃmaddaṣṭākṣarātmakaṃ

tadeva sūkṣmaparamaṃ sthūlaṃ cāpi sadeva hi

ādyaṃ trayīmayīvījaṃ praṇavākhyaṃ samātanaṃ

acoyajūṃṣi sāmānisāhi śrīramṛtāsatāṃ

akṣarāṇāmakārosau prathamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ

ādyonārāyaṇaḥ sākṣādakāreṇocyase mune

iyaṃ trayīmayī nityā mama vāmāṃkasaṃścitā

tasmāt praṇava vācyaṃ yallakṣmīnārāyaṇāṃ

praṇavākhyamidaṃ vījaṃ śrīvījamiti kathyate

aviprairapijapyāḥ syurmaṃtrāḥ praṇavavarjitāḥ

avaśyaṃ brāhmaṇairjapyā nikṣipta praṇavā api

iyamaṣṭākṣarīvidyā svābhāvapraṇavādikā

muktilakṣmī vījabhūtājñānalakṣmī phalapradā

aṃte nārāyaṇapadaṃ caturthyaṃta samanvitaṃ

praṇavena paraṃbrahmaśrīrapyuktā mahāmune

iyaṃ samastavidyānāṃ vījabhūtāparāsmṛtā

nārāyaṇaivyaṃ jīvasya kathyate namasāmune

nārāyaṇapadaṃ dānaṃ nārāyaṇaparaṃtapaḥ

nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃjyotirātmānārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ

japtṛṇāṃ brahmahatyādi mahāpātakanāśanaṃ

vidhānāsayaṇāvarṇāvarṇituṃ naiva śakyate

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryaṃ brahmajñānamakalmaṣaṃ

etesādhyāstayāssiddhā ācāryacaraṇārcakaḥ

etemadvadanīdbhūtā vedāssāṃgāssanātanāḥ

athattecaturovedāmūrtimaṃto mahāmatiṃ

sāṃkhyāyana mahābhāgavākyamasmākamādarāt

ayaṃ samastajagatāmādyo nārāyaṇocyutaḥ

aṇoraṇīyānmahatomahīyānahamīśvaraḥ

maṃtramūrtirayaṃ sākṣātparamātmā sanātanaḥ

hitāya sarvajagatāṃ svasvarūpopa labdhaye

asmābhiḥ kathyate yadyatatkartavyaṃ dvijottamaiḥ

trividhaṃ karmakartavyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ pratyahaṃ mune

dātavyaṃ sakalaṃ dānamihāmutraphalāptaye

asmābhirapi yadguptaṃ tadvakṣyā mokṣasāṃprataṃ

sarvavidyāmayīṃ viddhitāmeva paramāṃmune

tadraśmi vidyāsahitāye yaṣṭākṣarī śubhā

api prāṇaiḥ kaṃṭhavātairnavaktavyādurātmane

vidyārahasyaṃ vijñeyaṃ japtavyāṣṭākṣarī śubhā

bhūyopi janmasaṃprāpyamajjaṃti narakārṇave

saṃsārasāgaraṃ tartuṃ janmamṛtyu jarā api

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīvidyā yena nityaṃ prajapyatte

muktā asya na saṃdehāḥ pitaropi pitāmahāḥ

pāvanā api tanmitra vāṃdhayājñātayastathā

bhagavāneva jānāti nānyo jānāti tatvataḥ

śakrādyā aṣṭakikpālāḥ kaikaryaṃ prāpnuvaṃtyapi

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā japahomādibhirmune

vāṃchitaṃ samavāpnoti yogināmapidurlabhaṃ

malamūtrāttayaṃ māṃsamajjāmedosthisaṃyutaṃ

sravatyeva divārātraṃ durgaṃdhimalamevahi

yadvastu jihvayāyuktaṃ tatsarvaṃ dehamadhyagaṃ

sadyomuktaṃ vastujātamapyudgīrṇaṃ punarmukhāt

upastheṃdriyatomūtraṃ srakameva divāniśaṃ

saṃtānakāraṇaṃ vījamapi durgaṃdhi satataṃ

atra krodhaśca kāmaścarāgadveṣādayoguṇāḥ

khastrīyoniṣucāsaktaḥ kāmīparvadine api

anubhūyamahaduḥkhaṃ mātṛyone vinirgataḥ

sasataṃ malamūtrādyaipīḍitaḥ kṣudhayānvitaḥ

yauvane viṣayāsakto dhanārjanarataḥ sadā

bhavattītthaṃ mahārājaḥ prākṛtopi narassadā

nidrayāyadṛtaṃ kiṃ cidāyurvyādhimirapyathā

ālasyena ca vṛddhatve kiṃcidāyurgataṃ mune

vaddhau sau kālapāśena dharmarājasya kiṃkaraiḥ

atha pātakaśeṣeṇa punarbhūtatmamāgataḥ

saṃprāpyaśvāna janmādijñataścaṃḍālayoniṣu

puṇyaśeṣeṇaviprāṇāṃkule jāto bhavenmune

tadā svargasukhaṃ bhuṃkte punarbhūmau ca jāyate

svargasaukhyaṃ duḥkhamiśraṃ duḥkhameva samīrītaṃ

piśarjitamapi dravyamathavā prapitāmahaiḥ

svaprāṇānapi datvāsmaiciraṃ śuśrūṣaṇorataḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojayugalaṃ kalmaṣāpahaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ bhagavadraśmipūrvikāṃ

ācāryapādayugale śuśrūṣaṇaratttassadā

avaśyaṃ paramaṃdhāma punarāvṛttivarjitaṃ

na vidyāmātradāne namahānācārya ucyate

anyatra vidyānyāsādīngṛhītvāpi mahāmate

vahuyojanasāhasramapi gatvā mahāmate

śubhedine śubhelagne caṃdratārāvalānvite

kārtikemārgaśīrṣe vā dehaprāṇadhanairapi

gṛhṇīyāt pūrvamācārya vaktrājādraśmimālikāṃ

gṛhītvācāryapādābja dvaye sarvasvamapyatha

prātaḥprātassamuṣyāya nityamācāryatoṣaṇe

samastanyāsasahitāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japet

aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ vā vijñaddhiścātulā śubhā

sarvajñatvaṃ manojñatvaṃ kāmarūpatvamakṣayaṃ

brahmatvamapi rudratvaṃ viṣṇutvaṃ ca na durlabhaṃ

pādukāṃjanakhaḍgāṣva api sidhyaṃti siddhayaḥ

aṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ yogaṃ prāpya khecaratāmapi

śvetadīpādi bhagavatyadeṣu viharatyasau

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dṛḍhabhakto dine dine

jāti smaraṇamāsādya lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakaṃ

etadeva paraṃ śreya idameva tapaḥ paraṃ

ayameva paroyoga idameva paraṃ phalaṃ

idamva trayovedāssaśāstrāṇyāgamāni ca

pitāmātāsuhṛddhātāprāṇavaṃ dhussuhṛtsakhā

ācārya eva bhagavān brahmāviṣṇurmaheśvaraḥ

iti matvā sadācāryaṃ vidyāmaṣṭākṣarīṃ japet

sarahasyāṃ mahāvidyāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japet

sarahasyāvayaṃ sṛṣṭā iṃdirāpatināmune

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇamāhātmyaṃ sarasvatyaivadatyapi

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇa māhātmyaṃ maharṣibhyaḥ prayacchati

ayodhyānagare paścādbhūtvā daśarathātmajaḥ

vaśiṣṭādi maharṣīṇāṃ sannidhau jānakīpatiḥ

tatraṃ dāśarathīyākhyaṃ vedasāraṃ mahodayaṃ

śrutvā samastavedānī sārabhūtaṃ yaśaskaraṃ

sanatkumāraḥ sarvajño vaśiṣṭaśca mahāyaśāḥ

dattātreyo mahāyogīsau bhagavadaṃśajaḥ

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇa māhātmyaṃttepi vakṣyaṃti sādaraṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇamāhātmyaṃtepi vakṣyaṃti sāraṃ

brahmarṣayovaśiṣṭādyābhāradādyāḥ surarṣabhaḥ

vasurudrārkacaṃdrādyāḥ siddhāḥ sādhyāśca saṃtataṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇa tatvajño yatra tiṣṭhati bhūtale

yatrācārya padāṃbhoja dṛḍhabhakto viyatsvapi

tatra gaṃgāgayākāśīkurukṣetraṃ sāgaraṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā japahomārcanādibhiḥ

tuṣṭo bhavati bhaktasya prasīdatyapi satataṃ

martitritayamagnīṃnāṃ tritayaṃ ca jagattrayaṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇavidyāyāḥ padātraya samudbhavaṃ

brāhmādyāmātaropyaṣṭau bhairavā asitādayaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyā aṣṭavarṇasamudbhavāḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ bhāvenatāṃ sadā japan

iti sāṃkhyāyanaṃ brahmasutaṃ yogivaraṃ prabhuṃ

utkābhavāvatāsākaṃ vedāṃ aṃtarmukhānṛpa

tadārabhya sadācārya padaṃ dvaṃdvaṃ samarcayan

kiṃciduktaṃ mahīpālasādhanaṃ ca pravakṣyate

pravakṣyāmi samāsena samahitamanābhava

praṇamyaśayanasthopi daśadvādaśadhā tataḥ

smṛtvā gaurīmaheśaucavāṇīṃdhātāramapyatha

prāṅmukhodha sudhāṃ natvā bhūtale dakṣiṇaṃ padaṃ

ācārya caraṇāṃbhojārūḍhaṃ śrīpādukādvayaṃ

ananyadarśano dṛṣṭvānatvā sāṣṭāṃgamādarāt

ananyadarśino dṛṣṭvā dhenu pṛṣṭamathāpi vā

śuddhaṃ vāsaḥ parīdhāya śrīmadācāryapāduko

bhagavadraśmividyāṃ cayaṃcadhā vāsredhā japet

sahasrāṇāṃ mahāvidyāmekāvṛtyātha vā japet

prātaḥ srātvā viśuddhātmāmārjayedvārdravāsasā

yosau tapanmudetyādi saurasūkte na nityaśaḥ

yosau tapanmudetyādi sāvitraṃ sūktamanvahaṃ

ādityāmukhoptvā dīrghamāyurarogatāṃ

dhanena paśubhirvāpi na viyuktau bhavetyasau

prāṇānāyamya saṃkalpyamaṃtrasaṃdhyāṃ samārabhet

udake vilikhedyaṃtraṃ sāṃgaṃ vījādisaṃyutaṃ

ārṣachaṃdo daivataṃ ca vījaśaktīnyasettataḥ

āvāhanāhimudābhiḥ pūjayitvā tataḥ paraṃ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣaraṃ japtvā vāmahaste viniḥkṣipet

mārjayitvā vaiṣṇavena ṣaḍarṇena jalaṃ pivet

arghyaṃ datvā tridhā paścādgāyatrīṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ jayet

nārāyaṇapadaṃ paścādvidmahe padamapyatha

dhiyoyonaḥ pracodeti yadityakṣaramuccaret

bhuktimuktipradāsākṣādbrahmadarśanasiddhidā

chaṃdo gāyatramityuktaṃ paramātmāśriyaḥ patiḥ

aṣṭāṃviṃśatidhānityaṃjapedvādaśadhyāpi vā

ādityamaṃḍalagataṃ dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ vibhuṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidhāṃ nyaset sakalasiddhidāṃ

stutiṃ kuryādbhagavataḥ paṃcādhā vā tridhaikadhā

tarpayitvā pitṛdevānmanuṣyāṃśca tataḥ paraṃ

ekena janmanāmuktimucchitā sarvasiddhidā

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyā japahomādikaṃ sadā

nityahomaṃ samāpyāthanatvācāryapadāṃvujaṃ

yatkiṃciddakṣiṇāṃ hīmapyācāryapadāṃvuje

atha vā pratyahaṃ hutvā smṛtvācāryapadāṃvujaṃ

sūyaṃ śākaṃ ghṛtaṃ vāpi yatkiṃcidapi dakṣiṇāṃ

karotyavaṃ divasamapiyāgaphalaṃ labhet

śrotriyāyāthavā dadyādapi teṣāmasannidhau

apātrebhyo dhanaṃ dattamapi merusamaṃnṛpa

vaidikācārasahito vedavicchotriyaḥ smṛtaḥ

avedaviduṣedānaṃdattaṃ yatkiṃcidapyatha

japahomasamiddīptehutaṃ śrotriyapātake

dānamācāryapādābje daṃtayatkiṃcidapyata

vipramātre pratijñātamapi vācādhanādikaṃ

pratijñātaṃ ca yadvācāṣaṇmāsābhyaṃtarenṛpa

ācāryebhyaḥ pratijñātamapi śākaghṛtādikaṃ

ihāpi devatāśāpaṃ prāpyāṃte narakaṃ vrajet

yatkiṃcidapi datvātha siddhiṃ cāpnoti durlabhāṃ

saṃkrāṃtau bhārgavevāre dattamakṣayasiddhidaṃ

api sākṣādbhagavatena deyaṃ śreya icchatā

japaṃtīṣvapi sarvāsudattama evapi siddhidaṃ

alabhyayogeṣu dattamakṣayaṃ bhogamokṣadaṃ

bhārgave vāsaredānamakṣayaśrīsamṛddhidaṃ

anaṃtasaukhyadaṃsvargabhogamokṣasamṛddhidaṃ

prathamaṃ sumahaddānaṃ vahuśasyaughasaṃkulaṃ

yathoktadakṣiṇāyuktaṃ mahīdānaṃ mahīpate

śrotriyāya daridrāya vivāhamadravyamapyatha

dvitīyaṃ sumahaddānamettadvaṃśābhivṛddhidaṃ

padmarāgamaṇisvarṇakuṃḍaladvitayānvitaṃ

sālaṃ kṛtaṃ vahukṣīraṃ savatsaṃdhenupaṃcakaṃ

dattamācārya pādābje parvakāle viśeṣataḥ

sadyaḥ sakalaḥ duḥkhauhyaharaṇaṃ bhuktimuktidaṃ

navamaṃdirasaṃyuktaṃ samastakleśavarddhanaṃ

tulāpuruṣamukhyānidānāni sumahaṃtyapi

āṃdolikāṃkaśivikāṃyaḥ prayacchati parvasu

sadya eva vimuktosau pitṛmātṛkulānvitaḥ

kalpyāṃtepyakṣayānbhogānbhuṃktesau brahmaṇaḥ pade

varṣāśatamitadravyadakṣiṇāsahitaṃ nṛpa

bhuṃktepi durlabhān bhogānnaṃdanādivaneṣvasau

datvā sadyo vimukto sau mahāpātakarāśibhiḥ

vaikuṃṭhe paramedhāmnimodanekālakṣayaṃ

vastradānaṃ mahātpuṇyaṃ divyabhogasamṛddhida

ratnadānaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ merumūrdhisukhapradaṃ

karṇeṣu muktāmaraṇadānaṃ māheṃdrabhogadaṃ

mahāduritasaṃghānāṃ nāśanaṃ muktimuktidaṃ

dānaṃ kalmaṣarāśimaṃ saukhyadaṃ brahmaṇaḥ pade

tatadāvāryapādābje datvā siddhiṃ parāṃ vrajet

dhānyadānamihāmutra samasteṣṭaphalapradaṃ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dvayameva na saṃśayaḥ

duḥkhamiśraṃ sukhaṃ bhaumaṃ tasmāddharmarato bhavet

adharmonarakakleśasiddhidoyamamaṃdire

maharṣisiddhaṃ gaṃdharvaiḥ pūjyaḥ syādapidaivataiḥ

vaidikācāranirataḥ śrotriyaḥ pātramucyate

apātraṃ vedarahitaḥ sarvakarma vahiṣkṛtaḥ

ākalyaṃ narake ghore vahuduḥkhapradāyakaṃ

kulaṃ tārayate dāturddaśapūrvaṃdaśāparaṃ

ācāryāyāstu mātraṃ vā datvā yāti parāṃgatiḥ

śrīmaddaṣṭārṇa sidhyarthaṃ vibhumācāryamāśrayet

pitāmātāttathāvārya ihāmutrāpinaṃ tataṃ

yativeṣadharoviprovaṃdyaḥ pitrāpisataṃtaṃ

garbhadhāraṇapoṣābhyā.ṃ piturmātāgarīyasī

ācārya eva tatrāpi daivasaṃpūjyameva hi

ācārya eva tasyāpi pratyahaṃ daivataṃ nṛpa

ācāryasya parityāgānnarakaugheṣu saṃtaṃtaṃ

api sākṣātkṛta brahmasvarūpajñānināmapi

tasmādavaśyamācāryaṃ sarvaṃjñaṃ bhagavanmayaṃ
