kuṃḍalodaravaṃdhādyairmudrikābhiranekaśaḥ

etadeva mahacchreyaḥ sādhanaṃ nānyaducyate

naddaivamadhikaṃ śrīśānnamuktisadṛśaṃ phalaṃ

nāṣṭārṇavidyājapahomapūjanādgatipradaṃ nāsti paratraveha

ācāryapādāṃvujayugmatīrthānnavidyate pāvanamanyatīrthaṃ

ācāryapatnyaivagirīṃdvakanyakāvāṇīśacībhūmiraruṃdhatī ca

abhyarcya saṃpoṣya dhanairanekairavaśyamaṃte harirūpametitaḥ

uktaṃ nārāyaṇe nādau yadyadbhagavatā dvijāḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ japennyāsapurassaraṃ

uktaḥ saptadaśodhyāyaḥ pātakaudya vibhedanaḥ

uktaṃ bhagavatāpūrvaṃ pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ

chaṃdogāyatramityuktaṃ chaṃdaṃ sāmuttamottamaṃ

nārāyaṇāstrijagatāṃpatirdevassanātanaḥ

viniyogo bhagavataḥ prasādārtha sudāhṛtaḥ

jamadagnirvaśiṣṭaśca kaśyayogastya eva

gāyatryudimaganuṣṭupyavṛhatīpaṅktireva ca

dharodhruvastathā soma āyaugnirvāyureva ca

kruddholkāyāgnijāpāṃtai kṛṣṇavarṇā yadṛdyatha

vīro kṛpāgni jāpāṃte piṃgaleti padātparaṃ

hemavarṇāyakavacaṃ sahasro klāyaśabdataḥ

aṃgulīpaṃcakanyāsamevameva samācaret

vinyasya nama sāyuktānnābhau guhye tataḥ paraṃ

sa viṃdukākṣasyāsaḥ kartavya iti bhūyate

astraṃ tathodare pṛṣṭhe nyāsamaṣṭāṃgamācaret

aṃtavīhirmātṛkākhyānyāsaṃ kuryādanyadhīḥ

devatāmātṛkāsākṣāccidrūpāśrīḥsarasvatī

hrasvadīrghāṃdyaṃtayutaiḥ ṣaḍvirvargaiḥ ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ

ādikṣāṃtākṣaraśreṇīmāṇikyākalpadhāriṇīṃ

athāṃtarmātṛkānyāsaḥ kaṃṭhahṛnnābhiguhyake

daśapatre ca ṣaṭpatre catuḥpatredvipatrake

ekaikavarṇamekaikapatrāṃtarvinnyasenṛpa

śirovadanavṛttepi cakṣuḥ śrotrayugepyatha

urdhvādhodaṃtapaṃktau ca vinyaset ṣoḍaśasvarāt

ṭa ta vargadvayaṃ pādasaṃdhyagreṣu tathānyaset

hṛdormūlakakulkakṣahṛdādikarapavaye

paṃcāśadakṣaranyāsaḥ krameṇaivaṃ vidhīyate

māyālakṣmī kāmavījapūrvonyastavya iṣyate

kāṃtyai tathā mādhavāya tuṣṭau iti nyaset namaḥ

madhusūdanaśāṃtī ca trivikramakriye tathā

medhāyai ca hṛṣīkeśoharṣāyai ca namastathā

lajjāyai vāsudevāya lakṣmyai saṃkarṣaṇāya ca

aniruddhā paratyai ca svarāṃte pravadedatha

durgāyai ca prabhāyai ca khaḍgine vinyasedatha

haline ca tathāvāṇyai tathāmuśalinevadet

pāśine vijayāyai ca tathaivāṃkuśinevadet

vimadāyai namastataḥ naṃdajāsunaṃdāyai naṃdine ca smṛti tathā

samṛddhyaiharayeśuddhyai kṛṣṇaṣuddhīnataḥ paraṃ

śauraye ca kṣamāyai ca śūrāya paramātataḥ

kledinyaiṣiśca mūrtiśca klinnāyai tadanaṃaraṃ

puruṣottamaśca vasudāvaline ca parā tathā

valāyāpyatha sūkṣmāyai vṛṣāghnāya tataḥ paraṃ

haṃsāya ca prabhāyesyādvarāhāya niśātathā

vidyutāyaitvagātyā ca hyasṛgātmā ca hyasṛgātmā tataḥ paraṃ

jīvātmane ca paramātmane nama itīrayet

vinyasyaivaṃ kramādbhūpavirāḍrūpī na saṃśayaḥ

śirovadanavṛtādiścayaṃ nyāsakramaḥ smṛtaḥ

dehināṃ nyāsamātreṇa brahmatādātmyasiddhidaḥ

vinyasya satataṃ dhīmānbhagavanmayanāṃ vrajet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvā sākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvā yathā śaktyā dinedine

ācāryānugrahādbhūtvā mahatīṃ siddhimaśnute

keśavādirayaṃnyāsonyāsamātreṇadehināṃ

tatvanyāsamatho vakṣye śṛṇuṣvāvahitonṛpa

mādayaḥ pratilomyenakāṃtāḥ sparśasaṃjñakāḥ

jīvaḥ prāṇaśca buddhiścāpyahaṃkāromanastathā

pūrddhni ghrāṇe ca hṛdayepyupastho pādayorapi

jñāneṃdriyāṇiyaṃ cāpi tatasthāne nyaset punaḥ

tathaiva tatatsthāneṣu tattadeva pravinyaset

ākāśānilatejāṃsi salilaṃ pṛthivī tathā

śahau ca saṣalāḥ paṃcatathāyaśca valā-api

hṛtpadma somasūryāya svakalāyuktaṃ maṃḍalāṃ

parameṣṭhī ca puruṣo viśvavyāpīnivartakaḥ

mūrdhnāsye hṛdiguhye ca pādayorvyāpakaṃ tataḥ

atatvasyāpya pūjyasya tatprāpterhetunāpunaḥ

yaḥ kuryātatvavinyāsaṃ sa eva bhavati dhruvaṃ

tatassa eva tatvāni sarvaṃ tasminpratiṣṭhitaṃ

sarvatatvamayaḥ sākṣātatvadhidvavidbhavet

viśvaṃmarovijāḍātmāsākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavet

ācāryānugrahaṃ prāpya śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ japan

tanmūrtipaṃjaranyāsasya tanmūrtisiddhaye

namo bhagavate vrūyādvāsudevāya ityatha

śrīmaṣṭākṣarau vidyāmādāveva samuccaran

vāsudevadvādarśārṇasyaikekākṣaramuccaran

nāmaikaikamuapadāya sūryasyāpi ca nāmasu

lalāṭenābhi hṛdayakaṃṭhapārśvāṃsakaṃdhare

keśavaśca tato vrūyānnārāyaṇa iti dvayaṃ

trivikramo vāmayaśca śrīdharo navamaḥ smṛtaḥ

viṣṇorddhādaśanāmānicāmūnimunisaptama

iṃdrocitravasānpūṣā ca parjanyo daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ

tanmūrtipaṃjaranyāsastubhyamuktomayāvibho

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvācāryānugrahātsadā

tanmūrtipaṃjaranyāsaṃkṛtvā tanmūrtisiddhaye

varṇanyāsaṃ daśavidhaṃ pravakṣyāmi tataḥ paraṃ

ādau yogāsanamayaṃ svadehaṃ bhāvayeddhiyā

pīṭhoparitalaṃ pārśvadvayanābhimukhātmakaṃ

navaśakti mahāpīṭhamaṃtrayukte tataḥ paraṃ

svātmānameva dhyātvātha na gavanmayamacyutaṃ

ādhāre hṛdivakaktre narormūlayugale tathā

ekaikaṃ mūlamaṃtrārṇanamoṃtaṃ praṇavādikaṃ

kaṃṭhe nābhau ca hṛdayestanayugme ca pārśvayoḥ

mūrdhnāsye netrayugalokarṇayugme tataḥ paraṃ

vāhusaṃdhyaṣṭakepādasaṃdhiṣvaṣṭa subhūpate

aṃgulīṣvaṣṭasunyāsaḥ ṣaṣṭoguṣṭhaṃ vineritaḥ

tvagasṛṅmāsamedosthimajjāśuṣkamāyānnṛpa

prāṇarūpaṃ svahṛdayāṃvuja evākṣarānnyaset

hṛdayeccoderapaścādūrujaṃthāpadeṣu ca

kapolaskaṃdhayugaloruddhayecaraṇadvaye

itthaṃ daśavidhaṃ varṇanyāsaṃ kṛtvā dine dine

mūlamaṃtrākṣaranyāsaṃ kṛtvānyāsaṃ kṛtvā daśavidhaṃ nṛpa

mātṛkākṣaravinyāsaṃ kecidbrahmavidomalāḥ

mātṛkāvarṇavinyāsa ādyaḥ śuddha itīritaḥ

māyāvījādikonyāsaścaturthaḥ samudīritaḥ

vāṅmāyākamalādyaścanyāsaḥ ṣaṣṭhā udīritaḥ

haṃsamaṃtrādikaḥ paścānnyāsoṣṭama udīritaḥ

vaiṣṇavomātṛkānyāsodarāmaḥ samudīritaḥ

kecidbrahmavidonityaṃ kurvaṃtya khilasiddhidaṃ

vedāgamapurāṇeṣu kathitā brahmavittamaiḥ

nyāsaṃ keśavakīrtyādyamavaśyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ nyaset

vinyasya mātṛkāmaṃtarvahiḥ pūrvaṃ dine dine

tatvanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvānyasya tanmūrtipaṃjaraṃ

itthaṃ vinyasya vidhivat sākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavet

nyāsaṃ caturaśavidhaṃ kṛtvedyaṃ nityamādarāt

upadiśyaivapuratonyāsajālamidaṃ guruḥ

caturdaśavidhanyāsamajñātvāyogururnṛpa

nāvātmavaṃcakau proktau vubhāvapi na saṃśayaḥ

satataṃ siddhamaṃtrasya vahujñasya mahātmanaḥ

sahasraṃ yojanaṃ vāpi gatvā vedārthapāragaṃ

pradadyādapi gṛhṇīyācchrīmaddaṣṭākṣarīṃ śubhāṃ

nyāsaḥ syānmaṃtrasantāhonyāsahīno na siddhidaḥ

caturdaśavidhinyāsaṃ kṛtvā japtvā dine dine

caturdaśavidhanyāsapūrvikāmiṃdirāpateḥ

tamācāryaṃ vijānīhi sākṣānmadrūpiṇaṃ vibhuṃ

sarahasyamanordātājñeya ācārya īśvaraḥ

vahuyojana sāhasramapi gatvā mahīpate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ samastanyāsapūrvikāṃ

sa eva sakalanyāsa pūrvavidyāpradovibhuḥ

tameva daivatāṃ viddhi sadācāryaṃ ghṛṇānidhi

paramātmā paraṃbrahma sa eva harirucyate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāṃ samastanyāsapūrvikāṃ

caturdaśavidhanyāsakartā svācāryamīśvaraṃ

adevo na yajñedevamityeṣāśāścatīśruti

ācāryānujñayā nityaṃ kṛtvetthaṃ nyāsamādarāt

ājñāsiddho mahāyogī bhavedbrahmasamonṛpa

tīrtvā siddhimavāpnoti devānāmapi durlabhāṃ

sakṛsmaraṇatovāpi japakoṭiphalaṃ labhet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsiddhiṃ kalpakoṭiśatairapi

sārātsāratadaṃ puṇyamāgameṣu śrutiṣvapi

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vidyāmādāvuccārya yatnataḥ

vidrūparmātṛkānāma prokteyaṃ sarvakāmadhuk

cidrūpamātṛkānyāsamavaśyaṃ pratyahaṃ nyaset

vinyasya pratyahaṃ japtvā śatamaṣṭocaturaṃ tu vā

caturdaśavidhaṃ nyāsaṃ nyasyācidrūpamātṛkāṃ

samastanyāsasahitā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubhā

brahmatādātmyasaṃsiddhimaṇimādiguṇāṃśriyaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāprajapyā sarvasiddhidā

śrīdaṣṭākṣarīṃvidyāṃ dadyācchiṣyāya sadguruḥ

taṃtre dāśarathīyesminnadhyāyoṣṭādaśaḥ smṛtaḥ

dhyāyennirāmayaṃ vastu sarvabrahmāṃḍanāyakaṃ

anuttamaṃ durādharṣaṃ durvijñeya manūpamaṃ

ādimadhyātarahitaṃ niṣkalaṃ niraṃjanaṃ

mūādi brahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ tejaḥ paramakṣayaṃ

dhyāyet svahṛdayāṃbhoje paramātmānamacyutaṃ

kalpadrumagaṇākīrṇāṃ divyaprākāraśobhitaṃ

tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃdhāma vaikuṃṭhamiti viśrutaṃ

tanmadhye nagarī divyāsā'yodhyeti prakīrtitā

divyākṣarogaṇaiḥ strībhissarvataḥ samalaṃ kṛtāṃstoraṇairvṛtā

caṃḍādidvārapālaiśca kumudādyaiḥ surakṣitā

ārūḍha yauvanernityaṃdivyaṃ nārīnarairvṛtā

ratnaprākārasahitaṃ hematoraṇaśobhitaṃ

divyākṣarogaṇaiḥ strībhissarvataḥ samalaṃkṛtaṃ

māṇikya staṃbha sāhasrajuṣṭaṃ ratnamayaṃ śubhaṃ

madhye siṃhāsanaṃ ramyaṃ sarvadevamayaṃ śubhaṃ

dharmajñānavaraiśvaryavairāgyaḥ pādavigrahaiḥ

ādhāraśakti cicchukti mukhyaśaktibhirāvṛtaṃ

chaṃdomayaṃ sarvamaṃtramayaṃ ratnavibhūṣitaṃ

tanmadhyeṣṭadalaṃ padmamudayārkaśataprabhaṃ

āsīnaṃ paramātmānaṃ śriyā sahaparātparaṃ

yuvānamudayādityasamakoṭi samadyutiṃ

prabuddhaṃ puṃḍarīkākṣaṃ kāmadevāya samabhruvaṃ

muktāphalābhadaṃtādyasasmitadharavidrumaṃ

taruṇāditya varṇābhyāṃ kuṃḍalābhyāṃ virājitaṃ

maṃdārapārijātāḍhyakavarīkṛtakeśakaṃ

hārasvarṇasragāsaktakaṃṭhuṃgrīvavirājitaṃ

pīnavṛtāyatabhujaiścaturbhirupaśobhitaṃ

vālārkakoṭiśaṃ saṃkāśakaustubhādivibhūṣisau

divyamālāṃvaradharaṃ vanamālīvirājitaṃ

vālātapanibhaślakṣṇapītavastrayugāṃvṛttaṃ

sajyothyacaṃdrapratimanakhapaṃṅktivibhūṣitaṃ

divyācaṃdanaliptāṃgaṃ divyapuṣpasraganvitaṃ

varadābhayahastābhyāmitarābhyāṃ tathaiva ca

hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīṃ suvarṇarajatatprajāṃ

ratnakuṃḍalasaṃyuktāṃ vālīyugalaśobhitāṃ

maṃdāraketakījātīkusumāṃcitakuṃḍalāṃ

bhagavaccāyasadṛśa bhūyugmena virājitāṃ

alaṃkaśaraccaṃdra sahasrasadṛśānanī

ekaviṃvaphalaprakhyādharaṃ dvayavirājitāṃ

susrignanīakuṭilakuṃtalairupaśobhitāṃ

ādyabhūṣaṇaciṃtākapadakādivibhūṣitāṃ

taptakāṃcana varṇābhāṃ taptakāṃcanabhūṣaṇaṃ

nānāratnavicitrāḍhyakanakāṃvujamālayā

bhujadvayadhṛtodagra padmayugmavirājitāṃ

mātaraṃ sarvajagatāṃśaraṇāgatavatsalāṃ

kāṃtyāvirājitapadāṃbhaktatrāṇaparāyaṇāṃ

aṃ takoṭibrahmāṃḍenāyakaṃ bhaktavatsalaṃ

pārśvayodharaṇīdevyānīlādevyā samanvitaṃ

anaṃtavihagādhīśaṃ senānyādyaiḥ sureśvaraiḥ

sarvadevottamairvaṃdyaṃ dhyeyaṃ sakalayogibhiḥ

itthaṃ lakṣmīpatiṃ dhyātvā nārāyaṇamajaṃ vibhuṃ

ācāryavadanādevadhyānasūktamidaṃ śubhaṃ

brahmatādātmyasaṃsiddhiṃ vrajatyāśu na saṃśayaḥ

vedāśvarathamārūḍhaṃ pītāṃvaravirājitaṃ

suvarṇaratnakhacitamudrikābhiralaṃkṛtaṃ

vālāditya sahasrābhaṃ kāmakoṭi samadyutiṃ

suvarṇaratnakhacitamudrikābhiralaṃkṛtaṃ

kaustubhodbhāsitoraskaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitaṃ

munibhirvālakhilyādyairbrahmarudrādidaivataiḥ

vīrāsana samāśīnaṃ vidyutkoṭi samāśriyā

ekonaviṃśakaḥ

yatra saṃpūjya lakṣmīśaṃ brahmānaṃdasukhaṃ labhet

avaśyaṃ sakalanyāsāḥ kartavyā gurvanujñayā

tataḥ hṛdayāṃbhoje dhyāyennārāyaṇaṃ vibhuṃ

tatpralīmapi ca dhyoyettrijaganmātaraṃ śriyaṃ

kirīṭavidyāmuccārya manasā kusumāṃjaliṃ

trayīvījaṃ kirīṭetike pūreti padaṃ tataḥ

śaṃkhacakragadāpadmahastaśabdamataḥ paraṃ

vakṣasthalapadaṃ paścācchrībhūmisahitetyatha

padaṃ dīpti karāpānaṃtādityeti padādatha

ayaṃkirīṭamaṃtraḥ syādbhagavanmūrti siddhidaḥ

taddivya bhūṣaṇaṃ proktamanyanmānuṣabhūṣaṇaṃ

avaśyaṃ bhagavadrūpī svayameva bhaveddhruvaṃ

taduktamālaṃkaraṇameteṣvapi mahīyate

suśuddhapadmarāgākhyaṃ maṇiviṃduvibhūṣitaṃ

ācāryakarṇayordatvā praṇipātapurasaraṃ

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcya ṣaṇmāṣādiṃ dirāpatiṃ

ācāryarūpiṇaṃ viddhimāmeva jagatīpatiṃ

makarākṛti saṃtaptakāṃcanena vinirmitaṃ

ācāryakarṇayordatvā śubhalagne śubhedine

uddhṛtya kāṃcanamayaṃ vimānaṃ divyamakṣayaṃ

brahmāṃḍamakhilaṃ bhitvā padaṃ prāpnoti śāśvataṃ

putrapautravatī māyurā rogyasahitāṃ labhet

brahmānaṃda sukhāvāptikaraṃ tatanna saṃśayaḥ

tadeva bhagavatyalyāśriyāpidhṛta bhanvahaṃ

grahaṇādiṣu puṇyeṣu kāleṣu niyatavrataḥ

brahmatādātmyasaṃsiddhimavaśyaṃ prāpnuyādasau

ācāryāya kapalyai tatsutebhyopi saṃtataṃ

yatra saṃpūjya lakṣmīśaṃ brahmānaṃdasukhaṃ labhet

tadvahirvilikhedvṛtaṃ caturasramataḥ paraṃ

sādhyaṃ karma samālikhya ṣaṭsukoṇeṣvataḥ paraṃ

śrīmadṛṣṭākṣarī varṇān vilikhedaṣṭapatrake

likhitvā dvādaśāreṣu vāsudeva mahāmanoḥ

nṛsiṃhānuṣṭubhanorekaikaṃ varṇamālikhet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīviyāyaṃtramatadudīritaṃ

caturasraṃ vināṣṭārṇayaṃtraṃ laghusamīritaṃ

yaṃtrametalikhitvādau saivarṇaṃ rājataṃtu vā

prāṇasaṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtvā subhalagne śubhedine

alaṃkāraiścavāsobhirapi tasya kalatrakaṃ

triśagreśubhedine *********

alaṃkāraiśca tādhika sāhasraṃ japtvā hutvā śataṃ ghṛtaṃ

athavādhārayeyaṃtraṃgulīkṛtya yatnataḥ

vilikhya dhārayitvādau pūjayedyadisaṃtataṃ

calaṃti saṃpadonyasya maṃdire kutracit kvacit

rakṣākaraṃ śubhakaraṃ sarkāriṣṭavimardadanaṃ

ācāryānugrahātasye mokṣa śrīśāśvātīśrubhā

atha pūjāvidhānaṃte pravakṣyāmi dvidhāndaya

vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānākriyāyogā tathaiva ca

śaṃkhaṃ oṃ namaḥ yadamāmāṣyapadaṃ bhagavate tataḥ

vāsudevāya śabdaṃ ca tataḥ sarvātmaneyaddaṃ

anena pīṭhaṃ maṃtreṇapīṭhaṃ bhagavatī yajet

śrīśrīśabdāvṛśākṣeryā samāvāhyaśriyaḥpatiṃ

mūlavidyāṃ śriyāṃśriyo maṃtrānbrahmavidyāṃ sāmuccaran

hṛdayādi ṣaḍaṃgāti ṣaṭkauṇaiṣathāccayaṃt

dikṣvabhyacyeściyaṃ vāṇīṃ ratiṃ prītiṃ vidikṣu ca

kaustubhaṃ muśalaṃ khaḍgaṃ vanamālā mataḥparaṃ

garutmaṃtaṃ śaṃkhanidhiṃ dikṣu vidmeśvaraṃ tata

caturthāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ dikpālaiḥ paṃcamaṃ vidaḥ

itthaṃ taṃtre sasamabhyarcyaramānāthaṃ jagatpatiṃ

vṛsūjāvidhānaṃte rahasyamapi saṃtataṃ

caṃdraṃ pracaṃ ītyagdvāreyāśebhadrasubhadrakau

dhātāraṃ ca vidhātārasutaradvāti pūjayet

aṃvukarṇaṃ saṃvaṃ netraṃ susamukhasujhatiṣṭitaṃ

tanmadhye bhagavaddivyāṃtaṃ pureti gṛhāya ca

maṇiprākyarasahritatomareti yadāsaraṃ

vimānaṃ tatra saṃpūjyaṃ tanmadhye divyaśabdataḥ

tanmadhye yadasaccārya rājāsthānayaṃdaṃ tataḥ

itthaṃ mamḍayamabhyarcya tanmadhye yadamadhyatha

ratnamaṃḍapamabhyarcyaṃ caturthyaṃtaṃ namocitaṃ

tanmadhye sarvarvaveheti samaddiṣyapadāṃtparaṃ

ratnasiṃhāsanāyeti nabhimuccāmaṃḍapamabhyacye caturthyaṃtaṃtanānvitaṃ

tanmadhye sarvavedeti sumaddivya padātparaṃ

siṃhāsanaṃ saṃmabhyarcya dharmaṃ jñānamataḥ paraṃ

ajñānamapyavairāgyamanaiśvaryaṃ vidikṣa ca

sāmavedaṃ tato vedamatharvaṇamataḥ paraṃ

abhyarcya maṃḍalaṃ vahneryarjadarkasyamaṃḍalaṃ

cainateyaṃtrayīrājaṃ yajecchaṃdāṃsyataḥ param

yā yaddivyapadaṃ yogapīḍāyanama ityatha

tanmadhye yadamuccāryavāla sūyaśatetyatha

padmamabhyarcya tanmadhye sāvitryai nama uccaret

vījaśatī tatonyasya devadevaṃ śriyaḥ pati

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃvidyāṃ vāsudevamanaṃtataḥ

savovacārānkurvīta śrīśrībrahmavidyayā

ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ yaścātkalpayāmi namaḥ padaṃ

ratnasiṃhāsaṃsanaṃ yādyamarghyamācamanīyakaṃ

ca sradvayaṃ svaparṇaccoyavīḥtayaṃ tataḥ

nānāvidhāni divyāniṃ kusumāni ca kalpayet

dhūpadīpaṃ kalpayitvānīrājanamataḥparaṃ

rakṣīmācamanīyaṃ ca bhūyo naivedyamādarārāt

tāṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca caturviśatyupacārān prakalapayet

ṣālagrāmaśilācaktodhyāyaṃtteḥthavāṃ tataḥ

bhūdevīṃ dakṣiṇeyāśvaivāmenīlā samarcayet

vāyavye madhyabhāge ca sarvadikṣu krammatraya

vimalāmāditobhyarcya tata utkarṣiṇīṃ nṛpa

iṃśāmānugrahe paścārcayedvasudhāyate

saṃkarṣaṇamathovāṇīṃ pradyumnaṃ ratimapyatha

keśaṃ ca nārāyaṇaṃ mādhavaṃ goviṃdaṃ viṣṇumeva ca

śrīdharaṃ na hṛṣīkeśaṃ yamanābhamataḥ paraṃ

pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca puruṣottamamapyatha

upeṃdraṃ ca hariṃ paścāccharī kṛṣṇaṃ cakramāghanet

nārasiṃhaṃ vāmanaṃ ca jāmadagryamataḥ paraṃ

buddha ca kalkimihyetānaṃvāṃ ratārāntramādyajet

anaṃtaṃ sarvamabhyarcya viṣṇarṣunamataḥ paraṃ

paṃcamāvaraṇaṃ proktamṛgvedaṃ yajuṣāṃgaṇaṃ

dharmaṃ ca yajñapuruṣaṃ ṣaṣṭhāṃvaraṇamīritaṃ

muśalaṃ sasamaṃ proktaṃ hevasyāvaraṇaṃ śubhaṃ

tāmasīnisṛtīyayaścabhārgavīvarūṇau tathā

rudrāṇīśaṃkaraupaścādurddhve vākcaturānanau

vajrāyudhaṃ śaktidaḍaukhaḍgaṃyāśaṃdhvaṃjaṃgadāṃ

dhyānmaruhuṇāścaiva viśvedevānataḥ paraṃ

kapilādi mahāsiddhāṃ nṛtātreyādiyoginā

jñāninojanakādyāścanṛyākṣaśarathādikān

arkāvṛtyā samabhyarcya lakṣmīnārāyaṇau vibhū

tata ācāryamuddiśya bhagavatsatridhau nṛpa

tāmraṃ dhānyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vāpi prasahaṃśceya icchatā

avaṃdhyaṃ divasaṃ kuryādanyathā pāpabhugbhavet

mahāpātakasaṃdyaudyanāśanaṃ bhuktimuktida

māsimāsyathā vā kuryāt prathamebhāgarvadine

alabhyayomegeṣu sadākartavyaṃ pūjanaṃ nṛpa

itthamarcyataḥ puṃsaḥ śreyo rāśirdinedine

itthaṃ śrīśrīśayajanaṃ mahānyāga udāhṛtaḥ

nyūnātirikta saṃpūtyai yāgasāphalyasiddhaye

ācāryadakṣiṇāhīnaṃ yaddinaṃ vasudhāpate

yamabhītihaṃ puṃsāṃ bhogakhargāyavargarda

lakṣmīnārāyaṇainityamitthamarcatonṛpe

pūjāvidhānametate proktaṃ janakabhūyaja

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā jayāsaktasya saṃtataṃ

samastābhīśitaṃ tasyā sidhyapi sudurlarbha

iti dāśarathīyetraviṃśodhyāya udīritaḥ

vedoditaṃ sadācāraṃ manvādyairapyudīritaṃ

yathāśriyathatistuṣo bhaktānāṃ sataṃ bhavet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī vidyāraśmimālāsamanvitā

purā bhagavatā prokaṃ rudrāya paripṛcchate

sādhya nārāyaṇe nādāvuktaṃ rājñe mahātmane

brahmacaryaṃ vagāhamsthyaṃ vanavāsaścatikṣutā

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā samastāśramiṇāmapi

sadācāravatānityaṃ samastanyāsasaṃyutā

saṃkṣeyādadittaṃ pūrvaṃ sadācāraṃ mahāmate

śṛṇu rājanpravakṣyāmi sadācāraṃ mahāmate

brāhmaṇākṣatiyāvaiśyāstrayovarṇādvijāḥ smṛtā

yeṣāṃ kriyābhiṣekādi śmaśānāṃtā ca vaihikī

tasyaṃdanātyākyuṃ savanaṃ sīmaṃtīnnayanaṃ tataḥ

nāmahyekādaśegehāñcaturthemāsinikṣamaḥ

śamamenī vrajatyeva vījagarbhajameva ca

samamevāṣṭame devā sāvitrīṃ brāhmaṇohaṃti

brahmatejobhivṛdhyarthaṃ viprobdeyaṃ vamerhati

mahāvyāhṛti pūrvacivedamadhyāyayedguruḥ

dhūrvoktavidhinā śaucaṃ kuryādā ca manasadā

srātvāṃvudetairmaṃtraiḥ prāṇānāyamyayasrataḥ

agnikārye tataḥ kṛtvā śrīmadadaṣṭākṣarīrataḥ

vuvaścasāvahamitisvupāptīta tato guruṃ

āpurvalaṃ yaśokṛddhirvarddhetaheraharniśaṃ

karmaṇāmanasāvācāhitaṃtasyatvaret sadā

śaktā kṛtajñāḥścacayodrīhikalyānasūyakāḥ

śrīmadaḥṣṭākṣarīṃ sāṃgāṃ jñātuṃjasu ca saṃtataṃ

vedīdhyayanasidhyarthaṃ prātyayeṣṭākṣarastha ca

dhārayenmekhilādaṃḍeya vīlājinameva ca

brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāmādimadhyāvasā tataḥ

vāgyato gurvanujñātībhuṃjītānnamakutsayan

anāyurogyamanāyuṣyamasurgyaṃ cāti bhojanaṃ

na dvirbhujītacaikasmin divākvāpi dvijottamaḥ

madhumāṃsaṃ prāṇihiṃsāṃbhāskarālokaṃnāṃjame

aupanāyanikaḥkāso brahmakṣatraviśāṃparaḥ

itothyūrvena saṃskāryāḥ patitādharmavarjitāḥ

sāvitrīyatitaiḥ sārdhaṃ saṃsārgaṃni samāvaret

upavītaṃ krameṇasyātkāryāsaṃśāṇamāvikaṃ

vilvayālāśayordvaṃge brāhmaṇasthanṛyasthatuṃ

āmaulitvālalāṭaṃ vā nāsikoddhe pramāṇataḥ

pradakṣiṇaṃ parīsāgnimuyasyāpadivākadaṃ

mātṛmātṛdyasūpitṛpitṛṣṇasṛpurasmarāḥ

aupanādya nikekāletato viprāgryamaṃdira

yāvadvedamadhītesaucaredvedatānitra

upakurvan gurornityaṃ tadakteṣu naiṣṭikaḥ

gṛhāśramaṃ samāśrisayaḥ punarbrahmacaryabhāk

anāśramī na tiṣṭheta dinamekamapidvijaḥ

jayaṃ hīmaṃ vrataṃdānaṃ svādhyāyaṃ pitṛryaṇaṃ

mekhalājinadaṃḍāścaliṃgaṃ syādbrahmacāriṇaḥ

tridaṃḍādiyateruktamupalakṣaṇamatravai

jīrṇaṃ kamaṃḍalaṃ daṃḍamayavītādine api

vidṛdhyāsṣoḍaśevarṣekeśāṃtaṃ karma cakramāt

tapomajña vratebhyaśca savesmācchrubhakarmaṇaḥ

vedāraṃbhe visargeva vidadyāstraṇavaṃ sadā

vedasyavadanaṃ proktaṃ gāyatrīnniyaddāparā

sahasraṃsādhikaṃ kiṃcittrikametajjayaṃtyamī

ātryavdṛmetadhobhyasthe srutidyastramananyadhīḥ

trivarṇamayamoṃkāraṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svāmititrayaṃ

dakṣrametāścajayaḥ vyāhṛti pūrvakaṃ pūrvakaṃ

viḥdhikratordaśaguṇo japakraturudīrit

aśvamedhādiyāgebhyo dvāvuktāvaṃdhikaukrat

adhikaṃ proktamācāryaivedavedāṃtaceddibhiḥ

saṃpūrṇadhānyadharaṇīddānasyakalamaśrute

śrutyabhyāsohi viprasya paramaṃ tapa ucyate

sadogdhīṃ dhenumutsṛjyagrāmakoḍaṃ dadhukṣati

sukalyaṃ saradvasyaṃ ca tamācāryāṃ vidurvadhāḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsāṃgālabhyateyanmukhāṃvujāt

yodhyāya vedekadeśaṃ śrute raṃgānyathāpi vā

yathāvidhi niṣaṃkādiyaḥ karma kurute dvijaḥ

athādhānātyākayajñādagrīyomādikānmakhyāt

piturmāturūpādhyāyā'harorācāryatopi ca

sahasraśodhikaḥ prokta ācāryobhagavānasau

ṣoḍaśārṇādividyāśca nṛsiṃhānuṣṭubhādayaḥśu

sa sa koriyannūnāṃvaśāstrāṇāmapi saṃtaṃtaṃ

śuddhaiva śrīmadaṣṭārṇā rājñīnātodhikāparā

yaḥ prayacchati viprosau sādhakāya mahīyate

brahmaviṣṇūmaheśebhyopyadhikaḥ śataśonṛpa

ācāryacaraṇaṃbhoje bhaktassadā bhavet

vaiśyānāṃdhānyadhenataścaturthānāṃ tu janmataḥ

tathā vipronadhīyāmastrayomīnāmadhāriṇaḥ

brahmacārīvaredaikṣyaṃ veśyasuprayatīnvahaṃ

na nāma pratigṛhṇīyātparokṣepyavahaṃ na

karṇaupidhāvavāstheyaṃ yātavyaṃ vātatonyaktaḥ

matsakṣarīkṣadrakīraḥ syāti raskārādbhāvet krimiḥ

puṣṭirvaṣa sahasrāṇivaṣṭāsukṛmitāṃ vrajet

kṛpiviṃśativarṣeṇajñāṇāgurudoṣayoḥ

pramadāsaprabhāvaṃti kṛcinnaivavipaścitaḥ

svavaśaṃ cāpi kurvaṃti sūtravaddhaśakuṃtavat

valavaṃtīdriyāṇpatramohayaṃtyapikovida

vidyāsidhyatitalpaścādbrahmapaśyatikevalaṃ

śaktyā varṣaśatenāpinokartuttasya niṣṇutiḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsāṃgā bhagavadraśmisaṃvutā

tasyoyakartaṃ kenāpi na śakyaṃ kalpakoṭibhiḥ

janakemātariguṇairuṣutuṣṭeṣu saṃtata

trīnyussamatikramya trikalpaṃ niyatatyatha

pītāmātā ca veddānāyuyadeṣṭā tataḥ paraṃ

caturvargaphalaṃ proktaṃ caturgurusamarcanāt

brahmavidyāpradācāryaṃ prāṇāṃtepinaḥ saṃtyajet

taṃptaktānarakeghoretiṣṭhasācaṃdratārakaṃ

sarvā api makṣavidyā yatrā tiṣṭhaṃti siddhidaḥ

sa eva sākṣādbhāgavānbhogasvargāyavargadaḥ

raśmividyāpradāvāryosvasamīpasthite paraṃ

ratnajātaissūvahnibhismatoṣyācāryamanvahaṃ

nirvāṇāryapraktoyaṃ sadācāraścadhīmatāṃ

vidyājātaṃyavitvā tu gṛhāśramamathāśrayet

ātrukūlyaṃ hidaṃyatyostrivargaphalahetave

pratikūlaṃ kalatrecennaskeṇahi kiṃ satataḥ

svabhāryāyaṃ vinītāyāṃ trivargovarddhate dhruvaṃ ca

jalaukākevalaṃ raktamādadānāmamasvinī

pramadā sarvaṃmādate cittaṃ vittaṃvalaṃ sukhaṃ

udvahena tato bhāryāṃ savarṇe sāhulakṣaṇaṃ

udvahetadvijobhāryāṃmyāsyāṃ mṛdubhāṣiṇīṃ

sulakṣaṇamucāritrāya surāyurvivarddhayet

kulakṣaṇāpiyāsādhvī sarvalakṣaṇayūsusā

lakṣmīśānugrahādeva gṛheyopi daṣavāpyate

nānāvidhairalaṃkāraissurūyāstābhavaṃtihi

tālāvarāyataraṃgirāyo bhavaṃti hi sulakṣaṇaḥ

nārāyaṇapriyāśrīrvāśacīvāśakravallabhā

svādhīnayatikānāṃcasuśīlānāmṛgīdaśāṃ

sulakṣaṇaiḥ smacāritrairapi maṃdoyuṣaṃpatiṃ

ācāryapatnīśrīvudhyāvāsolaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

te sarvabhāgyasahitāḥ kaṃdarpasadṛśaprabhāḥ

adhyāya ekaviṃśoyaṃ taṃtre dāśarathīrite

yeṣāṃ vijñānamātreṇa sarvayāyaiḥ pramucyate

gāṃdharvorākṣasaścīpiyaiśācoṣmaducyataṃ

dīyate tatsutaḥ eyāsuruṣānekaviṃśati

parāhādāpagoddaṃ dvayārṣastajjaḥ punātiṣadra

tatkanyāyāṃ samuhānnovaṃśyānaṣṭau punātisaḥ

āsuraḥ krayaṇādravyairgāṃdharvonyonyaṃdyaṃtaḥ

chalena kanyāharaṇātyaiśācogahiṃtoṣṭamaḥ

aṣṭamasteyuyāpiṣūḥpāṣiṣṭhānāṃ hi saṃbhavet

pratodorveśyayādeyovāsoṃtaḥ śūdrayā tathā

savarṇābhista sarvābhiḥ pāṇirgrāsvāsvayṃvidhiḥ

adharpyairdharmarahitāmaṃdabhāgyadhanāyuṣaḥ

strīṇaṃ varamanusmṛtvā yathā kāmyatha vā bhavet

śrīddhārhasarvaparvāṇi yatvāsājyānidhīmatā

ṛtukālābhigāmoyaḥ svadāratirataścayaḥ

ṛtuḥ ṣoḍaśayāminyaścatastrastāsuvahiṃtāḥ

yuktvā caṃdramasadusthaṃ madyāṃmūlaṃ vihīya ca

śuciṃ putraṃ prasūyeta puruṣārthaprasādhakaṃ

śuklamarāyapi kanyāyāḥ kanyāvikrayāya yakṛt

atonā evapi kanyāyā upajīve sitādhane

kevalaṃ nirayagāsteṣāmapi ca yūrvajāḥ

tatra tuṣṭhā mahālakṣmīrnivesedvānavāriṇāḥ

kuvivāhaḥ kriyālopaḥ kulepāta na hetavaḥ

paṃcayajñakriyāṃ vāpi patnīdainaṃdinīmapi

khaṃḍinī yeṣiṇī cukṣo khadakuṃcamārjanī

ṛtavaḥ paṃcanirdviṣṭāpṛhīśreya vivarddhanāḥ

atrodakupayobhūlaphalaivopi gṛhāśramī

satkṛtya bhikṣave bhikṣāṃ datvā tu māpnuyāt

tārayedvighnasaṃghebhyaḥ yāyaudhādapidustarāt

ājanmasaṃcitātpuṇyāpṛtchaṇātsavahirbhavet

etānyapi pradeyāni sadābhyāgatatuṣṭaye

praṇodhātithimannāśīkiliṣī ca gṛhāśrīmī

na pūrvakāla āyā tīnacadeṣṭa tataḥ kvacit

adatvātaṃvaliṃ tasmai yathā śaktyannamapye yet

atithiścārthinopyeṃtebhojyā nātra vivāraṇā

svārthaṃ yacannadyaṃ bhuṃkte kevalaṃ svodaraṃbharaḥ

aparāmṛśyayobhuṃṅkte sabhuṅgapāpamakṣayaṃ

vaiśvadevavivihīnāye ātithyena vivarjitāḥ

akṛtvā vaiśvadevaṃ tu bhuṃjate yadvijādhaḥ

vedoditaṃ svakaṃkarmanityaṃ kuryādataṃdritaḥ

ṣuṣṭyāṣṭamyosmyajedbhayaṃ tailaṃ māsasyabhakṣyaṃsāṃ

nodayaṃtamavekṣyet nāstaṃ yāṃtaṃ na madhyagaṃ

na vīkṣetītmanīrūpamasadhāvennavarṣati

devatāyatanaṃ vipraṃ dhenuṃ madhumṛdeghṛtaṃ

aśvatthacaisavṛkṣyaṃ ca guruṃjalaghṛtaṃ ghaṭa

hajasvalāṃ na sevetanāṣṇīyāt sahabhāryayā

nāṣṇaṃtīṃ striyamīkṣetatejaskāmodvijottataḥ

asaṃtarpye sadācāryaṃ na ratnaṃ dhārayet kvacit

na mūtraṃ gauvrajekuyonnavalmīkena bhasmani

viprasūrya vāyvagricaṃdrārkovagustapi

divāṃ saṃdhyāsukarṇasya brahmasūtra udamukhaḥ

mukhenopadhamennāgriṃ nagrāṃ nekṣetayoṣitāṃ

praṇihiṃsānukuvitanāṣṇīyāt saṃdhyayordvayoḥ

virāmūtraṣṭhivanaṃ nāsyukuryāddīrghajijīviṣuḥ

naikaḥ svadhyātkṛcicchatyenaśayānesavodhayeyet

nadivoddhatasāraṃ ca bhakṣayedadhinoniśi

yaṃtrikapriyo nasyātkāṃ syeyādaunadhāvayet

dātuḥ śrīdvraphalanāstibhoktā kilviṣa bhāgbhavet

nabhinnabhojaneṣṇīyānnāṣṇītāgnyādihṛṣitaṃ

vālātayaṃdivāsvāyaṃtyajedīryajajīviṣuḥ

hastauśironudhanuyānnākarṣehāsanaṃ yadā

karajaiḥ karajaccheraṃ tṛṇavedaṃvavarjayet

nāyadvoreṇagaṃtavyaṃ khaṃ veśmaparaveśmanoḥ

naśayīta kṛcigraḥ prāṇairbhuttotanaiva ca

saṃviśennārdracaraṇonīcchīṣṭaḥ kvacidavrajet

soyānatkaścanācāmannatiṣṭan dhārayārṣivet

na ca vīkṣetavirāmūtrenocchiṣṭasaṃspṛśedviraḥ

patitaissahasaṃ vā saṣyatanāyaiva jāyate

brāhmaṇyāddhāyate vipraḥ śūdrodhamoccahīyate

dvijuśrūṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śūdrāṇāṃ paramaḥ smṛtaḥ

āṃdolanaṃ karābhyāṃ ca keśānāṃ keśaluṃchanaṃ

brāhmaṇaḥ sānvayo yāti narakānaikaviṃśati

mahāvātardhanaurātrāvanadhyāyāḥ prakīrtitā

saṃdhyayordṛṣaloyāṃte rājavādhe ca sūpake

pratipadyapi pūrṇāḥ vāmamāyāṃ sarvayasu

upākarmaṇivotsargenāvibhānautarautale

anadhyoṣu caiteṣunādhīyīvradvijaḥ kvacit

bhūtāṣṭamyoḥ paṃcadaśyorbrahmacārī bhavet sadā

tasmāta dūratastyājya variṇāṃ copasevanaṃ

mavatāṃ yasmāchreyovidyānadarlabhā

prīpaṃcanānṛtaṃ vrūyādeṣadhamau mahīyate

bhadrairavahisaṃsargonābhadraiśca kadācana

etavīkṣītanāśricissatyabrahmarṣigrahatārakāḥ

utkocadhūtadīnārtadravyaṃ dūrātparityajet

na spṛśedanimitetasvānikhānitvanāturaḥ

pādaśododakaṃ sūtrannmucchiṣṭatrodakāni

aharniśaṃ śratijoyāṣṇautā vāniṣevaṇāt

vṛddhānprayatnādvaṃdetaneṣāṃdadyāt khamāsanaṃ

śrutibhūdevadevānāṃ nṛpasādhutayasvināṃ

namanuṣyastutiṃ kusyātrātmānaṃ vahumānayet

adharmānsevate pūrvaviṣūnavisarjayan

uddhṛtyaṃ paṃcamṛtpiṃḍātsāyātyarajalāśaye

kuśrāddhayāṃ pātrabhūtāyatkiṃcidīyatevasu

ācāryadānaleśasyakulāṃ nāṃhīti ṣoḍaśīṃ

svargerṇautāvubhausyātāṃya tato śraddhayā svadhaḥ

kṣaretkīrtanatodānamāyurviprāvamānataḥ

maṇimālyagṛhaṃdhānyaṃ grāhyametadupasthitaḥ

abhyudyatāni grāhyāṇi cetānyapinigṛṣṭātaḥ

mādhyasyamāśrayeddedīsutevidyagnisṛḥjya ca

avaśyaṃ gṛhiṇīkāryamācāyecaraṇārcanaṃ

ekana tanmanāmuktirnaranārāyaṇāśrame

janmanāmuktirācāryacaraṇarcanāt

sadācāra paro nityaṃ misamācāryasevakaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvā sākṣādbrahmamayo bhavet

yamākarṇya narodhīmānmuṃcatyajñānajataṃmaḥ

yaṃ śrutvopi narohīmānmucyateyāyarāśibhiḥ

tatra puṇyaṃ bhavedviprasmṛtivartmanānubhājanāt

kalikālādvapi sadāchidraṃ prāthajighāṃsataḥ

kalikālāhapi hato brāhmaṇe raṃdhradarśanāta

tadrarataḥ parityajya naro na nirayī bhavet

adauvapitryaṃ palalamavatsāṇoya yasyajet

vayavekaśaphaṃdvepatakramerakamāvikaṃ

āyuṣkāmaiḥ svargakāmaiḥstyājyaṃ māṃsaṃ prayatnataḥ

tyajetyaryuṣitaṃ sarvamakhaṃḍasnehanirmita

kālyamitthaṃ yalaṃ bhakṣayanna ca doṣabhāk

yādaśāṃbhavatīpratyalaulyānmāṃsoyajñevinaḥ

nighrannahiṃsakoviprastāsāmapiśubhagatiḥ

atra hi sātha hiṃsāsyādviṃsānyatra sudastarā

darācārasyatastheha nāmatrāpi sukhāṃ kvacit

upakartāghātapitāhiṃsakā aṣṭadhā smṛtāḥ

aṃmāsabhakṣako yastu dvitīyotra viśiṣyate

sukhaduḥkhāni tulyāni yathātmrani tathāpare

yakvātaṃ hi punaḥ yaścitsārvamātmanisarbhavet

kriyāṃ kriyāhīne kuto dharmo dharmahīne kutaḥ sukhaṃ

tasmāddhermotrakartavyaścāturvaṇe na yatnataḥ

dānaṃ ca vidhinādeyaṃ kāleyātrevamāvataḥ

na kevalaṃ hi dhāti śeṣaṃ tasya ca naśyati

tadakṣaṃya bhavedatra yaratra ca na saṃśayaḥ

grahaṇādiṣu kāleṣu yodhvādādārādhṛta

varṣyaśanamitaṃ taddravyaṃ śataniṣkamudāhṛtaṃ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoje varṣāśanamitaṃ dhanaṃ

tulāpuruṣamukhyāni mahādānāni bhūriśaḥ

pitṛvaśyaśataiyukto mātarvaśya śatairapi

mātāpitṛvihīnaye maujīpāṇi grahādibhiḥ

brāhmaṇāyadaridrāyapāṇigrahamitaṃdhanaṃ

nāgnihotrairnatarccaiyonāgniṣṇomādibhirmakhaiḥ

pittagehepiyākanyārataḥ paśya sasaṃskṛnā

yastāpariṇayenmohāt sabhavedvaṣalopatiḥ

vijñāpya doṣabhayoḥ kanyāyāśca varasya ca

striyaḥ pavitrāḥ satataṃ naitādaṣyaṃtī kutravit

kalyāṇavāṇīṃ gaṃdharvastenamedhyāssadāstriyaḥ

śrutodbhavettugaṃdharvāstalyāge ca pradīyate

pitaghnāvikṛtarajāsratastāṃ parivarjayet

anyathā tatphaladātuḥ pratigrāhīya tatyadhaḥ

devabhuktāṃ dadadātā na svargamadhigacchati

yajñeyātrāṇi sarvāsinaduṣyaṃti vudhāḥ kvacit

nāryorati prayogeṣyaśghrāmṛgagrahaṇeśuciḥ

brāhmaṇāh yādato medhyāḥ striyomedhyāśca sarvataḥ

natyātyādūṣitānārīnārupāgonavidhīyate

saṃśuddhārajasānāryastaṭinīvegataḥ śuciḥ

somayāsasaukhyānibhuṃjecātrīpikīrtibhāk

kanyāpradaḥ prarvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ

gamyaṃ tvabhāvedātaṇāṃ kanyākuryāt svayaṃvaraṃ

paribhūtāmadhaḥ śayyāṃ vāsayedhabhicāriṇī

garbhabhartavadhādau ca bhakṣatyapicayātake

gattiṃ utyādyaputraṃ śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇyādevahīyate

devāghāstanavāśraṃti sa vahva ca svārgaṃ na gacchati

kṛtyābhirnihatānīvayaśyeyuṣmānyaṃ saṃśayaḥ

bhūtikāmaitaraitityaṃ saṃkaṭeṣūtkaṭeṣu ca

ramaṃtevatāstatra tatrasyuḥ saphalāḥ kriyāḥ

ācāryapatnī saṃpūjyā viśeṣādbhārgavedine

tāṭaṃkahemaciṃtākavālī yugmādibhūṣaṇaiḥ

vimuktaḥ sakalāyabhṇedustarātsaṃkaṭādapi

yatra tuṣyati bhartrā strīyayābhartācatuṣyati

tayo hato hato hato homaḥ prahutobhautikovaliḥ

paṃcayajñānimānkurvanbrāhmaṇonāsīdati

brāhmāṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchedvāhujātamanāmayaṃ

jātamātraḥ śiśrustāvadhāvadaṣṭau samāḥ smṛtāḥ

bharaṇaṃ yoṣyavargasya dṛṣṭādaṣṭaphalodayaṃ

ācāryaḥ svayittāmātvapasāni samāśritāḥ

sajīvati yumānyatraivahnibhiścīyajīvyate

do nānāḥtha viśiṣṭebhyo dātavyaṃ bhūti kāmyayā

vibhāgaśīlasaṃyuktodayāvoṃśca kṣamānvitaḥ

śarvarāmadhyamauyāmauhutaśeṣaṃ ca yaddhaviḥ

navaitāni gṛhasthena kāyorāyabhyāgateguro

abhyutthānamihāyātasastehaṃ pūrvabhāṣaṇaṃ

tathaivavyayayuktāni kāryāṇyetāminaiva

śayyātṛṇajalābhyaṃgadivāgārhasthāsiddhidāḥ

paiśūnyaṃ yaradārāścadrohaḥ krodhānṛtāpriyaḥ

navāśyakakarmāṇi kāryāṇi prativāsaraṃ

vaiśca devaṃ tathātithyaṃ navamaṃ pitṛtarpaṇaṃ

janmarkṣamaithunaṃ maṃtrīgṛhachidra ca vanaṃ

navaitāni prakāśyānirahaḥ pāpamakutsitaṃ

kanyādānaṃ guṇotvā ṣonānyatkenāpi kutracit

mātāpitṛgṛruṣvevanavakaṃdatamakṣaya

kuvaidyekitave dhūrte śaṭhe malle ca vaṃdiṣu

putreṣu sātsasarvasvaṃdārāśca śaraṇāgatāḥ

yo dadāti sabhūḍhātmā prāpraśritairviśūdhyati

deyaṃ ca viśvavidyāgenānyatkasmāpi kutracit

anyati navakaṃ vacmi sarveṣāṃ svargamārgadaṃ

usteyamiṃdriyākrodhassarveṣodharmasādhanaṃ

satāmabhimatāṃ puṇyaṃ gṛhasthonāvasīdati

yasyaitaivinayāḍhyāśca tasya sarvatra gauravaṃ

syāyonyagṛhavāsaścanārīṇāṃ dūṣaṇānī ṣaṭ

savārdhuṣika ityuktassarvakarmavahicchrutaḥ

daṣṭāṃkāmayāto sonāmrāmāhiṣikah smṛtaḥ

atevārdhuṣikaṃ dṛṣṭvā nirāśāḥpirāmāḥ pītarogatāḥ

tāvadaśraṃtitaroyāvabhīktohavirguṇaḥ

krīḍatyoṣadhayaḥ sarvāyāsyāmaḥ paramāṃgatiṃ

rodityannadīpamānaṃ kiṃ mayāduṣṭataṃkṛtaṃ

satārayati dātāraṃ daśapūrvāndṛśāparān

navarātrauvaserjāṣṭena kuryādvaidikāṃ śrutiṃ

suvāsinīnāmevaṃ syānnāthaścāmuṃḍanaṃ bhavet

keśānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāyaṣvādviguṇadakṣiṇāṃ

yo gṛhītvā vivāsinī nāmevaṃ syānnānyathā muṃḍanaṃ bhavet

keśānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya dadyādviguṇadakṣiṇāṃ

annatasya na bhoktavyaṃ kṛthāyākohi saṃsmṛtaḥ

parivetā sa vijñeyaḥṣarivitī ca pūryataḥ

pīṭhedeśāṃtarasthe ca sūke pravratitaṃ jaḍe

vedākṣarāṇi yāvaṃti niyuṃjyādarthakāraṇāt

yasvasuvrātito bhūtvāsevagtemaithunapunaḥ

śūrasaṃsaṃparkaṃ śūdreṇa sahāsanaṃ

śūdrādāhṛtyanirvāyaṃye yatyavudhādvijāḥ

hastadatātilavaṇavyaṃ janādīni saṃtataṃ

āsase naiva pātreṇayadannamupadīyate

aṃgulyādatakāvyaṃ pralakṣalavaṇaṃ ca yat

agratonivasensūkhohṛraiścaguṇānvitaḥ

brāhmaṇāti kramonāsti vipre vedavivarjita

sānnikṛṣṭamadhīyātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ yo vyatikrāmeta

gorakṣakān vāṇitakātstathā kāmakuśīlakān

kāmakuśīlakān

devadravyanibhāgena brahmasvaharaṇena ca

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojepuraścaraṇākarmaṇi

māsāsyaṃtaratovaśyamathavā dakṣiṇāśubhā

māsādvayāttribhirmāsairdāḥ tavyāvṛddhisaṃyutāḥ

māsaṣasūmatikramyasvarṇeyīpatatyadhaḥ

māsenanenerusadṛśaghṛṇaṃdasudhāyate

sadya eva pradātavyaṃ puraścaraṇakarmasu

sadyovitaraṇaṃ kāryamācāryārthe mahīyate

bhikṣānnamapivācāryakule bhikṣe tatraiva hi

sadharāvapradātavyāmāsatritayatoṣi vā

vidyasāśī bhavennityaṃ cāgratabhojanaḥ

hastau prakṣālyagahvaṣayaḥ pivedbhojanottaraiḥ

****** gaṇā aṃgaṇikānnaṃ cayāke

pakṣe vā yadi māse vā yasyageheti na dvijaḥ

satriṇāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ ca yatīnāṃ brahmadhāṇāṃ

ajīrṇotyuditavāṃte śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune

caisuvṛkṣedhiṃtiṃpūyaṃ śivanimolyabhojanaṃ

agryāgrāragavāṃgoṣṭhedeva brāhmaṇasannidhī

khalakṣetragataṃ dhānyaṃ kūpavāriṣu tajjalaṃ

yadveṣṭitaśirābhuṃkte ghāndaṅge dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ

dharmaśāstrarathāruṣavedakhaḍgadharādvijā

gaṃdhābharaṇayātyāniyaḥ prāyacchati saṃtataṃ

amṛtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ syābhaṃkṣatriyānnaṃ yayaḥ smṛtaḥ

vaiśvadevena homena devatābhyarcanairjayaiḥ

vyavahārānurūpeṇanyāyena yaddayājjanaṃ

pravāhānaddha vāyamatramutvādhamicchati

ajñānatimirādhasyamadyapānaratasyatra

navṛthāṃśayathaṃ kuryāt svalyepyarthenarovudhaḥ

kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavāṃ bhaktaṃ janakṣaye

nayamaṃ yamaṣṇāharātmāvaiyama ucyate

ekaḥ kṣamāvatāṃ doṣānadvitīyaḥ kathaṃcana

yadenaṃ kṣamayāyuktamaśaktaṃ manyate janaḥ

svādhyāyayogeratamānasasya mokṣo dhruvaṃ nityamahiṃsakasya

śrīdvratsatyavādī ca nityamācāryasevakaḥ

devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ vāpyanugrahāt

adhyāyoyaṃtrayo viśastaṃtre dāśarathīrita

valīyavita saṃyuktasūtīyāśramamāśrayet

patnīputreṣu niḥkṣipya yatmāvāvanamāviśet

śākamūlaphalaivopi paṃcayajñānpravartayet

amūlaphalabhikṣābhirarcayedbhikṣukātithīn

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojaśuśrāṣaṇaparāyaṇaḥ

vaitānika ca juhuyādagnihotraṃ yathāvidhi

svayaṃkṛtaṃ calacaṇanāyānmadhuphalapradaḥ

utyabhūmāścignemāsityajenṛtyūvaṃ saṃcitaṃ

triṣadvadvādaśamāsānnaphalamūlādisaṃgrahī

cāṃdrāyaṇavratīvāsyātyakṣabhugvāthamāsabhuk

tapasāśoṣayedvehaṃ pitṛdevāṃśca tarpayet

bhikṣayet proṇayātrārthaṃ tāpasān ṣanavāsinaḥ

itthaṃ ranāśramīviprobrahmatokemahīyate

āyuṣyasyaturīyāṃśetyatkāsaṃgānparivrajet

tathā yajñānaniṣṭācamokṣanicchavrajatyadhaḥ

sarvabhūtāni tasyeha prayacchaṃtya bhayaṃ sadā

sidhyarthamasahāyaḥ syādbrāmamannārthamāśrayet

kālameva pratīkṣeta nityamoṃkāratāyakaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāmavaśyaṃ nirato bhavet

vastraprakṣālanādyaiśca nityamāvāryasevakaḥ

vṛkṣabhūlaniketaśca mumukṣarihaśasyate

yateścatvārikarmāṇiyaṃ carmanopayadyate

vījjāṃ kurāṇāṃ jaṃtūnāṃ hiṃsā tatra yara yato bhavet

vācaṃ vadedanudvegosakṛdvākenacitkvacit

samadhyātyaniratonīcakeśanaravodarī

alāvudārumṛdveṇāpātraṃ śastaṃ nayaṃcama

ekākālaṃ varohaikṣyaṃtakuyo tatra vistaraṃ

dateśarāvasaṃyāt bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ careyatiḥ

rāgadveṣa vinirmukto bhikṣurmokṣāyakalpate

kiṃtasvānekataṃtreṇa kṛtakṛtyaḥ sajagatpate

nirdṛhivyatitsarvamekarāprovinoyatiḥ

dehatyatāṃyunagarbhagarbhakleśaṃ ca dāruṇaṃ

apriyaiḥ sahasaṃyogasadharmaduḥkhasaṃbhavaṃ

karmadīṣasasandratānṛṇāṃ jātīranekadhā

kurvaḍetamuktayeyatraṃ yatra kutrāśramerataḥ

avaśyamācāryayadadvaṃdvaśruśrūṣaṇerataḥ

raśmividyāsamānyuktanyāsajālaṃ samanvitāṃ

kṛnatretādvāpareśukramādāśramasaṃyutāḥ

brahmacaryaṃ vanevāsoyugetiṣṭhyena vidyate

putramitrakalatrādiyoṣaṇāsaktamānasāḥ

daṃbhatībhānṛtayutāḥ śaucāravivarjitāḥ

vṛthā śāyaṃ vimuṃcati bhikṣārthamapi saṃtataṃ

tāsu saṃsarganiratāḥ putradarśanalālasāḥ

gārhasvapnevasakalāśrameṣūtamamucyate

prāyaścitairvimuktaḥ sthānnānyatrāsakta āme

asminkaliyuge ghore gṛhasthā api saṃtataṃ

paranārīṣuniratādaṃbhalobhānṛtapriyāḥ

eteyamātithībhūtāssarvenarakabhāginaḥ

kurvatnācāryaśruśūṣāṃ kurvanprāṇadhanairapi

śrīmadṛṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvā śuddhakaivalyamaśrute

yauṇaṃ māsyāmamāyāṃvāmāsīmāḥsisamāhitaḥ

ekaikaśohunedātyaṃ mūlanāṣṭotaraṃśataṃ

niṣkatekanadardhaṃ vā pradatvā dakṣiṇāṃ nṛpa

amāsomena raviṇā sahitā yadi bhūyate

datvā śeṣatoniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā prayatnataḥ

prasahaṃ labhate vaśyaṃ tasminmāsi na saṃśayaḥ

anusyādyasutānuvipronarakeṣu nimajjati

agniṣṭomādayoyāgāḥ kāyoḥ saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇāḥ

atha vā sarvayāgīnī phalamicchanmahītale

nyāsa yuktā prajaptavyā hotavyā haviṣā sadā

grahaṇādiṣu koleṣu navarātravrate śubhe

yathokta dakṣiṇāsāṃgādātavyā ca sudhāyate

nārāyaṇabhidhaṃ sūtāṃmapi pātakabhaṃjanaṃ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja śuśrūṣānyahamādarāt

mokṣalakṣmīsādhanāni catvārye tāni bhūyate

tatrāpyācārya śuśrūṣā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīratiḥ

gṛhastha eva devānāpi gṛhastha eva devānāpi rahaṇāṃ yogināmapi

yaṃgadhovadhirā aṃdhāvaidikaṃ kartamakṣamāḥ

śraute karmarāyaśaktānāṃ caturthāścama īritaḥ

kṛtvācāryasya śuśrūṣāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ tayan

brahmanaṃdamaṣīktimāvaśyaṃ abhate nṛpaḥ

nyāsassahāṣṭārāminuprajāyīhītāṣṭātādyairmithunārcakaśca

vaiśākhaśukledivasekṣayākhyeyogeghalabhyeṣurameśayoge

japtvā ca hutvā gurupādayumisamarpyavittaṃ vahulaṃ mahīśa

tāṭaṃkavālīyugalādibhūṣaṇairabhyarcya viprālathabhojanīyāḥ

āyuṣyamārogyamathārthasiddhiṃ ratnānimaktāhayahastisaṃghān

mātuḥ piturvaśaśatānvitotedivyaṃ vimānaṃ maṇīkāṃ canāhyaṃ

bhṛgossutāpi dine dine yojasvāvahutvāvidhivanmanuṣyaḥ

punnaghapautrerassahitāṃ samṛddhiṃ prāpyātha sākṣīddhari hṛyameti

bhakṣyai rājyabhokṣyaiśca vastraiśakalyairvātauṣapitvā dvijāgryaḥ

vaṃśābhivṛddhiṃ samavāpya cāte viṣṇoḥ padaṃ tatpuramaṃ prayāti

kṛtārthāmanuje iṃ nityaṃ saṃprātyakāmā athayāṃti muktiṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīnyāsa chaṃda ārṣādikaṃvuva

ṣaṭmekuruṇāmūrtekṛtyayā dharmanaṃdana

chaṃda āṣādikaṃ sarvaṃ yadvātvā siddhimānbhavet

chaṃdogāyatramuddiṣṭaṃ vidrayā śrīḥsarasvatī

aklīvadīrghahrīsvādhavargauḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ

trinayanāṃ vāgdevatāmāśraye

na siddhilabhate maṃtrīpuraścaryā śatairapi

kṛtvā pūrvoditanyāsamārṣachaṃdādikaṃ tathā

bhagavaīmapi kāṃcanābhyādhyāyetanyā samārṣachaṃdādikaṃ tathā

bhagavatīmapikāṃcanābhyādhyāyeskarājjaghṛtapustakavarṇamālāṃ

brahmarūpī bhavatyeva satyānaṃdacidātmakaḥ

ṛṣiḥ prajāpatiḥ chaṃdogāyatradevatāhyāparā

pāraṃgato sau vidhānāṃ bhavatyeva jaḍopi ca

ṛṣi prajāpati chaṃdogāyatrasamudāṃhṛtaṃ

hrasvadīrghāṃtaragataiḥ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ praṇavai smṛtaḥ

sakalaśaśinibhāṃśāradānāṃ namāmi

rodhikā dīpikā caiva recikā mocikā parā

āpyāpinī vyāpinī ca vyomanī ca ka sāddhamā

sadāśivasamudbhataḥ ṣoḍaśoktā maho

sthitiḥ sīdviriti prīkādeśavahyakalā imāḥ

varadāhrādinī prītidīghoviṣṇukalādaśa

utkārī mṛtyurityuktādaśarudradakalā imāḥ

īśvarāṃga samudbhatāḥ kalāḥ paṃcāśadīritaḥ

ājñāsiddhiṃ samāsādyatejomayatanurbhavet

nyasecchīkaṃṭhapūrṇādi paṃcāśān mithunātmakaṃ

sa dhātuprāṇaśaktyātmayuktāyāhiṣite kramāt

prokto devatāṃ tannavedibhiḥ hasāṣaḍdīrghayuktena kuryādagānideśikaḥ

adyoḥśobhārabhūteśotithīśaḥ sthāṇanāṃharaḥ

akrāśca mahāsenaḥ śivaṣoḍaśaśamūrtaya

athaikarudrakṛrmokanejñādvacaturānanāḥ

arddhanārīśvāraścīmākāṃtaścāṣājaḍaṃḍinau

chagalaṃḍa**(?)dviraṃḍeśaumahīkālakapālinau

śvetabhṛnvīśalakūliśivassaṃvartakustataḥ

pūrṇodarīsthādvirajāśālmalītadanaṃtaraṃ

sadīrghamusvigomukhyaudīrghajihvā tathaiva ca

jvālāmukhī tatojñeyā paścādulkāmukhītathā

mahākālīsarasvatyau sarvasiddhisamanvitā

ātmaśaktibhūtamātā tahā laṃvodarīmatā

rūpiṇīvāriṇī paścātkyakodatanā

kālarātriśca kubjīsyāt kapardinyapi vajrayā

vāruṇīvāyavi proktā yaścāddrakṣopadhāriṇī

etārudrāṃ pīṭhasthāḥ siṃdūṇaruṇavigrahīḥ

śrīkaṃṭhamātṛkāmetāṃ vinyasyādau dine dine

rudramūrtissamahartā kartā sākṣānna saṃśayaḥ

kartavyassatataṃnyāso brahmatādātmyasiddhidāḥ

sa dhātu prāḍaṇaśaktyātmayuktāyādiṣuviṣṇavaḥ

nārāyaṇodvelakṣmīśodevatānnasamīritā

puṃḍarīkākṣalakṣmyoḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ tatvā sākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavet

bhavedbrahmamayaḥ sākṣādvaiṣṇavīnyasyamātṛkāṃ

ṛṣiḥśaktiḥ spṛtaḥ chaṃdīgāyatraṃ devatāvudhaiḥ

dīrghāddhayujāṃgāni kuryānmāyātmanāvudhaḥ

divādvojagatīśvarotrinayanāpadmeniṣastāsukhaṃ

āśrayassarvabhāgyānāṃ bhavatyatra na saptayaḥ

ṛṣirbhṛguḥ smṛtacchaṃdīgāyatraṃdevatāmatā

prāktrastunena vījena kuryādaṃgānisādhakaḥ

kucanatāṃ dhyāyejjagatsvāminīṃ

ācāryānugrahātsākṣādbhavecchrīkalayānvitaḥ

ṛṣiḥ saṃmohanaḥ proktaḥ chaṃdogāyatramucyate

smareṇadīrghayuktena vidadhyādaṃgakalpanāṃ

vaśīkarotinnailokyaṃ kiṃca brahmamayo bhavet

ṛṣissaṃmohanachaṃdogāyatraṃ chavatāmanoḥ

bhāvartitaistribhivījaiḥ ṣaḍaṃgāni prakalpayet

cūḍāṃ

sarvasaubhāgyasaṃyamabhassakalaiśvaryasaṃyutaḥ

haṃsavidyādikāṃ nyasenmātṛkāmakhileṣṭado

samastavastusaṃprāptikaraṃ tejobhayaśiva

khāhādyauḥ paṃcamunibhiḥ paṃcāgāni prakalpayet

vedādiśaktirajayāparamātmamahāmataḥ

samastaphalasiddhidyāhaṃ sādyomāttakāṃ nyaset parabrahmamayīmatha

aṇimādiguṇauśvaryasahito bhagavānasau

ācāryānugrahāllabdhā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarījayanaṃ

sarāvasarvajagatāṃ kartā hartā ca pālakaḥ

ekādaśavidhanyāseṣaśaktovasudhāpate

tatvanyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryādyena tatvamayaḥ pumān

ṛṣirbrahmātatacchaṃdogāyatraṃ daivataṃ smṛtaṃ

ṣaḍdīrghamāyayākuryāt ṣaḍaṃgaṃ praṇāvādyayā

hṛdabjasthitaṃ yuktaṃ citkalayāśrayehamaniśaṃ nārāyaṇākhyaṃ mahaḥ

vinyastha śrīmadaṣṭārṇa prajapanvudhaḥ

tanmūrtiyaḥ jaranyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryādinanyadhoḥ

chaṃdasmṛviturmaṃḍalasthitaḥ ādityātmājagatsyāmīdevonāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ

keyūravāmakarakuṃḍalavānkirīṭīharīhiraṇmayavupurvṛtaśaṃkhacakraḥ

bhavet sa bhagavānmūrtiḥ śabdabrahmamayaḥ pumān

ṛṣirasyāhamevāḥsmisādhyānārāyaṇonṛpa

harirnārāyaṇaḥ svāmīdevatānna samīritaḥ

suṃdāramacyutasuramuni brahmeśa śakrārcita

ācāryānugrahātpaścādavaśyaṃ harirūpabhāk

kuryādavaśyaṃ sakalanyāsa jātaphalaprahaṃ

chaṃdaścadevīgāyatrī paramāśriyaḥpatiḥ

sadbhirūdīritaḥ ṣadbhirvargauḥ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vā vinyasedvasudhāyate

sarvanyāseṣaṣṭeśaktopi svācāryānugrahānnṛpa

cidrūyamātṛkāṃ nyasya sākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavet

brahmatādātmyamanvicchanamuktimekena janmanā

saṃsevya vahuvarṣaṃvāratpraprāṇārdhanairapi

avaśyamapi gṛhīyāt samastanyāsagūrvikā

avaśyaṃ sakalanyāsā ācāryacaṃdanāṃvujāt

avaśyaṃmupadeṣṭavyāḥ karotu na karotu vā

ācāryonuprahāhātvānyāsajālamidaṃ śubhaṃ

japtvāmāsatrayaṃ yaścāsaśyatyeva śriyaḥpatiṃ

tejomayaturmṛtyujarāmuktetbhavatyasau

kṛtvā dine dine vaśyaṃ nyāajālamidaṃ śubhaṃ

vaṃdyamānī vratavratyena mṛtyuṃ māsānnasaṃśayaḥ

śubhe dine śubhelagne nyāsajālamidaṃ śubhraṃ

sasadvoyavatī bhūrmirdhanadhānyaudyasaṃkulā

varṣāśanamitadravyamathavāguraveryayet

kṛtvā vaśyamidaṃ nyāsajātaṃ pratyahamādarāt

bhārgavevāsarevāpi yakṣevāmāsimāsi vā

mahatiṃ siddhimicchadbhirnyastavyaṃ pratyahaṃ budhaiḥ

nyāsajālamidaṃ puṇyaṃ bhagavadrāvasiddhidaṃ

āśvinenavarātre ca vijīyedaśmimīdine

samastanyāsasahitā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubha

avaśyaṃ mahatīṃ siddhimaṇimādi guṇānvitāṃ

prāprotyatra na saṃdeho vivāhyemajayādikaṃ

tatvanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvānyāsaṃtanmūrmirtīyaṃvrara

śrīmadaṣṭāṃkṣaraṃ maṃtraṃ japennityaṃ mahīyate

varṇanyāsaṃ daśavidhaṃ nyasya cidrūyamātṛkaṃ

nyāsya avaśyaṃ nyastavyāssavaisandurvanugrahān

avaśyaṃ sakalanyāsā kartavyā hariśāsanāt

japaṃtīṣṭhāṣi sarvāsu bhārgavāsarepi vā

vyatīyāttepi vā kāryaṃ nyāsajālamidaṃ śubhaṃ

vaidikayāvakecu hṛtvā havirdādaśadhāpi bhūya

bhojyaṃ ghṛtasūyasaṃyutaṃ varṇāmuktāphalabhūṣaṇādikaṃ

tyadhvāvibhānaṃ maṇihemanirmitaṃ prāprotyathāṃte bhagavatpadaṃ ca

hutvā vidhivatsamarcyacāryavalyaimaṇikāṃcanādika

vaṃśāmivṛddhi samavāpya bhogā āproti sākṣādbhagavatyudaṃ ca

nyāsesmahāṣṭārṇajayī śivaścanyāsaissacūṣṭākṣaramaṃtrajāyī

nyāsaissahānārāyaṇassarva carācarasya gururgarīyānapi viśvavaṃdyaḥ

etadeva paraṃ tatvaṃ vijānīhamahīpate

puraścaryābhidhaṃ karmavakṣyamiva sudhāyate

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojaṃ dhyātvā brahmavileśubhe

dhyātvātha śrīmadaṣṭārṇā yathā śaktyānvahaṃ japet

japtvā hṛtvānyahaṃ bhūyatvalite jātavetavedasi

vatsarān mahatīṃ siddhiṃ devānāmapi durlabhāṃ

kaṃdarpa samasaubhāgyaṃ vācāṃ siddhimanuttamāṃ

mṛtānāmapi sāhasrakarasparśanamātrataḥ

aṇimādigarṇoryuktovatsarātsvecaro bhavet

uktayeta mahābhāga yadivighro na jāyate

dvāpareravilakṣaṃ syātkalau ṣoḍaśalakṣakaṃ

tathā sarasvatītīraṃ yamunātaṭamapyatha

samudratīraṃgoṣṭhaṃ ca suvarṇāmukharītitaṃ

sutedyāccāryasānnidhyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ prāhuriṣṭada

naktāśyekāṃtarāśī vā phalamūlāśanopi vā

tayet ṣoḍaśalakṣāṇi śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

hunet ṣoḍaśasāhasraṃ tata ācāryayādayoḥ

dvādaśakaṃ vatsaṃ vahukṣīrasamanvitaṃ

datvācāryapriyāputrānapi saṃtoṣyavastubhiḥ

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāmividhināvakṣudhāyate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī siddhāpado bhavati bhūyate

athastaprepivāsākṣāddhāṣvarudrādivivaṃditaṃ

nārāyaṇaṃ parabrahmarūpirṇayaśyati dhruvaṃ

bhavatyeva na saṃdehasrasminvarṣe mahīyate

japahomāccaṃ naṃdānaṃ vrataṃdeva dvijārcana

tasmādavaśya kartavyaṃ puraścaraṇamādarāt

mahatīpuraccaryā saṃpūrṇoktāmayāta

ānāyāsenavidhāyāḥ śirdviguptatarāmapi

vaiśākhemāsi śukevāt tīyākṣayasaṃjñitā

hohirāyāṃ saṃsthito jīvemeṣarāśigateravau

yogoyaṃṣaḍvibhirvaṣairllabhyate nānyathā nṛpa

pitṛṇāmapi sarveṣāmakṣanaṃdakārakaṃ

tatraiva bhṛguvāreṇaptahitedevaseśubhe

asminnevadine bhūyarohiṇyāṃ bhagavān vibhuḥ

kiṃ ca dharmasyatayasātoṣitovasudhāyate

prājāpatyarkṣasahite bhṛguvāsarasaṃyute

vaiśākheśuklapakṣehamutyanosmindine nṛpa

dharmasya tapasātuṣṭodākṣāyaṇyāmahaṃ nṛpa

asminnevadine pūrvarīhiṇyāṃ bhṛguvāsare

kiṃ cāsminnevadivaserohiṇayāṃśaśiśekharaḥ

tasmādakṣayanāmnīyaṃ tṛtīyā sarvasiddhidā

etai samanvito yogauralakṣyoyoga ītirataḥ

rohiṇī bhṛgucāreṇa sahitātra bhavedyadi

rīhiṇīsasthitijīvemesurāśigateravau

akṣayākhya tṛtīyaṃ yaṃmahatparvamamerirta

ṛṣayassiddhasaṃghāśca pratyahaṃ pūjayaṃtimāṃ

matokṣayalaṃ prāpya gacchaṃti tridivaṃsurāḥ

matmasādbhavatyeva diname tatrathāthākṣayaṃ

nārāyaṇāśramonāmnātanmanasthā na mucyate

dākṣāyaṇīsutaṃ nāṣāravyātaṃ nārāyaṇāṃ muniṃ

koṭijanmārjītaiḥpāyasaṃghairapi sudāruṇaiḥ

akṣayesmin dine tasmāduktayogobhavedya hi

alabhyayoge saṃprāptodivasekṣayasaṃjñite

naisakaṃ karmakṛtvātha tadanujñāpurassaraṃ

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ śrīmaṃtrānyatamaṃśataṃ

vivapatrairghṛtābhyaktaiḥ śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

śrīmaṃtrānyatamenāthahutvā dvādaśadhā ghṛtaṃ

utpalairvilvapatrairvāyairvākaravīrakaiḥ

sāṃgāvaraṇapūjāṃ ca kṛtvādau niyatavrataḥ

adhyetavyā raśmimālāśiṣyeṇaniyatātmanā

avaśyamasyindivase śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubhā

adhyetavyā prayatnena sarvanyāsapurassarā

tata ācāryapādābjedhairyagāṃbhāryasaṃyutaḥ

varṣāśanamitaṃ dravamathavāvaśyamaryarpat

ācāryakarṇayordeyamavaśyaṃ ratnasaṃyutaṃ

pītāṃvarayugaṃdādyātssakṣyakāryāsakaṃtu vā

avaśyaṃ guravedeyaṃyānasaṃ phalapaṃcakaṃ

dṛṣṭvātphulanicānyāni dhanārḍhyā bhūmiyo yadi

dhānyarāśimathāśvaṃcagatānāṃdolikāṃdikaṃ

tata dakṣayamevasyādyādyadasmindine kṛtaṃ

avaśyaṃ yānasaphalaṃ yaṃcakaṃtritaḥ prātayaṃtu vā

varṣośanamitadravyaṃdayamāvaśyakaṃ vudhaiḥ

daridrodhanahīnīpidakṣiṇāṃguraveryat

avaśyaṃguravederyadarivairapi sādhakaiḥ

akṣayāṃ putrapautrādisamṛddhijānamakṣayaṃ

ākalyamakṣayāsiddhiḥ śrīmadṛṣṭārṇājāpibhiḥ

ācāryāliṃganaṃ prāthatadāśībhiḥ kṛtādaraḥ

bhojayedvipravarṣoścasuvāsinyoṣṭabhūyate

pītvā kṛtārthatāṃ prāpyabhuṃjīyādvaṃdhabhissaha

vinūśāṭhyaṃ parityajya kṛtvokta vidhinā śuciḥ

tādṛśīnāśataṃ sāgraṃ kṛtvā yatphasamaśrute

śriyācidrayayāthāsthiyoge phalamudīritaṃ

sutena yukte viriṃcisthita devadevasadūṇai meṣasthiterkedivasetṛtīye

umāmaheśābhidhamāhareketatraivagaurocare mātrajātā

takṣīpatiḥ prāhurabhūddharitryāṃ nārāyaṇonāmanaraiṇayuktaḥ

hutvā ca havyaṃ vidhisaṃskṛtegrauyūvauyadiṣṭāmapiraśmimālāṃ

bhūyo gṛhītvā gurupādayugmedayugmedatvā samastaṃ ca dhanaṃ maṇiṃ ca

phalatrayaṃ pānasaṃpaṃcakaṃ vāhyatvā sadācārya yadāraviṃde

putrābhivṛddhisakalāṃścanogāyurmahadbhāgyamathākṣayaṃ ca

pituścamātuḥ kulakoṭiyuktāmamaitadakṣapyayaṃdaṃ prayāti

dinamātreṇa kṛtvetthaṃ mahatīṃ siddhimaśrute

anena yogarājena samolabhyonavihvate

puraścaryorataraṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣāṃ vahnio nṛpa

bhāskaragrahaṇeprāpte caṃdrasyadivasepi vā

grahaṇasparśa samaye gatvā sāgaṇāṃ nadīṃ

snātvā śūmedhāvabhṛthasnānapuṇyasamanvitaḥ

aṃtassamastayāyebhyo muktaḥ syāt sadya eva hi

kṛṣṇaistilairhunedravyaghṛtaṃ yuktaistataḥ paraṃ

śirṣṭerdhanāḍikāmātre smṛtvā brahmāryaṇāhutiṃ

vahavinnayuktāṃ bhūmiṃmāvāryāya samarpayet

atha dvādaśaniṣkaṃvāṣaḍviṃtā samarpayet

dakṣiṇāvasuniṣkaṃ vā triniṣkaṃvātha dakṣiṇāṃ

dadāti padyadgṛhaṇesavituḥ śaśinopi ca

grahaṇeva sudhāṃ datvā satyadvīpavatīṃ mahau

śālagrāmaśilāmekā datvā pūrvoktadakṣiṇāṃ

dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ ca vā sāṃsibhūṣaṇaṃ kuṃḍalādikaṃ

samastābhīṣṭasaṃsiddhimatyaṃtaṃ mahatī labhet

svarṇadānaṃ merusaṃyaṃ prāhuścaṃdrārkaparvaṇi

dattāstra yastena bhavaṃtilokāyaḥ kāṃcanagācagahīṃ pradṛdyāt

dadāti mahatīsiddhiṃ daivatairapi durlabhāṃ

sīdhyarthameva maṃtrāṇiṃ nityaṃ yatnaparo bhavet

tadā tasya prasastābhikalyāṇābhi bhavaṃti hi

vidyāḥ samastā api divyabhogāna prāprotyathāṃte bhagavatyahaṃ ca

siddhigatā brahmasaṃrūpatāṃ ca śriyaṃ parāṃ sarvagurṇāyayabhāṃ

divyāmaraṇānidhānyaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣāsau

pituśca mātuścakulānyito sau paraṃpadaṃ yātinaṃ saṃyotra

yacchatvā pitaro dhīmān na jñānatimitaṃ viśet

pūrvoktayāgasaditā sarvasiddhipradetā

atiguptataraṃ vakṣye sāvadhānamanāḥ śṛṇuḥ

paṃcavādyeṣu natvādaugaṇanāyakaṃ

alaṃkṛtyasacāryaṃ punarnatvārtha daṃḍavat

śrīmannāvārya sarvajñā yāvajjīva mahaṃtava

tvahājñoryūghanaṃ nāhakaromi karaṇanidhi

śaraṇaṃ mama sarvajña gatirnānyāstime sadā

pitāmātāpi bhayavatvameva mama daivataṃ

prārthayitvetthamācāryaṃ prārthayitvā mahīyate

ācāryavadanāṃbhotā hṛhṇīyādva sudhāyate

upadiṣṭāṃ ca bhūyopi svācāryavadanāṃvujāt

itthaṃ maṃtraṃmayīṃ dīkṣaṃ sarvanyāsamayīmapi

svatojyotirmayauvidyāṃgacchaṃtīṃ bhāvayeddharu

śarīramarthaṃ prāṇaṃśca svācārya caraṇadvaye

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃmihaṃ dīkṣīdvayaṃ śubhaṃ

avaśyaṃ bhagavāṃstasyatasminmāminyasīdati

gṛhītvācāryasadhye pratiṣṭhāpyātha yācakaṃ

homaṃ śatādhikaṃ kuryāddekasāhasramādarāt

maṃtrānyatamenāpi śatamaṣṭottaraṃ junet

jayādibhistatohutvātvanyāyaśrittāhatīrhunet

mallikākusumaivāpinaṃdyāvartairathāpi vā

sāṃgāvaraṇapūjāṃ ca kṛtvādau niyatavrataḥ

tata ācāryayādāvuddhitayaṃ praṇipatya ca

samarpya pūrvamācāryamalaṃkṛtya tataḥ pare

madhyaphaleraṃdhramekaṃ kṛtvārājatayātrakaṃ

sarvasvamapi ratnauṣaṃdhanaudyaṃmaṃdirasthitaṃ

phalasyo paritatyātraṃ raṃdhramadhyonidhāya ca

tvadraśividyāsaṃsidhyau maṃtrarājasya siddhaye

siddhirbhavatumesvābhinnācāryakaruṇānirdha

arpayet sakalavastu dhanadhānyauva saṃkulaṃ

sarvasvaṃ dakṣiṇādeyātadardhevāṃ tadardhakaṃ

athavā kuṃḍalayugmaṃ tatyakāṃcananimitaṃ

niṣkadvāsśakasvarṇaṃ sūkṣmavāso yugānvitaṃ

pradatvā mahātau siddhimakṣayāṃ prāpnuyādasau

ṣaṇinaṣktamathavādadyāttriniṣkaṃ vā prayatnataḥ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ datvā maheṃdrasadṛśaḥ śriyā

mātāmahāṃśca śataśa uddhatyāsau mahīyate

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyā varṣāśanamitaṃdhanaṃ

datvā yathpalamānyoti tatphalaṃ samudīritaṃ

phalapaṃcakasaṃyuktaṃ datvācārya padāṃvuja

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ haricadanamuttamaṃ

kalpadamagaṇākoṇe nivasedbhagavatpade

annaṃ dadhiyutaṃ vāpi datvā viprebhya ādarāt

prāpnotyatra na saṃdehassatyaṃ satyaṃ dāmyahaṃ

tādṛśīnāṃśataṃ sāgramakṣayāyāṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet

vājiyeyaśataṃsāgrasaṃpūrṇevaradakṣiṇāṃ

asminmājjanmadivasetarvukāmobhavārṇarva

manorathānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavet siddhirihākṣayā

śeṣeṇāpi punagītaṃ sarvamapyatra vakṣate

vaiśāhamāsedavasekṣayāhavyemaṃtreṇa nārāyaṇavācakena

saṃtaptahaimaṃ maṇirviduyuktamākalpayugmaṃ gurukarṇayuge

kiṃ cātra siddho ca parāmavāpyaprātyāḥ syurbhāgāna yadurlabhāṃśca

vaiśākhamāse civasekṣayesmin nārāyaṇoktavratamādareṇa

sākṣāhuruḍādhirūḍhaṃ dṛṣṭakattārthāsyuranaṃtabhogāt

ārūtvasākṣātkamalādhi vā sapadaṃ prayāṃtyaivanasaṃśayonna

aṣṭākṣaro homakalaṃ viśiṣṭa samastapurāyeṣu dinekṣayesmin

itthaṃ kṛteṣṭākṣara homakarmaṇyasya prasīdatyasvisāmareśaḥ

na rājasūyo na ca vājñimedhonagosavīvāpihinekṣayāstye

aṣṭākṣarī homarataṃ dinesmin vaiśākhasāsekṣayasaṃjñitetha

śakrādayodikayutayopi siddhāst sadhyāśca sarve samaruhaṇaśca

gaurīramāvāpnukhaśaktaścaścatuṣṭāḥ prasannāvaradāṃ bhavati

ssrapṛṣyaupicaṃdanaiśca-bhakṣyaiścabhojyairbhikṣunānyathāpi

sānyotyavaśyaṃ sakarlāśca kāmānnārāyaṇāpaśyati cakṣurṣeva

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāmitthaṃ sidhyarthamācaret

śriyaḥpateḥ prasādopi bhavatyevākṣayo nṛpa

bhagavatsannidhāvaṃtebhavejatirathīkṣayā

bhūyopi saṃyavakṣyāmi samāhitamanaḥ śṛṇu

atigaṃḍena yogena yadvā yuktetyu karmaṇa

saumyavāsarayukte vā dvādaśīdivasepi vā

yogarājomajānuktoraviparvaśataprabhaḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇoyegaste naiva sama ucyate

varṣairdvāśabhirbhūmau labhyateyaṃ mahīpate

japahomādikartṛṇaṃ dadātyakṣayamāśitaṃ

mahādāna sahasraṃ vājiyeyāyuṃtaṃ tu vā

alabhyo nāmayogoyaṃ sarvayogottamomaḥ

srātvā navāṃvidhānena devānṛṣiyohṛnapi

ācāryacaraṇaṃbhojayugmaṃ natvā tataḥ paraṃ

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ samasanyāsasaṃyutāṃ

sāmrājyalakṣmīvidyāṃ vā ṣaḍarṇīmathavāśriyaḥ

yathā rucijayedvidyāṃ komalaisulasīhalaiḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇi paścātriśataṃ brahmavidyayā

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahnau tilairgavyaghṛtaplutaiḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ datvā śrīmaṃtreṇājyamādarāt

prāyaścittāhutā hṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhuti

ācāryakarṇayordatvā kuṃḍaladvayamādarāt

sūkṣmaṃ vāsoyugaṃ datvā śālagrāmaśilāṃśubhāṃ

prārthayitvāpi vahuśastilānapi samarpayet

tadardhaṃ vā tadāvāryamaṃciresthāpayedvudhaḥ

pratigṛhyāṇamātraṃ vā pātakaṃ na labhet kvacit

ācāryācaraṇāṃbhojadeyamāvaśyakaṃtitannivṛttirathocyate

śrītriyāyatathānyasmaidatvā mucyetapātakān

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojedeyamāvaśyakaṃ dhanaṃ

dhenupratinidhidravyaṃ yadvādadyāt sakṣiṇaṃ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇenāmnāyogesmin sarvavīmidvide

brāhmaṇānbhojayetpaścādbhaujayecca suvāsinīḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇoyogeśrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsiddhimāvaśyaṃ mahītīmapi

prāpnotyatra na saṃdehassatyaṃnītra vicāraṇa

tādagdvādaśākaṃtenakṛtaṃ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ

ekāmāpyāhaṛtirkṛtvā lakṣmīhomaphalaṃ spṛtaṃ

ekāṃ sa dakṣiṇā datvā koṭi brahmāṃḍadānataḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇe yogeniṣkaṃvākāṃcanaṃ śubhaṃ

ācāryakarṇayugale kuṃḍaladvayadāmana

lakṣmīnārāyaṇe yo sahasrajayamātrataḥ

yatphalaṃ sa bhavāpnoti tadeva phalamīritaṃ

kṛtvetthaṃ nāmnasaṃdehobhagavaṃtaṃ praśasyati

statyeyaśyatyasau sarvakāmadaṃ bhaktavatsalaṃ

viyuktaḥ pāparīśibhyaḥ kalyāṇāni dine dine

vaṃśābhivṛddhimatulāṃ putrapautrādisaṃkulāṃ

divyaṃ vimānamāruhyakulaviṃśatiṃ saṃyutaḥ

na divasenoktaṃ punaranyatpravakṣyate

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāmi śūṇuṣva vasudhāpate

tatroktā śrīmadaṣṭārṇa puraścaryāmayātava

śrāvaṇemāsipaurṇabhyāṃ caṃdrasyagrahaṇaṃ yadi

vasunakṣatrayuktaṃ vāyogolabhyo mahānayaṃ

sarvāsāmanividyānāmavaśyaṃ siddhiruttamā

śrīmadaṣṭarṇavidhāyāssiddhiktuṃ na śakyate

prātastasmindine srātvā svācāryacaraṇadvayaṃ

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ prajapetriyatavrātaḥ

paṃcadhā bhagavadraśmividyāsūktaṃ tato japet

keśavādīni nāmānimaunenamanasoccaran

vāsasīparīdhāyāthatrirācamyayatavrataḥ

pītvā vimuktaḥ payaudyairvahiraṃtasthitairapi

hanedravyājasaṃmiśraistilaikṛṣṇaiḥstataḥ paraṃ

hunityaṃ paṃcaśataṃ vāpi catuḥśatamathāpi vā

ekadhāvādvidhā predhāraśmimaṃtrairhunaittṛtaḥ

pradakṣiṇyaṃ namaskṛtya jātavedasamādarāt

sarvasvamaryadvīmān yadvāvarṣāśanaṃ dhanaṃ

vaṇiṣkaṃ cātriniṣkaṃvātanyūnaṃ na samarpya yat

sālagrāmaśilāmekāmavaśyaṃ guraveryapet

vibhave sati bhūdānaṃ godvayaṃ ca samarpayet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāsiddhiṃ sumahatīmapi

caṃdracūḍāmaṇirthyojastriṃśadvatsaratī bhavet

koṭiśastilahomasya phalamānyosanuttamaṃ

tulāpuruṣakoṭīnāmavaśyaṃ labhate phalaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāpuraścaryāśatātphala

tasminnevadirnasvaye yadvā sākṣācchriyaḥ patiṃ

dṛṣṭvā samastabhāgyānāmāśrīyosaubhaveddhruvaṃ

cirāyuṣyamathārogyaṃ vācāṃ siddhiṃ ca durlabhāṃ

saṃprāpyasakalānbhogāndivyānapi ca mānuṣān

vimānavaramārūṭholabhate bhagavasada

navarātramiti protaṃ navanavapyaṃtaṃ mahīyate

divase divaseja saṃhutaṃtta ca bhūyate

yā mahānavamī proktā navārātre tathāśvirna

sakṛjjasaṃhatamapi tatrānaṃta phalapradaṃ

maredyurvaiṣṇavaṃ tāraṃ yadi sā navamī parā

varṣamadhyasthitamidaṃ yarvaratnaṃ mahīpitaṃ

navarātre pratiyadi svācāryājñāpurassara

saṃpūjyādaugaṇādhīśamadhyarcyācāryamādarāt

svācāryacaṇāṃbhojasannidhauśaṃsitavrataḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃvidyāṃ sahasraṃ ca catuḥśataṃ

sāmrājyalakṣmīmaṃtraṃṣāśrīṣaḍarṇamathapi vā

catuḥśatādhikaṃ yadvā sahasraṃ pratvatuḥhaṃ japet

śrīmrājyalakṣmīmaṃtraṃ vā śrīṣaḍarṇamathāpi vā

navarātravṛte yadvākaumāistulasīdalaiḥ

śrīśrīpatīsamabhyarcyastāṃgācaraṇapūrvakaṃ

ātharvaṇa mahāsūktaṃ lakṣmīdudayanāmakaṃ

paṃcādhikāṃ vā catvāriṃśavṛtiṃjapennu vā

prāṇānāyamya sakalpyajvalite jātavedasi

śrīmadaṣṭākṣariṇātha sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

madhutritayayuktena dvādaśārṇa śriyā hunet

atha sāmrājya lakṣmyāvāśātādhika sahasrakaṃ

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtraiśca ślokarūpairataḥ paraṃ

jāyādi homaṃ kṛtvātha prāyaścittāhunīrhunet

śrīśrīyātī samabhyarcya pūrvavadbrahmaviyā

namaskṛtyārpyayedvomānakuṃḍaladvitayaṃ śubhaṃ

vāsobhirgaṃdha puṣpādyairvahūśasyaugha saṃkulaṃ

bhagavatmannidhau datvādācāryāyamahātmane

niṣkadvādaśakaṃ madhyadakṣiṇā ca trivinirmitā

dṛdyādavaśyamācāyekarābjeḥ dakṣiṇānvitāṃ

ekāṃ sopaskarāṃ śayyāmapi dakṣiṇaṃ yānvitāṃ

caturvṛṣabhasaṃyuktādātavyā satataṃ mahī

paṃcaviṃśatinirṣṇuvā tadardhaṃ vātvardhakaṃ

ekaniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā vittalobhaṃ vivarjayet

niṣkārvamathavādeyaṃ dhenumūlyaṃ mahīyate

śrīmadaṣṭārṇe sidhyarthaṃ kāryamāvaśyakaṃvudhīḥ

hutvaikāmāhutimapi koṭihomaphalaṃ labhet

mahānavamyāṃ datvāsau satyadvīyapradānataḥ

mahāvamyā prātyāyāṃ kuṃḍalakṣitaṃyaṃ śubhaṃ

kiṃ vāsyapitaromagrānarakaudyeśu koṭiśaḥ

mahānavamyāṃ bhagavatsālagrāmāśelīśubhāṃ

phalamāsādyaśuddhātmā bhagavaṃtaṃ prapaśyati

tanmālya svalpasvarṇamathavālakṣagodānajaṃ phalaṃ

prāpnoti bhagavaddhāmaśāśvataṃ divyamakṣayaṃ

tulāpuruṣamukhyāni hānānivasudhāpate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā puraścayo sahasrajaṃ

prāpnoti mahatīṃ lakṣmīṃ dhanratnaudyasaṃkulā

vidhā siddhiṃ ca mahatī nigrahonugrahakṣīmāṃ

avāpyaiccharīrāṃte prāpnoti hārisatridhiṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī siddhiyādaśī navarātrake

navarātravrate yāvat prasāhaḥsyācchriyaḥpateh

navarātravrataṃsāṃgaṃ kṛtvethāṃ vidhinādarāt

avātha brahmarudrādyauḥ śakrādyairapi pūjitaḥ

prāpnoti bhagavadvāmakulānāṃśatasaṃyutaḥ

ācāryatoṣaṇātuṣṭābrahmaviṣṇumaheśvārā

śakrāyā āpi dikpālāḥ keṃkaryaprāpnuvaṃtihi

navarātravratamidaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ samācaret

navarātra pratakuryācchriyaskāmo dvijottamaḥ

ariṣṭāṃ jāyate tasya tasminvarvenāsaṃśayaḥ

navarātravrataṃ kuryācchromadaṣṭārṇe siddhaye

udīritā mahāsiddhirbrahmavidbhisamoṣṭatāḥ

navarātravratamihaṃ samastādhaudhabhaṃjanaṃ

ācāryānujñāyā prātaḥsnātvā pratipadāditaḥ

saṃkalpārkāsahasrīṇi prajayennavarātrake

prajapedaṣṭasāhasraṃ paṃcasāhasrakaṃ tu vā

śrīrāmajanmanavamīdivase sarvasiddhide

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahāvikṣukhaṃḍairghṛtatthatai

juhuyādvitvāpatrairvāgavyājyatribhadhuplutai

athavā śuddhagavyenadyānena juhuyāśuciḥ

puratorato juhuyānmaṃtrairṣṭahadāvaraṇoditaiḥ

jayāhi homaṃ kṛtvātha kṛtvā brahmarpaṇāhatiṃ

ācāryācaraṇāṃbhojadvayasāyyaṃgamādarāt

sālagrāmaśilāṃ śuddhānikṣityāṃkāpyapātrake

niṣṇerdvādaśabhiḥ svarṇairvasuniṣkairathāpi vā

prīṇā tu bhagavānsākṣācchīrāmātmāśriyaḥpatiḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāsaṃsiddhimahatīmapi

iti tradvayenātha sālagrāmaśilāśubhāṃ

sūkṣmavāsodyugaṃ datvā haricanamuttamaṃ

athācāyalatrasyakarṇe laṃkaraṇādikaṃ

navabhojyāḥ savāsinyovāsobhirdikṣiṇādibhiḥ

vāsaṃte navarātrepivrataṃ kṛtvetthamādarāt

śrīrāmajanmadivase śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyā

śrīrāmajanmadivisesālagrāmaśilāṃśubhāṃ

brahmāṇḍakoṭidānasya phalamatyaṃtamaśrutaṃ

puraścaryāśataṃphalaṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

āvaśyaṃ mahatīṃsiddhimihaṃ ca prāpyasādhakaḥ

saṃprāpya mahatīṃ lakṣmīvākyadviṃ vā tulāṃ śubhāṃ

saṃprathakulasāhasrasrasahito bhagavatpade

athāparaṃ pravakṣyāmi viśiṣṭaṃ parvasiddhidaṃ

amārkavāsarayutāsomenesahitāpi vā

ravicūḍāmaṇirvāmnāyogoyaṃ samudāhṛtaḥ

atrajasasaṃkṛdvāpi koṭiśojaptamevahi

atrekaniṣkasvarṇe vā datvācārya yadāṃvuja

raviparvaṇi saṃprāpte tatpūrvadivaṃ satrayaṃ

bhāskaragrahaṇesparśakāle prāte tataḥ paraṃ

stutvāmasastupāyaudyairvimuktassadyarāvahi

ācāsannidhāvagniṃ pratiṣṭhāpyavidhānataḥ

maṃtrairātyenagavyena tatastrimadhusatyutīḥ

kṛtvā śrīmanunāyaścīcchātamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet

prāyaścittahutīrhutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

datvā ca strīsūkṣmīsisvarṇa niṣkasahasrakaṃ

yāvacchakāmalaṃkāraṃ datvā śrīguravetha vā

paṃcaniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā niṣkatrayamitaṃ tu vā

śrotriyebhyaḥ pradedyānidaśadānānyataḥparaṃ

abhyarcayettriniṣkādiyāvacchavacamitaurdhanaiḥ

itthaṃ kṛtvā vidhānenena grahaṇe sāṃgamādarāt

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāstatopi mahatī parāṃ

āyurārogyasahitaḥ śriyādhanadasannibhaḥ

prāpyavaṃśābhivṛddhiṃ ca kalpātaṃ vasudhāpate

kulasāhasramādāyavarīdviṣṇuyurrasukhī

ramyamokṣāṃte śuddhārāvahitātayaḥ

ekena divase naiva samāhitamānāḥścarā

pūrvoktā yogasahitā daivatairapidullebhā

bhūyopi saṃvaḥ pravakṣyāmi viśeṣādvasadhāyate

yatrāvirāsotkāmalābhṛgoḥkhyātyāṃtrayīmayī

yatroyamebhagavāncyutaḥ priyamīśvarīṃ

tatrākṣara tṛtīyāyāśrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī siddhiravaśyaṃ bhavati dhruvaṃ

amāvāsyāpūrṇimā ca tadvādaśa samāsmṛtā

savivāre saṃkramaṇaṃ tadvādaśasaṃmasmataṃ

caṃdracūḍāmaṇisamoyegaśrīddhaudayaḥsmtaḥ

vaiśākhemāsiyāścaktratatīyākṣayasaṃjñit

akṣayāṃyātatṛtīyāyāṃ muktaṃ yogatrayaṃ śubhaṃ

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyā muktoyogobhavedyadi

vidhānāmupadiṣṭānāmapitasmin dineśūse

avaśyaṃ mahanīsidvissadyarānākṣayā bhavet

napusakāścayurmatrāḥ strīmaṃtrāvaidiṇastathā

niṣiddhamāsatāreṣu labdhāśca śubhasiddhidāḥ

puraścaraṇanīnāścanyāsahīmāśca saṃtataṃ

prāptā avaśyaṃbhūyopi sacchiṣyairdvityavekṣaye

tatra prātaḥ samuhyāyanatvācārya padāṃvujaṃ

avaśyaṃ bhagavadraśmimālikaṃ paṃcadyāsmaret

triśataṃ vātayecchadvānānprathāsiddhibhāgasau

prārthadvaṃśaḥ sākṣādīvāṃ bhagavanmayaṃ

bhavatpādāvrayugmasya kiṃkarīssidinedine

karomitavapādātvakaikarpaśāśdhimāṃ vibho

avaśyaṃ tavayāddātva kiṃkarosmidyaśādhimāṃ

bhūyaḥ samastavidyānāṃ grahaṇaya samāhitaḥ

*** ācāryopi kṛpāsidhurvaidikaipi vā

bhagavadraśmividyābhireke kāmāhuti hunet

hyavāmabhāge saṃsthāpya sacchiṣyaṃ karuṇānidhiḥ

dattātreyaṃ mahātmānaṃ kṛṣṇadvaiyāyanaṃ muniṃ

śiṣyasyadakṣiṇe karṇe prahṛdyādvasudhāpate

śiṣyasya dakṣiṇekarṇo pradadyāhraśmimālikāṃ

upadiṣṭāṃśca bhūyopinyānuyaddiśedvadhaḥ

rājñī samastavidyānāśrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubhāṃ

kṛtārthosmītimatvāsāvartavāsīmahāmatiṃ

vidhānāṃ grahaṇārthāya sarvasayassamṛddhaye

yasminmāsetasamapidattaculukamātrakaṃ

upānehau ca chatraṃ ca madhudadhyohanādikaṃ

yasmanāsīyaurṇamālīviśārayāsahitāśubhā

yogā bhavaṃti yadyanna mayā pūrvamudīritāḥ

rājarājamimaṃ prāhulakṣmī nārāyaṇābhidhaṃ

āvaśyaṃ sarvavidyānāmacāryavadanāṃvujāt

itthaṃ samastavidyānāmācāryavadanāṃvujāt

ācāryanujñayā tatra vidhivat saṃskṛtenale

kṛtvāpya śrimadīṣṭārṇa manunāsādhakottamaḥ

śatādhikaṃhānecchīmaddaṣṭāryo na sahasrakaṃ

bhagavadraśmividyābhirhuneddavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ tridhā

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtraṇaśatādhikasahasrakaṃ

bhagavadraśmi vidyābhistridhā hutvājyamādarāt

pradakṣiṇaṃ namaskṛsajātavedasamādarāt

mallikākusumairvāpikarevīrairathapi vā

ārcadvilvapatre vā tulasīdalamiśritaiḥ

phalapaṃcakamādāyānasaṃsamanoharaṃ

nikṣipya bhūtale śūdve yatkiṃ vitpātramadhyataḥ

nikṣipya bhagavanmūrtisālagrāmaśilāṃśubhāṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ niṣkasuvarṇairbrahmavidyayā

brahmavidyāṃ samuccāryalakṣmīnārāyaṇau yajejet

dadhyodanaṃ tato dadyācchīśrīśrībhyāṃ mahīyate

ācāryaṃ samalaṃkṛtyaṃ vastrālaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

sūkṣmavāsopugaṃdatvā sālagrāmaśilāṃ śubhāṃ

samāhitamanādayācchīmadaṣṭārṇasiddhaye

phalasyāsyapradīnena mamasaṃtumanorathāḥ

śaktādyā api dikpālānivaṃsaṃtyamarādhiyaḥ

nivasaṃtisadīsarvasiddheyopi jagattrayaṃ

sarvadevamayīṃdīsye prītomestaśriyaḥpatiḥ

dadyādādhāryahastānnaṃ śrīśrīśaprītikāriṇīṃ

sālagrāmaśilāṃ śuddhasāṃpūjya kusumādibhiḥ

tatsannidhāvakṣācāryakarṇayoḥ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

sālagrāmaśilāṃ śraddhāṃ pūtitāṃ yātra saṃyutāṃ

sarvasvaṃ dakṣiṇaprokā varṣāśanadhanaṃ tu vā

dakṣiṇāprokta varṣāśanadhanaṃ nu vā tannyanaṃ nahi bhūyate

samarpya śrīmadaṣṭārṇasaṃsiddhiprāpnuyātparāṃ

vastrairābharaṇairdivyairbhojanādyairathādarāt

paṃcaviṃśaṃtisaravyākaviprebhyodadhisaṃyutaṃ

tataḥ ṣaḍgasasūyājyabhakṣyabhojyaiḥ suvāsinīḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja prakṣyātitajala.,m śubhaṃ

upānahau ca chatrāṇivyaṃ tanānyapi bhūyate

ācāryāliṃganaṃ prāpya tadāśīrbhiḥ kṛtādaraḥ

koṭī sahasrasaṃkhyākasvarṇadakṣiṇayānvitaṃ

itthametuddhataṃ sāṃgaṃ kṛtvā tatphalamaśrute

tādaśīnāṃ śataṃ sāmaṃ kṛtaṃ tena na saṃśayaḥ

nivatātanayārūḍhe brahmarudrādibhirvṛrta

ikṣā tadāditomāsimāsi svapne śriyaḥpatiṃ

sākṣātsvamepi vā dṛṣṭe kamalādhipatauhaṇai

cirādyuṣyamanārogyaṃ bhāgyaṃ māhadacaṃcalaṃ

sarvavidyādhiyatyaṃ ca jātismaraṇanuttamaṃ

parakāyaṃ praveśādi sāmarthyaṃ vākṣayaṃ nṛpa

trailokyanāyakaḥ svāmī bhagavāniṃdirāpatiḥ

yāryatīnaṃdi caṃḍīśagaṇeśebhyo mahātmanā

sa śobhane vā bhṛguvārayukte viriṃcitakṣannayute tathaiva

yogaṃ mahārājamudāharaṃti lakṣmīpatiyogamathāhuranye

ālabhyarāṣaudhuhavaṣalabhyogādhirājobhūvidyārthiveda

prāptāstaddaivākṣayasiddhidāḥ syuratrāpyamutrāpisadādvijānīṃ

svedyā gṛhīturdvivasekṣayesmin homādikartaścavaṇan dadāti

saṃprāptavidyā āpi sarvasiddhisamanvitāḥ syurnahiṃsaṃśavotra

hutvātha japtvāya gurormukhāhādvadhāpi sākṣādbhāvatameti

uvātha siddhimahatīṃ pitṛṇāṃ śatānvito divyapadaṃ prayāti

avaśyamācāryamukhāraviṃdādṛṣṭākṣaraṃnyāsasametamādau

ihāpi sīddhīṃ mahatīmavāpyasidyaṣṭakaṃ cāpuraṇegatāṃ ca

vimānasāruhyaramādhivāṃ saprayāti vaikuṃṭhapadaṃ mahīśa

anenalakṣmīśamasūtratvāsakṛdvāpi jagatpatiḥ syāt

samastadevairapi pūjyamānāprayāṃti sarveśitasiddhimagryāṃ

jānī hi māmeva puruṃguruṇāmācāryamīśaṃ jagadevanātha

mayāyirakteṇa viriciṃ mukhyairniścityaniścitya ca bhūdhabhūya uktaṃ

nānena yogena samosti yogaḥ satyapunassatyamidaṃ mahīśa

viśeṣatoṣṭākṣarasiddhidoyamācāryasaṃtoṣaṇatonṛpāla

sahasramekaṃ triśataṃ śataṃ vā sadhomahāsiddhisamauprayāti

vailvaiścapatraistrimadhutprataṃ ca svācārya pāhāsrayugethabhūya

datvāmra saṃbhūta bhavāni sadyoramāramādhīśa mahāprasādāt

lakṣmīkalāpūrṇaśarīradhārīkaṃdarpasāhasrasaṃmānatejāḥ

prayāti lakṣmīpatidhāmadivyaṃ sa prīprāpyadīrghāpurarogatāva

niṇītamatyakṛṣṇarudramukhyaiḥ surottamaiḥ

hutvā japtvāpi vā vidyāṃmāryavātha tatsṛt

saṃsevyavatsarayugaṃ pādaṃ saṃvāhanādibhiḥ

vidyāsiddhimavāpyāsaubhagavaṃtaṃ prapaśyati

dīkṣiṇāmūrtināpyetahītaṃ bhatyebhya ādarāt

akṣyayesmin dine bhūyakuruvratamanuttamaṃ

bhaviṣyati na saṃdehau mākusadyātrasaṃśayaṃ

gāṇayatyamahāmaṃtrīramāyāmanavopi ca

kṣetrapālakamaṃtrāścacādīmīnavopi ca

sadyaḥ siddhābhavaṃteva saṃśayonātravidyate

akṣayābhīṣṭasaṃsiddhiṃ prāptot yatra na saṃśayaḥ

ekonatriṃśa uto yamadhyāyokhilakāmadhuk

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāmi samāhitamanāḥśūṇa

sārdhatrioṭitīrthānāṃ sa tridhyaṃ gautamītare

mahāpuṣkarayogetradevatāmunibhissaha

samastadevasānnidhyaṃ samastamunisamidhiḥ

āāvaṃte dvāśāhaṃ puṣkareṇa samāgamaḥ

ekaikaṃ dinamapyatraraviyarvaśataprabhaṃ

stātvā japāṃdikaṃ kṛtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ

sarvāsāmapi vidhānāmaṃtrahomajapādibhiḥ

māhāpuṣkarayoyetra snātvā prātaransanyadhīḥ

uditetha sahasrāśau nityahomaṃ samāpya ca

abhyarcyagotamīdevīmurācāryamapi prabhuṃ

ācāryānujñayā śrīmadaṣṭārṇanyāsaṃ saṃyutaṃ

dine dine japet darvalorrāgayussastūnamaskṛtyapi gautamī

prajayehnautamītīrisvācāryānajñayānvahaṃ

triśataṃ juhuyādāṃtyamadhutritayamiśritaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇa śrīmaṃtreṇaśataṃ hunet

ayodhyādhuracakrethalakṣmīnārāyaṇābhidhe

śālagrāmāśitācakre vṛccakretha vā yajet

kusumairatteyenni saṃvilvapatravimiśritaiḥ

arcayettriśataṃ nityamupacārān samaryat

sa vīyacārairabhyarcya śataniṣkaṃdinī naṃdane

ācāryapādukāyugmetiṣkamekamathāpi vā

ācāryapādukābhāvepādakāmaṃtramādarāt

ekaikasmin dine dadyādvanaṃ lobhavivarjitaḥ

avaśyaṃ tatkalaṃ yatatyautrāyāpi vāryayet

avaśyamantraviddhīmānsarvasaṃyatsamṛddhaye

kṛtvā pūrvoditaṃ homajapādikamanukramāt

kāpilājyaṃ hutennedhākevalagavyameva vā

tataḥ śrīgurūsarūmabhyarcya svācāryaṃ harirūpiṇaṃ

sālagrāmaśilāṃ śuddhāṃvat kīṭavinirmitāṃ

abhāvevahulavyaratnīdīnāṃ mahīyate

ṣaṇinaṣkamathavādadyāttriniṣkavātha dakṣiṇaṃ

ācāryāprapradātavyamekācāpiyayasvinī

ekāsuvāsinī vāpi śrīvadhyānityamarcayet

mahāpuskarayogitrasaṃbhojyaḥ paścimedine

anvahaṃ prapiveddhīmān brahmadarśanasiddhayaṃ

mahāyātakasaṃghaiśca koṭijanmakṛtairapi

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsiddhirdaivatairapi durlabhāṃ

yatpuraścaraṇaṃ sāṃga mahatprāktaṃ mayā purā

śrīmadaṣṭākṣrī siddhirdevatairapidurlabhā

mahāpuṣkarayogesminautamītīravāsibhi

prayāṃtodripuraraṇyaṃ yāvaccaṃdradivākarā

mahāpuṣkarayogete satyalokevasaṃtiti

caturṣṭaṣabhasaṃyuktāvaktuṃ śakyaṃ na tatphalaṃ

mahāpuṣkarayorgatravahmātādātmyesiddhida

sūkṣmanastrapradānaṃ vaca bhagavadvaṣṭisiddhidaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇasaṃsiddhipradasadyomahīpate

yathotavidhināvarṣārjjātismāṇasiddhidaṃ

bhūmiyāledhanāḍhye vāṇarūpaṃ vā nanaṃsthitaḥ

śvetāśvadānaṃ kurvīta hasnidā na mahatphalaṃ

vahuvastrapradānaṃ ca dhānyadānaṃ dine dine

nadārāpūrvakaṃ hānamācāryebhyovidhīyate

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhojedasāddevya mataḥparaṃ

pāritoṣikamevāsmai svācāryāyāryayeddhanaṃ

dātavyaṃ sakalaṃ dānaṃ nānyathā siddhibhaścyute

dadhikṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ avyaguḍaṃ madhu ca śarkarā

samastasaṃpatsāgrājyasidvayuktoyamutibhāk

mahāpuṣkarayogetra kṛtvā siddhiṃ vrajet parāṃ

paśyatyeva svacakṣurbhyā sākṣādevaśrivaśriyaḥ pati

svacchaparaṇe bhūtvānidhissakalasaṃpadāṃ

mānuṣānapi divyāṃśca bhuktvā bhogānmanoramān

puṣpemāddivase viṣṇunakṣatrerkasamanvite

bhānūdaye savijñeyoyogoddhodvayānāmakaḥ

somavāsāsarayuktaścenmahodaya udīrataḥ

bhāskaṃrodayavelāyāṃ tadāstātvā mahodadhau

lakṣmīpatisaṃmārādhyasetkāskṛte jātavedasi

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇatilairgavyaghṛtasutaiḥ

śrīmaṃtretraṇahunedājyaṃ gavyaṃ triśatamādarāt

jāyāhi homaṃ kṛtvāthā prāyaścittāhutīstataḥ

śrīśrīyatyarcayetyaścī triśataṃ brahmavidyayāṃ

namaskṛtya samabhyarcye vāso laṃkaraṇadibhiḥ

ācāryāmahāṃdadyādvahṛdaśiṇayāvvitāṃ

bhūdānedakṣiṇāṃdadyāṃtta yathā vanmahīpate

ācāryakarṇayodvedhānmuktā saṃkaraṇaṃ śubhaṃ

avaśyaṃgodvayaṃ dadyātyasatsaṃdakṣiṇānvitaṃ

brāhmaṇānbhojayetpraścādbhojayecca suvāsinī

samarpayetkāṃsyapātrabhojanārtha sadakṣiṇaṃ

samarpayedathācā itthamardhodaye kṛtvā kṛtvāpi ca mahīdaye

tadārabhyahariṃmāsisvame prapaśyati

prāpnotyavaśyaṃ bhūpālasaṃśayo nātra vidyate

datvā yatphalamāpnoti tatphalaṃ niṣkamātrakaṃ

tatīdhikaphalaṃ sāṃgaṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

satataṃ varadāstasya bhavatpatra na saṃśayaḥ

caturdaśīvatipātasahitāsvātisaṃghutā

caturviśatibhirvarṣaiyogosaulabhyate bhuvi

vāsasīyaridhāyātha pitṛnsaṃtarpya yatnataḥ

ācāyo sānnidhautasya putraṃ patnīmathāpi vā

lakṣmīvidyāsusarvāsu vidyāmīṣṭatamāṃtataḥ

saṃskṛtya jagātānāthaṃ tatrābhyarcyoyacārakaiḥ

madhvatritayasaṃmiśraṃ gavyājya srutamādarāt

bhagavadraśmividyāmiṣṭatamekaikaśo hunet

namaskṛtyāpimācārya pādābja daṃḍavannabhet

paṃcaviśasalaṃ vapi viṃśatyalamitaṃ tu vā

catvāriṃśatpalamittaṃ viṃśatyatamitaṃ tu vā

sthūlaṃ sumadhuraṃ cataphalarāśiṃ samarpya ca

maṃgalavārayuktāsvātīvyatīpānayutāyadisyāllakṣmīnṛsohoyamalabhyayogaḥ

samarpyamātotthaphalāni vāso yugyaṃ ca gaṃdhaguḍanīrapātra

ahaṃprasannocchasamarthasiddhiṃdadīmilakṣbhāvalāṃ ca vidyāṃ

bhogānatha divyamānuṣānprayāti vaikuṃṭhapadamamāṃte

bhūtvā yatnaparonityamajjeyeddakṣiṇādhanaṃ

agnichomādayoyāgājapahomādayo ca

mahīyate saphalādakṣiṇāyuktā aphalādakṣiṇāṃ vinā

tasmādvarmaratonityamarjadyedvanasaṃdharya

kṛtvā kāryaśataṃ vāpi yogoyā dine dine

māna ārcayapatnīputrādyāḥ poṣaṇīyāne dine

ācāryasyājñayānitye śreyaskāmīvasennapa

svarvasvadakṣiṇaṃ dadyādvarṣāśanamitaṃ tu vā

ravaudugrahaṇaṃ varṣe varṣepi satataṃ bhavet

tatrāvaśyaṃ puraścaryā kṛtvā sūryasya parvaṇi

ukteṣvalabhyayogeṣu saṃprāptyeṣu mahīyate

vittaśāṭhyaṃ parityajya dakṣiṇāmadhamānuvā

kṛtvā vaśyaṃ puraścaryaṃ yoge labhye mahīyate

bhagavatkalayāyuktassadyarāvamavatyasau

sāṃgadakṣiṇayāyuktā navarātravratekṛtāḥ

sahasrakoṭisaṃkhyākasvarṇadakṣiṇayāyutaṃ

puraścaraṇakṛtyenateneṣṭaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ

gulaṃ samastavedeṣu śāstreṣvapi sugopitaṃ

vaiśākhe śrāvaṇe vāpi prathame bhṛguvāsare

varṣadvādaśakaṃ vā viprāṇāṃtaṃ vā mahīpate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarījasvāyāthāśaktyā sasāhitaḥ

triśataṃ juhuyādravyenājyenaniyatavrataḥ

bhagavadraśmividyāmirekaikāmāhutidṛt

johvaprāvālairathavāśrīśrīśabrahmavidyayāa

āvaśyabhojayedbhakṣyabhojyairājāyevallabhāṃ

bhārgave vāsarevaśyaṃ niṣkeniplārdhamevā vā

satataṃ bhārgavevāridadyāditthaṃ dhanādhikaṃ

ācāryapatnī saṃbhojyāmāsimāsyathavādine

niṣkādyaṃ vāyaṃ yeddhīmānbhakṣyādyairapitauṣayet

gurupatnyā āsābhidhye lakṣmīlakṣmīśa sannidhau

ādimebhārgavevāre gurupādāvatorpayet

prathamebhārgavevāre māsmisītthamācaret

ekaṃ vāmīthunaṃdadyathāśaktyā ca dakṣiṇaṃ

icchanvratamidaṃ kuryādavaśyaṃ siddhimaśnute

bhārgavavāsare prāpte prārthayitvā guruṃ vibhuṃ

dviguṇāddakṣiṇādeyā tadā cāyoyadhīmatā

kṛtvāvaśyaṃ mahāsiddhiṃ vrajatyatra nasyaṃśayaḥ

ācāryānugrahāllakṣmīdhanadhānyādi saṃkulāṃ

āyuṣyaṃ mahadārogyaṃ śriyamṛṣaguṇā api

na viyukto bhavetyalyāpatrairvāyaśrubhirdhanaiḥ

icchatā mahatīṃ sisiddhiṃ śroyaścāpyakṣayaṃ paraṃ

etaccharīrayātāṃte daśāpūrverdaśāparaiḥ

ārūsvabhagavaddhāmaprayāsatra na saṃśayaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāsiddhiyoryadi bhaviṣyati

iti dāśarathīyesmin triṃśodhyāya udīritaḥ

sādhayetkāmyakarmāṇi samastāni tataḥ para

darāricārmāsigadāyārācāyeṣudhāriṇaṃ

sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ vāpi kṛtimaṃcāpi yadviṣaṃ

ravigrahaṇe prāptetaddine sagupoṣitaḥ

spṛśanbrāhmīraraṃvidhāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ jāpan

pāragassarvavidyānāṃ māsābhyaṃtarato bhavet

palāśā kusumairhutvādaśasāhasramādarāt

caturṇāmapi vedānāṃ pāragovāgvibhūtimān

tulasīvanamadhyastho japtvālakṣaṃ yatavrataḥ

dīrghāpuṣpamavāpnoti svacchaṃdamaraṇe bhavet

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ samastanyāsasaṃtāṃ

paṃcaviṃśatidhānityamabhirmaṃtrya jalaṃ pivet

paṃcaviṃśatidhānityamabhimaṃtryābhamādarāt

pratimāsavyatīpāle yoge lakṣmīpatiśriye

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtvā śrīmaṃtratriśataṃ japet

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇa triśataṃ juhuyādatha

bhagavaddaśmividyābhirekaikāmāhutiṃ hunet

niṣkaniṣkārdamathavā svācāryāyā samarpyayet

mahāyāyoyayāyīdyaurapi rorogairvimucyate

bhūvyā samastavidyānāṃ yāragassudarākṛtiḥ

vilvamūle samāsīno maṃtrarājaṃ śriyaḥpateḥ

hutvātha daśasāhāsraṃ rājyalakṣmīmavānyuyāt

lakṣamekaṃ prajaptvāthatapatrairghṛtaplutaiḥ

mārgaśīrṣe paurṇamāsyāmācāryānujñayāśuciḥ

samastanyāsa sahitāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ śrībhā

triśataṃ prajayetyaścādiṣṭamekaṃ śriyomanuṃ

sālagrāmaśilāḥ cakramayodhyānagarābhidhaṃ

pa ekadhaivobhayatrāpi lakṣmīnārāyaṇai vibhū

pūrṇaṃ niśākaraṃdasūsāyaṃ sadhyāmupāsya ca

nniśataṃjuyādagrauśrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

bhagavadraśmividyābhiryatamekaikaśo hunet

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoje niṣkamānasuvarṇakaṃ

haviṣyāśī phalāśīvāvareṣkarmithutadvayaṃ

bhuṃjīta vaṃdhubhiḥ sārdhamiṣṭamannaṃ tataḥ para

bhagavaṃtaṃ śriyāsārdhaṃ paśyatyeva svacakṣuṣā

acalāṃ śriyamānyoti prasādārhidirāyateḥ

mahākavirmahāvāhrīputrapautrāyautrādisaṃyutaḥ

prāpnoti bhagavaddhāgaderhāte nātrasaṃśayaḥ

yaḥ karoti viśuddhātmājātiṃssaratiyaurvikī

yogamadyāṃgasahitamajarāmaratāmapi

paurṇamāsīvrataṃ puṇyaṃ viśeṣarkṣudinānvitaṃ

citrāyukteduvāreṇasahitā caitraptāyā

ācāryānujñayā tatra snātvā prātassamāhita

sīnāssamastanyāḥdyauvabhaṃjanī ācāryānujñayā tatra sasaṃyutāṃ

triśataṃ prajapetyaścīdiṣṭaṃ śrīmanumādarāt

pūrṇaniśākaraṃ iṣṭvājvalite jātavedasi

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇa triśataṃ juhayādatha

āṣṭottarathathaiḥ puṣpaiḥ śrīśrīśadvādaśārṇayā

dviniṣkamekaniṣkaṃ vā dakṣiṇāmaryayedatha

mithunatritayaṃbhojyaparedyuddhiṃtayaṃ tu vā

idameva vrataṃ kṛtvā ravisaṃkramaṇānvite

etadevrataṃ kurvannidagrahaṇasaṃyuta

ekena divasenetthemavasyaṃ siddhimānbhavet

viśākhāsahipūṇīvaiśākhāmahatisṛtā

naityakaṃ karmakṛtvātha samīmastanyāsasaṃyutāṃ

trirātraṃ vrajayetpaścādiṣṭaṃ śrīmaṃtramādarā

daṣṭaniśākaraṃ pūrṇejvalitetājātavedasi

śrīmaṃtreṇahunedravyamājyaṃ triśatamādarāt

catvāriṃśatpalavīpiviśatpalamittaṃ tu vā

śayyāṃ soyaskarāṃ datvā yathā śaktyā sā dakṣiṇaṃ

samarcya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādekaniṣkadhanaṃ tu vā

mithunadvitayaṃ bhojyaṃ pare pūrvasudhāyate

śrīmadaṣṭāṇaividyāyāḥ puraścaryādvayaṃ śubha

kṛtetthameva savisuśāṃ krameṇaptamanvite

yadīdgrahaṇaṃ tatra vai**(?)reva paurṇasī dine

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyā mahatīṃ siddhimaśrute

triguṇādakṣiṇādayā caṃdraparvasamanvite

jyeṣṭānakṣatrasaṃyuktā mahatījyeṣṭapaurṇamā

ācāryājñāṃ samādāya prāteḥ srātvā viśuddhadhīḥ

japtvāyīṣṭamanuṃ lakṣmyāḥ sādhakastriṃśataṃ tataḥ

triśataṃ śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyā trimadhusrātaṃ

bhagavadraśmividyāmirājyena juhuyāttridhā

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoje niṣkamānadhanaṃ tu vā

ācāryapatnyai śrīvudhyāsatpālaṃkāramarpayet

mithunacitayaṃbhojyattasminneva dine vudhaiḥ

puraścaryātrayaphalaṃvratenānena jāyate

yadi saṃkramaṇaṃ tatra paurṇamāsyāṃ prajāpate

triniṣkadakṣiṇāṃ vāpi datvācārya padāṃvujaḥ

āṣāḍhe paurṇamāsīyavaiśvaddaivatyasaṃyutā

dvaipāpanaḥ purātasyā mamūnmatvā layānvitaḥ

paurṇemāsyāmāthāṣāṭhyāprātaḥsrātvā viśuddhadhīḥ

tarpayitvāpi hṛnyaścāt samastanyāsa saṃyutā

triśata prajayellakṣmīmaṃtrami.ṣtataṃmaṃśutaṃ

sāyaṃ saṃdhyāmuyāsyātha madhutyritayamiśritaṃ

śrīmatreṇa hunet praścīdṛṣṭottaraśataṃ ghṛtaṃ

arcayettriśataṃ brahmavidyayākusumādibhiḥ

gurupādābjayugmetha dadyātriṣkadhanaṃ tu vā

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāphalaṃ sāṃgamavāpnuyāt

yadi saṃkramaṇaṃ tatra kṛtvetthevratamādarāt

grahaṇaṃ yadi tatra syāt sudhāśorvasudhāyate

yādṛśī jāyate siddhistāmevāpnoti śāśvāto

īdavāsasaṃ yuktā mūratī samudīritī

tatrādhāryājñāyāsrātvā kṛtvā dharmāthanaityakaṃ

triśatādhikasāhasraśrīmadāṣṭākṣarī japet

sārye sadhyāmuyāsyāthajvalite jātavedasi

pāyasaṃ juhuyāllakṣmīmaṃtreṇātha ghṛtaṃ śubha

arcayettriśataṃ puṣpaiḥ patrervā brahmavidyādyayā

puraścaryāphalaṃ sāgasadyarāva labhedhruvaṃ

puraścaryātra ekalaṃ prāprotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

caṃdracūḍāmāsinonnāyoṇai labhya udāhṛtaḥ

varṣāśanamitadravyamathavāguraverpyayet

āthavācāryahastā datvā samāhatī parā

ājanakṣātrasahitāpūrṇā bhādrapadepiyāḥ

tatrācāryajñayā prātarnityakarmasamātha ca

śrīmadaṣṭākṣīrījāptvā śrīmaṃtraṃ triśataṃ japet

madhutritayagavyājyaplutaṃ pāyasasādarāt

śrīmaṃtreṇahunedātyamaṣṭottaraśataṃ nṛpa

triśataṃ tulasīrṣatrairarcayadvabrahmavidyāyā

kṣīrāśī vā phalāśī vā divasetra tataḥ paraṃ

puraścaryāphalaṃ prīpyasiddhiprīpnoti śāśvatīṃ

phalaṃ prāpnoti somasyagrahaṇaṃ jāyate yadi

phalaṃ prāpnoti somasya grahaṇṇaṃ jāyate yadi

guruvāsarasaṃyuktāmaktāmahatīsāsasīritā

kṛtvā pūrvavadārabhyadatvācāryasya dakṣiṇaṃ

puraścaryātrayaphalaṃ sāṃgamaṃtravrajatyasau

paṃcavārapuraścaryāphalasāṃgamapruyāt

kṛtvācārya padāṃbhoje samathotha triniṣka

kārtike paurṇemāsīyāvahidaivatyasaṃyutā

ācāyonujñāyā tatra nityakarmasāmāpya ca

śrīmadaṣṭākṣaritasvāśrīmaṃtratriśataṃ japet

śrīmadṛṣṭvāṇaṃ maṃtreṇa triśataṃ sādhākottamāḥ

rāśiṃdhātrī phalānāṃ ca saṃsthāpyācārya sa sannidhau

dakṣiṇāṃ gurupāānnedatvādhātrī phalānvitaṃ

pūrṇaṃ niśākaradṛṣṭvā pradatvārghyaṃ tridhedave

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidhā siddhisumahātīmapi

ravisaṃkramaṇaṃ tatra yadisyādvaṣudhāyate

dviguṇaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādācāryasya padāṃvuje

sudhāṃśugrahaṇe tatra vratamevedamādarāt

catuḥṣaṣṭhipuraścayāṃ phalaṃ sāṃgramavānyuyāt

bhūgavāsarasaṃyuktāmahatāsaṃprakīkīrtita

trisattādhikāsāhasraṃ samastanyāsasayatāṃ

madhutritayagavyājyamiśraṃtilvadṛsānvitaṃ

śrīmaṃtreṇahunerājyaṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vudhaḥ

mithunatrinayaṃbhojyaṃ bhakṣyasūpaghṛtādibhiḥ

niṣkaśyaṃ pradātavya haridreṇāpi bhūpate

sāyaṃ saṃdhyāmupāsyātha japecchrī hṛdayastavaṃ

āṣṭāvārapuraścaryāsamuddrūtāṃ vrajatyaso

vilvapatraissumabhyarcya triśataṃ brahmavidyayā

mṛgāṃkagrahaṇaṃ tatra yadisyadva sudhāyate

caṃdracūḍāmaṇāvukta homaṃ kṛtvā sadakṣiṇaṃ

puṣpamāsyarkacāreṇa sahitāyauṇaumīyadi

ācāryānujñāyāpyatramātaḥ saṃdhyo samāpya ca

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarītayet

ācāryasaccidhāṣvagniṃ saṃskṛtyavidhi ca tataḥ

triśataṃ juhuyādagnau pāpasaṃtrimadhuplutaṃ

bhagavadraśmividyābhiḥ kuryādājyāhutiṃ tridhāḥ

tikadvaya suvarṇaṃ vā dṛdhāhācāryapādayo

cūḍāmaṇirayaṃ yogonāmnālabhya udāhṛtaḥ

triniṣkadakṣiṇāvāpi dṛtvācārya padāṃvuje

mṛgāṃkagrahaṇaṃ puṇyaṃ yaditaprabhavennṛpa

śuddhamaṃḍalaryaṃta kartasyādviśeṣataḥ

kuṃḍaladvayasaṃyuktāṃ pradṛtvācārya pādayoḥ

prāthāvaśyaparāṃ siddhiṃ vrajatyatra na saṃśayaḥ

mādyamāse paurṇamāso pitṛnakṣatrasaṃyutā

guroranujñāyāpyatra samāṣyāvaśyakaṃ vidhiṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ jāhvā śrīmatratriśata juhuyāddhaviḥ

mithunatritayaṃbhojyaṃ bhakṣasūyādikairathai

utpalairarcayet paścātriśateva vahavidyayā

puraścaryātra phalaṃ sāṃgaṃ prāpnoti śāśvataṃ

ṣaṭkasya labhate phalamuttayaṃyudīdagrarāhaṇaṃ tatra jāyate sudhāpate

nayacaṃdracūḍāmaṇāvuktajapāhikamathādarāt

catuḥṣaṣṭipuraścarya phalaṃsāgaṃgaṃ vrajatyasau

bhānuvāsarasaṃ yuktā mahāte sā samīritā

japahomādvikaṃ kārya māghavadvasudhāpate

taddeva kalamuddiṣṭamatrāpi nahi saṃśayaḥ

yathoktametatkṛtvāso siddhavidhaḥ prajāyate

tattanmāsepaurṇamāsī mahatī sā prakīrtitāḥ

ekasyāṃ paurṇamāsyā vā vrataṃ kartavyamādarāt

yeṣu pūvasudyatsāṃgaṃ japahomādi ki śubhaṃ

na vidyā sidvimātyoti nāsyasiddhiti vāṃchitaṃ

nānarakadaḥ khānicārya ca punaḥ punā

śrīmadāṣṭākṣarisiddhiyādisyāddaṣudhāyarta

alabhyayoge saṃprāpte bhikṣitvā śūdrakānapi

sadya eva pradātavyaṃ hānadravāṃ mahīyate

annaṃgrāsamitaṃ vāpi yācayennaguroḥ kuleḥ

prayatne nāpi saṃpādya dātavyaṃ gurave dhanaṃ

sadya eva pradātavyamīcchatāsiddhimuttamāṃ

kuṃḍaladvitayāpyātaṃ yadalaṃkaraṇamuttamaṃ

sadyasīddhimavāpyā saupīndṛn svargaṃ na yatyati

arddhasīddhipradāproktā dakṣiṇāmāsamātrataḥ

urddhaṃ māsatrayādattāniskalāmātrasaṃśayaḥ

pradakṣīṇā sadyau māsādvāphaladāyinī

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīti bhavartavyā phaladāyinī

adhyā ekatriṃśoyamuktobhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ

punarāhakṛhādevaṃ brahmarṣīṇāṃ ca sannidhau

yacchrutvāpīnarodhīmānasīddhīmetāṃ mahattarāṃ

lakṣaṣoḍaṣakaṃ proktaṃ yadyaitsadyasīddhihetukaṃ

tatpuraścaraṇādevasīddhiḥ kṛtayugādiṣu

*** rogādoṣa vahvalatvataḥ tayā tathā

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāmasīddhāyāṃ mahītale

lakṣmīpatisādasyāleśopī na hi jāyate

avasyaṃsīddhimāpnoti puraścaraṇakṛtyataḥ

navarātrovrate cāpī puraścaraṇakarmaṇā

navarātrevrate prāpte ravīṃvugrahaṇe tathā

mahāpuskarayogetrayogepyardhodayānidhe

yatpuraścaraṇaṃ muktamādauṣoḍaṣalakṣakaṃ

sarveṣvalabhyayogeṣu puraścaryā yadīrītā

ākṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ tato daśaguṇoparā

akṣayedinamātrepī kṛtvā majayādikaṃ

kimuktayoge sahite vaiśākhedivasekṣaye

ukta vaiśākhe dīvasekṣayemadaṣṭārṇavidyā

japahomādibhīrnītyamācāryeṇa dīne dīne

sacāsādmapi vidyānāṃ bhūyaḥ ṣīśyopadeśataḥ

śīṣyaśyadakṣīṇe karṇayāyāṃ vidyāṃ dadātyasau

uttamāṃ dakṣīṇāṃ dadyācārya padāṃvuje

madhyadakṣiṇayāsīddhirmadhyemeva vidhīyate

taśmādavaśyamācārya pādādvayugale śubhe

uttamāṃ dakṣīṇāmeva datvācāryamukhāṃvujāt

grahaṇe naiva vidyānāṃ sadya aiṃ vamahattarāṃ

ācāryaṇāvīśīṣyebhyasma prāpte divasekṣaye

sarvasvasya pradātre vā prāṇānāmapi yatnataḥ

ācāryatapasaḥ siddhīryataḥ śīṣyagacchati

tadā prabhṛtisacchiṣyaḥ ācāryasya padāṃvuje

grahaṇādiṣu yogeṣu tatādṛksīddhisīddhirīrītā

homenāpī mahāsīddhīsāravedīvasekṣaye

mithanāni samarbhyarcyatebhyo datvātha dakṣīṇāṃ

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ vā śatādhikaṃ

vāsu devamahāvidyāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ ca ṣaḍakṣarī

taśmīdīnephalāśītavāhaviṣyāśi bhavedvrati

guruḥ prasādo bhoktavyaḥ paradyurgīrījāyate

puraścaraṇakṛtyeṣu samāpteṣu tataḥ paraṃ

anena sarvapāpaidyanīvṛrtiḥ sīddhiruttamā

tapaḥ sīdvagurorvaktrācchīṣyaiḥ sādhakapuṃgavaiḥ

bhūyopī saṃgrahitavyaṃ datvottamadakṣiṇāṃ

vidyājālaṃ na dātavyaṃ taponāśīnadhīmatā
