vahūprārthanayādeyaṃ yogelabhye yaśaskare

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpī japatvā nītyaṃ labhet phalaṃ

nārāyaṇe nayatproktaṃ janakāya mahātmane

saṃpūjyaṃ dvādaśākṣaryā prajasvāṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

aṇīmādiguṇeśvaralabhate nātra saṃśayaḥ

daśasāhasrakaṃ hūtvā dīyudīrghāyusānnīrāyama

asaubhāgyena yuktā pīsubhagaḥ kāmavadbhavet

bhagavatkalayāyuktaḥ kāṃtimānarkavadbhavet

jaḍopīparvavīdyābhīrvacasyati samo bhavet

bhagavatkalayāyuktaḥ kāṃtimānarkavadbhavet

yanasasya phalaidūrtvānadhūtrimadhusaṃplutaiḥ

hūtvā vilāphalainīrgavyaghṛtātrimadhumīśrītai

ghṛtatrimadhusaṃmīśrāṃ hūtvā pāyasamādarāt

ghṛtatrīmadhusaṃmīśraihūtvā tu kadalīphalaiḥ

ayutaṃ sutamāpnoti bhavatkalānvitaṃ

tulasīdassīśrairhūtvā śohūtvā nīlotpalaiḥ śubhaiḥ

drākṣāphalairgavyaghṛtamadhutrītayamīśrītaiḥ

ikṣūkhaṃḍairghṛtābhyaktairmadhūtritayāmīśrītaiḥ

kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhūtaiphalaiḥ ṣaṣṭhīmītaiśubhaiḥ

aṣṭāṃgayogamāsādyakhecarojāyate dhruvaṃ

ayutaṃ cittamāpnoti vāṃchīnaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

bhavatyusāvathā puṣpān dhaneśo vākyanirbhavet

ayutadvītīyaṃ svarṇaśarīrobhavati dhruvaṃ

hūtvā vā laravīprākhyaḥ kāṃtyā bhavati sādhakaḥ

anunadvitayaṃ paścātsuvarṇasadṛśaḥ śrīyā

sarpī strīmadhusaṃmīśraihūtvā guggulubhīḥ śūcīḥ

ayutaṃ sarkharāṃ khaṃḍairgavyājya trīmadhusrutai

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ vīdyāṃ japtvaikaṃ lakṣamādarāt

lakṣadvayaṃ japeyastu śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

caturlakṣya japādāśrumahatīṃ śrīyamakṣayāṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ japtvā vastulakṣaṃ jīteṃdriyaḥ

lakṣadvādaśakaṃ japtvā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīmanuṃ

śrīyādhānadaśaṃ bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

śaṃkhādīnāṃ nidhānānāmapīyo jāyate dhruvaṃ

svatyārūpaṃ pradatvā smaikalpāyusmamacaṃcalaṃ

paṃcaviṃśati lakṣāṇī śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ mamuṃ

divyadṛṣṭau samāsādyakāmakāmarūpī bhavatyasau

dvātriṃśa lakṣajapatiḥ sīddhānāmadhīyā bhavet

ṣaḍvīṃśannakṣajāpena mahāvidyādhārorbhavet

paṃcāśalaksajapataḥ sīddhānāmajīto bhavet

adhīroti tejaptvī divyakanyā satānvitaḥ

ajarāmaraṇo bhūtvā devatvaṃ labhate dhruvaṃ

prāpnotidraṃ samo bhūtvā jarāmṛtyu vivarjitaḥ

ajarāmaraṇo bhūtvā satyaloyo bhavet

dakṣīṇāmūrti dṛṣovidyābhīrdīvyadehabhāk

aṇīmādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ pādukādyaṣṭakaṃ tathā

aṣṭādhikaśataṃ lakṣajapena niyatavrataḥ

brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāmapīvaṃdya padāṃvujaḥ

avāpyayogamaṣṭāṃgaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ manojñatāṃ

avāpyadi kanyānāṃ sahasreṇa samanvītaṃ

bhṛtyānāmagragaṇyosau bhūtvā bhagavato mahān

nārāyaṇasamaṃ daivanāstīnāstī mahīpate

anyatrayoge japahomatarpaṇaḥ na tādṛśī sīddhimupaiti vipraḥ

nārāyaṇastayadhārīvaṃdyapūjyaśca gurugarīyān

prajāpīvaṃdhyaṃ na kuryāddivasaṃmanuṣyaḥ

nyāsaisahāṣṭākṣaramaṃtrajāpīnārāyaṇānamasmanā eva

bhagavānuvāca nārāyaṇenāmaranāyake na rājñe yaduktaṃ paramaṃ pavītraṃ

avaśyatsāgarekūrmamahāṃtaṃ divyavarcasaṃ

nāhaṃ dhanya iti prāhakurmastaṃ nāradaṃmunī

dhanyā bhavatya ityāhūto āpo nāradomunīḥ

nāradastābhuvaṃ prāhadhanyāsīti vasuṃdhane

sādhārabhūtāgīrayadumedhanyāmahāmate

dhanyānavayamītyāhūrnārabhūdharā apī

samīpaṃ brahmaṇo gatvādhanyosītyāhataṃ munīḥ

sṛjyaṃte sakalokāvaidainṛtyaṃ sanātaneḥ

ityukto nāradovedānāhanatvādaṃḍavat

vedāśca nāradaṃ prāhvadhanyā navayamītyapī

yuyaṃdhanyatarāyajñā iti nāra āhatān

ijyate bhagavān sākṣādevadevaḥ śrīyaḥpati

sarvaviyāmayaḥ svāmī trijagatpatirīśca

pitāmahastutogatvā sannidhaṃ praramātmānā

nāradaprāhaviśvātmādakṣiṇābhīssahetyatha

dakṣiṇābhīssakathaṃ dhanyo bhagavannīdīrā bhave

uvāca lakṣmī sānīdhyamūrti sa dvedaśanīdhau

ete vakṣaṃti vedāste niścītaṃ manmukhodbhvaḥ

bhuṃjahītī bhūstvāyannagāśanavāhanaḥ

vedā apī brahma apīsucustamucastatvaṃ sa nivīścītaṃ

vayaṃ lokahītārthāya sṛṣṭā bhagavate mune

avaśyamapīkartavyaṃmasīdājñāvaliyasī

valīyasenarakaudyeṣu majjatyācaṃdratārakaṃ

sakalāyāgāsaṃpūrṇavaradakṣīṇārahītopīca

dakṣīṇāyuktameva karyaṃmate *****

dakṣīṇābhīsahetyuktaṃ vaco yatparamātmanā

svalpadakṣīṇayāgopīdakṣīrahitopīca

yajñeśvaro yajño bhoktā sākṣādyajñapumān vibhuḥ

sakuṭosthe ca locavyaktaḥ paramātmā śrīyapatiḥ

yaṣṭavymahāyāgaisaṃpūrṇa varadakṣīṇaiḥ

avaśaśahitarūpa eva labhate gatiṃ

darīdreṇāpīdeyasyānītyaṃ śākādīne mune

viśeśataḥ kalau prāpto nārakodā namovahī

japahomādīkaṃ karmakṛtvā pratyahamādarāt

saṃkapamāsīmāse vā dātavyaṃ bhūtimīcchasī

japahomādikaṃ tāsāṃ kṛyate yai sadakṣīṇaṃ

tatrāpīśrīmadaṣṭārṇomahaṃ naraḥ

tasyaiva raśmayaḥ sarvasṛjaṃvīdyāṃtiyāṃti ca

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyātmāharīrnāyaṇo vibhuḥ

śrīmadarṇavīdyāyājapohāmādikaṃ śubhaṃ

nāsvamedhasadvastrairvānarahādānakoṭibhiḥ

cūṇāvaradakṣīṇāṃ kṛtaṃ tena na saṃdehaḥ paśyapīramāpatiṃ

brahmarūdrādibhīrdevairapyasau pūjyate pune

adatvācārya pādābdenasīddhīṃ mahatiṃjet

sarvasvamīti tatmokṣaṃ taduktā pūrṇadakṣīṇā

vastrādikaṃ ca sakalaṃ sarvasvaṃ tatsamīrītaṃ

athavarṣāṣanamīnadravyaṃ cottamadakṣiṇāṃ

aṣṭākṣarīpuraścryāsadṛśaṃ na mahāmate

purāṇajātaṃ sakalaṃ kalpasūtrādikaṃ ca yat

bhagavadṛśīsahitā samastanyāsaṃyūtā

yasya sīddhirthititaṃ vṛddhititaṃ vṛddhī brahmarūpīṇamacyūtaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāṃ sīddhāyāṃ divīvā bhuvī

aṇīmādiguṇaisvaryasaṃpūrṇakhyātirakṣayā

aṣṭāṃgasahitoyāgojātijātiśmaramaṇapyathā

ācāryacaradvaṃdvassalasīddhidā etadevījānīhīparabrahmaikasīddhīdaṃ

brahmadarśaśaṃsīddhi hetubhūtamudirayet

dharmārthamokṣadharmāṇāṃ nidānamamītikathate

kathyate śrūyanityamyarcyate dhyāyae tathā

sādhakaiprāpnumakṣaramanojayet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣaśrīddhīyatnakartavya ādarāt

ācāryavadanāṃbhojātsaṃprāpyāṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

nārāyaṇamunerjanmadivasekṣayasaṃjñīte

samastalakṣmīvidyānāṃnyāsadhyānādikaṃ vadha

vidhānamapī saṃkṣīptaṃ śrīmaṃtrāṇāṃ pravakṣate

agnivīṃdu samāyuktaṃ dvītiyaṃ vījamūdhṛtaṃ

bhūvijaṃmūkhakṣatatyaṃ vāyumārtaṃḍasaṃyūtaṃ

aṣṭākṣarī mahāvidyā mahālakṣmyādudīrītā

dārīdradukhasamanīsamastai sarvasīddhida

chaṃdogāyatramītyuktaṃ mahālakṣmīstrayīmayī

saḍaṃgaṃ vinyaset paścācchrīyā ṣaṭdīrghayuktā

pītāṃ varāṃ laṃkṛtāṃ mahālakṣmīmīti dhyātvā japennityamamemanuṃ

aṣṭalakṣaṃ paraścaryadutyatrairvāthapaṃkajaiḥ

hūtvācārya padāṃbhoje datvā vahvalakṣīṇāṃ

vaisākhe phālgune vāpīvāyetvā śrāvaṇepīvā

saṃkalpya paurṇamāśyaṃtaṃ paṃcaviṃśatsahakaṃ

dṛśāṃśaṃ juhuyādmai vilvapatraidathāpīvā

dakṣīṇāsahitaṃ datvā kāṃcanena vīcanevinirmī

datvā dvādaśabhuṃjīvādalaṃkṛtya suvāsīnīḥ

sīddhimavāpnoti vidyāyānātraśaṃśayaḥ

ādhīvyādhī prasamanī sarvasaṃpṛtsamṛddhīye

ālakṣmīkalahādhārātadgehenavatyagvacit

aṣṭākṣarīṃ mahālakṣmyā japedbhāgyasamṛddhaye

pradipadvīramābhyanavamyaṃ tathamādarā

dvādaśahasraṃ kṛtvā saṃkṛtyamādarāt

navamyāṃ jvalivahau śūddhaṃ gavyājyamādarāt

aṣṭārṇavidyayālakṣmyāmadhutitayamīśrītaṃ

viṣṇvapatra samāyuktaṃ havītīlānvitaṃ

karṇayopaścādvahūśyaśaudyaśaṃkulāṃ

sadakṣīṇaṃ tapto dedyāddhe tu dvayamalaṃkṛtaṃ

alaṃkāraiśca vāsaubhīrdakṣīṇābhīścabhaujane

vivāharahitākanyā avaśaṃpūjayennaraḥ

atha vāvau ca nārūḍhāḥ yatra vatyoguṇānvitaḥ

vibhave satyalaṃkāraṃ tāsāṃ vā sāṃsicāryayet

bhojayitvā mṛṣṭhānnaṃ dakṣīṇābhīścatoṣayet

evaṃ kṛte vidhāne navarātravrajeśu **

daditimahasatisīddhitrailokyanāpīdurbhāṃ

bhojayītvāthamīṣṭānnaṃ bhagavātyaṃkulīmahahīśrīyaṃ

dadāti bhagavāṃstasmaiśaṃśayonātra vidyayo

yayajñeśurasedyūrīgu umīśvajalena vā

dhanasenasamolathā jīvaṃ bhagavatyasau

aṣṭākṣarīṃ mahālakṣmyā japtvā lakṣamataṃdriyaḥ

saulanatevītavṛddhīṃ ********

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā japakartādīnedīne

parā

sīdhaṃti yasya japato brahmedrādivībhūtayaḥ

vā tavahnī samārūḍhāṃ vāmalocanabhūṣītaṃ

viṃddhahīnaṃ vahnīyutaṃ maṃtasyadyamataḥparaṃ

śrīṣaḍakṣaravīdheyamuktāpapaudyabhedīnī

dhanadhānyagajāścaudyamūktā-māṇīkavarṣīṇī

śrīvījasahitāmaṃtrāvairīṇopi mahātmane

śrīyuktameva sakalaṃ pūjyatejagatitale

na pūjyate bhūtale lakṣmīnkṣūdravadūsudhāpate

trijagatsvapīnārcya syādbhavasyāpīniditaḥ

apīpuṣpaphalānvitaḥ na dṛṣṭumapīśakyaṃte

******** pūjameva jagatpate

abhyarcitaḥ svādbhūyādyāladevatsakalairjanaiḥ

saṃpūjyate mahīpāla śrīyāyukto jagariye

sa eva brahmavidyāna sa evāryo dine dine

śrīyāvīrahitaḥ sākṣādbhagavānabhūpate

ṣaḍaśaramīdaṃ tasmācchīyodevyāḥ phalapradaṃ

ṣaḍakṣarīḥ śrīyovidyāsargāḍhyītyaṃtakārīṇī

bhṛgurasyamunīśchāṃdonīvṛdgāyatramīrītaṃ

dvitivījamaktaṃ ca śaktirnatirudirītā

kṣomāvaddhaninaṃvadhīṃ valalītāṃvaderavedastītāṃ

śrīyamabhyarcayannīsugaṃdhī kusumādibhīḥ

japāṃto juhuyānmaṃtrītilairvāmadhurāplutaiḥ

athavā śrīmadaṣṭārṇa catuviṃśata ṣahasrakaṃ

pāpasaṃjūhūyāt paścātcchīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyā

maṃtreṇāpī juhuyātāvadeva ghṛtaṃ śubhaṃ

sarvasvamarpayeddhīmānathācāryapadāṃvuje

alaṃkāraiścavāsobhirabhyarcāryavallabhā

**** śrīśaḍakṣaravidyāyāḥ kṣarasya ca

agnīśāsuravāyamadhyadīkṣaṃgapūjanaṃ

hīmamītamamāleṃdūnīlāyabhatavāsasaḥ

vidigātreṣu pātreṣu damakādinyajedṛthā

yacchechaṃkhanidhidevyādakṣiṇedayitānvītaṃ

nyonyāliṃganaparauśaṃkhapaṃkajadhārīṇau

vasuṃdhārāthastaniṃtamataḥ paṃkajanidhīṃ prīyayāsahitaṃ yajet

nisaradalavarṣābhyāṃ padmābhyāṃ mūhvīlāṃchīnau

dalāgreyajedevatālākyādyāḥ samatalaḥ

vibhīṣīṇīmālikā ca śāṃkārīvasumālikā

lokeśāṃnpūjayedativajrādyāstrāṇī tadvahiḥ

dhanyadhānyamṛddhīchīyamopnotyaniditāṃ

saṃyuto maṃtrī devīṃdhāyetvārkamaṃḍine

savilasya mūlasvasya mūlamadhyāsthe maṃtravit

** madhuratrayuktai juhuyājātavedasai

aśoka vahū juhuyātatpuṣpaurājatolataulatoḥ

juhuyātadvalaiḥ śuddhairayutaṃ trimadhuplutai

juhuyādvaidike vahnau tadvalergaughṛtahnūtaiḥ

prakṣāmīmahārājakīcinmātmamīṣṭadaṃ

teyasyāvijñānamātreṇa palāyaṃ mahāpadaḥ

vātanaṃ vahnisamārūḍhaṃ vāmanetrāvibhūṣītaṃ

vasuṃdharākṣaraṃ paścādvāyuvaikuṃṭhasaṃyutaṃ

viṃduhinaramāvārṇa vaikuṃṭhavasudhākṣaraṃ paścā

vāṇītaṃdrāvāyutaṃ mātṛkāṃtyarṇamavyatha

vedavedāṃtaśāstraughasārabhūtāsanātanī

sarvavidyā vibhedinī mahābhāgya samṛddhīdā

sīddhavidyāmimāṃ prākūrmunayo brahmavādinaḥ

dvādaśārṇa mahālakṣmīrahitāniḥ sthūlāsmṛtāḥ

yogīnopi mahābhāgājīvamuktājīteṃdrīyāḥ

samastaṃ nivṛtyarthaṃ sarvasaṃpadavāptave

āyuyārārogyajananīmadropadravanāśīnī

dvādaśākṣarīvidyāsonāsarādhya brahmadarudrādayopi ca

ṛṣīrvraṣṭhāsyaimaṃtrasya bhṛgurvāmunīsīsyate

paścāt ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ ṣaṭdīrghayuktayālakṣavinyaset praṇavādyayā

svamurddhīmuktāralaughavarṣachatreṇaśobhītāṃ

sataptahemasākāśapītāṃvarayugāvṛtāṃ

lasadvāmākanilayānāsīnasya jagatpateḥ

mahālakṣmīṃ bhagavatiṃ dhyātvātha mādarāt

dvādaśāṇaṃ mahālakṣmyāśatamaṣṭādhīkaṃ japet

pratyekamathā lakṣmīsaṃpūjayenṛpaḥ

caturddhāra samāyuktaṃ catusramataḥ paraṃ

āgnīsāśuravāyathāmadhyadikṣvaṃgapūjanaṃ

sarasvati brāhmyādyā aṣṭakoṇeṣadīrghasvarṇavibhūṣītāḥ

caturasrasyāṣṭadikṣuvajrādinyāyudhānīca

artayedrupacapācadyairupacārairattuttamai

daśāṃśatarpayedikṣurasenapayasāpivā

**** sātāṃ sāgraṃ sṛṣṭānamapībho japet

dadyāniskaṃśataṃ vāpī suvarṇakuṃḍalānītaṃ

ācāryasannidhautiṣṭe vādyājītaṃmateṃdriyaḥ

prāpnoti mahatiṃ sīddhiṃ purāṇāmapīdurlabhāṃ dīkṣītaḥ

na navarātrābhīdhaṃ parvavarṣevarṣyemahattaraṃ

tatrālakṣīvidyānāmatrasīddhiṣeśataḥ

ekaikaṃ divasattaravīparvasumaṃviduḥ

mahati navamī tatraccha ḍāmaṇīśataḥ prabhaḥ ekaikaṃ

dvādaśārṇaṃ mahāla apītatra mahāmate

japet aṣṭamyaṃ tathādarāt

śrīmadakṣarīvidyāṃ japedarkaśrūtirūpaphalapradaṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityamarcayt brahmavidyāyā

parīcchahasamanvītaṃ rājaputrodhanyādyopīniskasāhasramarthayet

cedguruverpayet sālagrāmaśīlādyānāṃ bhūdānaṃ ca dakṣīṇaṃ

godānamapīśayyāpādānamāvaśyakaṃ smṛtaṃ

varādyālakṣaṇaḥ saṃpūtāṃ pautrikanyakāṃdadyātsurāpāṃcasulakṣaṇāṃ

ācāryacaraṇḍadvaṃdvaśuṣruṣaraṇaparo bhavet

kaharācitākādyalaṃkālaṃ kālaiśca vāsanā

athavā na vijirbhājyāsuvāsīnyo dṛśavratā

itthaṃ tvāpuraścaryāṃ navarātre dṛḍhavrataḥ

prāpnotyapībhṛpraśaṃśayaḥ nātra vidyate

mahālakṣmīrbhagavate vā tasya trayī mayī

tasmī saṃvatsare tasyā gṛhe ratnodyasaṃkulā

guṇaiśvryamayī tasyā śuddhīsiti

nirāmayodīrādyusmatātu sukhaṃ medhate

śreyāṃśrīśalabhate nityakalyāṇānidīnedīne

gajāsvāsīsamṛddhiśca navarātramṛtādbhavet

māmevartivijānīhīsṛddhīmaṃtraṃ jīteṃdrīyaḥ

divyakanyāśatākīrṇaṃ sarvabhogasamṛddhita

samṛddhīsamṛddhistādaśī bhavet

navarātravrate kṛtvā puraścaryāṃ yatavrataḥ

navarātravrata samaṃnānyatsīddhīpadaṃ nṛpa

navarātrenvahaṃ bhaktyāṃ navamyāṃ navaniskamaṃ

yatheṣṭavidhīnā kṛtvā navarātravrataṃ śubhaṃ

svacchaṃdamaraṇau bhūtvā śrīyamaṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

vāsaṃte navarātrepīkuryādītthaṃ vrataṃ śubhaṃ

***** niskaṃ vā samarpyācāryadakṣīṇāṃ

navarātravrataṃ sāṃgaṃ kṛtamāśvinamāsī caret

navarātravrataṃ sāṃgaṃ krīyate nāsvīneyadiyadī

sūtakepīnasaṃtyājaṃ navarātravṛtaṃ śubhaṃ

ācāryaputrairathavākārayedvidhināvrataṃ

tasya saṃvatsarehānirbhavīṣyati mahīpate

sāṃgadakṣiṇayāyuktamavarśaṃ vratamācaret

kartavyaṃ śreya icchadbhissāṃgadakṣīṇāṃ yāyukta

********** vā svarṇamarpayet

ekasmīndivase vāpīsīddhīdavamudhāpate

vaiśākheśrāvaṇe vāpīkīrtikemādyamāsi vā

tattadvāsarasaṃyuktā paurṇamāsya bhavedyadi

viśeśātsomavāreṇasahitaṃ caṃdraparvacet

amāpīsomasīhitāyogaḥ padmaka ucyate

samanvitaṃ

tathaiva pratyahaṃ lakṣmīdvādaśārṇajapedapī

snānaṃ kuryātsparśakāle hṛdevāpī na jījale

catuviṃśatidhā snātva vimuktaḥ pāparāśībhīḥ

śrīmaddaṣṭākṣareṇāditrīṃśataṃ hūyātilaiḥ

dvādaśārṇamahālakṣmīśuddhaṃgavyājyamādarāt

bhagavadraśmividyātiraikaikāmātiṃ hūnet

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvesāṣṭhāṃganatipūrvakaṃ

atha dvādaśaniskaṃ cāpaṃ ca maniskamathāpī vā

trīniskaṃ vārpayet suvarṇaṃ niskamātraṃ vā tadardhvaṃ vā samarpayet

ācāryādāśīkhaḥ prāpyasvamyaṃmūnyītavāgyataḥ

mahatiṃ sīddhimāpnoti devānāṃmapīdurlabhāṃ

kṛtvā sīddhimāpnoti śāśvatī nātra saṃśayaḥ

itthaṃkartuḥ puraścaryāṃ sīddhivaktuṃ na śakyate

tatra kartupuraścaryāṃ sīddhivaktuṃ na śa

tatra kartuḥ puraścaryāṃ puraścaryā śatodbhavaḥ

śrīśrīśānugrahātrasyatrapautādyaipaśuratnā

rāma yathā puṣpaṃ cīraṃjīvanahenatukaṃ

kavitvādyāsīddhiḥ kavitvādyāsīddhitvaiva na saṃśayaḥ

bhagavatyadamaṃtesyādaśapūrverdaśāvarai

prātaḥ prātvā viśuddhātmā gāyatrīṃ triṃśataṃ japet

mithūnātriyaṃ tu bhojyaṃ mīthūnadvitiyaṃ tu vā

ācāryapatnīṃmāsādibhārgavevāsare vrati

niskaṃniskādharmekaṃ vā svarṇaṃ tasyai samarpayet

ācāryapatnyasānnidhye bhavatsannidhau nṛpa

tatputrāyātha vā dadyātatyautrāyātharvārpayet

kṛtvā samastavāre supūjyamīthūnānyatha

kuryādivyaṃ vṛdhimānyāvajjīvamathā vā

ramāmaraṇabrahmabhaṃ na patnyā vā na putrai vā na yujyeta vaṃdhubhīḥ

muktāmaṇīgaṇākīrṇaṃ gajavājīrathākulaṃ

mānuṣānapīdīvyāṃśca bhogānāpnoti śāśvatāścatān

kalyāṇaṃ jagatinityaṃ nītyaḥ śreyaḥ parāṃ parāṃ

******** keśavasya śrīyopī ca

bhagavadṛśmī vidyābhīkṣādaṃte brahmaṇilīyate hūtvaikaikāmathyate hūtiṃ

kumudhaimairvāpī bhojayedyugma paṃcakaṃ

ācāryapatnyaṃ datvāthasīddhiṃ prāpnotyathāthā

śreyaskāmasmadākuryāṃ brahmasāyujyasīddhidaṃ

dvāvevatārakauloke sarvāmadbhyo nīraṃtaraṃ

samastanyāsasahitāṃ śatādhīka sahasrakaṃ

śatādhika sahasraṃ ca prajaptvā saṃskṛtenale

******** śrīmadaṣṭottaraṃ hūnet

niskamekṛtarpa vā samarpacāryapādayoḥ

vyatipāteti kṛthvemāmekaṃ vāsarevratataṃ

mucyate mahāpāpaiścadāruṇaiḥ

avalaṃ mahadaiśvaryaṃ yogaṃ cāṣṭāṃgasaṃyutaṃ

saṃprāpya cākṣayaṃ saukhyāṃ dehāṃte bhagavatyadaṃ

śrīddhīdaśārṇamaṃtreṇa juhuyādaruṇāṃvujaiḥ

vailaiḥ phalairhunedyastu sahasratritayaṃ śuciḥ

sthūlairgavyaghṛtābhyaktai-jvalītavaidasai

hūtvā śrīdvādaśārṇaśetriśataṃ jūhuyādvṛtaṃ

arcayaitrīśataṃ puṣpaiḥ patraibrahmavidyayā hūtvā

triniskamathavā datvā gurupatnyai tataḥ param

ciṃtākābharaṇaṃ vāpīniskaṃvārthaṃ tadardhakaṃ

ācāryādāṣīśaḥ prāpyalakṣmīśavīdyayogamaṣṭāṃgasīhītaṃ lakṣmī

akṣayaṃ mahadaiśvaryaṃ dhanaratnaughasaṃkulai

athayaṃ yogamaṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ putrapautrakai

jātismaraściraṃ bhūtvā yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇahutvāṃ triśatamādarāt

ghṛtenāhutimekaikākṛtvā śrīpatiraśmibhiḥ

kṛtvācāryadadvāṃdveniṣkadritayadakṣiṇaṃ

samarpya tatpareṣuścabhojayenmithunāniva

sabhāsupūjyatasarvabhūpālānāṃ ca sannidhau

śrīmadaṣṭvārṇavidyāyāvidyāśrīdvādaśākṣarī

devatāpitṛkāyaiṣu saṃbhojyāḥ paḍiyāvanāḥ

ehiyāva naciprāgyānpravakṣyāmī mahīyate

pubhāti paṅktisāhasraṃ dvijānāmapi pāpinā

phunātyasauyaṅdhiśatapayipāpātmamānṛpa

nipaṃcadhāprajapannanvahaṃ paṅktiśataṃ sāgraṃ punātyapi

brahmabhūtaḥ paṅkti śatamapi nityaṃ punātyasau

apipaṅktiśataṃ sāgramassainityaṃ punātihi

brahmabhūtaḥ paṅktiśatayāvamosaubhavatyapi

punāti paṃkti sāhasramapi nityaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

punāti paṅkti sāhasrāmapithāyavatāḥmasau

sādhitvāmevanārīṇāṃ pavitratvādikāraṇaṃ

tebhya eva sadādeyaṃ daivepitrye ca karmaṇi

bhasmānīvahutaṃ vyarthaṃ bhavatvatra na saṃśayaḥ

aśrotriyebhyaḥ pradadātiyosauvyarthaṃ bhaventasya sarvaṃ hi dānaṃ

anmaṃ pradatvāpyānadhīta vipre prāprotyathāṃte narakusṭhaghoraṃ

rohitpannaṃ dīpāmānaṃ kiṃ mayāduḥhṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ

ācchādanairbhojanādyaiḥ pūjyā eva samṛddhaye

svarṇaṃ cihuṃkamātraṃ vāhutamācāyaryādayoḥ

śrīdvādaśārṇasahitāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubhā

ālārpacaraṇadvaṃdvedajasarvasvadakṣiṇaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyāyāṃ dvādaśārṇepi cāśriyaḥ

sa eva brahmavidyegītigrahānugrahakṣamaḥ

tameva pūjayennityaṃ ratnairathairenekaśaḥ

paścimaṃ nahiyajjanmanabhavedyomadaśajaḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvadṛḍhabhaktairnirataraṃ

dvādaśākṣiravidyāyāṃ mahālakṣmyāssadāratiḥ

śuddhakaivalyasahitapaścimaṃ janmanatsmṛtaṃ

itite kathirtalakṣmīhṛdayasyātha kathyate

ataḥ paraṃ pracākṣyāmi sāvadhānamanāḥ śūṇḍa

vyajanādyamathādvratyavaikuṃṭākṣaramapyathā

brahmavījaṃ mahīvījaṃ mukhavṛtavibhūṣitaṃ

prasīdayugalaṃ yaścādvrāṃtaṃ vahni samanvitaṃ

caturthasvaraviṃdvāṣyamaṃtastha paṃcamaṃ tataḥ

aṃtasthādyāmathoccārya śivaṃ dīrghavibhūṣitaṃ

savitṛsvarasaṃyuktaṃ tadapaṃcasamuddharet

samastapāyasaṃ hatrīsarvādhivyādhibhaṃjanī

lakṣmīhṛdayanāghrīyaṃ vidyāsakalasiddhida

tathaiva lakṣmīihṛdayajāturnityasukhaṃ bhavet

jñātvaitannpatrasaṃdehe nityaṃ kalyāṇabhāgasau

āyurārogyajananī sarvopadravaghātinī

satataṃ suprasanneyaṃ mahālakṣmīḥ phalapradā

tasmāttribhuvaneśānīvījaṃ śrīvījamapyatha

tasmaditatsamāvidyāna dṛṣṭā kutracit kvacit

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryavradā kaivalyasiddhidā

vidyomenāṃ samārādhyā prāhmavāmacalāṃ śriyaṃ

abhūtsamastavitānāṃ nāyakaḥ prabhurīśvaraḥ

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyairapi sarvatrapūjitā

agninādāhaśakṣitvaṃ nipatṛtvayamena ca

asyā evopāsanayā prāptaṃ pūrvaṃ mahātmabhiḥ

lakṣmītdayanāśnīyaṃ mahāvidhāna labhyate

lakṣmīhṛdayanāśrīyaṃmahāvidyānalabhyate

lakṣmītadapavidheyaṃ japtavyā vaśyamanvahaṃ

sevyaṃ bhajavānvahaṃ paścādvidyā śrīt dayāmidhā

śrīkāmibhirviprairnajapyeyaṃ mahīyate

japedetāṃ mahāvidyāṃ mahāsiṃhāsateśvarīṃ

lakṣmītadapavidyeyaṃ jahmavyāvasyamanvahaṃ

jasavyāvahuśonityaṃ vidyeyaṃ sarvasiddhīdā

ādhipatyaṃ ca devānāmicchatāni yatātmanā

sāmrājyalakṣmīvidyepaṃjapyate yena nityaśaḥ

bhūmau samastabhūpānāṃ sannidhāvanimānyatāṃ

yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ vā gatvācāryapadadvaye

saṃsevyavahudharṣaṃ ca vidhāmenāṃ śriyonṛpa

rājyalakṣmī mahāvidyāpradātaivamahānpitā

etatkālevarāṃtepitamācāryaṃ na hityajet

na śakyate mahīyālasastravadanairapi

sākṣānmāmevajānī hi sarvajñatyādilakṣaṇaṃ

ācāryameva satataṃ pūjayet sarvavastubhiḥ

na tathāvāti medhyānāṃ sahasreṇāpi bhūyate

tato ṣaṇena saṃtuṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaraṃ

āvāyārādhanaṃ hatvā manovāpkāryakarmabhiḥ

tasya lakṣmīyatirlakṣmyāsahanityaṃ prasīdati

asyā ārādhanenāśusaṃsidhyaṃti mahītale

tadgahadvāritiṣṭaṃti satataṃ kiṃ karāivi

rudrākrodhāgninādagdhaḥ punaḥ prācetasobhavet

trailokyasvargakaraṇasāmarthyaṃ ca parāṃ śriyaṃ

yameya trilokānāṃ mātāsarvatrapīmayī

yoginopi mahānāgāmunayopiyatavratā

aśvamegha samoyāgo vidyate vejjagattraye

pārijātena sadṛśo vidyate yadi bhūruha

gaṃgāsnānasahasrāṇi vājimedhaśatāni ca

sarvalokasya sāmrājyamapyayodhyāpuraśriyaṃ

ciṃtāmaṇimayaṃgahamapiralauthasaṃkulaṃ

kalpadrumagaśānvāpi pārijātavanānyapi

padyācārya haṭhābhaktiyāmvajjīvaṃ mahīpate

asyā ārādhanenāśusidhyalpasya na saṃśayaḥ

kaṃdarpasāhasrasamānakīrtitejasvitāṃ dhārmikatāṃ ca nityaṃ

avāpya divyānapi mānuṣāṃśca bhogānathāṃte bhagavanmayaḥ syāt

bhūtvā maheṃdrapratimānalakṣmīmavāpyamuktothā bhavatyavaśyaṃ

samastasiddhairapi pūjito sau prāpnotyaśeṣānapivāṃchitārthān

samastasaṃpatsamavāsiheturnānyāstivedeṣu purāṇasaṃghe

sāmrājyalakṣmīhṛdayaṃ prajaptvā kṛtārthatāmāpusnutamāṃ ca

sa eva sākṣājjagatāṃvidhātā nārāyaṇo sau bhagavān śivātmā

ācāryavataṃ paripūjayaṃti datvā samastānapi vīchitārthān

aiśvaryamakṣapyāmavāpyamavāpyapaścānnārāyaṇātmābhagavaṃtamiti

dakṣiṇamūrtināgītamarhaṣīṇāṃ ca sannidhau

sāmrājyākamalāhṛdayākhyāṃ mahāmanuṃ

putrapautrādisahitāsamṛddhirdhanasaṃkulā

jarāmaraṇarāṃhityemahadārogyamakṣayaṃ

lakṣmīhṛdayavidyāyājapahomatātmabhiḥ

bhṛguvāre ca puṣpārkagrahaṇādiṣu parvasu

vāsobhirapyalaṃkārairgurupatnīsamarcanāt

caṃdracūḍāmarṇoyogeravicū.āmaṇāvepi

arddhodayābhidheyogejapahomādikaṃ śubhaṃ

kṛtvācārya priyāyaścāddarvapitvā vibhūṣaṇaiḥ

rājyapradāyarājñe vā prāṇadāyāyivaṃdhave

lakṣmihṛdayavidyāyāṃ siddhayāṃ vasudhāyate

yadicchedātmanaḥ śreyasiddhimatyaṃ tadurlabhāṃ

pitṛmātṛgurudrohakāriṇe vā'thakāriṇe

prasannalakṣmīvidyeyaṃ na dātavyāmahīte

parāmukhosya bhagavānmahālakṣmī samanvitaḥ

atyaṃtadāruśāśāyaṃ pradāsyati jagatpatiḥ

ācaryeṇa pradātavyaṃ śubhemāliśubhedine

ācāryasya sakṛddhyāṃyimidhyābhāṣaṇa tatparaḥ

pinarnidāparo nityaṃ mūrkha ācāryarnidakaḥ

parityabjedamūnaśiṣyānapi srvasvadāyakān

prasannalakṣmīvidyeyaṃ dātavyātaḥ paraṃ śubhā

prasannalakṣmīvidyāyājapakartāramanvahaṃ

asurāṇī ca rājyaśrīḥśakrūṁaṃ ca durātmanāṃ

vedavedāṃga tatvajñodharmaśāstreṣuniṣṭitaḥ

iyaṃ samastavidyānāṃ sāmrājñī sarvasiddhidā

śṛṇu rājanmahīpālayacchatvāmucyate nṛpa

saṃdhyoyāstiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nityahomaṃ samāpya ca

prāṇānāyamyamūle nagāpatryāvātridhānṛpa

ṛṣirbrahmāsyamaṃtrasya trailokyādhimatirvibhuḥ

chaṃdogāyatramityuktamahāsiṃhāsaneśvarī

devatoktājaganmātāmahālakṣmīstrayīmayī

praṇavaṃkīlakaṃ prāhurathāṃgāni pravinyaset

śikhāyāṃ ca prasīdeti kavacaṃ ca tribhirnyaset

pṛthak aṃgulīnyāsamarpyaṃ kuryādbhapālasādhakaḥ

kalpadumasamākīrṇepārijātavamāvṛt

pārijātatarormūle navarātravinirmite

divyākalpaśatākīrṇakālāṃbhodharasannibhaṃ

śrīvatsavakṣaṃsaṃdivyapītāṃvaradharaṃ vibhuṃ

tasyotsaṃgesamāsīnāṃte pūjāvūnadaprabhāṃ

vilasatyamarāgādi divyaratnavinirmitaṃ

atha sīmaṃta siṃdūrasahitāṃ caṃcalālikāṃ

muktāratnasamāyuktavālīyugalaśobhitāṃ

kāmakodaṃḍasaṃkāśabhūdvayena virājitāṃ

muktāmaṇigaṇākīrṇanāsābharaṇaśobhitāṃ

dājimīvījalasadaṃtāvalivirājitāṃ

sukurṇamaparcitākamuktāhāchibhūpitāṃ

muktāmaṇigaṇakīrṇaṃcukenavirājitā

kalpabhūruhasaṃbhūta kusuprasavibhūṣitī

suvarṇakaśākārapīnīnnadyanastanī

śānodī pṛthuśroṇīṃ nimnanabhisamanvitāṃ

kadāphalasaṃkāśajaṃ yugalābhūṣoṣitāṃ

suvarṇaratnakhacitahasrapādāṃgulīyakāṃ

muktāsuvarṇaralaudyapūritaṃ vasupātrakaṃ

caturbhujāṃ sphuradratnanūpurāṃbhūṣaṇejvalā

virājitapaddadvaṃdvāṃ bhaktatrāṇaparāyaṇaṃ

ratnaughavarṣiṇamūrdhnidhṛtachatreṇadhīmatā

dhṛtanāmaravaryeṇakāmenā ca sutena ca

sarasvatyāgirijayāśarcamukhyābhirarcitāṃ

purato nṛtyamānābhirdivyakanyabhirāvṛtāṃ

sarvasaubhāgyajananīmaṇimādibhiṇavṛtāṃ

samastasaṃpadādhārāṃ sarvavidyādhināyikī

karuṇārasasaṃpūrṇaṃ locanāṃ parameśvarīṃ

sarvasammohanakarīṃ sarvākarṣaṇakāriṇīṃ

samastaiśvaryasaṃsiddhihetubhūtāṃ sanātanī

sarvatatvamayīṃ bhogabhogasvargapavargado

sarvadhamaṃmayī sarvatīrthadaivatarūpiṇīṃ

nārāyaṇakanilayāṃ mahālakṣmīmupāsmahe

āthavābhagavadrūpadhāracakrādi brahmāṃraṃdhrāṃtamīśvarīṃ

tejomayīdarādhaṣīṃ brahmāṇḍavyāpinī parāṃ

athavā bhagavadrayamāvāryaṃbhāveyedviyā

sākṣādācāryapatnī vā mahālakṣmīdhiyānvahaṃ

anenaiva pramāṇena sākṣādācāryamīśvaraṃ

tasmādavaśyamācāryaṃ patnīputrādibhirvṛtaṃ

yaktāsvapitaraupatnīputrabhrātrādikānapi

naratnahematuraṃgaudyasaṃkulaṃ śriyamicchatā

dhyātvetthamahanvahāṃ viprovidyāratnamabhoṣṭadaṃ

saumyaṃ paramaṃ prāpyādhanaṃ prāpnotirvāchitaṃ

acalāṃ labhate śīghraṃ dhanadhātmākulāṃbhiyaṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvādaśadhāpi vā

asaubhāgyavinirmuktaḥ kāmavatsubhago bhavet

dhanadhānyādisaṃpūrṇaṃ muktāratnau samāṃkulāṃ

kaṃdarpasadaśaḥ kāṃtyā bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

gavyājya manalehutvā nityaṃ dvādaśadhāpi vā

sahasravājisahitaṃ mahadaiśvaryamaṣṭute

putrapautravatīmṛddhiṃ prāpyārogyamathākṣayaṃ

sābhāsu pūjyate nityaṃ vāksiddhiṃ cātulāṃ labhet

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā gavyājyaṃ jvalinenale

bhūtvā rājasahasrāṇanārīṇāṃ prapivallabhaḥ

prāpnotyāśu mahālakṣmīmacalāṃratha saṃkulāṃ

gaṃgāpravāhavatasya vāgvibhūtirvijṛṃbhet

vaikuṃṭerametatedivyakanyāśatānvitaḥ

saṃprāpya virajīvalvamahadārogyamakṣayaṃ

aṃprāpyaśaṃkhayamadinidhonāmadhiyo bhavet

kalyāṃtamapi rājyaśrīstaṅgahanavimuṃcati

durlabhabhuvimartyānāṃ sarvakalyāṇasiddhidaṃ

vaidikādhāraniratovidyārallamidaṃ japat

jātasyanigrahakuryāt kāmasyātargatasya ca

śayīta śuddhaśayyāyāṃtarūṇyā sahabhāyayā

ācāryapatnīśrīṣudhyāpūjayedvranasaṃcaryaiḥ

śrīśrīpatidhiyābhūyamithunānyeva bhojayet

kamalaṃ vilvapatraṃ ca tulasīdalama

avaśyaṃnityakarmāṇi kuryāddedyānvahaṃ gṛho

ācāryāyāryayeddhīmānnityasaṃyatsamṛddhaye

śrīvidyā ca tathālakṣmīrmahālakṣmīstataḥ paraṃ

khaṃḍatritayasaṃyuktāvidyā paṃcadaśākṣarī

ekākṣarī sadālakṣmīrmahāsāmrājyadāyinī

mahālakṣmītivikhyātā sarvasāmrājyanāyikā

ādāvaṃte śriyo vījasahitāṣṭākṣarī śubhā

icchatā sarvasāmrājyalakṣmī trailokyadurlabhāṃ

bhidhekartevyāvidhināphajānavarātravratādiṣu

tāṃvūlaṃ natyajeddhīmāsajedvāsaścakutsitaṃ

ācāryaputrepyācāryadhiyapūjanamācaret

iṣṭāprāśvānayoniśataṃ gatvā caṃḍālekṣabhijāyate

paranārīṣu saṃsarga'kulepatanahetavaḥ

tulasīvilvavṛkṣaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā praṇatimācaret

itthamācāryaniratoviṣṇupūjana tatparaḥ

kollāpurana mahālakṣmīślokāṣṭakamathāpi vā

bhārgaveṇa purādṛṣṭhaṃ ślokarūpaṃ yaśaskaraṃ

aṣṭādaśākṣarī vidyāmathavā pratyahe japet

lakṣmīhṛdayavidyāyāḥ sīddhikāmedinedine

yacchutvapinaraudhīmātsarvāpabhyo vimucyate

kiṃjalkeṣu dineśapatravilasanmaṃtrākṣaraṃ tadvahiḥ

dharāvirbhvābhyāṃ-vaṣadurtayātvaritayāyatraṃ likhedveṣitaṃ

mahālakṣmīyaṃtramirdasarvaiśvaryaphalapradaṃ

kimatra vahunoktena paramasmānnāvidyate

ādāvagāni saṃpūjyavahnīśāraturadikṣu ca

vāsudevaṃ saṃkarṣaṇī pradyumnamaniruddhakaṃ

gāyatrīrpārvatīṃcāṃdrīśavīṃ dikṣu prapūjayet

vidikṣu pūjayedetānsarvakāmārthāsiddhaye

anurāgovisaṃvīhovijayovallabhomadaḥ

śakrāhīnaṣṭhadikpālān caturasrāṣṭhadikṣu ca

arcākramāṃtaramathovakṣyami śṛṇu bhūpate

pūjayedakṣiṇeyāśvedevyāḥ śaṃkaranaṃdanaṃ

aṃgāni upūrvamukteṣusyāneṣu vidhivadyajet

umābhūḥ śrīsarasvatyaudurgādevīratistataḥ

jaṅgusūryasutebhyarvyepādaprakṣālanopate

dhṛtānapatravaṃruṇarvayetpaścime tataḥ

arghayedimājān dikṣu caturdaṃtavibhūṣitān

puṣkadaṃtaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ supratīkaśvate kramāt

āgamoktena vidhināstugaṃdhaiḥ sumanoharaiḥ

itthamabhyarcya vidhivat prasannāṃ kamalālayā

āyuṣyā mahadārībhyaṃ prāpyakaivalyamaśrute

sugumamapi vakṣyāmi yaṃtrarājaṃ mahīyate

aṣṭhamaṃcatalekhyaṃdaśāradvitaya tataḥ

ṣoḍaśāraṃ tato lekhyaṃ punarvṛta samanvitaṃ

ayodhyāpuranāmedaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇabhidhaṃ

mātṛkālekhanaṃ kuryādvidhānenātrabhūyate

ayodhyānagaraṃ nāmnā mahāsāmrājyasiddhidaṃ

navayonyātmakaṃ cakraṃ śrīcakraṃ ceti kathyate

tripurālalitānāmnā mahālakṣmīrnigadyate

nava cakrātmakīmidaṃ sarvatsaramaryaśubhaṃ

trailokyamohanaṃ caktaṃ caturaśratrayaṃ smṛtaṃ

sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇaṃ cakramaṣṭayatradvaryaviduḥ

vahirdaśāraṃ kathitaṃ sarvasaubhāgyadāyakaṃ

sarvarogaharaṃ cakramaṣṭāramiti kathyate

sarvānaṃdamayaṃ cakraṃ vai devapāramaiśvaraṃ

trailokyākārṣaṇākaraṃ jagadgajanakārakaṃ

sarvasaubhāgyadaṃbha i samastai"varyasiddhidaṃ

sarvasaṃgrāmajapadaṃ sarvaśatruvimardanaṃ

sarvaduḥkhapramanaṃ sarādhivyādhināśanaṃ

atra sā lalitāsākṣātpūjyate kamalālayā

pūjātratrividhāprākādevavedāṃtavedibhiḥ

bhogāṃgākhyā tṛtīyāsyāditi pūjātridhāmatā

trividhārdhātrakartavyā navarātravrātādiṣu

kathitāpīṭapūjeyaṃ nityasaubhāgyasiddhidā

upacarya ṣaḍaṃgānāṃ nityāmapi pūjanaṃ

aṇimā siddhimārabhya śrīdevīpūjanītimaṃ

trividhā cātukurvāṇe māsamekaṃ tu vā nṛpa

caturviṃśati saṃkhyākairūpacārairathāditaḥ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇai savamithunāvaraṇanvitau

asmintaṃtre mayā prokte vedasārārthasaṃgrahe

cakrasya pūjāvistorodvitīyeśe pravakṣyate

atra sā kathyate vidyā caturvargaphalapradā

daśadhāpi smarannūyanityaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt

sarvavedamapraproktaṃ sarvadvamayataṃthā

yena prapūjyate nityaṃ sa eva bhagavanmayaḥ

godāvavarī bhīmararthākāverīkṣaṣyaveṇikā

cakrarājasya sannidhye nivasaṃminaṃ saṃśayaḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ dṛḍhabhakto yatavrataḥ

laghukracakraṃ vṛhaccakramayodhyā nagaraṃ tu vā

likhitaṃ kāṃcaneraupyetāmre vā bhūrjapatrake

etādṛśīmahavidyosarvasākrājyasiddhidā

saṃpadassarvadevānāmapi yogaratātmanaṃ

kinnaraṇāṃ ca siddhānāmasurāṇāṃ ca bhūyate

saptadvīparghātaṃ tvaṃ vā gajāśvarayasaṃkulā

siddhatyasya na saṃdehaḥ prasanna śrīasādataḥ

śṛṇu rājanpravakṣyāmi samāhitamanāḥ śṛṇu

na siddhiḥ kathyate loke siddhiṃ kuryātataḥ pumān

na siddhiḥ sarvamaṃtrārṇakathyate puruṣarṣabha

pūrṇadakṣiṇayāyuktā sa sadyassiddhimaśrutaṃ

na tādṛśaphalaṃdāna sahasreṇapi labhyate

viśvātmāyajñapuruṣe ekaeveti kathyate

patnībhagavataḥ sargasthitisaṃhārakāriṇī

upāsya bhagavadrūpaṃ saṃprāpnoti hi nānyathā

brahmatādātmyasaṃsiddhiḥ śrīśrīśopasanavinā

nārāyaṇaṃ trijagatāṃpatimacyutamīśvaraṃ

mahālakṣmīmupāsyātha brahmatādātmyasiddhibhak

bhaktāḥ putrā iti jñeyāḥ śriyaścakamalāyate

mātaraṃ sarvajagatāṃ mahālakṣmīharipriyāṃ

ta eva bhagavatputrāmahālakṣmyāḥ sutā api

prāsannakamalāvidyāsiddhāyasyamahītale

prasannalakṣmīvidyāyāssidhyaniyatavrataḥ

sadyassiddhimavāpnoti mahatīṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

puraścaryā kṛtoyanagulīlaghī ca vā nṛpa

ācāryaḥ pūjyate yatra puraścaraṇakarmaṇi

tatpuraścaraṇaṃ sāṃgaṃ sadyaḥ siddhipradaṃ bhavet

koṭiśo vipuraścaryā niṣphalā sā prakīrtitā

ācāryapatnīmathavā tatpautramathavārcayet

arcayedbhātaraṃ tasya pautrābhāvemahīpate

sannidhāvarpayitvātha satpātrāya samarpayet

vidhināpi kṛtānīhamahānānibhūriśaḥ

śuddheti yāvane deśepuraścaṇamācaret

yatra sālākanaṃndābhūrnigatāhimavadgireḥ

tīrtharājaḥ prayāgotrayamahāsiddhidharaḥ smṛtaḥ

ayodhyāmathurāmāyākāṃcikāvaṃtikāpurī

gokaṇanaiviṣam puṇyaṃ kurukṣetramataḥ paraṃ

prabhāsaṃsiṃdhutīraṃ ca padmanābhamataḥ paraṃ

satdyāvalomaheṃdraśca malayaḥ śrīgiristathā

kubhakoṇaṃ setuvaṃdhodakṣiṇemathurāpurī

atha siṃhācalādīnipurāyakṣetrāṇibhūtale

gaṃgātīraṃ sarasvatyāyamunāyāstaṭaṃ paraṃ

suvarṇamukharītīraṃ sa godāvarītaṭaṃ

kṛṣṇaveṇītaṭaṃ śuddhesadyassiddhipradāyakaṃ

uktānyetanilokeṣu sadyasmiddhikarāsmitvi

matyadepi na siddhiḥ syādvinācāryasya sannidhi

tadeva sarvavidyānāṃ siddhidaṃtramīritaṃ

prasannalakṣmavidyāyaḥ puraścaṇāmācaret

vipradrohamasūryā ca niṣiddhānnasyabhakṣṇa

ācāryaniṃdāpatrasyātadeśaṃ ca parityajet

bhātaraṃ svasutaṃvapi patnīputrīmathāpi vā

ācāryaniṃdākartāraṃ yadiśakte na daṃḍayet

kiṃcānena kṛtaṃ yāgaśataṃ saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ

brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ manvādyaiḥ paramarṣibhiḥ

ācāryaniṃdāpatra syātaṃ tyajedaṃtyajaṃ yathā

saptadvīpapavatī pṛthvīdhanadhānyaudyasaṃkulāṃ

niṣkamātraṃ pratijñātamapyācāryāpabhūpate

brahmāṃḍakoṭidānāpātrabhūtebhya kṣādarāt

māsānmāsadvayādvāpi tribhirmāsairathāpi vā

tadarṇabdaddhisahitasyavāṃ

na smādācāryayādābja dṛḍhabhaktoyatavrataḥ

sidhyarthaṃ brahmavānādaupuraścaraṇamācaret

daivatairapi pūjyaḥ syādvidyāsiddhomahītale

bhuṃjānovāhaviṣyānnamekabhaktāśanopi vā

paṃcagavyāśano vāpi nityasrāpī jiteṃdriyaḥ

adhaḥśāyī brahmacārī sahoṣṭhīniratassadā

ācāryasannidhī kṛtvasādhayedgavatpriyāṃ

lakṣadvayaṃ syāttretāyāṃ trilakṣadvāpareyuge

kṛte yuge sadā viprāvedādhyayatatatparāḥ

gurvarthemeva satatamarjayaṃti dhanaṃ vaddha

agnihotroratā nityaṃ yajvānodhūri dakṣiṇaḥ

prāṇairathairthimācāpi svācāryārcana tatparāḥ

tasmallakṣadvayaṃ proktaṃ tretāyāṃ siddhaye śriyaḥ

tasmāllakṣatrayaṃ proktaṃ yugedvāparanāmake

śrautācāravihīnāśca mithyā bhāṣaṇatasyādāḥ

ācāryaniṃdāśravaṇaritāḥ

svayaṃ naṣṭhāḥ parāḥ srūṣṇīṃ nāśayaṃti pratārakāḥ

daṣṭācāryarpinaṃdaṃti maṃtronāstyapadaivataṃ

bhagavanmayamācāryaṃ bhogasvargāpavargadraṃ

pitaraṃ mārararmapityajaṃtyeva kalau yuge

prāpte kaliyugedyeriyugyadharmo mahīyate

datyajaṃtimatsaragrastā ācāryahohakāriṇaḥ

vedikācāryatāṃtrikācārajatparāḥ

svapūjārthamathācāryāsapiśāstreṣvapāragāḥ

nyāsayaṃtradhyānasūktamajñātvāpi mahāmanūn

itthaṃ kaliyuge ghore mahāpātakisaṃkule

ekesti bhagavadyiṃtātasarovijiteṃdriyaḥ

ācāryacaraṇardvadvaśuśrūṣākṣīṇakelmaṣāḥ

kaveḥ pātakavāhulpyāraprasannaśrīmahāmanoḥ

cāturlakṣayaṃ kṛtvā evokti vidhināśuciḥ

tadaśāṃśaṃ hunedājyamadhutritayamiśritaiḥ

suvāsinīśataṃbhīrjyaviprāṇāṃśatamapyatha

ācāryacaraṇadvādhanarāśisaṃmayet

amarairapi pūjyaḥ syācchiyaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā

śrūyate dūrato vā vārtasaśatayojanagāpi ca

kaiṃkaryartasya kurveti maheṃdraghamarā api

svayamevāsya vaktrābjāntirgadyatinasaṃśayaḥ

dadāti vaḍgalaṃ dradhyayāvadīpsitamakṣayaṃ

nāṣṭākṣarī samāvidyāsadaśīramayānapā

mahavīyaṃ puraśvaryāsāṃgāsaṃpūryādakṣiṇā

alpāpuṣpānmanuṣyāṇāṃ cāṃcalyānmanasopi ca

iḍuvāsarasaṃyuktāpauṇīmāsīpadābhavet

ravivārayutāmāvāyogoyaṃ padmakaḥ smṛtaḥ

caṃdracūḍamaṇiḥ prokto ravicūḍāmaṇistathā

ekabhaktāśanonityamadhaḥśāpī jiteṃdriyaḥ

vārṇanyāsaṃyutāṃ vāpi śatādhikasahasrakaṃ

triśatāvika sāhasraṃ japtvā śrīhṛdayastavaṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhāṅgatvātedamātrādyajaṃgavāḥ

prasannabhagavatpatnī-vidyayāṣyetaraṃ śataṃ

caṃdraparvaṇisaṃprāpteravipavīṇivāpyatha

vidhivat saṃstūtevṛhauvaṃdamātrādyataṃ garvā

aṣṭottaraśataṃ ūtvā vilvapatrairghṛtapnutaiḥ

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtreṇa śatādhika sahasrakaṃ

prāyāścitāṅgānī kṛtvā prāṇamyāgnimataṣparaṃ

arpayet sakalaṃ dravyamapi svagṛha saṃsthitaṃ

athavā kuṃḍalayumaniṣkadvādaśasaṃyutaṃ

karṇalaṃkāramanyaṃ vā yāvacchakyaṃ samarpya ca

ṣaṇnikamtha vā ityāniṣkatrayamitaṃ tu vā || 113 |||

yathā śatmāsemābhyarcya vāsolaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

anaṃtaramathācāryapūjāyāvasudhāyota

datvā bhukteṣu vipreṣu dāsaṇī ca samarpayet

ācāryānugrahātsadyassiddhimāprovyanuttamāṃ

ācāryāya mahīdaṃdyādvahusya samanvitāṃ

prasannalakṣmīvidyāyāssidhyarthamatha vā nṛpa

pūrvokta dakṣidadyādvāsolaṃkārasaṃyutāṃ

ācāryacaraṇardvadve tata dakṣapātāṃ vrajet

cūḍāmani prasannalakṣmīvidyāyāssiddhiṃ sa labhate parāṃ

acalāṃściyamāpnoti śāśvatīmanapāpinī

saubhāgyaṃ mahadāsādyasamaddhiyaṃtrapautrakaiḥ

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāṃ grāhaṇe ravicaṃdrayoḥ

jātismaraściraṃ bhūtvā dhanadhānyājākulā

putrapautravātī mūrddhividhī sumahatīmapi

ateva sati vaikuṃṭhe kulasāhasrasaṃyutaḥ

ekaikaṃ divasaṃ tatra cūḍāmaṇi samaprabhaṃ

prasannalakṣmīvidyeyaṃ tatrāvaśyaṃ phalapradā

mahatī navamī tatrāyā proktā paramarṣibhiḥ

bhaguvāsarasaṃyuktā vaiśvadaivatyasaṃyutā

ācāryājñāmanuprāpyaprathame pratiyadine

abhyasyatriśataṃ vāpi vidhivājjvalitenale

śrīmadaṣparṇayāyaśvādaṣṭāviṃśatidhā hunet

divase divasepītyaṃ homakarmasamāpyā ca

sāmrājya śrīmahāvidyāmetāṃ paṃcaśatādhikaṃ

yadvā dvādaśasāhasraṃ saṃkalpya navasatrake

ātharvaṇaṃ mahuḍgardyalakṣmī hṛdayamiṣadaṃ

bhojyāḥ suvāsinyo mahālamīdhiyātṛpa

aṣṭaviṃśatidhāṅgatvā vedamātrāpuroghṛtaṃ

sāmrājyalakṣmīmāvādyasamabhyarcyopacārakaiḥ

triśatādhikasāhasramiṃdirāviyāhunet

vilvapatrasasāyuktaṃ tilaiḥ kṛṣneḥ samanvitaṃ

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtraiścallokarūpairataḥ parama

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtraiścallokarūpairataḥ pasra tataḥ paraṃ

japādihomaṃ kṛtvātha prāyaścitāhutastataḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇipatya ca

godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāyuktaṃ samarpyācāryapādayoḥ

sahasraṃ śataniṣkaṃ vā tadarthaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

paṃcaniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā triniṣkavātha sadgurāḥ

samarpyācārya patnyai ca vāsa ākalpasaṃcayaṃ

ācāryasannidhau tasya patnī putrādyasannidhau

ācāryapatnīputrāṇehṛtaddhanaṃ

caturvaṣabhasaṃyuktaṃ kuṃḍaladvitayānvitaṃ

bhojyājathasuvāsinyodvādaśa brāhmaṇā api

prasannalaptamī vidyāyāmahatīsiddhimaśrute

kṛtvakāryaśataṃ vāpi dravyaṃ pādyayatnataḥ

sapūrṇadakṣiṇānaṃ kāryaṃ siddhimabhīpsatā

puraścaryāśataphalaṃ sāṃgamāpnoti niścitaṃ

yādṛśī jāyate siddhirnavarātravrate śubhe

navarātravrataśaṃśopasyapuṃsaḥ prajāpate

navarātravrataṃ kṛtvā vidhinā sāṃgamādarāt

mahṛdārogyamāsādya dhanadhānyaudyasaṃkulāṃ

acalāṃ labhate lakṣmī trailokopisudurlabho

vrasatyeva na saṃdeho dhandhānyādi saṃkulā

japtvā paṃcaśataṃ protaṣṭāviṃśamidhāghṛtaṃ

itthamācarataḥ puṃsobhāgyarāśiḥrmadimane

jāyate dhanadhānyāśvagajaralasamākulā

kaikaryaṃ prāpnuvaṃtyasya sarvenapavarā api

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāmanvahaṃ prātarādarāt

tatrā calājayaśrīḥ syāt sanmānaṃ pauruṣaṃ yaśaḥ

vaṃśābhivṛddhirācaṃdramārkumasya bhavedvudhaṃ

asādhyāmāpi devānāmapi dvīpāṃtarasthitaṃ

kulasāhasramuddhatyamagraṃ ca narakārṇave

vāsaṃte navarātrasya puraścaraṇamādarāt

atha vakṣyami bhūpālasādhanātarami

śrāvaṇekārtikevāpi mārgaśīrṣetathaśvine

prathame śuklapakṣasya svācāryājñāpurassaraṃ

prajahvatriśataṃ vāpi jvalite jātavedasi

śrīmaraṣṭārṇamaṃtreṇāpyaṣṭhāviṃśātidhāṅganat

hutyāgavyaghṛtaṃ śuddhaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇau yajet

triśatādhika sāhasramiṃdirāhṛdayaṃ japet

mithunadvitayaṃ vāpi yugmamekamathāpi vā

prasannaśrīmahāvidyāmaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet

śreyaskāmojecchucchaḥ pratibhārgavavāsaraṃ

śrāvaṇe pūrṇimoyāṃtabhārgavevāsare śubhe

hutvā triśatamājyena pūrvavat prajapetataḥ

niṣkatrayaṃ dviniṣkaṃ vā niṣkamekamathāpi vā

tatrāpi triśataṃ hutvā lakṣmī hṛdayavidyayā

mārgaśīrṣe pauṇaimāsyāṃ lakṣmīhṛdayavidyayā

pūrvavadakṣiṇaṃ dadyāt svācārya caraṇadvaye

itthaṃ kṛtvā bhṛgorvārevratamatyaṃdapāvana

prāpnoticirasmāpuṣpaṃsamastāssaṃpadopi ca

avāpya mahatīlakṣmīsaṃtativyāpinīmapi

divyāṃjanamavāpnoti siddhidarśanahetukaṃ

bhogānaṣṭavidhāścapi samāsādhāti durlabhān

ihāmutrāpi saṃsiddhimicchatāniyatātmanā

anuttamaṃ yaśolābhaṃ japalakṣmīmacaṃcalāṃ

viśeṣeṇa vrataṃ kāryaśreyovṛddhikaraṃ nṛpa

śaktaśca navarātrādivrataṃ sakalamācaret

prasīdati na saṃdehassatyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyahaṃ

pūrvamuktāmayābhūyasāhāsiddhidathinī

ipsitā nāmasādhyānāmapi siddhistato bahvet

grahaṇena samaḥ puṇyakālonāstijarīttraye

na saṃtyājyamiti prāhumunayo brahmavadinaḥ

samastadānaṃ saṃghānāṃ mahākālassa eva hi

japahomādikaṃ sarvata trākṣayaphaladaṃ

graṣuṇeṣu puraścaryākāryāsīgāsadakṣiṇā

samastāriṣṭaśamanaṃ varṣe varṣe na saṃśayaḥ

navarātravrate vaśyaṃ bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

bhṛguvāravrataṃ proktamavaśya sarvasiddhidaṃ

catvāriṃśopamādhyāya uktassarvayaśaskaraḥ

sādhayetkāmyakarmāṇi samastāni mahīyate

lakṣamekaṃ prajaptvaimāṃ vidyāṃ sakalasiddhidāṃ

hutvā tatra samāpnoti dhanaratnaudyamakṣayaṃ

madhutritayagavyājyasaṃplutairmaṃḍaladvayaṃ

sākṣātsvapnepi vā paśya sau vigrahadhāriṇīṃ

jāyate tadgahe sadyo mahatīdurvabhāpi ca

aruṇaṃbhohahairnityamaṣṭottaraśataṃtraya

viramāpuṣpamāsādyasaubhāgyamacalaṃ śubhaṃ

caṃpakaijuhuyānnityamaṣṭottaraśatatrayaṃ

maṃḍalenīnasaṃdehaṣkaṃdarpasamakāṃtimān

rājyalakṣmīmavāpnoti svānvāpavyayinīṃ nṛpa

maṃḍalaṃ jvalite vahno hutvānniśatamādarāt

sāṃgāssamastavidyāścanirgacchaṃti mukhāṃvujāt

nirāmayo sau dīrghāprabhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

vaśīkaroti bhūpālānatha tatkiṃkarānapi

hutvā yutaṃ vipraḥ paśyatyeva haripriyāṃ

guḍucīrājyasaṃsiktā juhuyāt saptavāsaraṃ

hutvā tilairghṛtābhyaktaidīrghamādyuravāpnuyāt

śālībhirjuhvatonityamaṣṭottaraśatatrayaṃ

prasūnairjuhuyānmaṃtrīllakṣmīvallīsamudbhavaiḥ

mahatī śriyamāpnoti mānyate sarvajaṃtubhiḥ

samṛdvairājyasaṃyuktairapūvairījyatolataiḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityaṃ maṃḍalādvanadobhavet

japāpuṣpāṇi juhuyādaṣṭottarasahasrakaṃ

tilakaṃtanupātena sarvavaśyakaraṃ paraṃ

jātīpuṣpaiyātāścavaiśyānaraktaut-palaiḥ śubhaiḥ

puṣpairmanohau hutvāvaśamānayatistriyaḥ

abhikaṃ prakurvīta vidhināsarvasiddhaye

śubhalakṣaṇān caṃdanāliptasarvāṃgānkūrcākṣarasamanvitān

sudhātīrthavaribhirāpūryasugaṃdhibhirataḥ paraṃ

madhyakuṃbhokṣityapreyaṃtrāvyaṃdeśikottamaḥ

kuṣṭhāgarutamālailābhāgaṃ saṃghṛṣyabhāgtaḥ

lakṣmīharvāsadābhadrāsahadevīmadhuvratā

gumudgagodhūmavrīhoścaivatilānupavān

dhānīpikucavilvānokadalīnārikelayoḥ

padmasaugaṃdhikajāti mallikāvakulaṃ tathā

pallavānivadyeśvatyahyakṣoduṃvaraśākhilāṃ

kuṃbhavaktāṇi vastrairācchādaye tataḥ

yajedumādhāḥ śiṣṭeṣṭhakaleśeṣṭaṣṭasukramāt

naivedyabhakṣyabhojyadyaistānspṛṣṭā prajayenmanuṃ

trisahasrājapasyāṃtai sādhyamānīyedeśikaḥ

ramyairābharaṇaivastrairalaṃkṛtyatamādarāt

arcitānāṃ dvijātīnāmāśīrvādapurassaraṃ

madhyasthaṃ kuṃbhamudvatmamahālakṣmīmanusmaran

vā karūktamukhyaiḥ sūktaiścavaichavaiḥ

keraṇasyaśiraḥsyaṣkā prayuṃjītāṣiśaṣauguruḥ

prasīduturakṣatutvāṃ sādādevī saṃpadassatusarvadā

athotthāyābhiṣaktaḥ sanvāsasoparidhāya ca

gaṃdhādyairapyalaṃ kṛtya svātmānaṃ sadhakastataḥ

dāsīdāsaiśca vidhivatoṣayediṃdirādhiyā

tadākṛtarthamātmābhaṃ bhāvayedgurvanugrahāt

padecchuḥ padamāpnotirījapujonasaṃśayaḥ

mahārogeṣu jāteṣu kṛtyāṃdeheṣu deśikaḥ

sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ puṃsāṃ sarvasaubhāgyasiddhidaṃ

abhiṣekamimaṃ prāhurāgamārthaviśyādāḥ

māhadāpuṣparogyagejān ratnānivājinaḥ

athāparamahālakṣmī hṛdayasyāsya sādhanaṃ

vaiśākhemāsi saṃpropte amiṇupha siddhide

ācāryasyājñayā prātasrātvā saṃdhyāyussara

aṣṭāviṃśatidhāhutvādedamātrāghṛtaṃ garvā

prasannarājyalakṣmyāthayanasasyaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ

triśatādhikasāhasrajuhuyānniyatavrataḥ

pratyekaṃ pānasairhutvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ phalaiḥ

ekaikaśastatohutvā prāyaścitāhutīstataḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ saṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇipatyatya ca

padmarāgasamāyuktaṃ pradatvācāryakarṇayoḥ

atha vā karṣemānenataptahe āvinirmitaṃ

ācāryavallabhākarṇayugmaidadyānāhīpate

datvā śrīcaṃdanaṃ sūkṣmavāso yugasamanvitaṃ

daṃdyādācārya sānnidhye vahudakṣiṇayānvitaṃ

paṃcaviṃśoyugaśatinirṣkavāniṣkadvādaśakaṃ tu vā

brāhmaṇanbhojayet paścatpaṃcavāpi suvāsinīḥ

tauṣayedakṣiṇabhiśca brāhmaṇānvasudhāyate

ācāryāliṃganaṃ prāpyatadāśībhiḥ kṛtādaraḥ

māsmādarvādgasaṃdeho brahmarudrāvivaṃditāṃ

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryaṃ jātismaraṇamuttaṃ

śriyamāsādyabhuktvābhogāśca durlabhān

bhūyo'pisādhanaṃ vakṣye mahāsiddhipradāyaka

śāpādurvāsaptonaṣṭaśriyobhagyavivarjitāṃ

samudramadhanaṃ cakruramṛtonmathanāpate

api yatra vacatāṃ teṣāṃ nāvirāsītrapīmayī

surāsurānirāhārālakṣmīhṛdayanāmakaṃ

samudramathanaṃ cakrurharīnāya mahāśriyaḥ

āvirāsīmmahālakṣmīssasāmudrājjagadaṃvikā

sahasrapatrakamalamālyayuktā trayīmapī

dṛṣṭvā bhagavato vakṣyasthalaṃ prāyasanātānī

samastasaṃpadādhārā abhabhavanvasudhāyet

śṛṇudhvaṃ brahmarudrādyāḥ śakrādyāśca saharṣayaḥ

nāmnā kārtikamāsesminyomāmartṛsamanvitāṃ

yosminmajjanmadivasobhāmarccayati suvrataḥ

vasāmi maṃdire tasye dhanaratnaughasaṃkulā

rahasyabhayi vakṣyāmi sarvasiddhikaraṃ paraṃ

ārabhyācāryayādābjadvitayaṃ praṇipatya ca

sahasraṃ triśataṃ vāpi prajaptvā vedamātaraṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā vedamātrāghṛtaṃ juvāṃ

ājyaṃ prasannāramapādunedaṣṭottaraṃśataṃ

saṃkalpyatadaśamyaṃtaṃ japedekakāmamānasaḥ

saṃkalpyārka sahasrāṇi daśamyaṃtaṃ japecchiviḥ

dvādaśākṣaralakṣmī vā śrīṣaḍarṇamathāpi vā

ekādaśīdinaiyaścādācryājñāpurassaraṃ

ācāryacaraṇādvamdvaṃ sākṣyaṃgaṃ praṇipatyaca

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ kamalairvākuśeśayaiḥ

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtreṇa juhuyāt sādhakottamaḥ

padmābhavevilvadalairgavyājyamadhumiśritaiḥ

tulasīmiśritairvilvadalaiśvakusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ

triśatādhikasāhasrāmāvītadbrahmāvidyayā

brāhmemuhūrte utthāya srātvātha dvādaśīdine

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā gāyatryāghṛtamādarāt

aṣṭottaraḥ śataṃ paścatpadmairmadvidyayā hunet

bhagavadraśmibhiḥ yaścodakaikāmāhuniṃ hunet

athācāryadadvaṃdvaṃ sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇipatya ca

padmarāgasamāyuktaṃ kuṃḍaladvayamarpayet

vāso yugmaṃ samarpyātha kṣaumaṃ kāpī sameva vā

savatsagaudvayapunāṃvahudākṣmiṇayānvitāṃ

dhanāḍhyorājaputraśca dadyāt sāhasradakṣiṇāṃ

paṃcaniṣkacaturniṣkaniṣkavayamitaṃ tu vā

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcyavāsolaṃkaraṇadibhiḥ

dakṣiṇābhiścatāṃ kūlaistauyeddevatādhiyā

kātikenasi kṛtvetthaṃ vratamatyatapovanaṃ

mahatīsiddhimrāpnoti sadya eva na saṃśayaḥ

avaśyaṃ maṃdabhāgyasya sannidhautasya saṃtataṃ

śaṃkhādīnāṃ nidhānānāmādhipatyaṃ ca śāśvataṃ

akṣayaṃ mahadāregyamakṣayāpuṣpamapyatha

akṣayaṃ sarvasaubhāgyaṃ dadāmyasya na saṃśayaḥ

madvidyāyā prasūnnāyāssāṃgasaṃpūrṇadakṣiṇā

tadvā daśaphalaṃ tasya bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

asādhyamapi devanāṃ sādhayatyeva vāṃchitaṃ

dādāmi mahatī lakṣmīmaṃtepi paramaṃ padaṃ

kṛtvādhanārjanaṃ nityaṃ datvācārya padāṃvujaṃ

avaśyaṃ phalamāpnoti saṃśayonātravidyeta

dhanārjanaṃ mahatpuṇyamāhurdivyāmahārṣayaḥ

brahmalāṃ dakṣiṇaṃ datvā brahma prāpnoti śāśvataṃ

yācitvāpi pradāyātha brahmaprāpnoti śāśvataṃ

kṛtvādhanārjanaṃ nityaṃ nimagrairapi koṭiśaḥ

dakṣiṇāmapi yatkiṃcidatvā siddhiṃ parāṃ vrajet

japtvā paṃcaśataprātaḥ sāyamaṣṭhotaraśataṃ

saṃprāpya sadvīyāmacirādadhiyo bhavet

dehāṃte bhagavaddhāmayāti vaṃśasamanvitaḥ

tasya puṇyaphalaṃ vaktuṃ nāhaṃ śaktāsmivāmarāḥ

paurṇamāsī ca vaiśākhīmahāsiddhikarīmmatā

ekādaśyapitanmāse mahāsiddhirī smṛtā

majjanmadivasevaśāṃ kuryādvatamataṃdritaḥ

kārtike sā sikṛtthaṃ varṣevarṣe vrataṃ śubhaṃ

maṃtramūrtirviśuddhātmā svātmānaṃ brahmarūpiṇī

śrūyate dūragāvannākhecarānapipaśyati

paśyatyeva svacakṣurbhyāṃ nārāṇa samanvitāṃ

svakaṭākṣekṣaṇenānyān śrīsamṛddhānkarotyapi

tṛṇīkaroti madbhaktaḥ svaśriyānātrasaṃśayaḥ

paśyatāṃ sarvadevānāmapi cātahitā bhavatu

yatkṛtvā sādhakoloke mahatī siddhi maśnute

vaiśākhe śrovaṇevapi sāmārabhyāśvinepi vā

samastasaṃpatyāśrājyakāraṇaṃ bhuktimuktidaṃ

ukteṣu śubhamāseṣu prathame bhārgavodine

nityaṃ paṃcaśataṃ japtvā svācāryājñāpurassaraṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā gāyatryādau gavāṃ ghṛtaṃ

prasannalakṣmīmaṃtreṇa triśataṃ juhuyādvyataṃ

ācāryapatnī saṃpūjyārātanaṃ bhārgadine

abhyacyajaṃghapuṣpādyairvāsolaṃkāramarpayet

samarpyasvaguroḥ palyaimahālakṣmīdhiyānṛpa

gurupatnyākṣasānnidhyedradyādācāryapādayoḥ

sarveṣāmapya sānnidhye bhagavatsannidhau nṛpa

athavācārya pādābjārūḍha śrīpādukāyuge

bhārgavevāsare kurvaśiṣṭaṃ vratamutamuttamaṃ

labhatamadaṃ bhāgyopi svarṇaratnasamākulāṃ

āyuṣyasyātha vṛddhiḥ syādārogyasvya ca saṃtataṃ

mīmāṃsādvayatarkadiśāstrapāragatopi ca

yogamaṣṭāgamāsābhuktvā bhogāṃścakṣubhān

mahāsiddhipradānyasyāssaṃtyanyāni śrutau smṛtau

durlabhamimanuṣyāṇāṃ mamarāṇaṃ ca durlabhā

dīkṣāyuktaḥ śucirnityaṃ virdyasājyaśriyojepan

kiṃciraktaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi

tadvidhānaṃ vadasvādya mama nārāyaṇaprabho

upāsanāśatenapi vināyena na sidhyati

toṣayetsalalairathaibhaktiśraddhā samanvitaḥ

svayamevā tathā kuryāditi katavyatā kramaṃ

śuśrūṣitaśca satataṃ bhaktyā śiṣyeṇāsādaraṃ

kuryānnāṃdomukhaśrāddhamādau ca svāstivācanaṃ

madhumāse bhaveduḥkhaṃ mādhaṣeratnasayaḥ

samṛddhiḥ śrāvaṇenhanavīryāṇaṃ saṃmadāmapi

jñānaṃ syāt kārtike saukhyaṃ mārgaśīrṣe bhavatyapi

samastabhīṣṭasiddhiḥ syāt phālgunemāsi saṃtataḥ

aśvinīrohiṇīsvātīviśākhāhastabheṣu ca

pūrṇimāpaṃcamī caiva tṛtīyasasamītathā

paṃcāṃgabhuddhadivase valavatyapi vā kāmau

caṃdratārānukūle ca śasyate sarvakarmasu

tatra yadyatkṛtaṃ sarvatadanaṃtaphalapradaṃ

savitugraheṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ somasya grahaṇadapi

kṛtasya karmajātasya phalaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate

gṛhītāpiyunāvidyā tatra dīkṣā purassarā

bhūyopi sarvavidyānāṃ grahaṇe caṃdrasūryayoḥ

anāyāsena siddhiḥ syāt sadya eva phalapradā

kartavyaṃ sarvayaleranaśiṣyāṇāṃ phalasiddhaye

suramyaṃ maṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā tatra dhānyāṃjalidvayaṃ

veṣṭītaṃ vastrayugmena paṃcaratnasamanvitaṃ

nārikela phalopetaṃ maṃgalaiḥ parito jvalaṃ

śriyācitkalayāyuktaṃ yaṃtreṣṭākṣārasiddhide

maṃtrairāgamikaiścaivaṣvaikṛvairnāthamacceyet

śrāvayetaiśca sūktāni maṃtrānviṣṇutsavetadā

athavā mātṛkāṃ śuddhāṃ mātṛkāṃ vaihuvīmapi

vārṇanyāsaṃ daśavidhacinmayīmātṛkāmapi

nyāsajālamiti nyasya pūjayetatra pūrvavat

uttamaṃ kāyennṛtyagītavādhairmanoharaiḥ

gaṃdhapuṣpāvṛtāṃ kalasuvāsobhūṣaṇādikaṃ

evaṃ tatrotsarvakṛtvātrikālamapi pūjayet

nirāhāraḥ phalāśīṣā pūrvepustri sahasrakaṃ

lakṣmī hṛdayavidyāṃ vā dvādaśārṇamathapivā

parehani tathā prātaḥ pūrvavat sarvamācaret

kuṃḍanirmāya vidhivat kṛtvā tatrapi maṃḍalaṃ

sāṃgāvaraṇamāvātdyatatrābhyacyaśriyapatiḥ

dadhidugdhājyasahitaiḥ sahasraṃ juhupatilaiḥ

kṛtvā pūrṇahutiṃ tatrā tatatra tatastaṃ kalaśeceyet

śiṣyavaryamathāhnapabhakti na amakalmaṣaṃ

pāvamānena sūktai na surāstvāmini maṃtrakaiḥ

nārāyaṃ svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ śiṣyesaṃnidadhātivai

iti saṃsmṛtyasasmṛtyatajjalairabhiṣecayet

dhārayitvāmgulīyādibhūṣaṇanyaṣi sarvaśaḥ

tanmūrtipaṃjaranyāsamṛṣyādinyāsa pūrvakaṃ

tatastacchi ravi svasya datvā hastaṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ

śrīmaṃtrānyatamaṃ paścācchatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet

ācāryotha prasannausmai śiṣyāyodakapūrvakaṃ

śrīmaṃtrānyatamenāthasveṣṭhamaṃtreṇasaṃyutaṃ

āgatāṃ bhāvayecchipyodhanyosmīti punaḥ punaḥ

tvātha śrīmadaṣṭārṇayaṃtrarājaṃ samarpayet

śiṣyasya dakṣiṇe karṇe tata iṣṭamanūnapi

kṛta kṛtyastataśiṣyastasme sarvaṃ nivedayet

gobhūhiraṇyābharaṇagṛhakṣetrādikaṃ nṛpa

aśleśādatra vastrādi dadyādvitānusārataḥ

sahasradakṣiṇaguktamācāryāya prasannadhīḥ

ādaudī dīkṣāvidhiṃ kṛtvā vidyāṃ dadyādgurustataḥ

kalaśasyāyanādīmi kṛtvā pūrvoditanica

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ śrīmaṃtrānyatamaṃ japet

śrīmadaṣṭākṣeraṇaśca sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

śrīmaṃtrānyatamenātha śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet

pūrvavatpūjayet kuṃbhe lakṣmīnārāyaṇavubhau

parīdhāpya ca ṣosaścadhārayitvāthābhūṣaṇaṃ

tato dehepi vinyasya vaiṣṇavīmātṛkāṃ tu vā

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāṃ brahmavidyāpurassarāṃ

paścādiṣṭhamanūnadatvā kārayedāśiṣastataḥ

sarvasvaṃ vā tādardhaṃ vātadardhārdhaṃ samarpayet

paṃcaviśatinikaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā suvarṇakaṃ

datvā vahnanivāsāṃsidhānyarāśimathākṣayaṃ

avaśyaṃ vahubhirvitaistoṣayenmanusiddhaye

arhaṇabhi"ca vahudhāvāsolaṃkatāṇadibhiḥ

kṛtatyo bhavet sadyassiddhaṃ maṃtro na saṃśayaḥ

upadiṣṭhāṃśca bhūyopi yogahlājimanūtamān

tāvatkālakṛtaṃ hāmajapādi phalamakṣayaṃ

tasmādācārya pādābje dātavyā pūrṇadakṣiṇā

vidyāssamastā datvā datvāpi rahasyamupadiśya ca

upadiṣṭaṃ ca bhūyopi maṃtraratnanidaṃ śubhaṃ

ravīdugrahaṇe puṇyeyadidīkṣābhicanaṃ

etatkalovārāṃte sau vaikuṃṭhe bhagavatpude

avijñāya valaṃmāsa vāracaṃdrādisaṃbhavaṃ

tasmatsarvajñamācāryamāśreyadbhagavaddhiyā

vāsudevādikānmātrānvahnanārāyaṇadikān

dvayaṃ bhāgavatī proktā dīkṣānārāyaṇīśubhā

na saṃdigdhāya mūrkhāya vedaniṃ vā parāya vā

yo dadāti mahāvidyāṃ śiṣyaṃ cātmānamapyasau

ajñātvāmāsavārāddivalamācāryavaktrataḥ

kiṃcāpyavivadaḥ sarvābhayaṃtīha dine dine

sarvavit sadgurorvaktrādgṛhītvā siddhimaśrutaṃ

dīkṣiteṣvapi sarveṣu dīkṣito vaiṣṇavodhikaḥ

ekena janmanau vāsau mukta ityabhidhīyate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā sakṛtsmatvāpi mucyate

aṣṭadaśakṣarīṃ vāpi brahmavidyāpurassarāṃ

ihāpi siddhividyo saumuktaḥ saṃsārasāgarāt

sadyaḥ pātakarāśighraṃ vahuśroyovivardhanaṃ

viṣṇudīkṣābhiṣeko vā yogoṣṭāṃgayutopi vā

nainaścitayatostyanyadbrahmasaṃprāptikāraṇaṃ

jānakīpatināpūrvaśreyoṣṭhaddhipradāyakaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarījyahvābhajadhdhvaṃ rāmacyutaṃ

kṛtakṛtyābhavaṃtotha brahmaprāptyatha śāśvataṃ idameva paratapaḥ

ayameve paro yogo dīkṣeya vaiṣṇavī śubhā

dīkṣito viṣṇudīkṣāyāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī japan

dīkṣābhiṣecānaṃ labdhā paṃcadhāvānnidhaikadhā

nāṣṭāṃgayogābhyāsenanayāgaipūrṇadakṣiṇaiḥ

bhūyobhūyaḥ pravakṣyāmi bhagavādīkṣayānviteḥ

dvicatvāriṃśodhyāyaḥ

śrunamasyabhiratyaṃtarahasyamapi siddhidaṃ

bhegavacchāstramasmādhiḥ śrutaṃ tvadvadanīvujāt

yogasvarūpaṃ pṛcchāmastadasmākaṃ vadaprābho

agānyaṣṭāviti proktamasya tatsakarlaveda

vaśiṣṭādi mahaṣīṇaṃ sannidhau janakīpatiḥ

yathā nārāyaṇeyogīnjanakāyamahātmane

śṛṇu rājanpravakṣyāmi yogamaṣṭāṃgasaṃyutaṃ

nityanaimitikaireva kurvāṇeduritakṣayaṃ

ajadurjanasargabajasādhusamāgamaṃ

aṣṭāvaṃgāni vakṣyāmi yogasāvasudhāyate

yamaścaniyamaḥ paścādāsanaṃ ca tṛtīyakaṃ

dadhāraṇa ca tathādhyānaṃ samādhiriticāṣṭamaṃ

yama"caniyamaḥ paścādāsanaṃ ca tṛtīyakaṃ

prāṇayāmāvratatadvibhajya pravakṣyamitatallakṣāṇamapyatha

kṣamādhṛtirmitāhāraḥ śauvaṃ ceti yamādṛśa

vāṅmanaḥ karmabhirnanamahiṃsetyabhidhīyate

svātyamityucyeta dhīraistadbrahmapraptisvadhima

asteyametadapyaṃgaṃ brahmaprāptessanātanaṃ

strīsaṃgatiparityāgo brahmacaryaṃ pravakṣyate

utsādanānu saṃdhānaṃdaye procyate vudhaiḥ

sarveṣāmapi kauṭilyarāhityaṃtvārjavaṃtu

vaṃdhuṣvivasamācāraḥ kṣamāsyādbrahmavitama

lobhavatsudhatiṃdhīśaḥ pravadaṃti satavara

atyugrahakaṭutiktālasavaṇādivivarjitaṃ

nirgataṃ navaraṃdhrebhyoromahūyebhya eva ca

gṛjjalābhyā vahissamyaṅgirmalīkaraṇaṃ tathā

vidurbudhāḥ etedaśayagābhūya brāhmatā pratihetavaḥ

siddhāṃtālocana caiva lajjādānaṃmatistathā

pratyekameṣāṃ vakṣyamilakṣaṇānihahīyate

anāyāsopapannenatṛṣṇarthe naiva jīvanaṃ

śrutyādyukteṣyuviśvāsamāstikāṃ pravadaṃti hi

trisaṃdhyamekasaṃdhyaṃ vā bhavatyeveśvarārcana

śruti smṛtipurāṇādi madhyāṃtoktorthadarśanaṃ

śrutyādibhirlekikaiśca yadyadatyaṃtaniṃditaṃ

yādiṣṭadevatādhyātvā tadarpaṇādhiyā

tarkeṇapyanusaṃdhānaṃ samyaktadasatorapi

gurorlabdhasya maṃtrasyaśaśvadāvartanaṃ hi yata

kartavyasya samastasya niyamagrahaṇaṃ vrataṃ

yamaiścanipamairyadyallataṃ tatsaphalaṃ bhavet

kṛtairdadyatedyaudyaḥ śuṣkaṃ dhanamivāgninā

saha ācāryārpinahraihasyacaitadāsanamucyate

tvāgasṛprāṃsemadosthimajyāśukrādisaṃbhavāḥ

prāyaścitamadyaudyānāṃ mukhyametadvahaṃtyapi

prāṇayāmaścasatataṃ kartavyo vidhivansune

svayamevāvaśiṣṭassanbrahmayāyakalpate

pradadyācchoṭikāmekāmiyaṃ mātrākanīyasī

trirjapedāyātaproṇaḥ prāṇāyāmopamucyeta

adhamaḥ purakethatrithovāryakuṃbhake pūrvavannṛpa

prāṇayāmaiḥ kṛtaiḥ śaśvannityaṃ ṣoḍaśabhirnapa

paropatāya japāyaṃparadravyāyahājaṃ

mahāmātakarjayāpaśatāvṛtopapātakaṃ

sarvāṇyapi pradatdyete praṇayāmaiśratuḥ śataiḥ

karmasvapi ca sarveṣu śubheṣudyaśubheṣu ca

āto yatnena kartavyāḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ śubhatithiḥ

na nārāyaṇe tadādhyāyanmuhurmahurananyadhiḥ

paraṃ jyotiḥ paraṃdhyāyataṃtarevamanujīyet

daśapraṇavasaṃyuktaṃgātryā sarvakarmasu

piṇaṃ marūpiṇaṃ vā satataṃ smarannarāyaṇadhibhuṃ

niyamyavāyuṃ kartavyaḥ prāṇayāmodyanāśanaḥ

na phalaṃtyevatāḥ sarvāyātrenāpi kṛtāḥ kṛtāḥ

pratyāhāro bhavatye tatsamyagiṃdriyanigrahaḥ

pūrvoktotamatrāśca prāṇayāmatrayodbhavāḥ

samyagālokanaṃ dhyānaṃ nidhāyahṛdayevibhuṃ

satyajñānasukhaikatvaprāyeprāptasādhakaḥ

samādhirathavājīvabrahmaṇeremacitaṃ

svatīpyananyasadbhāvātsvayamevāvaśiṣyate

evamaṣṭāṃgasaṃpatrayogupraktāssusaṃyataḥ

ya evamabhyasennityaṃ vinītaḥ śāṃtamānasaḥ

nirastāṣoḍaṣīrataḥ kāmakrodhādivarjitaḥ

karmayogena vā jñānayogenātho bhayena vā

karotyanudinaṃ yastu tatphale vigatasyahaḥ

suguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ vāpi nārāyaṇamajjaṃ vibhuṃ

dhyātvā karmānavekṣyātha yātyeṣa paramaṃ padaṃ

sapātyaivotamasthānaṃ yadbhavānānivartate

praviśasaniyamaijanmikediṣānuṣṭhitaiḥ

haṭhāsaṃsārasāgasthāsyayāraṃ prāptu yadocchāse

sarvayū uḥkhabhi bhūtānāṃ āṃtānāṃ hatacetasāṃ

sa eva jānakīmātho brahmarūpī jagatpatiḥ

prakṣīṇa śeṣapānāṃ jñānayogaḥ praśasyate

jñānena karmeśāvāpi devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ vibhuṃ

ādimadhyāta ititaṃ rāmaṃ samyagihārcayajanayogāḥ praśasyate

kaitaccharīraṃ hikṣayi vṛḍapiyatiṣṇu

śāstreyapāditaṃ samyaganutiṣṭhārtināśanaṃ

karipyaumikartavyamiti kaścidvicitayet

śvaḥkāryamadyakuvītamadhyāhne cāparāhnikaṃ

samyagvairāgyamiṣṭasya paratatvasthacetasaḥ

śarīritvamayi prāyo viraktasyaiva śobhayet

anyamedhyaṃ śarīrasyaṃ viṣṭāmidhyagatādapi

śarīresminnamedhyatvāmīdṛśaṃ nāma hehināṃ

śṛgālairapi ghṛdhraiścanīyete yadyahovidhiḥ

pūtat pratipadaṃ samyagāpadāṃpadamīdaśaṃ tataḥ

duḥkhai kānubhavārthyayavidhinaitatkṛtaṃ unaḥ

vyāmohito jagatparenmāpayaiva mahātmanaḥ

sukhaṃ mithyāduḥkhemavabhūyopyanubhavatyayaṃ

******** bhūtvā duḥkhāvrtepunaḥ

sarvātmanā parityājyai sarveṣāmapi duḥkhade

na kiṃcipikarmātraniḥphalaṃ vidyāte mune

tato na karmakartavyaphalarthitvena sūribhiḥ

na cevyatatisaṃsāre duḥkhākartarbhavimohitaḥ

yogābhyāsarato vāpi guruśuśrūṣāṇerataḥ

chiṃdhipṛcchāmitātātrānugrahaṃ kuru

punarāvṛtiratirahitaṃ śāśvataṃ brahmayātyapi

śrīmatābhihitaṃ brahmavidyānuṣṭhānatatparāḥ

nivṛtirevamuktistu pravṛtirbhuktirucyate

tvayārye sāya uktaṃ tatsatyaṃ satyavidāṃvara

nivṛtilakṣaṇatdveṣāmuktirityabhidhīyate

bhuktiṃ pravakṣyetelokevaiṣamyamubhayorapi

muktirātmānusaṃdhānenātmāvasthānameva hi

pravṛtau ca nivṛtau ca sarvadātmānubhāvināṃ

yenusmarati satataṃ nate sasāriṇo nṛpa

bhoktātra bhagavāneva bhojyamārpabhujiktiyā

ato na muktimārgasyarodhinī bhuktirīṣyeta

śrīrāmamiṃdirānāthaya ekasminnamanutiṣṭhita

yathavidhiniṣedhautumuktaṃ nevāpasarpataḥ

brahmaiva satyaṃ tadahaṃ bhagavānahamīśvaraḥ

iti yenusmaraṃtīha ciṃtayaṃti dine dine

yathā ghaṭaśca kalaśa ekārthapyabhidhāyakaḥ

ato rāmo hamityevaṃ brahmaivāhamathāpi vā

vadaṃti tatvataḥ sākṣāte sarvebrahmarūpiṇaḥ

maṃtratrasya chaṃdo vā munidhyānādikaṃ tu vā

atrānyatamamāyāyānmaṃtraṃ brahmaikyasiddhidaṃ

gacchan siṣṭansvāyanmujananugrannapi hasannapi

brahmabhūtamathātmānaṃ svayayeva prapaśyati

brahmatādātmyavidyāyāḥ smāṇainaiva siddhibhāk

ācāryāyapyeyeddhrīmānetedvidyāpradāyine

udakodanavastrādi kiṃcidatvāpi vānvahaṃ

ato brahmavidedatamānaṃtyāya prakalpate

kuṭaṃvinī daridrāśvaduḥkhinaḥ syurnasaṃśayaḥ

kārāgṛheṣu sarvasvayamevanigṛhṇate

ato brahmavidāṃdveṣo na kartavyaḥ śubhessubhiḥ

yestuvaṃtyaṃnumodaṃti vadaṃtyapi manīṣiṇaḥ

jñātvā tameva viśveśaṃ kṛtyaṃ kuru niraṃtaraṃ

sahasra kṛtvo gāyatryā japāḥ pratyahamādarāt

śrīmadaṣṭākṣrīvidyājayotha sarahasyakaḥ

gurūdaivatamaṃtrāṇāmaikyaṃ sa bhāvayet sadā

bhidyate hṛdayagraṃthirvinaśyaṃtyāpi saṃśayā

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja śuśrūṣā pratyahaṃ śubhā

śrutyāktācārasaṃyatiḥ pratyahaṃ samaye kṛtā

niyamavyatirikteṣu samayeṣu niraṃtaraṃ

brahmaprāptikarāṇahunaitasmādvidyate paraṃ

nāstikānāṃ na vaktavyaṃ kṛtaghrānāṃ durātmanāṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī japtvā brahmavidyājapaḥ

brahmaikyasiddhidaṃ nārayaṇadhiyānityamācāryaṃ

vaśo japan śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī japtvā brahmavidyāpurassarāṃ

mahākālaṃ durādharṣamapijitvā bhayaṃkaraṃ

nārāyaṇamayobhūtvāyātitadbrahmaśāśvataṃ

brahmadarśanaparyaṃtaṃ japenmaṃtraṃ samarcayet

śālagrāmaśilācakrecakrarājasamanvite

ihāmutrāpi mahātī siddhiṃ māpnotyanuttamā

prāpnoti vasudhāmāladānahomādibhirvinā

na pūjayedyovidhinārgājaṃ vrājatidāruṇa

ācāryerbhyo vinānyatra datvādhāgatimāpnuyāt

parivrāḍajñānamātreṇa dānahomādibhirvinā

parivrāḍaviraktaśca viraktaśca gṛhītathā

purāyastriyo gṛhasthoścamagalairmaṃgalārthibhiḥ

caṃdanāgarūkastūrīkarpūrasahimāṃvubhiḥ

puṣpamālyaiśca vahubhirdūrvābhidhaṃ valākṣataiḥ

jātīprasūnairvilvaiśca punnāgairvakulairapi

nāgavāṇadipuṣpaiścaṃgadhavadbhirmanoharai

phalaiśca vahubhiḥ patrairjalasyāla samudbhavai

samyakusaṃpādyatnenaśatmābhatmāśriyaḥpatiṃ

atyākaurvaṅgavidhaiḥ piṣṭakairiṣṭasiddhaye

dadhyodanānnapānādivyaṃ jānādibhiḥ

āvartakedhūpadīpaiḥ yajāvṛtyupakalpitaiḥ

vahnībhirdīpamālābhirarcayepuraharniśaṃ

kapūracūrṇasahitaistāṃvūlaiśca suvāsitaiḥ

svasvaśatmanusāreṇa sarvaṃ saṃpādya yatnataḥ

dadyurdānani juhupurarcitagnauśubhārthinaḥ

evaṃ gṛhasthaniyamastathaiva brahmacāriṇaṃ

yadikuryuḥ prayatnena pūjāṃtatsādhanairiha

etenaiva mahīpāla kalyāṇaṃ tava sarvadā

saguṇaṃ viṣṇumārādhyapūrvoktiḥ sādhanairiha

teṣāṃ bhuktiścamuktiśca bhavatyevahinānyathā

saṃsārānnanivartataṃ vidhyati kramadoṣataḥ

ahiṃsāparamodharmasteṣāmeva mahāṃpate

bhāvanākalpitairdravyairpatīnāṃ pūjanaṃ smṛtaṃ

sevyasevakarūpatvādgṛhīṇīmīśvarasyasya ca

jīvabrahmaikaciṃtātu punarāvṛtivarjitā

cayenateṣāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃciduḥkṛtotthānacāpadaḥ

yadgātvā mucyate tejaṃturjanmasaṃsāravaṃdhanāt

akarṇaḥ samṛṇetyetacchabdarūpaṃ paraṃ mahaḥ

samahāpuruṣaḥ puṃsāstrīrṇavyaktivilakṣaṇaḥ

sarveśvarassarvarūpassarvadevamayohariḥ

ajaḥ smaraṇamātreṇa janmādi kleśabhaṃjanaḥ

niyamenaiva sarveṣāṃ svāśramoktena sa prabhuḥ

svāśramoktaṃ parityajya ātmanamupāsate

vaṃdhanāt śrutismṛtipurāṇeṣuyo yonite

ato niyamabhādṛtthakuryāddhyānamananyadhīḥ

mithyādavyatiriktaṃ yadaśyatvāta tathānṛpa

ekākī yatacitātmaciṃtayeadananyadhīḥ

etatsvavyātiriktaṃ yadyatṛḥsvenaiva kalpyate

vālājñayāvāṃkobhedaḥ kalpyatatvakvacakṣuṣā

adhyātmavidbhikhyavijñāpitaḥ sarvagaḥ smṛtaḥ

na stridhovyatricāriṇyaḥ puruṣaścaivadhārmikaḥ

pūjyaṃte tithayo yatra guruśiṣya paraparaṃparāḥ

niyamopyāśramasteṣāṃ na kadācintivarjate

yeṣu vaṃśeṣu sarveṣāṃteṣāmeva prakāśate

svayameva paraṃ brahmatatonpannāsti kiṃcana

janmakoṭi sahasreṣu prakṣīṇeśoṣaduṣkṛtaiḥ

sukhāmṛta rasāsvāhasatyaikajñānarūpatā

ahopuṇyamahodharmonātaḥ parataraṃ piyaṃ

aṣṭākṣarānusaṃdhānamācaryacāraṇarcanaṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaśuśrūṣānvahamādarāt

tvadhanyosi mahārājatvameva brahmaviṣvakaḥ

bhavadbhāgyādayenaiva parabrāhmāmayo hariḥ

rāmābhidhānaḥ svervaṣāmātmārāmaḥ śriyaḥ patiḥ

yeyaṃ cihnapiṇī sākṣānmahālakṣmīstrapī

jānakītpakṣivikhyāṃtā divyakāṃti sāmacitā

tanjamaskaroti bhagavantathapādāṃvujadvayaṃ

gṛhītvā mithilādho śanayācyamapi kutracit

tubhyamuktaṃ mahārājavedasārārthasaṃgrahaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī sāṃgāṃ japtvā mukto bhaviṣyasi

nālyena yasālabhyā satya satyaṃ mahīyate

bhūyopi śrīmadaṣṭārṇasādhanaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāpi samāhitamanāḥ śṛṇu

sāmrājñī sarvavidyānāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī parā

vaiśākhemāsiyā muktā tṛtīyākṣayasaṃtitā

prātaḥ śucibhūtvā saṃdhyāmupāsya ca

panasai vījarahitairmadhutritayamiśritaiḥ

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ śrīmaṃtrānyatamena ca

gavyājyena tridhā hutvā kṛtvā homaṃ japādibhiḥ

pradakṣiṇaṃ namaskṛtyajātevada samādarāt

vāsobhirapyalaṃkārairalaṃkṛtyā tataḥ paraṃ

atha dvarūdaśaniṣkavāpaṃcaniṣkamathāpi vā

brāhmaṇanbhojayet paścādvādaśātha suvāsinīḥ

tādvine pānasādīni phalāni guraverpyayet

kṛtvotthaṃ mahatī siddhidaivairapi durlabhāṃ

akṣayaṃ saukhyamatyaṃtamāropyaṃ ca tathākṣaya

avāpyākṣayabhogāścamodate bhagavatpade

kṛtenaye narājedaṃ sarvasiddhiḥ prajāyate

sarvāsāmapi vidyānāṃ sidhyartha. kṛyate bhuvi

sidhyarthaṃ kṛyate yatrānavarātravrataśubhaṃ

kriyate satataṃ siddhaissamastaphalasiddhaye

pūrvapūrvadinātpurāpaśutaredivase kṣayaṃ

dhanadhānyagajāśvadimahāsaṃyatsasṛddhidaṃ

ciṃtitārthapradaṃ śreyaskaraṃ sadyodyanāśanaṃ

atrāvaśyaṃ prakartavyaṃ puraścaraṇamādarāt

satataṃ vijayī bhūtvā duḥkhasadyairvimucyate

caitramāsavratamapi navarātrābhidhaṃ tathā

śaktaścedubhayaṃ kuryādekaṃ vā navarātrakaṃ

pratipaddinamārabhya tatra vamyaṃtamādarāt

pratipadivasetatra prātarutthāyavāgyataḥ

raśmimālāmanusmatyapacadhāvātridhā tataḥ

samastanyāsasahitāṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ śubhāṃ

saṃkalpārkasahasrāṇi vratī nityaṃ japedbudhaḥ

prasannalakṣmīmathavāsaṃkalpyārkasahasrakaṃ

yadvā paṃcādhikāṃ catvāriṃśadāvṛtimabhyaset

padyāśatmāthavābhojyaṃ mithunaṃ bhagavaddhiyā

patraiḥ puṣpairyajet paścānniśataṃ brahmavidyayā

ātmānaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpādyairalaṃ kuryā dine dine

kuryādinedine vaśyaṃ mahotsavamataṃdritaḥ

sruvāyīnībhirdātavyaṃ sāyaṃkāle mahīyate

gavyājyaṃ juhuyādagnau aṣṭāviṃśatidhāśriyā

sadyassamastavipadāṃhatrīsaṃyatkarīśubhā

ācāryāṃnujñayāsrātvānityakarmasamāpya ca

ekaikavilvapatreṇa sahitaṃtramadhusutaṃ

hutvā śrīmanunāpaścācchatādhikasahasrakaṃ

lakṣmīhṛdayamaṃtraiśca tridhāgavyaghṛtaṃ hunet

prāyaścitāhutīḥ kṛtvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

arcayet puṣpasahitairvilvairvātulasīdalaiḥ

ācāryacaraṇaṃ dvaṃdvaṃ sāṣṭāṃgaṃ praṇipatya ca

suramyapadmarāgākhyamaṇibhissahitaṃ śubhaṃ

kṣayāṃṣacintukasauvarṇamathavā-kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ dadyādvitalobhavivarjitaḥ

kuṃḍaladvitayābhāvetanmānasvarṇamarpayet

vahuśasya samāyuktāṃ vasudhākṣiṇānvitāṃ

datvā godvitayaṃ paśvādadyādakṣiṇayāścitāṃ

pradastāvastudhādāneśatāṭhakamitāmahī

mahīpratinividravyaṃ paṃcāśatriṣkamutamaṃ

adharmasmṛtaṃ adhamādhamamevoktaṃ tatrāpyardhaṃ mahīyate

varṣāśanamitaṃ dravyamathavotamadakṣiṇa

tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā vitalobhaṃ varityajet

ācāryapatnī śrīvudhyāpūjayet tataḥ paraṃ

paṃcaviṃśati saṃkhyākamithunānnātyathabhojayet

vāsobhidaṃkṣiṇābhiścataṃ kalairapi tauṣayet

sadyassiddhimavāpnoti mahatīdurlabhāmapi

ācāryāya pradātavyaṃ niṣkameka suvarṇakaṃ

caturniṣkaṃ caturthyaṃ ca paṃcamyāyaṃ ca niṣkakaṃ

aṣṭaniṣkramayāṣṭamyāṃ navamyānavaniṣkakaṃ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ datvā godayaṃ dakṣiṇānvitaṃ

ācāryādāśiṣaḥ prāpyasiddhiyāṃ protyanuttamāṃ

paṃcathiṃśatisaṃkhyā vā sahasraṃ vibhave sati

sahasradakṣiṇāyaktānaṣṭhaugrāmānsamarpayet

yāvatīdaṃ yāvatī dakṣiṇaṃ dadyātsvācārya caraṇadvayai

vitaśāṭhyaṃ tyajeddhīmāśaktassaṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ

mahādaridraḥ kṛpaṇe yadya sausvasutāṃ śubhāṃ

ācāryapatnīputrāṇaṃ nityaśuśrūṣakot

gurūrūśuśrūṣaṇadevanityaṃ siddhirdinedine

ācaṃdrārka mahāghore narake sau nimajjati

ācāryadakṣiṇedeyāpuraścaryādikarmasu

tatkāryaniṣyalaptopaścālārakaduḥkhadaṃ

tatkarmasaphalaṃ proktaṃ sarvakalyāṇasiddhidaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyājapastāska ucyate

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsiddhideyamevavimuktidaṃ

mahatīyā puraścaryāmayā pūrvamudīritā

navarātravrate tādṛkupuraścaryāśatodbhavaṃ

navarātravrate kṛtvā puraścaryāmimāṃ śubhāṃ

nirāmayaścirāpuṣpaṃ prāpyāṣṭaguṇasamyutāṃ

gajāśvaratnasaṃpūrṇāṃ saṃtati vyāpinīmapi

saubhāgyaṃ mahadāsādyasarvalokavaśaṃkaraṃ

bhuktvāpi sakalānbhogānmahāyogīmahīmatiḥ

modate nātrasaṃdeho divyakanyāśatānvitaḥ

kuryācchuśrūṣaṇaṃ nityaṃ yāvadehāvasānakaṃ

śravaṇādyasya viprāṇāṃ palāyatau mahāpadaḥ

puṣpanakṣatrasahitā tatraṃ caṃdrasya parvacet

bhānuvāsara saṃyuktā vyatīyāta samanvitā

alabhyosau mahānukto yogorddhodayanāmakaḥ

trailokyaciṃtāmāṇiyoga uktassamastaviyā

yogarājo na labhyate sau bhuvilokanātha

iṃdravāsarasaṃyuktāyogoyaṃ padmakaḥ smṛtaḥ

mahānalabhyayogosaucaṃdracūḍāmaṇi smṛtaḥ

bhānuvāsaravyuktāvāpitṛnakṣatrasaṃyutā

saviturgrahaṇaṃ tapralabhyate yadi bhūyate

parvasveteṣu catuṣupadyekamapilabhyate

vidhānamekamevātrapuraścaraṇa karmaṇi

catuṣvateṣu yogeṣu parvokaṃ vā bhavedyadi

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ samastanyāsasaṃyutāṃ

japtvā sāmrājyalakṣmī vā dvādaśārṇāṃ śriyo yavo

japtvā samāhito nityaṃ śatadhika sahasrakaṃ

gavyājyaṃ juhuyādadāgrauściyāpyaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityamarcayedbrahmavidyayā

srātvā nadyāṃ viśuddhātmākeśavādyaiśca nāmabhiḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvajalaṃpītvā tataḥ paraṃ

sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ praṇipatyātha tadājñāṃ mūrdhni dhārayana

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

prasannārasayāvayidvādaśārṇa śriyāpi vā

aṣṭhottaraśataṃ hutvā vāsudevena cāpyatha

ekaikaśobhagavato raśmirbhirjuhuyādvataṃ

mahāvyāhatibhirhutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇahutiṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvasāṣṭāṃgaṃ praṇipatya ca

mahīśasya samāyuktāṃ dadyādavahuladakṣiṇaṃ

siddhi mama vāpnoti devānāmapi durlabhā

ācāryāya pradātavyā sālagrāmaśilāśubhā

dātavyānyapi vipreyodānānyanyāni bhūyate

bhagavatsannidhau datvā svācāryāsannidhau nṛpa

saviturgrahasoceṃdoryadyadvastu pradīyate

cūḍāmarāyādiyogeṣudatadravyasya bhūyeta

ekāhuti phalaṃ sūryasāmayorgrahaṇe kṛtaṃ

viśeṣācchīmadaṣṭorṇa homāditanitaphalaṃ

cūḍāmaṇyādi yogeṣuko vā varṇavitaṃ kṣamaḥ

himavanmerusadaśaṃprāhārāgamapāragāḥ

raviparvaṇi vā koṭibhūṣaṇāṃ pradānajaṃ

māturanvayasaṃbhūtāna śataśopi sahasraśaḥ

datāthaṃ vasudhāṃ pakvavahuśasya samanvitāṃ

yatphalamuddiṣṭaṃ tatsyātpūryedravarvaṇi

ācāryāyavratīdhīmānkoṭibrahmāṃḍadānajaṃ

itthamācārya pādābje pradatvā vahudakṣiṇāṃ

śrotriyāmadaridrāya vedābhyāsaratāya ca

kṣīyaṃte salilānyadhvermāścatrasareṇavaḥ

kṣīyaṃte salilānyabdhermomāścatrasareṇavaḥ

śakyaṃteyāṃśavobhūmergaṇituṃ vṛṣṭiviṃdavaḥ

vivāhārthaṃ dhanaṃ datvā grahaṇe caṃdrasūryoḥ

datvā yatphalamāpnoti tadekasminvivāhite

grahaṇeṣu puraścaryāṃ kṛtvā saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ

kṛtvā yatphalamāpnoti tatopyadhikamaśrute

yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti bhagavadvacana yathā

yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti grahaṇe caṃdrasūryayāḥ

koṭī sahasrasaṃkhyākadakṣiṇasahitaṃ nṛpa

sagaṃ dakṣiṇayayuktāṃ grahāṇe caṃdrasūryayoḥ

tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti vajimedha sahasrajaṃ

kṛtvā yatphalamāpnoti cūḍāmārāyākhyaṃ varveṇi

avaśyam tatphalaṃ bhūyaprāprotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

sālagrāmaśilāṃ datvā suvarṇaniṣkapaṃcakaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭarṇavidyāyāssiddhiprāpnotyunuttamāṃ

tasya lakṣmīpatiḥ sākṣātprasīdati na saṃśayaḥ

paradehāpraveśādi sāmarthyaṃ cāṇimādikaṃ

bhargavevoraviśeṣassaṃpravakṣyate

bhārgavevāsare prāpte svācāryājñapurassaraṃ

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahnau śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

prasannalakṣmīmaṃtreṇadvādaśārṇaśriyāpi vā

vāsudevasya manunāvaiṣvavyāthaṣaḍarṇayā

ekaikaśestatohutvā triśataṃ brahmavidyayā

ekaṃ vaptiyunaṃ bhojyaṃ mṛṣṭhātrerdakṣiṇadibhiḥ

ekaniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā dadyādācārya dakṣiṇaṃ

niyatovaṃdhubhissahabhūyate bhṛguvāravrataṃ kurvannityaṃ vatsarapaṃcakaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāsiddhimānīpranuttamāṃ

athamāsādimaidaityācāryavārepi vā nṛpa

karṣamānasuvarṇaṃ vā niṣkamekamathāpi vā

prāpnotyanuttamāṃ siddhiṣaṇmāsādvatsarādapi

śāyānugrahasāmarthyaṃ mṛddhiṃ putrādisaṃyutāṃ

mahatīprāpyadhanadhānyāśvasaṃkulāṃ

bhogānaṣṭavidhānkāyaṃsidhyādyaṣṭakamuttamaṃ

te nātra saṃdehodivyakanyāśatānvitaḥ

na dhanena ratnairvāviyogaṃ labhate kvacit

vratamitthaṃ śuciḥ kṛtvā bhagavatkalamānvitaḥ

prāpyāṣṭhaguṇamaiśvaryaṃ yoḍāmaṣṭhāṃga saṃyutaṃ

anyepi sādhanīyāyaḥ saṃtyasya vasudhāpate

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi bahmarṣitvādisādhanaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭhārṇavidyāyāḥ pravakṣyāmi tataḥ paraṃ

anenaiva śarīreṇa brahmarṣitvādisādhanaṃ śrīkarpadaṃ

vedapārāyaṇairvāpivājimedha śatenavā

amaratvadikaṃ prāpnutaṃ śakyaṃ vasudhāpate

ācāryasevayānityaṃ dakṣiṇādravyahānataḥ

tasmādaṣṭākṣarīvidyāṃ vinānnaiva kalevare

anenaiva śarīreṇa brahmarṣitvādikaṃ śubhaṃ

kīllāpurīmahā 13 puṇyādakṣiṇemadhurāpurī

11 sītārāmagiriḥ paścā 11 tprayāgasrīrthanāyakaḥ

yacchrutvāpi na rodhīmānamucyate pāparāśibhiḥ

setuvadhaṃ samāśritya svācārya caraṇadvayaṃ

sahasra kṛtvo gāyatromabhyasyatriṃśaṃtaṃ tu vā

vidrūpamātṛkāyuktāmathavā prajapecchuciḥ

śrīmaṃtramapi japtvaikaṃ lakṣyaṃ śuddhau jiteṃdriyaḥ

tadaṃte juhuyādagnautilai strimadhusaṃplutaiḥ

sahasraṃ śataniṣkaṃ vā tadardha vā tadardhakaṃ

brāhmāṇannojayet paścācchataṃ sāgraṃ suvāsinīḥ

sugrīvavāyuputrādikapināyaka saṃvṛtaṃ

tasyānugrahatassadyojarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ

śrīrāmasyājñayanityaṃ rājarājovibhīṣaṇaḥ

saprayacchatidhanāni divyavastrāṇi divyānyābharaṇāni ca

anenaiva śarīre śāmanovegī ca khecaraḥ

raṃganāthasya sānnidhyamāśrityanityaṃ sādhatavrataḥ

kurvananniṣavaṇasmānaṃ kuveratanayātaṭe

prajaptvā śrīmanuṃ paścāpaṃcadhājyaṃ tato hunet ipyekalakṣamabhyaset

vāsudevatyekaṃ daśasāhasraṃ prajaptvāniyataḥ śuciḥ

madhutritayasammiśrairgavyenājyena saṃplutaiḥ

śrīmaṃtreṇa tato hutvā trisahasraṃ tilairnṛpa

sahasramekaṃ juhuyātilairevaghṛtaplutaiḥ

sālagrāmaśivāṃ datvā śatadakṣiṇayānvitāṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdva datvā vipraśataṃ tataḥ

nasminnevadine sākṣādraṃganāthaṃ prapaśyati

tejomayatunurbhūtvā vaśiṣṭādi prapūjitaḥ

śeṣācalasya śikhare suvarṇamukharītaṭe

sahasra kṛtvo gāyatrīṃ prajaptvātriśataṃ tu vā

śrīmadaṣyakṣarīṃvidyāṃ prajaptvā lakṣamādārāt

vāsudeva mahavidyā vaiṣṇavī ca ṣaḍakṣarī

japedaṣṭhottaraśataṃ bhagavadraśmimālikāṃ

juhuyādaśasāhasraṃ tilaistatrāpi bhūyate

vidyayā vāsudevasya vaiṣṇvyātha ṣaḍarṇayā

bhagavadraśmibhirhutvā goghṛte naiva paṃcadhā

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvegośātaṃ dakṣiṇānvitaṃ

datvātasmindine sākṣāccheṣā valapatiṃ vibhuṃ

tatprasādena sadyo sau sarvavidyādharādhipaḥ

jarāmāṇanirmukto bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

svayamevahiniryāṃtiryānipurāṇasmṛtayopi ca

yogamaṣṭāmgamāsādyaśriyaṃ cāṣṭhaguṇānvitā

akṣayānsakalānbhogānbhuṅe kalyāṃtamapyasau

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāṃ samastanyāsasaṃyutāṃ

vāsudevarmahāvidyāṃ vaiṣṇuvīṃ ca ṣaḍakṣarīḥ

śatamāvartayedvīmānnaḍāvadraśmimalikā

hutvāya daśasāhasraṃ trisāhasraṃ śriyā hunet

viprāṇaṃ dviśataṃ bhojyaṃ nārīṇaṃ ca śatadvayaṃ

saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ yuktā dātavyāvaśyamādarāt

sākṣātpaśyati cakṣurbhyāmidirāsahitaṃ vibhu

rāmaṇanimuktovimānaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ

kāṃcīnagaramāsādyanityaṃ triṣavaṇerataḥ

śrīmadaṣṭhākṣarīvidyāṃ samastanyāsasaṃyutatā

japedvādaśa sāhasraṃ vāsudeva mahāmanuṃ

śatamāvartayepaścādbhagavadraśmimālikāṃ

hutvā dvādaśasāhasraṃ śriyā ca trisahasrakaṃ

hutvā sahasraṃ pratyekaṃ ghṛtenaiva mahīyate

brahmārpaṇahutiṃ kṛtvā śatamaṣṭautaraṃ dvijāna

samarpya pitvā svacāryakārṇayoḥ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

śataniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā tadadhavātadardhakaṃ

tasminnevadine sākṣātkāṃvīnagaranāyakaṃ

paśyatyeva svacakṣubhyo brahmeśādibhirarcitaṃ

nirāmayaścirāyuśca śriyamaṣṭhaguṇanvitaṃ

iṃdrārdvāsanamāsādya daivatairapi pūjitaḥ

khecaratvamanuprāpyadevaṣitviprayātyasau

narasiṃhasya sannidhye svācāryājñāpurassaraṃ

śrāvaṇemārgaśīrṣe vā vaddhaṃ bhāskaraparvaṇi

hutvātha bhagavadraśmi maṃtraisredhāghṛtaṃ hunet

tata ācāryayāhājvayugalapraṇiyavya ca

śataniṣkasuvarṇaṃ vā tadardhavitadardhakaṃ

paredyurbrāhmaṇānśatmābhojayeccasuvāsinīḥ

ācārye dūradeśasthe pādukādvitayori

ācāryapatnīputrebhyaḥ preṣayedvastu tatpara

vidhivatyaṃvadhākṛtvā sāṃgāṃ saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ

brahmādaṃ paśyati kṣipraṃ daityasāmrājyanāyakaṃ

prahlādānugrahātsadyoharibhaktima caṃcalo

prapaśyati mahālakṣmīsahitaṃ daivataivṛtaṃ

vāsāṃsiva mahārhaṇi śriyamaṣṭaguṇamapi

anugrahānnaraharermahārṣirjāyate nṛpa

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ natvā sāṣṭīgamādarāt

tadeva sumatyoṭaṃ mahālakṣmāyaśaskaṃ

mahāsiddhipradaṃ sadyo mahāpātakabhaṃjanaṃ

dyarmārthakāmamokṣadi caturvargaphalāthinaḥ

yajeddhīmān mahālakṣmīlakṣmīhṛdayavidyayā

kollāpure mahākṣetre svācāryājñāpurassaraṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāṃ japtvaikalakṣamādarāt

dvādaśākṣaralakṣmīvaṃ śrīṣaḍarṇamathapi vā

japtvā dvādaśasāhasraṃ bhagavadraśmimālikī

ghṛtamrimadhusammiśraistilairvilvadalānvitaiḥ

atha prasannalakṣmya ca trisahasraṃ hunevilaiḥ

hutvā sahasramājyena pratyekaṃ jvalitenale

tat japādihomaṃ kṛtvātha kṛtvā brahmāryaṇāhuti

sāṣṭāṃgaṃ praṇipatyotha ṣaṣṭicihvakahemaja

api codaravaṃdhādi bhūṣaṇani samarpayet

paṃcārvaśatinikaṃ vāsyācaryaraṇeryayet

svarṇalaṃkaranaivastrairviśeṣeṇa prapūjayet

tasmin dine svacakṣubhyaṃ mahālakṣmīsamanvitaṃ

jagajaratnadhanāśvaudyasaṃkulāmacalāṃ śriyaṃ

adhiyaṃ sarvasiddhānāṃ kapilaṃ ca prapaśyati

saṃprāpyayogamaṣṭāṃgasaṃyutaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

anenaiva śarīreṇa jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ

kapilānugrahānnityasasauviharatisvarya

parakāyapraveśādi sāmartha ca vrajatyasau

mārkaṃḍeyasaraḥ snātaḥ snātvā siṃdhau tataḥ paraṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīṃ japtavalakṣamerkayatavrataḥ

daśasāhasrakaṃ japtvā vaiṣṇavaṃ ca ṣaḍakṣaraṃ

tilaistrimadhugavyājyamiśritairayutaṃ hutvā

kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

praṇamya daṃḍavatasya karṇayoḥ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

padmarāgamutaṃ sūkṣmavāsau yuktaṃ samarpya ca

mahastaṃ dakṣiṇa dadyātadardhavātadardhakaṃ

śrenniyārṇāśataṃ sārgrasannaṃ bhojayecca suvāsinīḥ

sadya eva jagannāthaṃ subhadrāsahitaṃ vibhuṃ

paśyatyeva svacakṣubhyā tejomaṃḍalamadhyagaṃ

bhavatyasya sakhānityaṃ mṛkaṃḍutanayomuniḥ

mahepuraṃ sāmrājyamatha brahmapudaṃ tu vā

ayodhyā puramāsādyasrātvāthaśarayūjale

ravṛhatrakrasamabhyarcya śrīśamāvaraṇanvitaṃ

lakṣameka prajaptvātha lakṣamekaṃje yacchriyaṃ

pratyekamayutaṃ japtvāmālāmaṃtraṃ śriyaḥpateḥ

vidhivatsīskṛte vahnau śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

guyadvāgavyaghṛtaṃ śrīmadaṣṭārṇe kṣaryāyutaṃ hunet

vaiṣṇavakāpilājyena sādaraṃ guvadvāgavyaghṛtaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭhārākṣaryāyutaṃ hunet

vaiṣṇavyā ca śataṃ hutvā śrīrāmamanunā tataḥ

bhavadraśmibhihutvāṃ paṃcadhāgoghṛtena ca

agniṃ pradahakṣiṇīkṛtya svācārya caraṇadvayaṃ

padmarāgasamāyuktaṃ kuṃḍadvitayaṃ śubhaṃ

vahukṣīraṃ samarpyāthaniṣkasāhasramādarāt

datvācaryapriyāyai ca datvālaṃkaraṇadikayaṃ

tasmin dine mahārājaṃ bharatādibhiracitaṃ

sākṣātpaśyati cakṣubhyāṃ śrīrāmajānakīpatiṃ

abhīṣṭaṃ savalaṃ datvā śrīsamījagatīpatiḥ

hanūmataḥ prabhāvena siddhayoṣṭhāsimādayaḥ

jarāmaraṇarāhityamapi dīrghāpurakṣayaṃ

vārāṇasī samāsādya viśvanāthasya sannidho

viṃdumādhavamabhyarcya natvā svācāryapādukāṃ

lakṣamekaṃ prajaptvātha bhagavatsannidhau śuciḥ

vāsudevamanupaścādvaiṣṇavaṃ ca ṣaḍakṣarā

japtvā dvādaśasāhasraṃmayutaṃ brahmavidyayā

tataḥ paraṃ hunedagnau śrīmadaṣṭāṇīvidyayā

tilākṣatasamayuktairvitvapatrairghṛtaplutaiḥ

vāsudevasya maṃtreṇa vaiṣṇavyā ca ṣaḍarṇayā

maṃtreṇa juhuyādagnau sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

japadihomaṃ kṛtvātha praṇamyagnimataḥ pare

ṣaṣṭhicihukasaṃtaptahāṭakena vinirmitaṃ

muddikādyairalaṃkāraissamabhyarcya ca sādaraṃ

triśataṃ śataniṣka vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

mahālakṣmīdhiyāpaścātatpatnyā api bhūṣāṇī

dukūlamathavā sarścakaṃ kaṃcukaṃ ca samarpyayet

datvātha dakṣiṇadyaiścasaṃtoṣyavapathāmati

bhūtvā padeṣu bhuṃjīta brāhmāṇaissahavaṃdhuniḥ

paśyatyevasvacakṣurbhyāṃ viśveśvara samanvitaṃ

bhūtvā mūkopimahatī śriyā yamaṣṭha guṇanvitāṃ

bhūtvāṃte yāti sānnidhyaṃ kulakoṭi samanvitaḥ

prātaḥsnātvānvahaṃbhatmānityakarma samāpya ca

śrīmadaṣṭhāksarīvidyāṃ samastanyāsasaṃyutī

mahālakṣmī manuṃ japtvā vāsudevamadyatmanuṃ

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahau śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

trisahasraṃ tilaidevaghṛtamiśraiḥ śriyāhunet

hutvā sahasra pratyekaṃ bhagavadraśmibhistridhā

kuṃḍalādyairalaṃkārairācāryamatha pūjayet

triśataṃ śataniṣka vā tadarthaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

alaṃkāraiścavāsebhissaṃtoṣyatva tataḥ paraṃ

śrīśrīpatidhiyāpaścātoye dakṣiṇādibhiḥ

śrīśrīśāvardhayeddhīmānsahastaṃ triśatādhikaṃ

sākṣātsvapneyi vā paśyatyasau devaṃ śriyaḥ ratiṃ

śrīpate rājñayātasya śakrādyālokapālakāḥ

kalpahunaṃ kāmadhenuṃvitāratnamabhutamaṃ

pītvā mṛtamasau divyamajaraśca marastathā

viharatyakhilānlokānsasiddhagaṃdharvasevitaḥ

cālakanadāyānivyakarmasamāpya ca

lakṣamekaṃ prajaptvātha svācāryājñāpurassaraṃ

japtvātha vāsudevākhyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ca ṣaḍakṣaraṃ

aṣṭhottaraśataṃ japtvāvidhivat saṃskṛtenale

hunedgavyājyamathavā bhānusāhasramādarāt

vāsudevasya manunāmanunā vaiṣṇavena ca

hutvājvaṃjyaline vahnau homaṃ kṛtvā japādibhiḥ

naranārāyaṇaṃ vidyāṃ japedakaṃ sahasrakaṃ

bhūyojapādihomaṃ ca kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ ramyaṃ datvācāryasya karṇayīḥ

pītāṃvarayugaṃ datvā svācāryacaraṇadvaye

nniśataṃ śataniṣka vā tādardha vā tadardhakaṃ

ācāryapatnyai datvā yathā solaṃ karaṇādikaṃ

naranārāyaṇīvidhā prajepatadine śuciḥ

brahmarudrādibhirvedyaṃ niśimāṃ saṃprayaśyati

ajarāmaraṇonityaṃ paṃcaviṃśati vārṣikaḥ

mahāyogitvamathavā brahmatvaṃ vā maheśanāṃ

kṣaṇaṃkṛśaḥ kṣaṇasthūlaḥ śriyamaṣṭaguṇāṃ punaḥ

tiraśībhāṣitaṃ veti sarvajñatvaṃ ca saṃtata

kulakoṭiyutodhīmān praviśatyeva matpadaṃ

kṣetraṇibhūtale saṃti sadyassiddhikarāsayapi

ācāryānujñayā kṛtvā siddhimiṣṭhāṃ vrajatyapi

tatpatnīputrapautrāṇāṃ sānnidhyamathavā nṛpa

kāryaṃ tadakṣiṇadānaṃ pādukādvitayopadi

anena vidhināvapi siddhimiṣṭāṃ vrajatyatha

phalaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyaṃ syānmāyadhātrāpirśabhutanā

samājñātvātha tatparonityaṃ modate brahmarūpabhāk

etasmādadhikaṃ nāsti satyaṃ satyaṃ mahīyate

vasudharādividyānāṃ pravakṣyate sāvanaṃ tataḥ

nīādhidyāvidhāna ca śṛṇuśva śraddhayānvitaḥ

maheṃdrārūṭhagaṇapaviṃdunādasamanvitaṃ

tadeva viṃduhīnaṃ ca bhṛguṃ paṃcamasaṃyutaṃ

vāyuṃ dvādaśasaṃyuktaṃ hṛdayena samanvitaṃ

sarvatīrthamayī sarvakṣetraratnādisiddhidā

varāhosya muniḥ prokto vairājaṃ chaṃda ucyate

dvitīyaṃ vījamityuktaṃ hṛtchaktiḥ samudīritā

śukaṃ dadhānāṃ vasudhāṃ bhajāmahe

sarpiṣapāyasena juhuyā tadvaśāṃśataḥ

tragāni pūjayedādau bhūvahni jalamārutān

jvaliṣmājvālinīcadhūmrārcirititā kalāḥ

sarvasiddhamanormaṃtrī sādhayet samanorayān

ekasminnevadivase prajasrātha parodyapi

gauvarmasammitāṃ bhūmiṃ pakvasusya samanvitāṃ

mahī pratinidhidravyamathavāguraverpayet

madhutritayasaṃyuktairjuhuyādaruṇotpalaiḥ

priyaṃgupuṣpairmadhvaktaihutvā paṃcasahasrakaṃ

avirāllabhate maṃtrīnidhānāni ca paśyati

nityaṃ dvādaśadhā hutvā maṃḍāatsyādvarāpatiḥ

tāmaṃbhāsivinikṣipyaśovinetracaruṃ pacet

ṣaṇmāsaṃśukravāreṣu hutverthaprāmuyādvarāṃ

dharādhidyāṃ prajaptyaikaṃ lakṣaṃ vidhivadarcikte

śuddhātmā ravisāhasraṃ dhanaratnaughasaṃkulā

varāmevaṃ bhajanmaṃtrī paśuraladhanādibhiḥ

dharāyāṃ maṃtramahātmyamiti kiṃcinmayeritaṃ

trapīvījaṃ vṃduyutaṃ caturthasvaramapyathā

mukhavṛtamutāṃ bhūmidhyāṃtyaṃ rudrasamanvitaṃ

hṛdayena samāsuktāṃ nīlāvidyoyamīritā

sarvakṣetramayīsarvalokatrayavaśaṃkarī

nīlādevīdevatāsyādvojaṃ syādvāmalocanaṃ

kālāṃvudasamākārāṃkasuritilakāṃkitāṃ

divyādacaṃdanaliptāgīṃ divyapuṣpasraganvitāṃ

kamalaṃ dadhanīṃ hasteśaraccaṃdrasāmānanāṃ

nīlādevīmiti dhyātvā lakṣamekaṃ japetvaciṃ

ācāryadākṣiṇaṃ datvāvidyāsiddhimavāpnuyāt

japtyāhutvātrisāhasraṃ paredyuḥ kamalaisitaiḥ

datvācārya yadadvaṃdve siddhiṃ prāpnotyanujamāṃ

vasuṃdharāvatpūjāsyārnnīlāyāpibhūyateḥ

ghṛtatrimadhusammiśraṃ daśāṃśaṃ juhuyāddhaviḥ

palāśakusumairhukhā paṃcasāhasramādarāt

aruṇairutpalairhutvā ṣaṭsahasraṃjiteṃdriya

japtvā lakṣamimaṃ vidyāṃ tulasīvanamdhyataḥ

labhate sarvasāmrājyaṃ saṃpatimacalāmayaṃ

kanyāmiṣṭhāmavāpnoti rūpaśīlaguṇānvitāṃ

dhanaṃdhānye caralānividyāṃ ca mahatīṃ labhet

vāsudevādividyānyaṃ māhātmyamathavakṣyate

parabrahmātmakasyādyāvidyāṃ sakalāsiddhidāṃ

vateyadamayoccāryamukhavṛtayutāṃ sudhāṃ

varuṇaṃ dīrghasahitaṃ mārutākṣaramapyathā

samastavedavedāṃtasārabhūtārthasaṃgrahā

svāyaṃbhuvādayaḥ pūrvaṃ vāsudevamahāmanuṃ

dhuvopināradādeśādvādaśārṇaṃ śriyaḥ pateḥ

āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ yaśarthāvanasaṃvayāḥ

dvādaśārṇamanorjāyātsidhyatyakhilasiddhayaḥ

dvādaśārṇanusaṃvānānnityamasya prakāśate

vāsudevamahāvidyājasavyādvādaśākṣarī

vāsudevābhidhaṃ brahmakathyate daivataṃ vudhaiḥ

praṇavena hṛdinyasya namasāśirasinyaset

kavacaṃ paṃcamirvarṇaiḥ samaste nāstramācaret

valayāgadahārakirīṭadharaṃ navakuṃdanirbhapraṇatāmi sadā

arvaviraṇāsaṃyuktaṃ vāptyapīṭhe prapūjayet

aṣṭapatre vāsudevaṃ śriyaṃ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ tataḥ

prītirdvādaśapatre jvetkeśavamāditaḥ

madhusūdanamabhyarcyatrikramāmathaḥ paraṃ

padmanābhaṃ samabhyarcyadāmodaramataḥ paraṃ

itthamabhyarcyaviśveśaṃ vāsudevaṃ jagatpatiṃ

hutvā śubhratilairgavyaghṛtannimadhumiśritaiḥ

vāsudeva mahāvidyāsiddhimāpnoti śāśvatīṃ

dvādaśyāṃ ca samabhyasyā divārātramananyadhīḥ

visahasraṃ śubhatilairvarṣāśanamitaṃ vasu

kṣīrāktāmirasyakṣīrasamidbhirjvalite nale

goṣṭhe vṛṃdāvanevāpi lakṣajāpī payovrataḥ

pāragaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ bhagavatpatra na saṃśayaḥ

hutvā dvādaśa sāhasraṃ havirājya madhuplutaṃ

kaṃdrpasadṛśaḥ kāṃtyā jīveccaśaradāṃśataṃ

mirāmapaścirāpuṣpalabhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ

ayunatritayaṃ hutvā bhagavatkalayānvitaḥ

āyurārogyamatulabhadvi putrādi saṃyutāṃ

pathurāyāyāmadhaḥ śāyīhaviṣyāśī jiteṃdriyāḥ

kimanena japenāśunāsādhayatimaṃtravit

***** siddhaṃ tvaṃ khecaratvamathāpi vā

dyadaśākṣaravidyāpāyakrakukrapi vā japāt

kulānāṃ śatamudhatyaṃ modate paramepade

yasya vijñānamātreṇa palāyaṃte mahāpadaḥ

udvaredvaiṣṇavovidyāṣaḍarṇeyasudīritā

mahāpātakasaṃhartrī bhuktimukti phalapradā

devatā śrīmahāviṣṇuparamātmā śriyaḥpatiḥ

ekena ca tribhirdvābhyāṃ dvirāvṛtyāṣaḍaṃgakaṃ

mūle kalmatarordivyaratnasiṃhāsane śubho

śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ

padmapatraviśālākṣaṃ kāmakoṭi samadyutiṃ

sahitaṃ dakṣiṇe pārśve dhatacāmarahastayā

vimalotkarṣiragījñānādyaṣṭhaśaktibhirāvṛtaṃ

viṣṇuṃ viśvaṃbharavaṃdejajattrāṇāparāyaṇāṃ

jayet ṣaḍakṣarīṃ vidyāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ sarvasiddhidāṃ

ācāryasannidhīkṛtya daśāṃśaṃtarpayetyayaḥ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdve datvā vahuladakṣiṇāṃ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇeyoge paṃcaviṃśatsahasrakaṃ

hutvātilairghṛtābhyaktaiḥ strisahasraṃ samāhitaḥ

vidyāsiddhimavāpnoti sadya eva mahartarā

jatvālakṣannayaṃ vidyāṃ vidhivat saṃskṛte nale

aṇimādi guṇānprāpya sarvajño bhavati dhruvaṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityaṃ hutvāgrau vidhivacchuciḥ

pāragaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ bhavatyatra na saṃśayaḥ

ataḥ paramasaunityaṃ maheṃdrādīnprapaśyati

anenaivaśarīreṇa tejomapatanurbhṛśaṃ

ṣaḍakṣarī mahāvidyāmanvahaṃ prajapatrimāṃ

praṇavaṃ nāma sāyuktaṃ viṣṇaveśabdamapyathā
