varuṇaṃ dakṣanetrārghā dvitīyoṣmāṇamapyatha

ekādaśākṣrīvidyā vaiṣṇuvīyamudāhṛtā

āyurārogyasaṃpatikāriṇī bhuktumuktidā

mahāpātakasaṃhatrīṃ mahāritaghātinī

praṇavena māyuktā japtavyā satataṃ dvijaiḥ

icchatā satataṃ japyā viṣṇyaurekādaśākṣarī

traiṣṭumacchaṃda ukraṃsyānmahāviṣuśriyaḥ patiḥ

hṛtdyakriruktālakṣmīśaprītaye viniyujyate

punastrivarṇevasubhiḥ ṣaḍaṃganyāsa īritaḥ

paṃcalakṣaṃtuyenmonīpuraścaryārthamādarāt

tadvaśāṃśaṃ hunedagnau tilairājyamadhuplutaiḥ

sadya eva labhedvidvāsiddhiṃ sumahatīmapi

ārabhya paurṇamāsyaṃtaṃ paṃcaviśatsahasrakaṃ

trisahasraṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ niśitvāyatavrataḥ

prāpnotyatra na saṃdeho vidyāsiddhiṃ mahatarāṃ

suṃdarīlabhetaṃ kanyārūpaśīlaguṇanvitaṃ

mahadārogyamāsādyajīvedvarṣasahasrakaṃ

arkasāhasrekaṃ sākṣātkaṃdarpaśatasatibhaḥ

ghṛtannimadhusaṃmiśraṃ pāyasaṃ jvalite nale

grahaṇe sparśamārabhyamokṣakālāṃtamādarāt

pivedvāpāyayedvāpi sarvavidyāprakāśanaṃ

skhalahrīspaṣṭa vāgbhūtvā sakṛddhā śravaṇādapi

niṣkāṇāṃ paṃcakaṃ vāpi diniṣkaṃ vāpi kāṃcanaṃ

aṣṭhāṃgayogasidhyarthaṃ tulasīvanamadhyagaḥ

ghṛtatrimadhusaṃmmiśrerhutvātha jvalite nale

japādekādaśākṣaryā vaiṣṇavyānityamādarāt

yaṃtrapūjādikaṃ sarvamanayorvāsudevavat

guṇavaṃtaṃ śriyāyuktaṃ māyuṣmāṃtaṃ labhesutaṃ

bhūtvā jñānīlatenmuktiṃ kulānāṃ śatasaṃyutaḥ

kathitaṃte mayā bhūyamāhātmyaṃ kiṃcidiṣṭhadaṃ

yajñeśavidyāmahātmyamakhila śrutisaṃgrahaṃ

yajñeśavidyā mahātmyaṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhūyatai

hṛdayena samāyuktaṃ viduhīnaṃ śriyīkṣaraṃ

vasuṃdharākṣarapaścānmārutena samanvitaṃ

kṣetraiśākṣaramuccārya śrīmadaṣṭārṇamuccaret

aṣṭādaśākṣarī proktā sarvajñā'jñaparelapradā

ādhivyādhi harīnṛṇāṃ sarvasāmrājyā saṃddhidā

sarvalokahitodyuktā devairāpi sadārcitāḥ

dṛṣṭā praṇamya sāṣṭhāṃgaṃ papraccharidamāhṛtā

namaskurmovayaṃ yuṣmatpādābjebhyomaharṣayaḥ

bhaviṣyāmonṛlokesmin kathaṃ vā brahmarūpiṇaḥ

avunanvātaraśanānṛṣīndīrghatapasvinaḥ

jagatohitaṃ vedāṃteṣvaṣavedeṣu sārabhūtaṃ sanātanaṃ

devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca mahātmanāṃ

yāgairakhiladevānābhaṃ bhavenmukte kaṇatrayāt

kathyate sarvavedeṣu vedāṃteṣvapi bhūyeta

ṛṇatrapavimuktiḥ syāttānubherbhūridukṣiṇaiḥ

dvijaḥ saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ yāgaṃ kṛtvaikamapi vā bhuvi

paurṇamāsyāṃ tathā darśakṛtveṣṭimadyanāśinī

itthaṃ pavitroyajvāsau brahmāryaśādhipabhuviḥ

atyatyadakṣiṇe yagopanamānaṃ vināśayet

niḥphalānātra sasaṃdehaḥ kiṃcanārakaduḥkhadāḥ

brahmārpaṇadhiyāt yasya brahmasya brahmasālokyasiddhidaḥ

vedahīno vṛthā vipro vṛthā vidyāpya vaidikī

nāmadhādakāviprosāvayajyāsaṃmudīritaḥ

punāti sakalaṃ vaśaṃ daśapūrvaṃ daśāvaraṃ

pūrṇāyāmathadaśavāvaiśākhe kārtikepi vā

tasyā puṇyaphalaṃ vaktaṃ nāhaṃ śaktomibhūyate

yāgārtha dravyadānasya phalaṃ dhānnāmayātpi vā

mahādānaśataṃdataṃ kurukṣetre ravigrahe

śrotriyāyadaridrāyadrāvya vaivāhikaṃ dadan

vivāhārthaṃ dhanaṃ datvā viparvaṇivaiṇivairave

sāhasraśassamutvātyāsahitasraiḥ pitāmahaiḥ

ahutāgnirayajvā ca durbrāhmaṇa iti smṛtaḥ

tasrādavaśyaṃ kartavyoyāgassaṃpūrṇadakṣiṇāḥ

yāgeṣvanadhikarīyassu ācārya yadāṃvuje

vedavidyuḥ śrotriyebhyaḥ suvarṇaṃ śatakoṭiśaḥ

phalamāsādya tejasvī sahasrakulasaṃyutaḥ

brahmaṇāsahasāyujyaṃ prāpnovaśriyaḥpateḥ

puṣpabhyaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmah. suguptamapi vaidikaṃ

yajñanāyaṇīnāmnā śrīmadaṣṭādaśākṣarī

ācārya kṛpapālabdhā sarvaṛtuphalapradā

yajñanārāyaṇīvidyāṃ prajeyā susamāhitaḥ

hutvācārya padāṃbhoje datvā śaktyā ca dakṣiṇaṃ

mahatīṃ supuraścaryā laghīvāṃ pūrṇadakṣiṇaṃ

samastavidyāsramrājñī śrīmadaṣṭādaśākṣarī

anayāsadaśīvidyā jagatotra yayāvanī

śatakoṭimitasvarṇadakṣiṇasahitān ṛtūn

aṣṭādaśārovidyāpuraścaraṇo nṛpa

uddhātyānparakemagrānpitṛnathapitāmahān

aṣṭādaśākṣarīvidyāmunirbrahmā prajāpatiḥ

arddhāṃgaśrīyuto devaḥ kathyate jagatīpatiḥ

trapyādyayāśriyā kuryāt paścāvyāsaṃ ṣaḍaṃgakaṃ

nyasya vānyasā vādhīmān dhyāyet svahṛdavāvuje

āsīnaṃ jagadābhāsaṃ vīdālasamadyutiṃ

karjamuktāphalābhadaṃtāliṃ sasmitā dharavidumaṃ

vibhrāṇaṃ ca gadāṃ ghaurāṃ vāmāṃge vidyudāvṛtiṃ

kirīṭahārakeyūrakuṃḍalādivibhūṣitāṃ

sahitaṃ vimalādyaṣṭamahiṣībhissamanvitaṃ

sunaṃdanaṃdapramukhaiḥ pārṣaderūpaśobhitaṃ

vidājitapadadvaṃdvamāpannārtiharehariṃ

nārāyaṇaṃ bhajenityaṃ paramātmānmacyutaṃ

aṣṭādaśākṣarīvidyā japtvā ṛtuphalaṃ labhet

pāvano jagatyasmin sarvādyaudya vivarjitaḥ

mahāpātakayuktāśca nityamaṣṭādakṣmaṇakṣarī

aṣṭādāśākṣarīvidyā japtyeyamepivat

śrīmadaṣṭārṇavatproktamasyāyaṃnnārcanādikaṃ

jiteṃdriyo haviṣyāśī kṣīrāśī vā phalāśanaḥ

daśāstrārcanāśa juhuyātpatyairutpalairvādyutaplutaiḥ

ācāryacaraṇardvadvedatvā saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇāṃ

athaṇa vā kārtikemāsivaiśākheśrāvaṇopi vā

paṃśaviṃśati sāhasraṃ prajaptvāṣṭā daśākṣārī

śataniṣkaṃ tadardha vā vaṣīśanamitaṃ tu vā

paṃcaśitisaṃkhyākamithunānyapi bhojayet

atha kāmyāni karmaṇi sādhe yadatayādvijāḥ

daśāśakamalairhutvā ghṛtatrimadhumiśritaiḥ

paścimahāraliṃgetha vṛṣabhūnyevivālaye

tatra vilvadalairhutvā gavyājya madhumiśritaiḥ

muktāratnasamākīrṇamacalāṃ ca parāṃ śriyaṃ

nābhidaghrajalesthitvāmaṃḍalaṃ triśatādhikaṃ

śriyādhanadasaṃkāśo vidyayādhiṣaṇayame

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ japtvākṛtvā ghṛtaṃ śataṃ

svacchaṃdamaraṇe bhūtvā vyādhibhirnabhibhūyate

aṣṭonaraśataṃ nityaṃ hutvā prajvalite nale

karva āṣṭottaraśataṃ nityaṃ laghubhiḥ kadalīphalaiḥ

maṃḍala dvitayenā sau saptadvīpādhināyakaṃ

madhukaiṭamahaṃ tāraṃ dhyātvārdvāgaśriyaḥpatiṃ

duṣṭānajñabhayāti kṣipraṃ nigṛhṇātyapi rājasān

agnau dvādaśasāhasraṃ saṃprāpya mahatī śriyaṃ

amāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca śatamaṣṭottaraṃ ghṛtaṃ

suvarṇaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ datvātatpalyaitatsutāyavā

pūjyatepi mahedyaiḥ kimumartyermahītale

vṛhaspati samaḥ sākṣādatvāṃ vidyāsusādhakaḥ

satyalokevasatyaṃte kulānāṃśatasaṃyutaḥ

yajñanārāyaṇī japtvā paṃcavimśatsahasrakaṃ

sahasratritayaṃ hutvā varṣāśanamitaṃ dhanaṃ

avaśyaṃ hayamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnotyanuttamaṃ

maheṃdrasadṛśo lakṣmyavidyayādhiṣaṇa prabhaḥ

bhuktvā suvipulānbhogān divyānapyatha mānuṣān

svīḍugrahaṇe sarvasaṃkrāṃniṣṭapi saṃtataṃ

japtvā ca vidhivatilairājyavimiśritaiḥ

na śataśopi samuddhatya satyaloke nayatyasau

arddhāṃgaṃ śrīpati vahnau dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ hariṃ

japet paṃcaśataṃ vāpi catuḥśātamathāpi vā

sāyāṃ kṛśānusānnidhye śatamaṣṭottarajapet

caturṇamapivedānāṃ phalāṃ pārāyaṇodbhavaṃ

sāṃgadakṣiṇayāyuktahayamedhaphalaḥ tathā

yaduktaṃ maṃtramāhātmyaṃ maṃtramāhātmyaṃ mayātatubhyamīritaṃ

yeṣāṃ vijñānamātreṇa palāyaṃte vipadgaṇaḥ

brahmanārāyaṇīvidyā saṃproktā paramarṣibhiḥ

navākṣarīyaṃ saṃproktā brahmanārāyaṇī śubhā

brahmadarśanasidhyarmyamāśuvijñānasiddhaye

ārṣadyaṃdādikaṃ cāsyāḥ sarvamaṣṭārṇamaṃtravat

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīyaṃścadvaṃ sa nārāyaṇīritā

haṃsanārāyaṇīvidyā japtavyāmuktisiddhaye

prādhānyena na japtavyaṃ vidyādūyamidaṃ gṛhe

gṛhasthai ca vanasthaiścapatibhirbrahmacaribhiḥ

asyapi śrīmadaṣṭārṇamaṃtravatsarvamīritaṃ

nārāyaṇapadaṃ paścādvidyeyaṃ dvādaśākṣarī

ārṣādikamapi proktamasyā aṣṭārṇamaṃtravat

śropapadaṃ namasāyuktaṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ tataḥ

praṇākamalāvījaṃ caturthyaṃ samanvitaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīpaścādvidyeṣā ṣoḍaśākṣarī

aṣṭādaśārṇatvadvidyādvayāsvarṣādikaṃ smṛtaṃ

vakṣyethayājuṣīrghā lakṣmīnārāyaṇī śubhā

kurvāṇavīramityuktvāpadamātmana uccaret

annapātre ca śabdaṃ ca sarvadā padasapyatha

lomaṇaṃ paśubhiḥ paścāt sahasvāhonamaḥ padaṃ

ayamadhīgalakṣmīśamaṃtroyājuṣa ucyate

yājuṣīyaṃ mahāvidyālakṣmīnārāyaṇī smṛtā

lakṣmīnārāyaṇaḥsvāmīdeva ukto maharṣibhiḥ

puṃḍarīkākṣalakṣmyoḥ

puraścaryā viśoṣonnakṣyate vasudhāyate

ghṛtatrimadhuyuktena haviṣā juhuyādatha

vidyāyāssiddhimāpnoti śāśvatī nātrasaṃśayaḥ

ghṛtatriadhusaṃmiśraṃ havihutvā sahasrakaṃ

prāpnoti mahatī siddhiṃ dadyāyā nātrasaṃśayaḥ

mṛṣṭānnārāśilabhate vipinepi na saṃśayaḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā vidyāmenāṃ śriyaḥpateḥ

ācārye sannidhīkṛtya tulasīvanamadhyagaḥ

svabhāvavaidikīvidyā śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī śubhā

gāyatryādi mahāvidyā asyārasmaya īritāḥ

sakṛtsmaraṇatopyetāḥ siddhidāssadya ehi

ācāryacaraṇādvaṃdvaṃ sevāpratyahamādarāt

calācatvarpitāni bhagavātsānnidhyaṃ prāpayaṃti hi

hayagrīvādi vidyānāṃ māhātmyanayakṣyate

yasya vijñānamātreṇa vācāṃ siddhirbhavet parā

sarvavāgīśvaretyaṃte pravadedīśvaretyatha

vodhayadvitayotoyaṃ maṃtrastārādirīritaḥ

tādeṇayādairmaṃtasya paṃcāṃgāni pravinyaset

rathāṃgasaṃkhāṃcita vāhuyagmaṃ jānudvayanyastakaraṃbhajāmaḥ

daśāṃśaṃ vaiṣṇuve vahnau hātvācārya padāṃvuje

vidyāsiddhimāvāpnoti yadvāvarṇasvahasrakaṃ

trisahasraṃ hunedagnau triśādhikamādarāt

praṇama vā datvā svācāryakarapaṃkaje

aṣṭākṣarodite yaṃtre hayagrīvaṃ prapūjayet

vipadbhagusthamaghośavidrumadvījamīritaṃ

vidikṣvaṃgasmṛtinyāya sarvaṇasrāṇi pūjyet

vādyetlokeśvārāsteṣī vajrādyastrīṇiyaṃ yajet

vailvaiḥ phalaiḥ kṛto homaḥ śrīkaraḥpati kīrtitaṃ

manunānena saṃyuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ brāhmī rasānvitaṃ

vacāmanena saṃjaptvāṃ bhakṣayet prātaranvahaṃ

manorasya samonāsti jñānaiśvāryapradāyakaḥ

vedāharaṇaśabdaṃ ca hetubhūtāyaśavṛtaḥ

matsyā kṛteripavidyāśrīpateḥ paramātmanaḥ

aṃgironni prabhṛtayojayaṃtī mahāmanuṃ

dharmārthakāmamokṣākhya puruṣārtha phalapradā

chaṃdogāyatramevoktaṃ mahāmatsyā kṛtirhariḥ

vījaṃnnayīmape proktaṃ hṛcchaktiḥ samudīritā

śaratpūrṇedasaṃkāśaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasannibhaṃ

kuṃḍaladvayasaṃśobhimukhābjaṃ matsyarūpiṇāṃ

skaśaṃkhacakradharaṃ mudrocinmayī dakṣiṇe kare

vidhātre sakalānvedānprayacchaṃtaṃ jagatpatiṃ

bhagavaṃtaṃ bhajeviṣṇuṃ sarvadevemarpa vibhuṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityaṃ japtvā prāpnoti vāchitaṃ

kutaryaye tadṛśīśena kṣīreṇekṣurasena vā

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdve datvā vahuladakṣiṇāṃ

hutvā nīllotpalaisṣta sahasrāṇi ghṛtaplutaiḥ

japtva vidhāmimāṃ lakṣaṃ tato raktāṃvujai hunet

mahārṇaveni magnopi smṛtve maṃtramādarāt

japteyaṃ lakṣamātreṇamūkaṃ maṃdadhiyaṃ tu vā

vilvapatrairghṛtābhyaktairhutvā bhānusahasrakaṃ

tatprasādena labhate vāṃchitaṃ sarvamakṣayaṃ

kuṣvāsahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ nityaṃ haviṣyabhuk

samastābhīvṛsaṃpraptihaitu bhūtomanūjamaḥ

oṃ namaḥ padamābhāṣyapadaṃ bhagavate tataḥ

vidyāsaptadaśārṇoyaṃ śrīpateḥ kūrmārūpiṇaḥ

śrīpateḥ kūrmarūpasya brahmasāyujyasiddhidā

kūrmarūpimahāviṣṇurdevatājagatāṃpatiḥ

śyāmavarṇa caturvāhukaṭamaṭhākṛtimacyutaṃ

āsīnamāsanedivye hemaratnamayeśubhe

divyāmaraṇasaṃyuktāṃ divyasragvastradhāriṇī

vaṃdyamiṃdrādibhirdevaiḥ prasannavadanaṃ vibhuṃ

asyāśca matsyavidyāyāyaṃtrapīṭhārcanādikaṃ

lakṣameka prajaptvādau daśāśaṃkaravīrakaiḥ

vahulāṃ dakṣiṇaṃ datvā maṃtrasiddhimavāpnuyāt

śvāyānugrahasāmarthyaṃ labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ

prāpnoti mahatīṃ lakṣmīdhanadhānyādi saṃkulāṃ

sarvābhīṣṭamavāpnoti muktiṃ cāpnotyanuttamāṃ

anvahaṃ salabhedbrahmasāyujyamapidurlabhaṃ

iti kiṃcinmayāproktaṃ varāhasyātha vakṣyete

yaṃtrārcanaṃ bhūyayatkṛtvā siddhimānbhavet

yacchutvāpi na rodhīmānumucyatapāpadāśibhiḥ

rūpāyabhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ syātpatayetadanaṃtaraṃ

vahni jāyāvadhirmaṃtraḥ syāccatustriṃśadakṣaraḥ

bhūpatitvādisaṃsiddhiprado duḥkhaudyabhedataḥ

devātādivārāhātmā bhagavāniṃdiśapatiḥ

śikhātejodhipataye viśvarūpāvarma ca

mahādaṃṣṭrā paṃcāstrasyā paṃcāga kalpayet punaḥ

kṣitidharaṇalasadaṃṣtamādyaṃ varāhaṃ

juhudyāmadaśāṃśena svācāryacaraṇadveya

vahudakṣiṇaṃ pāyuktāṃ datvā siddhimavāpnuyāt

mārgaśīrṣābhidhemasikamalaistrimadhuchutaiḥ

sadakṣiṇaṃ mahīdatvā siddhamaṃtraḥ prajāpate

mūrtimūlena saṃkalpya vakṣyamāṇavidhānataḥ

astraṃ kīṇeṣvadhaścordhā cakrādyastrāṇi tadvahiḥ

saṃpūjya vādyet lokeśānvahirastraṇi pūjayet

prayacchatyeva pūjādyairdhanadhānyamahīśriyaḥ

paṃcagavyeṣu nikṣipya spṛṣṭātāmayutaṃ japet

śatrucoramahābhūtaiḥ kṛtāṃ vādhāvi nāśayet

mṛtikāṃsajapenmaṃtratāṃ punarvibhanennidhā

godugdhe paramālopraśodhitaṃ taṃḍulaṃ kṣipet

avatārya caruṃ paścādagnaudevaṃ yathāvidhi

juhuyā udite vahnau yāvadaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

prātaḥ kāle bhagorvāremṛdaṃ sādhyamahītalāt

virodho naśyati kṣetre sahacārādyupadravaiḥ

trisahahṛstraṃ prajuhuyātasyasyussarvasaṃpadaḥ

samṛddhadhānyasaṃghātaiḥ śābhate tasya maṃdiraṃ

kanyāmavāpnoti madhvaktairnijavāṃchitāṃ

atha yajñavarāhasya yaṃtraṃ vakṣye phalapradaṃ

parastādvasuhalakamalaikesarastha svarādye

bhūviṃvenāsya koṇeṣu bhūvījaṃ sādhyaṃ saṃyutaṃ

sādyīśarvidugaganaṃ vījaṃ vārāhamīritaṃ

gomayāṃbhasi saṃpiṣṭairlikhedyaṃtraṃ śubhe dine

svarṇapīṭe likhedyaṃtraṃ rājyaśrīsamavāhyeya

bhūrjapatrainijeṣṭāsyaikṣaumebhūsiddhayelikhet

kṣudrāya mṛtyucaurādibhūtavyālamahābhayaiḥ

yasya śravaṇamātreṇa layaṃ yāṃti vipadgaṇaḥ

mahāviṣṇuṃ tṛtīyasyā jvalaṃtaṃ padamapyatha

bhīṣaṇaṃ bhadramuktvātha mṛtyumathoccaret

maṃtrādau praṇavaṃ vāmalocanakhaṃ samuccaret

āhuṣṭabha mahāvidyāmirtthakecijjapaṃti hi

śuddhamānunuṣṭabhaṃ maṃtraṃ japaṃti bhuvikecana

nānayā sadṛśī vidyādaṣṭā sakalasiddhidā

chaṃda ānuṣṭamaṃ proktaṃ paramātmāsanātanaḥ

anaṃtāvījamityuktaṃ śaktiḥ śaktirūdīritā

tataḥ paraṃ punaḥ padadvāyenāpi ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vinyasetataḥ

śirolalāṭadāyugmamukheṣu karasaṃdhiṣu

galapārśvāsakakudikramādvaṇīnnyasenmanoḥ

dīptajihvadaṃṣṭograṃ bhūtakeśaṃvadanamapivahanyātumānārasiṃhaḥ

yadvā śāṃtākṛtiṃ dhyāyennārasiṃhaṃ jagatpatiṃ

idhavalaṃ lakṣmī nṛsiṃhaṃ bhaje

mūtiṃ mūlena saṃkalpya pūjayītvepacārakaiḥ

pakṣīdraṃśaṃkarānaṃtaudiṣu brahmāṇamarghayet

bhaskārīvaśyakārītathāmāṇākāryapi

vidyetpāvaraṇe pūjyāstṛtīye vasudhāpate

śaṃkhaṃ kheṭhaṃ gadāṃpāśamarcayedevamataḥ

śrīvatsaṃ dkṣiṇe madhye vanamālāṃ ca pūjayet

bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvāṇi purībhāge tu pūjayet

bhayānakāṃ ārdradīptiṃ cāṣṭadikṣupūrvādi kramato yajet

yadvā śivāvṛtiṃ dhyātvā nṛsiṃha prajapenmanu

āṃtramālādharaṃ raudrakaṃṭhahoraṇaśobhitaṃ

dhanuśramuśalaṃ caiva tathā cakraṃ ca bhinnataṃ

iṃdraṃ gopakanīlābhaṃ caṃdrasvārṇasannibhaṃ

eva mugrahari dhyāyet sarvavyādhinivṛtaye

yoryayahāmahatkarmatṛdāṣoḍaśahastakān

dvauvihahaṇakarmāḍhyaudau cāṃtroddhrāraṇocitau

khaḍgakheṭadharau dvau gadāpadmadhāriṇai

iti ṣoḍaśakodaḍaṃmaṃḍinaṃ nṛhariṃ vibhuṃ

dhyeyomahatamekāryeṣoḍaśadvayahastanān

dakṣiṇe cakrapameva paraśuṃpākṣameva ca

satathāpadiśaṃtidiyātaṃ cakhe'muhuratomarān

śūlakaṃ agniṃ ca varadaśaktiṃ kuṃḍikāṃ ca tataḥ paraṃ

karābhyāṃ ca riporjānumastapīḍanaṃ

aṃdhasthitābhyāṃ vāhubhyāṃ hiraṇyakavidāraṇaṃ

nṛsiṃhaṃ saṃsmaderedittha mahāmṛtyubhavāyahaṃ

kṣudraviṣagrahāmapariyupradhaṃsanaṃ śrīkaraṃ

sutīrthātoyasaṃpūrṇanyastraralauṣadhānvitān

nṛsiṃhaṃ pūjayeṣṭau pūrvādiṣu yathā kramaṃ

abhiragrahonmādāvinaśyaṃtyiribhissaha

yathāvatpūjayedbhaktyāśaktyācārya padadvayaṃ

lakṣamekaṃ puraścaryādayutadvitayottaraṃ

trisahasraṃ hunedagnau ghṛtamiśraiḥ kuśeśayaiḥ

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃtvā vidyasiddhimavāpnuyāt

trisahasraṃ hunedagnau ghṛtamiśraiḥ kuśeśayaiḥ

varṣārānamitaṃ durvyadatvācāryasya dakṣiṇāṃ

juhuyādathāpsuśāṃtipraṣātyeva tathopasargā ayohiśāṃtā iti ca śrutiḥ syāt

prāprotyakhilajanatriyaścābhūyāt

japamātamaṃtraśaktyāsusvaprobhavati kṣaṇodeva

asādhitaṃ sadhitameva tasya bhavatyavaśyaṃ mithileśabhūya

ativiṣonabhṛṇapiviṣeṇāpyasau vimuktaḥ sukhī bhavatyavaśyaṃ mithileśabhati

aṣṭottaraśatajapenābhimaṃtritaṃ ca bhasmādyaṃ sa śiroṣṇi

maṃtrauṣadhābhicārakṛtānvikārānayaṃ manuḥ śamayet

saṃprāpyamedhāsaṃtudharmaśāstrairiṣṭārthasiddhiṃ labhate varṣāt

ayutaṃ nāmadhunāktaiśca daridro na bhavetkule

aṣṭapalakṣahomenadena samogbhavet

śatādyonairmalyamāpnoti prasādaṃ cādhigacchati

madhūkapuṣpahomena sarvābhīṣṭaprajapeta

saktuhomenaśālāgnau jagadvaśyaṃ prajāpate

kadalīphalahomena sarvavighnaurvimucyeta

juhuyādayutaṃ yo sauśataṃ jivati roḍājit

japtvā jihvāsau yamṛtyuśaṃtaṃ vārṣāṇi jīvati

vāṃkasthitayālakṣmyāliṃgitaṃ padmahastayā

siddho bhūtve hasataṃtaṃ sākṣādbrahmamayo bhavet

padmamaṣṭadalapūrvaṃ viracapya samāhitaḥ

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ sumanoharaṃ vilikhet praṇavaṃ tatra nṛsiṃhaikādarṇagarbhitaḥ

māpayāveṣṭhayitvātha vasupatraimanohare likhitvā

dvātriṃśāraṃ mahāpadmaṃ tadvahirveṣṭayedathe

dvātriṃśadatpadmapatrāṇiśatmātātyapiveṣṭhayet

tataḥ tatpūrvabhāge saṃlikhyahādīnekā daśākṣarān

tadanataravarṇānapyaṣṭau tatra samālikhet

vṛtasya pārśvayorarṇadvalekhyaṃ prapalataḥ

pūjayeddhārayedyadvāsarvapāpaivirmucyeta

avāpya mahatīmedhāmaṃte brahmamayo bhavet

āyurārogyasaṃpatikāraṇaṃ putravarddhanaṃ

iti kiṃcinmayā proktaṃ vāmatasyātha vakṣyate

pathīpadiṣṭāṃ vakṣyamividyāṃ sakalasiddhidāṃ

dakṣaśrotrayutaṃ ṣāṃtyaṃ dvitīyāṃ tasya mapyatha

vāyurudrasamāyuktamaṃtasthādyamataḥ paraṃ

ṛṣirdhanadaputrosya nalakūvā ucyeta

vinyaseddhīmān varṇanyāsamathanyesan mūrdhni

keṭeca vāhuyugale nābhau pṛṣṭhai ca guhyake

jānvopādayuge varṇannyasediti yathākramaṃ

vibhracchrī vāmanākhyaḥ satatamavatunoviriṣṭorthadāyī

savyasahasre dadhānaṃtarati sakalaḍaḥ khādvāmanaṃ bhāvayedyaḥ

mūlenamūrtisaṃkalpya vāmanaṃ viṣṇumarcayet

prītistuṣṭiśca puṣṭiścavṛddhirṣṭatistataḥ paraṃ

śaśinī durmātā caiva lakṣmī chāyā tathaiva ca

etābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ proktaṃ dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ śubhaṃ

tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ proktakumudaḥ kumudekṣaṇaḥ

sarpanetraśca sumukhassupratiṣṭhita ityapi

yamaṃkhaḍgaṃ ca muśalaṃ śārṅavāṇānataḥ paraṃ

itthamabhyarvyasatataṃ vāmanaṃ viṣṇumacyutaṃ

turlakṣaṃ japedādau daśāṃśaṃ tarpatyayaḥ

gośataṃ dakṣiṇaṃ dadyādāthāryasya padadveye

paṃciviṃśati sāhasramaya vāmrāvaṇe japet

varṣāśanamitadravyasahitaṃ ca sa vatsakaṃ

inityaṃ dvādaśadhā vahnau hutvā dadhyannamādarāt

sahasraṃ prajayeprastunityaṃ niyamataḥ śuciḥ

atra kāmohunennityamaṣṭhāviṃśati saṃkhyayā

apūyaṣaḍgasoṣetamannameṣṭa sahasrakaṃ

dadhyannaṃ śarkamarāyuktaṃ hutvā daśasahasrakaṃ

hunedvilvasamīpasthaḥ padmākṣairayutaṃ śuciḥ

vidyāthī prajapellakṣaṃ dhyātvā devaṃ janārdanaṃ

putrakāmo hunellakṣaṃ putrajīvī phalai hunet

dhyātvā trivikramadevaṃ raktābhaṃ karavīrakaiḥ

rājyakāmoyikamalairhutvā daśasahasrakaṃ

apārmāgedalairhutvālaivaṃgairvāmadhuplutaiḥ

ārogyakāmo juhuyādayāmāgaissahasrakaṃ

āyuṣkāmastrimadhaktaistiladūrvākuśakṣataiḥ

smṛtvā trivikramaṃ devaṃ japtvā daśasahasrakaṃ

muktovadhaṃ na tassadyobhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

śiṣṭārāyijñīllasitāṃtyapatramaṣṭhākṣaraihṛjvalapatrakārthaṃ

darṇamakhaṃḍa viṃveyaṃtraṃtvidaṃ śrīmadamīṣṭada ca

ihabhuktvārivalānbhogābhaṃte vahyapadaṃ vrajet

yasyā vijñānamātreṇa vinaśyaṃti vipadgaṇāḥ

jāmadagnyāyaduṣṭeti kṣānniyeti padaṃ tataḥ

reṇukāputraśabdaṃ ca hṛdayena samanvitaṃ

samastaduḥkhaśamanīvairivargavidāriṇī

trayīvījaṃ hṛcchaktiḥ samuddīritā

ṣaḍbhirdvābhyāṃ ṣaḍaṃgāninyasediti samāhitaḥ

jamadagnisutaṃ vīraṃ dadhānaṃ paraśuṃ śubhaṃ

namāmibhārgavaṃ rāmaṃ śiṣyaṃ gaurīyetarvibhuṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā śāśvatīṃ siddhimaśnute

daśāṃśaṃ pāyasaṃ hutvā ghṛtatrimadhumiśritaṃ

labhate mahatī siddhiṃ samastaphalāsiddhirddā

triśatādhikasāhasraṃ hunetyāya samādarāt

siddhiprāpnoti mahatī vidyāyānātrasaṃśayaḥ

siṃhavyāghrādibhītauvarakṣaḥ kūrṣmādujebhaye

vimuktassakalāyadbhyaḥ sadya eva bhavatyasau

japtvā vidyāmimāṃ nityaṃ sahasratrayamādarāt

gīṣṭhe samudgatīre vā guroścaraṇasannidhau

hutvā palāśakusumairdatvācāryasya dakṣiṇāṃ

sākṣāddhiṣaṇasaṃkāśo bhavatpatra na saṃśayaḥ

arkasāhasramāprīti vipulāṃśriyamakṣayāṃ

sākṣātpaśyati cakṣurbhyāṃ bhārgavaṃ rāmamavyayaṃ

āyuṇarogyamaiśvaryaṃ vasūni paśavopi ca

mūrtimūnaṃ na saṃsakalpyapūjayedbhārgavaṃ vibhuṃ

atrimapyanasūryā ca datātreyaṃ śriyaṃ tathā

iṃdrādīnaṣṭadikālāṃścaturasrāṣṭadiśvapi

vijayī satataṃ bhūtvā ramate brahmāṇathade

kiṃ citsādhanamuktaṃte śrīrāmasyāthavakṣyate

pravakṣyāmi mahāvidyāṃ sarvavedāṃtasaṃgrahāṃ

raghunaṃdanaśabdaṃ ca caturthyamta samanvitaṃ

** prasannavadanāyāmite tajesa udīt

mukhavṛtayutayādyayārghrātastha tataḥ paraṃ

uccarejjānakonāthavidyeyaṃ samudīritā

mahādāridya śamanīrājyaiśvarya kalapradā

samastasaṃyatsāmrājyahetubhūtāśivaṃkarī

samastaiśvarya phaladāsarvavidyāpradāyinī

vālmīki mukhyāḥ kavayoḥ nāradādyā purātanāḥ

tārakabrahmavidyeyamakhilānaṃdasiddhidā

rājyābhiṣiktaḥ śrīrāmaḥ paramātmā jagatpatiḥ

praṇavākhyaṃ mahavījaṃ śaktirnatirūdīritā

saptavarṇaiḥ hyataḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ samabhidaṃ śabhistadyā

ayodhyānagareramyekalpadumavanāṃtare

samāsīnaṃ trijagatāṃ pītanīlāṃvudadyutiṃ

akalaṃkaśaraccaṃdrasamavakraṃ madvīpatiṃ

dadhānaṃ cinmayīṃ mudrājānvāsaktamujaṃ vibhuṃ

vaktrāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃdātāraṃ sarvasaṃpadāṃ

vāmāṃkāsūṭajanakakanyayādivyabhūṣayā

vibhīṣaṇāṃ janeyādyaiḥ kiṃkarairnityamarcitaṃ

sugrīvādyaiḥ parivṛtaṃ sumaṃtrādyaṣṭamaṃtribhiḥ

śaraṇyaṃ sarvajagatāṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalaṃ

ṣaṭkāṇaṃ padmamaṣṭāraṃ caturasra samacitaṃ

tanmadhye vilikhet sādhyaṃ vījaṃ kāmasamanvitaṃ

daśākṣareṇabhūyopitahlonaṃ keṣṭhayennṛpa

huṃvījaṃ pratikoṇāgraṃ kesarāgresuvasvarān

daledale likhitvāṃtyayatre paṃcākṣarān likhet

caturasrasya saṃproktaṃ yaṃtrametadramāyateḥ

upacāraiḥ samabhyarcya tadvāme janakātmajāṃ

ātmāṃtarātmaparamajñānātmānastataḥparaṃ

pradyumnotharatiḥ paścādatiruddhastata'paraṃ

āgrehanūmānsugrovo bharatītha vibhīṣaṇa

dhṛṣṭirjapaṃto vijayaḥ siddhārthaḥścārthasādhanaḥ

samabhyarcya vaśiṣṭhau vāmadevastataḥpare

viśvāmitrothavālmīki nīdadaścāmahān ṛṣiḥ

śarabhaśca tatormedodvividovitataḥ paraṃ

gurutmaṃtaṃ samabhyarcya viṣkakasenaṃ samarcayet

ityabhyarcya punardhūpadīpādyairapitoṣāyet

vidyaiśvaryanidhirbhūtvā modate bhagavasade

ācyaryaṃ sannidhīkṛtya ghṛtatrimadhumiśritaṃ

samarpya vahulaṃvitaṃ maṃtrasiddhimavāpnuyāt

ekabhaktāśino bhūtvā tatyurvadivasatrayaṃ

hutvā kṛṣṇatilairagraughṛtannimadhumiśritaiḥ

datvā varṣaśanaṃ dravyaṃ vidyāsiddhimāvāpnuyāt

ārogyaṃ mahadasādyasarvavidyānidhirbhavet

rājyalakṣmīmavāproti daśasāhasrato nṛpa

pāyasenājyayuktena hutvā viṣvānidhi bhavet

jadyāt paṃcāgnimadhyasthaḥ kamalairmadhumiśritaiḥ

dūrvābhirājyasiktāmirhutvā daśasahasrakaṃ

hutvā nīlotpalaiḥ samyagayutaṃ sādhakottamaḥ

hutvā raktāṃvuje ragrāvayutaṃ ghṛtamiśritaiḥ

ghṛtamiśraṃ yayo hutvā ṣaṭsahasraṃ yatavrataḥ

vilvaprasūtairjuhuyādābjatrimadhumiśritaiḥ

karavīrakusumai hutvā lakṣannimadhumiśritaiḥ

aṣṭāviṃśādhikaṃ nityaṃ cirāśataṃ yojayetchutiḥ

rājyābhiṣiktā śrīsamavidyeyasamudīritā

yadanuṣṭānamātreṇa sarvasaubhāgyasiddhibhāk

goviṃdāyadaṃ paścāduteyījan padaṃ tastataḥ

kṛṣṇavidyeyamākhyātāmahāsaubhāgyasiddhibhāk

aināṃ vidyāṃ purādhyā nārado bhagavānapi

jagatritaya sāmrājyahetubhūtāsanātanī

ṛṣirasya vidhirbrahmāgāyatraṃ chaṃda ucyate

kāmarājaṃ vījamuktaṃ śaktiḥ pāvakavallabhā

punaścaturbhirdvābhyāṃ ca ṣaḍāṃganyāsa īritaḥ

kalyātarostriṃbhagilalitadhyāyejjaganmohanaṃ

caturlakṣaṃ puraścaryātsādhakaḥ śuddhamānasaḥ

tadaśāṃśaṃ hunedagnau paṃkajairmadhumiśritaiḥ

ācāryacaruṇadvaṃdve daśāṃśaṃ suvarṇakaṃ

yadvāṣṭādaśasāhasraṃ nabhomāsi japcchuciḥ

hutvā saranuvarargena nirmitaṃ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

datvācāryayadadvaṃdegoyugmaṃca savatsakaṃ

vilikhya ṣaḍaraṃ pūrvaṃ vasupatrāṇyataḥ paraṃ

caturviṃśati patrāṇi vilikhyātha mahīyate

kāmavījaṃ likhenmandhye sādhyakarmasamanvitaṃ

kṛṣṇāṣṭākṣaravarṇaśca vasupatreṣvatho likhet

aṣṭādaśasuyameṣu maṃtrarājākṣarāstratha

tadvataṃ kāmarājena veṣṭayitvā tataḥ paraṃ

tadvataṃṣiṃḍagopālāmaṃtreṇa pariveṣṭayet

pāśenāṃ kuśavījena tṛtīyamatha veṣṭayet

sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ sarvadāridryaudyavibhedanaṃ

mūlenāja samāvādyat yajetkṛṣṇaṃ jagamaṃ

dvitīyaṃ vāsudevādyaiḥ svasvaśakti samanvitaiḥ

sutaṃdāmapi kāliṃdī tato jāṃvavatīmapi

valabhadraṃ revatīṃ ca ca sudevakī

catuthaviraṇebhyarcya śakrādīnatha pūjayet

saṃprāpya sakalābhīṣṭaṃ devavanmodatenvahe

dhyātvārddarātre saptāhaṃ sahasratritayaṃ japan

goṣṭe gopayasāhutvā sahasratritayaṃ śuciḥ

dadhyodanaṃ hunedyastu nityamaṣṭottaraśataṃ

kadaṃvadumamūlakṣyo dhyātvā kṛṣṇaṃ jagatpati

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ

murkadaḥ

dhyāyanamaṃtrīmaṃtrajāpī ca nityaṃ pāpādvidvyaṃnmatratraprayāti

devavanmānuṣeṣu *********

ayutaṃ subhurcirbhūtvā saptarātraṃ dvijo jayet

bhikṣāhārojayenmaṃtra saṃvatsaramataṃdritaḥ

kanyārthī khādirai hutvā daśasāhasramādarāt

dadhimadyājayukūlājerpadvā hutvā yutaṃ vudhaḥ

navanītāśanaṃ kṛṣṇa dhyātvo lūkhaladhanaṃ

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ jatvāpivaṃtaṃ pūjanāstanaṃ

samastaviṣanāśārthaṃ kṛṣṇāṃ kālīyamardanaṃ

vimatātanapārūḍhaṃ haraṃtaṃ pārijātakaṃ

vijayīsatataṃ bhūtvā devavadbhuvimodate

aṣṭottaraśataṃ śuddhomiyate nāyamatyubhiḥ

āyurāṇopamāsādyāvalaṃ tejañcāviṃśati

kaṃdarpasadṛśaḥ kāṃtyāvidyayādhiṣaṇaprabhaḥ

bhūtvā bhuktveha sakalān bhogānapi manoramān

etate kṛṣṇavidyāyāmāhātmyamadhnāśanaṃ

pāsāvijñānajīvāpi pāparāśirvinaśyati

vedamārga pratiṣṭhāṇakartre padaṃ tataḥ

buddharūpasya vidyeyaṃ viṣṇostrijagatīpateḥ

munirasyerito brahmāchaṃdogāyatramīritaṃ

tribhīrvarṇeścaturvaṇairekāda daśabhirakṣeraiḥ

śyāmaṃ prasannavadanaṃ catuvāhuṃ kirīṭinaṃ

buddharūpaṃ mahāviṣṇumiti dhyātvā manuṃ japet

daśāṃśaṃ kamalairddhatvāghṛtannimadhumitiśrataiḥ

vidyāsiddhimavāpnoti mucyate pātakairapi

manumāhāpātakasaṃdyaiśca sadya eva vimucyate

nārāyaṇa jagannāthāṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ vibhuṃ

pravakṣyāmi mahāvidyāṃ mahāsiddhipradāpinīṃ

kalkirūpapadaṃ paścāccaturyyāṃta samanvitaṃ

viṣṇave padamābhāṣya namaḥ śabdaṃ tato vadet

samastakāmaphalaptamāhāyākabhedinī

paṅktiḥ chaṃdaḥ kalkirūpī śrīviṣṇurdevarucyate

ekenākṣarayugmena caturbhiḥ paṃcabhistayā

nīlameṣvanibhaṃ divyaratnasiṃhāsena śrute

sarvaduṣṭaharaṃ yogimunisiddhaniṣevitaṃ

kalkisvarūpiṇaṃ devaṃ sarvalokahiterataṃ

jātvā lakṣaṃ mahāvidyāṃ daśāṃśaṃ tulasīdalaiḥ

dakṣiraṇāṃ vasudhāṃ datvā vahusasyaudyasaṃkulāṃ

aṣṭākṣarodite pīṭhe pūjayet kalkimacyutaṃ

daśāṃśaṃ kamalairhutvā śvetāśvaṃ sa paricchaṃdaṃ

bhūtvehasārvabhaumausaudehāṃte muktimāpnuyāt

dhyātvā kalkiṃ mahātmānaṃ jatvā lakṣamataḥ paraṃ

puccakāmo hunet putrajīvairayutamādarāt

vidyākāmaścahaviṣābhogakāmastāthotpalaiḥ

sahasraṃ triśataṃ vāpi jatvā vā śatamanvahaṃ

nāmatrayamahyavidyāmathavakṣyāmi bhūyate

acyutānaṃtagoviṃdāścaturthīṃ nati saṃyutāḥ

nāmātrayāmi proktā duritaudya vibhedinī

vaśiṣṭho nāradoyogī kaśyapopyaṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ

vāmanodevatā prokto viniyogastu siddhaye

dhyātvā nāma trayaṃ nityaṃ japtvāsiddhimavāpnuyāt

ācāryadakṣiṇaivāsyāpuraścaryāsamīritā

sajapecchatasāhasramadhaḥśāyī jiteṃdriyaḥ

viṣamajvarayuktopi śatasāhasraṃmādarāt

kārayitvā tato homaṃ mamṛtāmirathāpi vā

dīrghamāyuṣmamanvicchanūdūrvāmirayutaṃ śuciḥ

hunetrimadhusammistilairmehasyaśāṃtaye

rājitaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ datvā sāṃgadakṣiṇayānvitaṃ

ājīrṇarogaśāṃtyarthaṃ japtvā śatasahasrakaṃ

sauvarṇāṃ dakṣiṇāyuktāṃ datvā tasmādimucyate

daśāṃśaṃ goghṛtaṃ hutvā maṃtrauṣadharasāya

nāmatrayamahāvidyāṇ japannanvahamādarāt

putrapautrābhivṛddhiṃ ca bhogānapi sudurlabhāne

praṇarvanamasāyuktaṃ padaṃtagavate tataḥ

anaṃtāpadaṃ paścādavyaktāya namaḥ padaṃ

uktāśaṃkarajapteyaṃ mahābhāgyasamṛddhidā

gāyātrameva chaṃdosya sākṣātsaṃkarṣarṇagevibhuḥ

śritiścaturbhiraṣṭābhiścaturbhiścaturakṣaraiḥ

sahasraṃ śirasaṃ devaṃ pharṇasāhasraśobhitaṃ

vāsukyādi mahānāgaiḥ sarvataḥ parivāritaṃ

ānaṃtaṃ paramātmānaṃbhajāmi jagatīdyatiṃ

vidyānāṃ pāragobhūtvā parāṃ muktimupetyasau

mūrtimūlena saṃkalpya samabhyarcyopacārakaiḥ

abhyarcya vasukitakṣakaṃ karkoṭakamapyathā

kumudaṃ ca tataścitraketuṃ nāradaparvatau

itthaṃ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ devabhyarcya trijagasati

hutvātha daśasāhasraṃ tilaiḥ śukermahīyate

vidyāsiddhimavāmmāsau sarvavidyānidhirbhavet

na sarpabhayamāpnoti na bhayaṃ viṣasabhavaṃ

samṛddhimahatīprāpyadhanadhānyādisaṃkulāṃ

ityanaṃta mahāvidyāviddhānaṃ kiṃcidīritaṃ

mamavidyāṃ pravakṣyāmi sadyaḥ sakalasiddhidāṃ

bhagavatpadadamābhāṣya caturthyaṃta samanvitaṃ

nātmyāyāviṃcanapdaṃti tāyapadamapyatha

parametyatha haṃsāyaparameti padaṃ tataḥ

hṛdayadvitayaṃ paścādvidyaiṣā samudīritā

bhedīnī sarvapāānāduḥkhasaṃdyanivāriṇī

nāradāyapurāproktāmayaiva karuṇāvatā

ahaṃ samastaloddhanāmācāryaḥ parikīrtitaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī dātā sohameva mahīyate

teṣāṃ bhuktiṃ ca pradāsyāmina saṃśayaḥ

vadarīkāṃtanenityaṃ nivasāminaranvitaṃ

brahmaviṣṇumaheśedradaivatākṛtiracyutaḥ

dharmeṇa prārthitasya putratvaṃgatavānahāṃ

bhūtvāhaṃ sarvabhaktānāṃ dāsyāmi paramapadaṃ

matparigrahatobhūyadarśanānmokṣasiddhidaṃ

srātvai vālakanaṃdāyāṃ sakṛdṛddhāpimāṃ vibhuṃ

ṣaṣkakṣarīmimāṃ vidyāṃ naranārāyaṇīṃ mama

saṃprāpya mahatī siddhite matpadamavāpnuyuḥ

caturvidhamāhāmuktisiddhideyaṃ sanātanī

tasyāhaṃ pitṛvannityaṃ pradāsye sarvavāṃchitaṃ

siddhavidyāmomāṃ prāhuryogino vījakaṣma

madvidyā siddhiruditāgurudakṣiṇayaiva hi

mamavidyāṃ japedetāṃ naranārāyaṇī śubhāṃ

aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhīnāmādhiyatyaṃma caṃcalaṃ

ādhipatyaṃ maharṣīṇaṃ brahmarṣīṇāṃ ca yogināṃ

naranārāyaṇīṃ vidyāṃ nityaṃ mama japan śuciḥ

aṣṭākṣarādividhānāṃ mayisiddhiṃ parāṃ vrajet

madvidhāyājayenaiva sidhyasaṣṭākṣarīmamā

ekāmapi muhāvidyāṃ mamajaptyānvahaṃ śuciḥ

aṣṭhāṃgayogasahitojarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ

nāradobhagavānasya munirukto maharṣibhiḥ

naranārāyaṇāṃ svāmī devatā procyate vudheḥ

tāṃganyāsa karanyāsau nāpi nyāsāṃtaraṃ nṛpā

suramyelakanaṃdāyāstare vadarikāśrame

vaśiṣṭavedavyāsādimaharṣimirabhiṣṭute

navanīlāṃvudaśmāmaṃjaṭhāmaṃḍaladhāriṇaṃ

dhanurvāṇadharaṃ divyapītāṃvaravirājitaṃ

divyaratnākitaḥ svamakuṭāvṛtamāstakaṃ

smitakaṣkaṃviśālākṣaṃ koṭimanmathasuṃdaraṃ

ācāryaṃ sarvalokānāṃ gurūṇaṃ paramaguruṃ

nārāyaṇaṃ jagadvaṃdyaṃ bhagavaṃtāmupāsmehe

naranārāyaṇīvivāmāmanityaṃ japedbudhaḥ

aṣṭhākṣarādividyānāṃ na siddhiṃ labhate kvacit

naranārayaṇoṃ vidyāmajatvā matavallabhāṃ

labhate na parāṃ siddhimiti vedānuśāsanaṃ

dvayameva mahāsiddhipradaṃ lokaṃ ṣubhūyate

mahādānāni kṛtvāpi puraścaryā śataṃ tu vā

sudarśanamahāvidyāṃ pravakṣyami tataḥ paraṃ

caṃdravījavyomayarṇaṃ caṃdraṃ dīrghā gnisaṃyuta

sudarśanimahāvidyā ṣaḍarṇeyamudīritā

jatavyeyaṃ mahāvidyāvighnasaṃghanivāriṇī

sarvaśatukṣayakarīviṣadāridryaghātinī

sudarśanātmābhagavāndevoviṣṇurūdīritaḥ

sudarśanān hariprītyarthaṃ viniyujyate

śūlapāśāṃkuśāgrīn vibhrāṇāṃ vajrākheṭaṃ

ṣaṭkoṇe saṃsthaṃ sakalaripugaṇaprāṇasaṃhāracakraṃ

dhyātvā japtvā yathā śaktāvighnasadyavivarjitaḥ

vimuktaḥ sakalāpadyaḥ puruṣārtha catuṣṭayaṃ

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vāyuputramahāmanuṃ

praṇavaṃ namasāyuktaṃ padaṃ bhagavate tataḥ

padaṃ mahāvalāyeti padaṃ hanumate tataḥ

maṃtrarāja iti khyāta āṃjaneyasyamāruteḥ

vijayaśrīpradonityaṃ lokatrayavaśaṃkaraḥ

chaṃdogāyatramityuktaṃ maṃjanānaṃdanovibhuḥ

vījaṃ trayīmayaṃ proktaṃ śaktirnati rudīritā

tribhi aśuturbhiṣaṃcārṇaiḥ paṃcabhiścaturakṣaraiḥ

dvābhyāmiti ṣaḍaṃgāni kṛtvā dhyāyenmarutsutaṃ

devedrapramukhāmarastutivahaṃ sītāviśokāyaharmojīrata

dhyātvetthamaṃjanātyanūṃ nityaṃ vijayasiddhidaṃ jatvāvijayamāmotidivivāṇe

puraścaraṇasaṃsiddhikaro proktā maharṣibhiḥ

jayalakṣmīpradonṛṇāmaṇimāṣaṣṭasiddhidaḥ

aṃjanātanapasyāmaṃmupratyahaṃ maṃtranayakaṃ

jvallāvijayamāsodyesarvasiddhiyuto bhavet

aṇimādiguṇeyetātahuheśrīracaṃcalā

yakṣarākṣasevetālayeśācādi mahābhaye

aṣṭaviṃśādhikaṃ maṃtraṃ japtvā triśatamanvahaṃ

viṃdasavala saubhāgyaṃ saprāpnotyanvahaṃ japaṃ

rājānoyāṃtidā satvaṃ śatravomitratāṃ tathā

samṛddhiṃ mahatī labdhvā muktiṃ prāpnoti śāśvatī

yāmupāsya vimuktasyātkālakūṭabhayādapi

mahāgaruḍaśabdaṃ ca caturthyaṃta samanvitaṃ

vajrayakṣaliṃ kṛtādyaśarīrāya padaṃ tataḥ

duṣṭānnāgān chidhi padaṃ duṣṭānaśroccaret

vadohu phaṭ padaṃ paścaduccarediyamiṣṭhada

ṛṣirbrahmāsyamaṃtrasya gāyatraṃ chaṃda īritaṃ

hemādriśikharākāraṃpūrṇacaṃdranibhānaṃ

anaṃto vāmakaṭakoyajñasūtraṃ tu vāsuki

padmastu dakṣiṇe karṇe mahāpadmastuvāmataḥ

dhyātve devaṃ trisaṃdhyādāvātmānaṃ garuḍānmakaṃ

ājānuhemavarṇabhamānābhatestu hinaprabhaṃ

svarṇavarṇaṃ suparṇaṃ

garuḍasya mahāvidyāṃ japtvānityaṃ phalapradāṃ

gāruḍasyāthagāyatrīṃ vaṃdāmi sakaleṣṭadāṃ

caturthyaṃ samanvitaṃ dhīmahīti padīyetaṃ tatrogaruḍa ityathā

praṇavādyā samastādyabhedinī vivanāśinī

chaṃdogāyatramityuktaṃ devatāgaruḍasmṛtaḥ

jadyātgaruḍagāyatrīsaṃharet sakalaṃ viṣaṃ

jadyānvahaṃ paurṇimāsyā mamoyāṃ vā sammahitaḥ

tataṃ tat putrapautrādinapi pādahatā api

yaṣṭottaraśataṃ hunet || kārayedathavānyena cakṣūrogairvimucyate

āyurārogyamacalamaiśvaryamaṇimādikaṃ

tadviṣṇoḥ paramāṃdhāmayātyaṃte nātra saṃśayaḥ

gotrayaṃ dakṣiṇā proktā tanmūlyaṃ vā sadakṣiṇaṃ

viprādvādaśasaṃkhyākābhojanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīsubhāyasyasiddhābhavetsvasyanāsādhyaṃ vidyate bhuvi

tatkṛtaṃ cenmahāsiddhirbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ

bhūyopi śrīmadaṣṭārṇasādhanaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

śṛṇurājanpravakṣyāmi sādhanāṃtaramiṣṭadaraṃ

sarvamaṃtramahārājñī śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīśubhā

śṛṇurājanpravakṣyāmi śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ

bhānuvāsarasaṃyuktāsāsaṃsīmāyānigadyate

ravisaṃkramaṇaṃ tatra jāyate yadi suvrata

āstive tatra saṃkrāṃtiḥ śatakratu phalapradaḥ

brāhmekāle yadi bhavetkratukoṭiphalapradaṃ

ravisaṃkrāmaṇaṃ vājimedhakoṭiphalapradaṃ

bhavedyadihārājatatpurāyaphalamakṣayaṃ

tutrāpi raviṇacāparāśisaṃkramaṇaṃdapadi

āśvamedhasahasrasya phalaṃ vaktu hi śakyate

mahādānasahasrasya rājasūyāyutasya ca

yadi brāhme muhūrte syādavisaṃkramaṇaṃ śubhaṃ

mahācūḍāmaṇirnāmnāyogolabhyo yamuttamaḥ

somena raviṇavāpi sahitaṃ hastasaṃyutaṃ

samastapāpaudyavināsaṃhetulakṣmīrāsaṃpūrṇakaṭhākṣahetuṃ

vāpi tatrāpi kadikubhānusaṃkramoyogādhirājyo bhuvi kathyate sau

raviṇavedunāyuktāmadhyāhnavasuvāpate

cāpasaṃkramaṇaṃ noryadi brāhmemuhūrtake

ekaikasmin dine tatra japahomādikaṃ kṛtaṃ

mahādānasahasrasya phalaṃ vaktu hi śakyate

ekaikasmindinenatra śrīśārcanajaṃ phalaṃ

ekaikasminhite tatra kṛsaṇohutiṃ śutāṃ

māse trīśrīmadaṣṭārṇapuraścaraṇamāharāt

śrīmadaṣṭhārṇavidyāyāmāsetravasudhāyate

utthāyāyararātrāṃnedhanuḥsaṃkramaṇeraveḥ

ācāryaṃ samalaṃkṛtyavāsolaṃkāracaṃdanaiḥ

śālagrāmasilāvakre śrīyetervasudhāpateḥ

uccaranniṃdirānāthabrahmavidyāphalapradāṃ

āthāmalakaparteyodūrvāgraiḥ kusumānvitaiḥ

jīrakīrdrakasaṃyuktaṃ mudgatraṃ ghṛtasaṃyutaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyāṃsahasraṃpratyahaṃ japet

japet paṃcaśataṃ vāpi dvādaśārṇāṃ śriyothavā

jīrakārdrakasaṃyuktaṃ goghṛtena samanvitaṃ

tathaivāṣṭotāraśataṃ hunecchī hṛdyena ca

bhagavadraśmividyābhirekaikāmāhutiṃ hunet

ūṣāsaṃkramaṇe prāpte tatrābhyarcādipūrvavat

vilvapatrasamāyuktaṃ tilagavyājyasaṃyutaṃ

prasannalakṣmīmaṃtreṇa dvādaśārṇā śriyā vidhā

hutvātharaśividyābhirjuhuyāhoghṛtaṃ tridhā

agniṃ pradakṣiṇī kṛtya bhagavaṃtaṃ śriyaḥ patiṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ sāṣāṃgaṃ daṃḍavannamet

padmarāga samāyuktaṃ vajraviṃdu samanvitaṃ

kuṃḍaladvitaṃ padadyādavaśyaṃ tasya karṇayoḥ

ācāryāyārdyayedvīmānmahatīsuvasuṃdharāṃ

paṃcāṭhakamitāmudgādeyā ācāryamaṃdire

grāmasya dakṣiṇā proktātiṣkasāhasrakāṃcanaṃ

nālpasasyāmahodeyānakaklośāvaduḥkhadā

mahādānāni kurvīta rājāvārājaputrakaḥ

putrapautrābhivṛdhyarthaṃ dadyātyāyanikaṃ

uṣasaṃkramaṇe tasminnekāhatyānaleśubhe

tatrācārya yadāṃbhoje datvā sasyayutāṃmahīṃ

datvā yatphalamāprātitadavaśyamavāpnuyāt

ākalpakoṭi dānasya phalamakṣayamaśrute

divyavastradharaḥsragvījarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ

nāṃśatamudvatyasudharṇadhvajabhūṣitaṃ

bhagavatsadṛśākāromodate paramepade

mahādānasahasrasya phalamakṣayamakṣutai

viśeṣahomaṃ kṛtvātra datvācārya padakṣiṇa

saṃprāpya sākṣātsvapnevāpaśyatyeva śriyapatiṃ

kulātanmaṃdirenivasanimahālakṣmīracaṃcalā

saṃprāpya putrapautrādi samṛddhi mahatomavi

avāśyamuktimāpnoti sahasrakulasaṃyutaḥ

dānārthaṃ śasyate bhūya vahudakṣiṇayānvitā

vasuṃdhūrā sahsradhenudānāścahaya sāhasradānataḥ

mahīdānaṃ mahādāna sahasrādapi siddhidaṃ

aśaktomaṃdabhāgvaśrodbhiśitvā śūdrakānapi

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcyapaṃcāniṣkamitaṃ dhanaṃ

dhenuṃ payasvinī datvā caturviṣkamitaṃ dhanaṃ

avaśyaṃ siddhimāpnoti daridropi na saṃśayaḥ

kūṣmāṃḍaṃ saphalaṃ datvā śrotriyāya kuṭuṃvine

prāpyaṃ vaśābhivṛddhiṃ ca maheṃdrayadabhāgbhavet

kūṣmāṃḍaṃ saṃpradāyāśubhaphalaṃ sumahadaśrute

dhyatīpāta samāyuktā mahatī tithirucyate

srātvā ca hutvā ca guroḥ padābje datvā parāṃsiddhimupaiti martyaḥ

yogeṣu ca vyatīyāto mahāsiddhiṃ prayacchati

snātvā mahānvidyāṃ śrīmadāṣṭākṣarośubhāṃ

hutvā paṃcaśataṃ śrīmadaṣṭārnenāditaḥ śuciḥ

vilvapatrairghṛtābhyaktairmadhutritayamiśritaiḥ

gavyājye na tridha hutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇahutiṃ

ṣaṇniṣkaṃ vā triniṣkaṃ vā niṣkadvayamitaṃ tu vā

alaṃkāra tathā dadyādyathā svaktidhanaṃ tu vā

śrotriyāyadaridrāyakūṣmāṃḍaphalamuttamaṃ

vimukto mahatīṃ siddhiṃ prāpnotyeva mahīyate

māsimāsihariṃ sākṣāsvapreyāpi prapaśyati

avāpya mahatī lakṣmī dhanaṃdhānyādisaṃkulāṃ

putrapautrābhivṛddhiṃ ca bhogānapi sudullībhāt

nārī vā puruṣo vāpi kārtike paurṇamīdine

saubhāgyamakṣayaṃ lakṣmīdhanadhānyādi saṃkulāṃ

bharturāpuṣpamicchaṃto saubhāgyaṃ ca manoramaṃ

dadyādavaśyaṃ kūṣmāṃḍaṃ kārtike paurṇamīdine

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāmi navārātravrataṃ śubhaṃ

navarātra maharatparvavarṣamadhyāmaṇivrataṃ

ekaikaṃ ca dinaṃ tatra cūḍāmaṇi samaṃ viduḥ

tatra dataṃ kataṃjatyaṃ śreyorāśikalapradaṃ

svargāpavargadaṃ sadyassamastepsitasiddhidaṃ

ācāryānujñāyātatra vrataṃ kṛtyāmahannaraṃ

āśvinemāsi saṃprāpte pratipadivaseśubhe

sahasraṃ prajayedvedamātaraṃ triśalatuvā

vidhivatsaṃskṛte vahnau dvādaśākṣaravidyayā

aṣṭautraraśataṃ hutvā ghṛtamaṣṭhārṇavidyayā

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā ṣaḍarśona śriyopi vā

japecchī dvādaśārṇāṃ ca saṃkalpārka sahasrakaṃ

ekottaraṃ japellakṣmīhṛdayaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ

mahābhavamyāṃ trimadhughṛtavilvadalānvitaṃ

vṛhadābharaṇairhutvā ghṛtena purataḥ śuciḥ

pūrvoktaṃ pāpasaṃhutvā dvādaśārṇaśriyāpi ca

bhagavadraśmibhirhutvā tridhā ghṛtamataḥ paraṃ

nityaṃ paṃcaśataṃ brahmavidyāyā pūjayet vratī

vimalādidhiyācaikāmarcayenna suvāsusinī

suvāsinī rūpaṃ pūjyaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragaṃ

tāṃkulairdakṣiṇabhiścatoṣayeścasuvāsinīḥ

phalāśanodhaḥ śāpī ca jiteṃdriyayutopi ca

pradadyādakṣiṇadravyaṃ vāso laṃkaraṇādikaṃ

śrīśadvādaśākṣaryālakṣmīnārāyaṇau vibhū

ṣaṣṭhicihrukasauvarṇānarmitaṃ kuṃḍaladvayaṃ

sauvarṇamudrikādyaiśca samabhyarvya tataḥparaṃ

dadyādvasudhārāyaścādvahusāsyaudya saṃkulāṃ

deyā anadruhaḥ paścāccaturobhāravāhekāḥ

aśvayugmāsi mahatī navamīyā prakīrtitā

ekaikaṃ dinamapyatra navarātravrate śubhe

mahatī navamīpātra tatsamaṃ parvasiddhidaṃ

mahānavamyāṃ vijayalakṣmīmicchatracaṃcatma

samarpya vahulāṃ nityaṃ japalakṣmīmavāpnuyā

avaśyaṃ phalamāpnoti viprāṇaṃ koṭibhojanāt

suvāsīno sahasrāṇāṃ mūjāphalamavāpnuyāt

datvā dvādaśadhā bhūmiṃ dhanaratnaudya saṃkulāṃ

avaśyaṃ matphalaṃ sāṃgaṃ labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ

dhenukoṭiprādānasyaphalaṃ sīgaṃ vrajatyartha

phalamāsādyaśuddhātmījīvanmuktaḥyusukhī bhavet

grāmadvādakṣakaṃ datvāścetāśvaṃchatrasaṃyutaṃ

sālakṛtāṃ svaputrīcaṃ svācāryāya samarpayet

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcya siddhimāpnoti śāśvatī

koṭi satāhutiphalaṃ samagraṃ puruṣoplute

tatanmerusamaṃdānaṃ prāhurdivyāmaharṣayaḥ

ācāryāya ṛtudravyaṃ tatra nikṣipyasādhakaḥ

dahaivatasyetejīśaṃ mamadāsyāmi suvrata

aṃtepi samahedrādipadaṃ kṛtvā tṛṇopamaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭakṣarī siddhidvādaśārṇaśriyopi ca

puraścaryā sahasrastha sāṃgasya phalamakṣayaṃ

nārāyaṇamanādyaṃtaṃ paśyatyevaściyaḥ patiḥ

dhanadhyanyapaśuvrānayuktāralaughaṃ saṃkulaṃ

saṃprāpya sarvaśreyāṃsi vidyāṃ ca mahatīśubhāṃ

sahasrakulaṃ yuktasradviṣṇoḥ paramaṃpadaṃ

lakṣmīhayāvidyāyāviśeṣotra pravakṣyate

srātvā prātarviśuddhītmānityasaṃdhyāmupāsya ca

āṣṭhojaraśataṃ hutvā madaṣṭhārṇavidyayā

paṃcaśataṃ nityamaṣṭārṇāṃ sarvasiddhidī

aṣṭamyataṃ japecchudraḥ phalāśīvāhaviṣyabhuk

avaśyamevatāṃ vṛlabhakṣyako bhūṣaṇānvitaḥ

saṃtoṣya sakalairarthe hṛdayaṃ śriya ādarāt

suvāsinyaḥ samabhyarcyāḥ pūrvoktavidhinānṛpa

mahālakṣmīvidyā sākṣādathavādvādaśārcayet

ācāryapatnīḥ śrīvudhyāṃ vāsolaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

mahānavamyāṃ jvalite jātavedasisādhakaḥ

śrīśrīpatīsamāvādyatatrāgrau brahmavidyayā

prasannayā mahālakṣmyāvidyayā trimadhuplutaṃ

gavyājyasahitaṃ hutvā pāyasaṃ jvalite nale

vaiṣṇuvairīśmibhisredhāghṛtaṃ hutvā tataḥ paraṃ

pradakṣiṇaṃ namaskṛtya jātavedasamādarāt

tatrāryapadāṃbhojadvitayaṃ praṇipatya ca

kuṃḍalādvitayaṃ datvā tatkarṇayugale śubhe

sūkṣmavāso yugaṃ datvā varṣāśanamitaṃ tu vā

tatrāpya śaktomati mānadaśaniṣkamitaṃ dhanaṃ

pradadyādgodvayaṃ vatsavahukṣīrasamanvitaṃ

datvā siddhimavāpnoti mahatīṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

sakṛtkṛtvāpi vā sākṣādbhagavatkalayānvitaḥ

tamanvahaṃ pālayati mahālakṣmīssvaputravat

bhagavat kalayāyukto jīvanneva vimucyate

niṣṭaṃti bhṛtyavatatsyasākṣātsakalasiddhidāḥ

prasannalakṣmīsahitāśrīmaṣṭhākṣarasyava

varṣāśanamitadravyamathavādakṣiṇoktamā

dakṣiṇāsahitā sarvasiddhidoktāvasuṃdharā

śriyamaṣṭhaguṇeyatāmicchatāni yatātmanā

lakṣmīnārāyaṇeyogeyogepyarvodayen sa

siṃharāśisthite jīveyogepuṣkaranāmake

japahomādikaṃ sākṣātparaṃbrahmaprapaśyati

ārdvodaya sahasraṃ vānatatrāsadṛśaṃ bhavet

mahāsiddhikarī proktā japahomādibhirnṛpa

dhanurmāsavratenapi mahatī siddhirīritā

dina dvayamidaṃ śreṣṭaṃ sadyassiddhipradāyakaṃ

bhīṣmapaṃcakamityuktaṃ mahāsiddhikaraṃ smṛtaṃ

karukṣārya śukraṣāṃ saivasiddhikarī smṛtā

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ śarīrāṃtepinatyajet

kartavyācāryapyu śrūṣā prāṇairapi dhanairapi

itthaṃ viditvā satataṃ kṛtvāpi vasudhāpate

ācāryājñāṃ samullaṃpya sakṛdapyavanīyate

ākalyaṃ narake bhūyanimajjati na saṃśayaḥ

prajaptvā mahāvidyābhogasvargāpavargadā

nārāyaṇe mahāyogī jannaivāṃtaravīyata

rāmo dāśarathiḥ prāharudrāya paripṛcchate

taṃtre dāśarathīyākhye pūrvīśassamudīritaḥ

śṛṇavanvāyi vimuktosau mahāpātakarāśibhiḥ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇamāhātmyaṃśrutaṃsāgarahasyakaṃ

sāmrājyalakṣmīḥ paṃcetisāmrājyaśriyaīritāḥ

tasyā māhātmamakhrlaṃvadanaḥ karuṇānidhe

prāhasītāpratimrudroḍauryāsaha jagatpatiḥ

brahmāparokṣavijñānarahasyaṃ bhuktimuktidaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīmānnamāhātmyamakhilaśrutaṃ

māhātmyamakhilaṃ tasyā vadamahyaṃ jagatyeta

māhātmyamakhilaṃ śabho śrīvidyāyā vadāmyahaṃ

taṃtre nārāyaṇīye smin sahasyaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

punardāśarathīyākhyaṃ taṃtraṃ vakṣyāmi sādaraṃ

saubhāgyodayanāmaitacchaṇurājan mahīyate

bhūtvā sakalabhāgyānāmādhāromuktimetyaṣi

paramātmā parānaṃdaḥ sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ

pūrvaśca paścimaścaivadakṣiṇaścottarastathā

pūrvāmnāyastu ṛgvedo yajurvedastu dakṣiṇaḥ

sarvopaniṣadāṃjālamūrddhāmnāyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ

śaṭiṃśatiḥ sāstatvāniśarīraṃ viśvaramaiśvaraṃ

***** jagaddhātā śrīśonārāṇobhiva

ācāryavadanobhūtavacaneṣu pramāṇatā

ācāryavatsadācāryapatnīputreṣu saṃtataṃ

gurudevatamaṃtrāṇāmaikyaṃ dhyāyet sadā dhiyā

ayameva hi siddhāṃta ukto vedāṃtavādibhiḥ

iyaṃ śrīlālitā vidyāyoṣyākulavadhūriva

śarīraprāṇavittānāmapyācāryasasarpakaḥ

parasto vimukho mānī satyadhāgatasūyakāḥ

etādṛśāyaśiṣyāya vidyādeyāśubhārthinaṃ

pāalayet sakalāyabhyorahasyamapivodhayan

śrīvidyāṃ prajapeddhīmānbrahmānaṃdasmāptaye

ādyāyāśuyato dīkṣāvaiṣṇavīvataḥ paro

ekatradīkṣito vāpi śucirbhavati saṃtataṃ

dīkṣānnitayasaṃyuktaḥ srimūrtirbhagavāvet

jasasakalavidyātāmadhikārī bhavecchruciḥ

dīyate sakalaṃ vastu gurevaprāṇasaṃyutaṃ

mūrkhāyatīrthadveṣaparāya

bhaktiyuktāyaśuddhāya nārāyaṇa kalātmane

sarvaśāstrārthavedārthajñānine suvratāya ca

maṃḍapaṃ vedikāṃ kṛtvā puṣpavaitānamaṃḍitāṃ

siṃdūraragamālābhirmaṃḍitaṃ ca vicitrakaṃ

siṃdūrarajasāvāpilikhitaṃ cakrumattamaṃ

caturastaṃ ca vilikhet suvarṇakalśānnava

jalapūrṇānviśeṣāḍhyānratnakharṇādibhūṣitān

vaśinyādyaṣṭakaṃ tatra kalāḥ kuṃbheṣu pūjayet

kramasamastamabhyarcyavidhivatsaṃskṛtenale

hutvāthavīmata śiṣyaṃ svāsanesthāpayecchaciḥ

ṣaḍadhvarūpaṃ śrīcakraṃ tasya mūrdhni viciṃtaye

ājñā saṃkramaṇaṃ kuryādyāvaliścalūtā bhavet

śūnyāśevamayaṃ vastu puruṣaḥ parameśvaraḥ

***** gvusthaulyaṃ saṃyogāśivayoryadā

śaktijognariti khyātā śivoniścalatā tathā

guṇānnayaṃ tu kathitā jāgnatkhapnaguṇa **

śivādhikya duṇātītaṃ vastu jānī hi bhūyate

tadātuyaṃ vijānī hi sarvadranikṛṃjanaṃ

śrīvidyāvojasaṃlīmāḥ śiṣyadehe prakāśayet *

taddhyānānmanasasthairyamumnānīti nigadyate

saṃkalpena vikalpena prakṣābhyāṃ caṃcalassadā

sannadvapiva ūlaḥ kṛtyākṛtya yadadvāyaḥ

avidyāviśmṛte niṃdāstuti śākhāsuśobhite

parṇamahīpāla pāpapuṇya phalānvite

kṣisāṃta samākramya jñānapāśena pāśanut

sarvarsakalparahitā kalā saptadaśī bhavet

tena pakṣadvayaṃ bhinnaṃ yadā bhavati niścayaḥ

unmanyāsahito yogīnonmanīyogināṃ parā

ata eva mahīpāladṛṣṭyāsaṃkramarṇa yajet

jādaukaracca saṃyojyasya śanairamahīṣatte

svarṇādyāghātavaḥ sarvelohāthā vasudhīyate

karasparśena mūpālaprapaṃcamānilādikaṃ

kerapuṣpaphalānkṛtvā brahmaraṃdhre tataḥ kṣipet

anirvācyopadeśoyaṃ sparśanādbrahmaraṃdhrake

varābhayakaronityo brahmarūpī sadānaghaḥ

paścāgra ṣaṭ cakra vijñānaṃ śiṣyāyaprātivodhayet

vādisāṃtaiścaturvarṇaṃstaptahamasamaprabhai

iṃdragopapratīkāśaispharādvidumasannibhaiḥ

nīlavarṇā tṛtīyaṃ himaṇipūrakaṃ daśārakaṃ

gadādharaharipraucchaṃ caturthaṃ vasudhāyeta

piṃgābhaṃ dvādaśāṃkhamnavarṇakalāsrakaṃ

jīvātmanādhiṣṭitaṃ tajjārnahitadanaṃtaraṃ

dvidalaṃ hakṣavarṇābhyām āmrābhyāṃ parimaṃḍitaṃ

nādīvilīnoyasmānnutasmādbrahma taducya

nādaḥ paramasayogātkalinyāmutoyādā

paṃcāśadvarṇarūpatvaṃ prāptāvān parameśvaraḥ

itthaṃ sarvajñatāṃ tasmaiśiṣyāyasadguruḥ

ratnābharaṇaśobhādyāyadṛvastadhanānvitāṃ

kṣitignāmamahārāmaramyavedī śatānvitāṃ

gurusaṃtoṣyavidhivatprasādaṃ prītisaṃṣuta

vālāṃ prakāśayet karṇedakṣiṇepūrvamādarāt

sarvavidyāmayodadyāt sarvasiṃhāsaneśvarī

na dadyātprāṇādāyāpinidhānamiva saṃsmara

tathābrahmamayometāṃ vidviśāstreṣuṇāpinī

sāmrājyanāyikā vidyāṃ datvā paṃcadaśarakṣaro

anena dīkṣāvidhinādīkṣiteyābhavedbuvi

nnidhādokṣeyamākhyātāplujamādhyamādhamā

uttamāsā samākhyātā dīkṣāsaṃpūrṇadakṣiṇā

śubhamāseśubhadine dīkṣāsyādayamānṛpa

avaśyaṃ mahatīṃ siddhiṃ prāptotyanna na saṃśayaḥ

mahārahasye saṃprokta ekodhyāyo mahīpate

māhātmyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yaduktaṃ śaṃbhunā purā

ginijāparipapracchaśaṃkaraṃ lokanākaraṃ

catuṣyaṣṭiśva taṃtrāṇi mātṛṇāmuttamāni ca

tatvaśavarakaṃ caiva bhainavāṣṭakameva ca

mahochuṣaṃ mahādeva lātulātuḥ lobhanaṃ

kalāvādaṃ kalāsāraṃ tathānyatkubjikāmatāṃ

paṃcāmṛtaṃ rūpabhedaṃ bhūtaḍāmarameva ca

sarvajñānottanadevamahāpicumaṃta tathā

kulodrīśaṃkulacūḍāmaṇistathā sarvajñānottanaṃdevamahāgnicumattaṃ tathā

kumānīkāmadaṃ devarūpikāmatameva ca

aruṇeśaṃ mohaneśaṃ viśuddhīśvarameva ca

bhavatoktyānimedevasarvajñānamayāni ca

tasyā māhātmyamakhilaṃ śrotumicchāmyahaṃ prabho

triśaktilakṣmīvidyāyāḥ sāmrājyaśrīmattorapi

śṛṇudevimahāvidyā māhātmyamadyanāśanaṃ

yasyasmaraṇamātreṇa vinaśyaṃtya tritayamoritaṃ

śrīmahāsutīvarṇā etatitakathitāṃ śive

dvitīyaṃ kāmarājākhyaṃ lokannayadhaśaṃkaraṃ

tṛtīyaṃ syāt parākhaṃḍaṃ jarāmṛtyuvināśanaṃ

ikaṃ śrīvidyānāmakaṃ vastu viṃdunnayavibhūṣitaṃ

śrīvidyākhyeyane tatve vidunnayavibhūṣite

vedaśāstrapurāṇādyā vidyāḥ sarvāḥ sanātanāḥ

mātṛkā sarvavidyānāṃ paṃcadvarṇarūpiṇī

praṇavarivyā brahmāvidyāyaṃ cāśadvarṇamātṛkā

brahmatādātmyasisadvipradā etānasaṃśayaḥ

ekaikamasyakhaḍaṃvāya upāste dṛḍhavrataḥ

vedānānnitayānhinnayaṃ kālannayaṃ tathā

puṣkarannitayaṃ varṇannayāmātmannayaṃ tathā

hṛllekhātritayaṃ yatra viṃdutritayamiṣṭadaṃ

bhanyannammopadaṃ gaurāyāvṛtyaiva kathite

iyameva gireḥ ṣunnidevīśaṃkaravallabhā

iyameva jaganmātāturīyā paramākalā

strīpunnapuṃsakākāraṃ yadbhūtamudaṃcitaṃ

iyaṃ nārāyaṇīlakṣmīgaudurgāsarasvatī

sarvavidyāmayo sarvakṣetratīrthasvarūpiṇī

sarvajñānamayo sarvapāpaghnīmaṃgalamama

yasyanopavimaṃ janmayaṃ yo vā na śaṃkaraḥ

vaktrakoṭi samāpi dātavyaṃ śipihasrairvījīhvākoṭiśatairapi

sājyapradā yajñevāsvasutāyāpivaṃdhave

śriyaḥ paṃcadaśārṇoyaṃ na dātavyā kathaṃcanaṃ

nayāṇāmapi vedānāṃ pārāyaṇaśaṃtu vā

aśvamedha sahasrāṇi vā japeyasya koṭayaḥ

kāśyāditpatīrthayānnāścatulāṃ nāyāṃti ṣoḍaśīṃ

sa nāmārādhyadhātāsau sṛṣṭeḥ kartāpurābhavat

kāmāmiyimāṃ rudramohaṣāmāsaṃ śailaje

sarvāsāmapi vidyātāmīśvaratvamavāptavān

sargasthityupasahārākartārājaigataḥ śive

trailokyaṃ mohayāmāsanaranārī guṇairyutaṃ

sidhyanmaharṣisaṃghāśca munivayoścakinnarān

śivasuṃdarīrdevagaṃdharvakanyā api manoharāḥ

raṃbhorvaśyādivyāśarasopi mama priye

yaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ sākṣādbrahmarudrādisaṃjñitaḥ

tasya śaktiriyaṃdevīsuṃdarīnāmacitkalā

sṛṣṭisthililayātkartumahaśaktaḥ śakta eva hi

asyā saṃśasamudbhūtā vedamātāpiśailaje

vidyācatuṣṭayaṃ sarvāvidyāsihāsaneśvaraṃ

śriyaḥ paṃcadaśārṇoyaṃ vedamātāpiśailaje

caturvagepradānityamaṇimādyaṣṭāsiddhidā

samastaviṣṇudyāsāmrājñī sarvasaṃpattihetukī

mahākālātmikā vidyātithinakṣatrarūpiṇī

ca bhūtamayī saptalokādhārāsanātano

jahasrakoṭiyoginyaptvā pratyahanekāṃte svācāryānugnahāchive

viputratparirakṣaṃtitaṃ brahmīṇyādimārataḥ

amarā āpi śakrādyāḥ samakoṭi maharṣayaḥ

sahasrayojanaṃ vāpi gatvācāryaṃ śivātmakaṃ

sadāsarvakhamapi vā datvā prāṇānputrāṃśca kanyakaḥ

lalitājapakarbhṛṇāṃ gṛhyasvargāyate bhuvi

strīṃ sagamopi kaivalyaheturasya na saṃśayaḥ

mokṣāye ca saṃsāro lalitāṃ vā muvāsituḥ

śrīsuṃdarītarpaṇa tatparāṇāṃ bhogaśnamokṣaścakarasya eva

ekoccāraṇavidyāyāstulānārhatiṣoḍaśī

yattonirakṣaraṃ vastu parātannatukāraṇaṃ

brahmavidyā svarūpayaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradā

gopitavyā sadā bhadresvayonirivasaṃ tata

vedyatannabhavetyaṃ icchiyaḥ paṃcadaśākṣarī

dṛṣṭā prajapate yastu samākṣoyoginogaṇaiḥ

bhuvanatrayasmaubhāgyaṃ dadāti vipulāṃ śriyaṃ

sudhātaṃragiṇīvegaścavṛcāturyadāyinī

parabrahmaṇilīnatvaṃ dadāti yaśaṭhannataṃ

kaṭhākṣanodevibhuvanāni caturdaśa

catussāgaraparyaṃtamahīsāmrājyapālanaṃ

brahmāṃḍakoṭijanaṃ bhūyo vācāṃ vijṛṃbhaṇaṃ

śrīvidyājapakṛtpuṃsonāsādhyaṃ bhavati priye

śrīvidyākṛtpuṃsonadurlabhamihapriye

nārāyaṇe na cāpyuktaṃ śrutvā guptikarābhava

viduyuktaṃ śriyo vījamidaṃ saṃyojyayoginaḥ

dātavyāṃ khapriyādārādeyāḥ punnāśca kanyakāḥ

yasyopadeśaḥ kriyatejayādijatitaṃ phalaṃ

saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇādānātpuraścaryādikaṃ vinā

catvāraṃ ihyibhratikṣemayaṃta iti saṃtataṃ

nityāyaṃ ca śākhyātā mahānityā tu ṣoḍaśī

ciṃtāmarāyādi ratnāni kalpavṛkṣādibhūruhāḥ

śrīvidyāṃ prajayedyastu satataṃ sādhakottamaḥ

tadvaṃśa saṃbhavā purāyāḥ pūtāstanmitravāṃdhavāḥ

śrīvidyājāpiṃ na natvā tulādānaphalalabhet

vājapeyaphalaprāpyabhuktiṃ muktica viṃdati

taddivyakṣennamuddiṣṭaṃ samaṃtādyo janāyataṃ

dharmārthakāmamokṣāstatkaretiṣṭaṃti saṃtataṃ

gopitavyaṃ prayatnenayadīcchedātmanohitaṃ

vaśiṣṭavāmadevādyādivyādaharṣayogyamī

sanakādyāśca yogośāmārka upādayopi ca

samprāpya sakalābhīṣṭaṃ japatyadyāpimatpriye

ayameva paroyoga idameva paraṃ phalaṃ

iti matvā śriyo vidyāmanvahaṃ prajapecchive

ithameva mahāvidyānānyoktā paramaṣibhiḥ

mahārṣatvaṃ śaktatva ca vidhātṛtāṃ

śrīvidyaiva prajaptavyā nānyā vidyā varānane

avaśyamicchatājapyā śrīnidyava varānane

japtvā sakalabhāgyānāmādhorādevavadbhavet

śrīvidyājāpakonityaṃ bhavatyeva jaḍātpatiḥ

samastādyaharaṃ puṇyaṃ kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi

saubhāgyavidyodaya uttarabhāge dvitīyodhyāyaḥ

kṛtāṃjali puṭā bhūtvā punārāha girīṃdrajā

śriyaḥ paṃcadaśārṇoyaṃ śrutā sarvasamṛddhidā

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ śriya ādarāt

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarīvidyā śrīvidyaivatataḥ paraṃ

vedāṃteṣvapi vedeṣu rahasyaṃ mahadbhutaṃ

brāhmemuhūrte utthāya brahmaraṃdhre nijaṃ guruṃ

nārādhaṇā kṛtivāṃpikhaśaktisahitaṃ vibhuṃ

dhyātvā plāvitamātmānaṃ smaredācāryapādukāṃ

udyatvaḍūryasahasrābhāṃ tejorūpāmatha smaret

brahmavidyāṃ prajaptvādau bhagavadraśmimālikāṃ

dvādaśadhā vidyāṃ śriyaḥ sakalasiddhidā

mukhaprakṣālanādīni kṛtvā campa prayatnataḥ

paraṃtoyāṃjaliṃ tridhā mūlamuccāryā śirasi kṣipet

tridhā sutartha mūlena lalitāṃ vānnayīmayo

nityasaṃdhyāmupāsyātha drāṁ hrīṁ draṁ sa itīrayet

bhaktyādya sahitāyeti vahijāyāmathoccaret

ravimaṃḍalamadhyetha smṛtvā śrīcakramādarāt

vidmeha padamuccārya kāmarāja mataḥ paraṃ

śaktivījaṃ samuccārya tannaḥ klinna pracodayāt

athācārya padāṃbhoja dvitayaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ

kumārīṃ prajapadādau yathā śaktyā girīṃdraje

pādukā saṃsmaret paścānmūlācāryasya siddhidā

sa eva saccidānanṃdanātha ācārya īśvaraḥ

kalpakoṭi śatenāpi na siddhistasya jāyate

vogaṃvaśinī tatpādukā manusmṛtya śrīvidyāmanvahaṃ japet

śrīvidyājasakalpaṃ kuryādādau yatavrataḥ

chaṃdaḥ pāḍūmiti prāhurdevatā vinmayī parā

śrīmahātripuretyutktāsuṃdarī paradevatā

lalitā śrīmahābhaṭṭārikā lakṣmīrūpayīmayī

devatā kathitā sarvajagabhyātā maheśvarī

vāgbhavaṃ prathamaṃ khaṃḍavījamuktaṃ trayīmayaṃ

śaktyādyaṃ śaktikhaṃḍaṃ ca śaktirityucyate śriyaḥ

kāmādyaṃ kāmarājaṃ ca kīlakaṃ samudāhṛtaṃ

śrīkaṃṭhānaṃtatātoyai dvirāvṛtyāṃgulīṣvatha

śrīcakramaṃtrāṇāṃ vāṅmāyāyā kamalāpuraḥ

kumārikā dvirāvṛtyā ṣaḍaṃganyā samācaret

vaśinyādyaṣṭa kanyā saṃtataḥ kuryādananyadhīḥ

uccāryābhāgaṃtyaṃ bhūsaṃsthaṃ vāmakarṇa samānvitaṃ

uccārya vaśinoti padaṃ tataḥ

anena śirasi nyasyaka varga proccaredatha

paścāccaturthī hṛdagdevatā padaṃ paścāccaturthyaṃtaṃ namonvitaṃ

mātabhūmi sudhārūḍhaṃ vidruvāmākṣi saṃyutaṃ

ṭadhargovasudhārūḍhaṃ vāyuṃ ṣaṣṭa samanvitaṃ

tavargaṃ cakravagāṃtyiṃ vaikuṃṭhārūṭhamapyatha

vāgdevatā padaṃ paścāccaturthyaṃtaṃ namonvitaṃ

jayinī vārgdevatāyai namo nābhauma vinyaset

saviṭharuvākarṇa ca tataḥ sarveśvaropadaṃ

liṃgenyasya śakarādi ṣaṭkakṣmīmiti vījakaṃ

vaśinyādyaṣṭakanyāsa uktoyaṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ

vācāṃ siddhi samāsādya saṃpadāmāśrayo bhavet

śrutidvaye ca vadane bhujayugme tataḥ paraṃ

mūlavidyākṣarāntyasma sākṣādbrahmamayo bhavet

sarvepi mātrā uccāryā devatābhāva siddhaye

mūlavidyākṣaranyāsaṃ nyasya sākṣājjagātpatiḥ

ṣoḍānyāsama kṛtvāpi śrocanyāsamapyatha

ṣoḍā śrīcakra vinyāsau durlabhau bhuvanatraye

naitya kanyā sa ukroyaṃ samastaphalasiddhidaḥ

viyāmimāṃ sākṣātparabrahmasvarūpiṇī

itthaṃ vinyasya vidhivannyāsajālaṃ dine dine

padmanibhādevīṃ vālārkakiraṇāruṇāṃ

padmarāga pratīkāśāṃ kuṃkumādarasannibhī

kolāli kulasakāśa kuṭilāla kaṣaluvīṃ

kiṃcidardedu kuṭilalalāṭa mḍupaṭṭikā

ānaṃdamuditollāsa līlāṃdolita locanāṃ

khaṃgaḍamaṃḍitā bhogajitedvamṛtaṃ kuṃḍalāṃ

tāmravidrumaviṃvābha raktāmṛtarasādhanāṃ

raktayā jagadbhāsi jihvāṃ madhurabhāṣiṇīṃ

anūpama guṇopeta cuvukoddeśa śobhitāṃ

raktotpala dalākāra sukumāra karāṃvujī

muktā hāralatopeta samunnatayodharī

lavaṇya sa tilāvartākāranābhi vibhūṣitāṃ

vitaṃ varvivadviradaromaṇajī varāṃśukāṃ

kaṃdarpa kadalītulya jaṃghāyugalamaṃḍitī

tanudīrghāṃgulībhokhannakha caṃdravirājitāṃ

śītāṃśu śatasaṃkāśakāṃti saṃtānahāsinī

raktavastraparīdhānāṃ pāśāṃkuśa karāghanāṃ

caturbhujāṃ trinetrāṃ ca vāṇadhanurddharāṃ

mahāmṛgamadoddāma kuṃkumāruṇavignahāṃ

jagadāhvādajatojadraṃjanakāriṇī

sarvamaṃtramayī devīṃ sarvasaubhāgyasuṃdarī

vaṃde pāśāṃkuśābhīṣṭyaṃ ca vāṇadhabhurddharāṃ

vālārkamaṃḍalābhāsāṃ catuvuddhi trilocana

pāśāṃkuśarāṃścāpaṃ dhārayaṃtī śivāśraye

yudekṣu cāpāṃkuśapāśavāṇahaste namaste jagodakamātaḥ

aṇidibhirāvṛtāṃ mayūkhairaharmityevavimā bhāvayebhavānī

tripurasuṃdarīmāśraye

smarāmyaṃvikāṃ

udyat sūryanibhāṃ vaṃde mahātripurasuṃdarī

amṛtānaṃda saṃdoha sayutyaitā bhajāmyahaṃ

yavoya cārān kṛtvātha daśamudrāḥ pradarśya

maṣṭauttaraśataṃ tu vāmahatī siddhimāsādya sākṣadvratyamayo bhavet

nārāyaṇālaye vāpi parvatāgnetha vā śuciḥ

ācārya sannidhau vāpi japellakṣatrayaśriyaṃ

daśāṃśatarpayedvidvān kiṃśukairājyalolitaiḥ

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoje datvā sasyayutāṃ maho

kuḍaladvitayaṃ datvā śrīvidyāyāḥ phalapradā

athavāgnahaṇe prāpte savituḥ śaśinī pivā

japtvā tatsparśa samaye snātvā nadyāṃ samāhitaḥ

ācārya sannidhāvagnau vidhivat saskṛte śubhe

madhu tritaya saṃmiśraghṛta saṃmiśra kiṃśukaiḥ

brahmāpāṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā namaskṛtyāgnimādarāt

ṣaṣṭi cihnuka sauvarṇa kuṃḍala dvitayaṃ śubhaṃ

vasudhāṃsasya saṃpūrṇaṃ datvā dakṣiṇayānvitāṃ

nidhissakalavidyānāmādhārassarvasaṃpadāṃ

aṇimādyaṣṭa siddhīnye nāyako jagatīpatiḥ

ācāryapatnī śrīvudhyāvāsolaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryā lalitāyāṃ mama priye

bhūtvāṃte paramadhīmayātiṃvaśa samanvitaḥ

tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ

yacchatvāpi narasyarva saubhāgyaibhāgyaikanidhirbhavet

japyate lalitā vidyā śriyaḥ paṃcadaśākṣarī

sasnaṃ cakramabhyarvya madhyacakramathāpivā

jvalatkāmāgni saṃtāpa pratamāstaptamānasāḥ

maṃtrasaṃṣūḍha hṛdayoḥ sphuratjaghanamaṃḍalāḥ

ekāmapi puraścaryā kṛtvā sāgā mama priye

madhyaṃ vā cakramabhyarcya vaidike jātavedasi

gurubhiśca sakṛcchrutvā hyaṣṭāviṃśatidhā śriyā

kaṃdarpasadṛśīkātirjāthotanyakalevo

navaratna samāyukta mukuṭhāvṛtamastakāḥ

jvalatkāmāgni saṃtaptāḥ sauṃdaryamadagarvitāḥ

saṃpadasmakalā divyā mānuṣā api saṃpadaḥ

jaḍastādho maṃdamatirapi prajñā vivarjitaḥ

gaṃgāpravāhavattasyavāgvibhūtirvijṛṃbhate

vyāsavālmīki sadṛśaḥ sarakhatyā bhavatyasau

vaṃśābhivṛddhi sahito bhavatyā caṃdatārakaṃ

vaśīkaroti trailokyaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

kṣamātreyaṃ japte tu kṣubhyate bhūte leṃganaḥ

yadi na kṣubhyate davi sādhakasya manāmanāk

tāsāmapi yadā nāsau kṣobhaneti manāgapi

gaṃdharvakanyākinnarayorūpavatyau manoharāḥ

saṃkṣobhayati viśveśīnnaikyaṃ sa carācaraṃ

divyaratna śamāyuktaṃ kuṃḍalayamuttamaṃ

vahudakṣiṇayā yuktāṃ pradatvātha vasuṃdharāṃ

sūkṣmaṃ vāso yugaṃ datvā kṣauma kāryāsakaṃ tu vā

prāpnotpanna na saṃdehasmatyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyahaṃ

siddhimāsādya mahatīṃ sarvabhāgya nidhirbhavet

kathita sarvadeṣursavaśāstreṣvapi priye

sarvasiddhipradaṃ sadyo mahāsaubhāgya siddhidaṃ

caitrepi navarātrākhyaṃ vratamasti mahattaraṃ

saṃprāpya nahatī siddhi daivatairāye pūjyate

navarātravrataṃ proktaṃ samastābhīṣṭa siddhidaṃ

sāṣṭāṃgaṃ daṃḍavannatvā tadanujñā purasmaraṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ natvā brahmavile śubhe

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā vāpi na raptvādau niyatavrataḥ

varṇasāhasraṃkhyāka saṃkalpa navarātrake

japtvā samastācaraṇasahitaṃ cakramarcayet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityaṃn hunecchī vidyayānala

piye ekaiko vā samabhyarkanityamatra suvāsini

tāṃvūla carvaṇarato nityamādhāryapūjakaḥ

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahau raśmimaṃtrai sakṛddhunet

kiṃśukairhṛtasamiśraiḥ kamalairvāghṛtaplutaiḥ

athavā pāyasenāgnau madhvājya sahitena ca

hutvā śrīvidyayā lakṣmīvidyayāṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

sāmrājya lakṣmīmanunāpyaṣṭottaraśaṃ hunet

hutvā japādibhirhutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāktyāṃ hutiṃ

svācārya caraṇadvaṃdvaṃ natvā sāṣṭāṃgamādarāt

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ kṛtvā divyaratna samanvitaṃ

vahudakṣiṇayā yuktāmācāryāya samarpayet

dukūlaṃ vātha kāryā sasūkṣma vā sasmamarpya

tāṭaṃka svarṇaciṃtākādyalaṃkaraissuśobhanaiḥ

aśaktena daridreṇāpyavaśyaṃ niṣkapaṃcakaṃ

ācāryapatnyai niṣkaṃ vā su varṇa vā sasārpayet

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryā navarātravate śubhe

paladvayamitaṃ raupyapātraṃ ṣalamitaṃ tu vā

yāgadavyaṃ pradadyādyaḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ

brahmaloke ca satyeva magnāśca narakārṇave

mahānavamyāṃ tatpuṇyaphalaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate

tulāpuruṣasāhasraṃ kṛtaṃ tena na saṃśayaḥ

vivāhārthaṃ dhanaṃ datvā gosahasraphalaṃ labhet

tāvatkasahesrāṇi kulānāṃ śatasaṃprutaḥ

avaśyaṃ vasudhādānaṃ kuṃḍaladayasaṃyutaṃ

ācāryapatnyalaṃkāradānamāvaśyakaṃ smṛtaṃ

puraścaryā śataphalaṃ prāpnotyakhilasiddhidaṃ

datvāya phalamāpnoti svācāryacaraṇadvaye

mahīpratinidhirdravyamathavā guraverpayet

mahīpratinidhi dravyaṃ śataniṣkamudāhṛtaṃ

dakṣiṇādravyadravyamayuktaṃ tathaiva girikanyoke

maheṃdrasadṛśī lakṣmī prāpnotyavana saṃśayaḥ

subhagaḥ suṃdarovāgmīśriyaḥ putro bhavatyasau

sākṣādbrahmamayaḥ siddho bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

daṃdarpa śatasaubhāgyaṃ jaga ayavaśaṃkaraṃ

vahukālamaiso jīvatyāpurorāgyaṃsataḥ

prārthyate divyakanyābhiraharniśamesau priye

sa evāhaṃ na saṃdehasya eva hariracyutaḥ

yasyā vārye dṛḍhā bhaktiḥ śrīvidyāyāṃ ca saṃtataṃ

navarātravrate vidyā siddhirbhavṛtiyādṛśī

ācāryaniṃdākartāramapi svapitaraṃ guruṃ

ācāryaniṃdā śravaṇāddevatāśāpa bhāgasau

ācāryaniṃdākartāraṃ daṃḍayaida vicārayan

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāṃ japet sāhasramanvahaṃ

sahasraṃ prajapennityaṃ triśataṃ vā catuḥśataṃ

dṛṣṭyā karoti dāridyai sahitaṃ ca śriyaḥ patiṃ

dṛṣṭyā vināśayatyāśu jvarānogāśca dāruṇān

svacchaṃdamṛtyuḥ sarvatra siddho bhūtvā jagattraye

iti śrījānakīnātha vadanāṃbhojanisṛtaṃ

ita ūrdhvaṃ mayā devi kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi

ṣoḍhānyāsa vidhāna ca cakranyāsavidhiṃ tathā

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yanmāṃtvaṃ pariṣṭacchasi

ajñātvā samayācāra maya*(?) gurupādukā

brahmatādātmyasaṃ siddhikāraṇaṃ bhuktimuktidaṃ

yasya vijñāna mātreṇa jīvanmuktaḥ prammete

parācāpya parāgauri tṛtīyā ca parā parā

dvitīyāhya parā pūjā sadā niṣpādyate mayo

uttamāsā parā proktā vidhānaṃ śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ

vidhānaṃ śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ

āpyāyī tajajadrūpāṃ paramāmṛtavarṣiṇīṃ

dahanāṃtaraṃ sa sarvannādāloka na tatparaṃ

vitkallolābhi dalitasaṃkocastvati suṃdaraḥ

nyāsaṃ vivartayeheha ṣoḍhānyāsa puraḥsaraṃ

aṃtarvahirmātṛkākhyasṛtīyonyāsa ucyate

mūlaṃ vidyākṣaranyāsaṃ paṃcama phaladāyakaḥ

ṣḍhā cakrādikanyāsaṃ kṛtvā kṛtvāpi vānvaha

ṣoḍhānyāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi brahmarūpa phalapradaṃ

ṣoḍhānyāsaṃ nāḥ kuryādyena brahmāṃḍanāyakaḥ

gaṇeśai prathamonyāso dvitīyastugnahairmataḥ

rāśibhiḥ paṃcamonyāsaḥ ṣaṣṭa pīṭhairnigadyate

evaṃ yonyastagātraḥ syāt sapūjyaḥ sarvayogitiḥ

sa eva pūjyaṃ sarveṣāṃ sa svayaṃ parameśvaraḥ

socirāt mṛtyumāpnoti narakaṃ ca prapadyate

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ dhyātvā kuryānnatiṃ sadā

śravaṇādapi yasyā śujāyaṃte sarvasiddhayaḥ

pāśāṃkuśa vā bhītikarān śakti samanvitān

vighneśvarastathā śrīśca vighnarājastathā hriyā

vighnakṛtyuṣṭi yuktaśca vighnacca sarasvatī

ekadaṃtaścakāṃtiścāddidaṃtaḥ kāmino yutaḥ

kapardabhṛttathā tīvrādīrghavaktrastataḥ priye

pṛṣa dhvajaśca subhagāgaṇanāthena saṃyutā

sūyaṃ karṇaścajayinī trinetraḥ satyayānvitaḥ

caturmūtiḥ kāmadā ca sadāśivayutā tataḥ

durmadaśca tathā ghūrṇī sumukhā bhūtimāṃstataḥ

dvijihvaścaramāyuktaḥ śūraścaiva tu mānuṣī

ṣaṇmukhaśca vivarṇā ca varadā bhrukuṭīlatā

doghaṃ ghoṇānvitā paścādviraṃḍā vā dhanurdharaṃ

mataśca caṃdrikāyukto vimalaśca śaśiprabhā

muṃḍī paścāpaddaṃḍī durbhayānvitaḥ

bhakṣya priyaśca durgā ca meghanādaśca kālikā

etāstu vinyasaddevi mātṛkā varṇavatpriye

raktaṃ śvetaṃ tathā raktaṃ śyāmaṃ pītaṃ ca pāṃḍuraṃ

dravi mukhyān kāmarūpānkansarvābharaṇabhūṣitān

śrūmadhye ca ca vargeṇa bhaumaṃ netra trayenyaset

hṛdayoparideveśipargeṇa gale bhṛguṃ

lakṣābhyāṃ tu gude ketuṃ nyasedevaṃ varānane

jvalat kālāgni saṃkāśāḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ

yugmaṃ yugmaṃ tathā yugmaṃ yugma yugma sarohiṇī

laluṭo netrayoḥ paścādvāmadakṣiṇakarṇayoḥ

puṣpāṃtaṃ ca pravinyasyaṃ khagārṇābhyāṃ tu sāryakaṃ

pūrvāphalgunī dakṣe carparecchaja pūrvikāṃ

jha ña varṇasthitaṃ hastaṃ maṇivaṃdhetha dakṣiṇe

kāreṇayutāṃ svātiṃ dakṣahaste pravinyaset

tathadasthāmanūrādhāṃ nābhau vinyasya pārvati

tapayūsyaṃ tathā mūlaṃ nyased vāmakaṭau priye

bhakoraṇottarāṣāḍāṃ vāmorau tadanaṃtaraṃ

parasthitāṃ dhaniṣṭāṃ tu vāmajānuni vinyaset

dakṣajaṃghogatāṃ paścāt pūrvābhādra padāṃ tataḥ

ṣasahasthottarā bhādrapadāṃ dakṣiṇapādake

vāmapāde pravinyasya yoginī nyāsamāttare

caturbhujāsaṃmairvaktrauḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ

vāmakarṇedunādāḍhyaṃ ḍākinyai nama ityapi

tvagātmane kaṃṭhadeśe viśuddhau vinyaset priye

pūrvavadvīja saṃyuktai rasṛṇāt mātra saṃvadet

lākinī ca tathā devi māṃsātmā maṇipūke

bhedasvarūpā vinyasya vasavarṇaistu yākinī

hakṣavarṇa sthitāṃ tadvaddhākinī dvidale nyaset

brahmaraṃdhre maheśāni nyāsoyaṃ yoginī yutaḥ

raktaṃ śvetaharidvarṇaṃ pāṃḍacitrāsitān smaret

akārādi caturvaṇairvinyaset sukhaṃdite

nyasejjānu nivedaistu mithunaṃ vṛṣaṇe tataḥ

siṃhakaṃ anusvāra visargābhyāṃ śavargeṇa ca kanyakāṃ

tathā dāma śirobhāge kavargeṇa tu lābhṛtā

ca vargeṇa tathārgeṇa tathā skaṃdhe vṛścikaṃ nityaset priye

makaraṃ tu tavargeṇa vinyasedvṛṣaṇe kramāt

yadargeṇa kṣakāreṇa mīnaṃ gulphetha vinyaset

teṣā nāmāni kaṣathyaṃte śṛṇuthā vahitā priye

varasthiraṃ kanyākubjaṃ pūrṇaśailaṃ tathārvudaṃ

kailāsaṃ bhṛgunagaraṃ kaṃdāraṃ caṃdrapūjakaṃ

kulūtthaṃ devakūaṃ ca gokarṇamārukteśvaraṃ

kollāpuramelāpuramoṃkāraṃ svajayāṃtikaṃ

uḍḍīśaṃ ca prayāgākhyaṃ vilvaṃ māyāpuraṃ tathā

maheṃdravāmanaṃ caiva hiraṇyapurameva ca

ete pīṭhāsmaddiṣṭā mātṛkārūpamāsthitā

kṛtvā ṣoḍābhidhaṃ nyāsāṃ virāḍatmāhariskhayaṃ

ṣoḍānyāsamimaṃ kṛtvā śrīcakranyāsamācarebhet

navayonyabhidhaṃ nyasedādauma priye

hṛṃsadvaye ca hṛdayenyaset kūrparakukṣiṣu

sakaṃṭhe navayonyākhyaṃ nyasedvīja trayātmakaṃ

punarvālāṃ samuccārya caturasraṃ viciṃtayet

ṣaṭcakreṣu lalāṭe ca sīmaṃte ca nirāvile

śrīvidyāṃ brahmaraṃdhre ca pṛṣṭatto guravaḥ kramāt

kharaparvenyasedvakte sarvasaubhāgyadāyakaḥ

aṃtaḥ śrīcakra vinyāsa uktoyaṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyairapi lokeṣu pūjyate

yasya vijñānatṇe vāpi brahmarūpī bhavatyasau

ghannavusya vidākhyātaṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ paraṃ

dakṣā sa pṛṣṭhaṣṭha pārāyagrasthitapārāgulodyatha

samūlatala pṛṣṭhayuvyāpakakatvena suṃdari

siddhāṃstadaṃtaśca tanu vyāpakatvena suṃdari

vinyasya tasyā sthāneṣu brāhmāṇyādyāstathāṣṭasu

vāmadakṣiṇa jānvośca vahiraṃsadvaye tathā

vinyased vyāpakatvena pūrvoktā tasya vigrahe

brahmāṇyādyaṣṭa sthānītasthā mānaṣṭānnyasedatha

tadaṃtaḥ ṣoḍaśadala padmāya nama ityapi

dalāni dakṣiṇa śrotra pṛsṭamaṃsaṃ ca kūrparaṃ

vāmapādatalādyevameva devāṣṭakaṃ mataṃ

vinyasetra daleṣveṣu dakṣaśaṃkhe ca jatruke

anekusumādyāśca śaktīraṣṭāraṣṭā ca vinyaset

vinyaset tasya koṇeṣu nyasecchakrīścaturdaśa

lalāhdakṣabhāgaṃ ca dakṣagaṃḍī sa madhyataḥ

vāmorvatarvāna pārśva vāmāsaṃ vāmagaṃḍakaṃ

tatādaśoracakrāya nama ityapi pārvati

kukṣīśa vāyukoṇeṣu jātu dvaya gudeṣu ca

sarvasiddhipradādyānāṃ śaktīnāṃ daśakaṃ tathā

vinyaset tasya koṇeṣu sarvājñādyaḥ pravinyaset

śivanī vāmamuṣka ca stanaṃ sakviṇi nāsike

tadaṃtara vṛṣṭakoṇādi cakrāya nama ityapi

cuvukaṃ kaṃṭha hṛdayanābhīnāṃ caiva dakṣiṇaṃ

catuṣṭayaṃ ca pārśvānāme tatkoṇāṣṭakaṃ punaḥ

śaracāyau pāśāsṛṇī trikoṇāya namastathā

kāmeśvaryādi devīnāṃ madhye devīṃ ca vinyaset

etadguptatarakāryatvayāmat prāṇavallabhe

guptāguptataraṃ cai tattavādya prakaṭīkṛtaṃ

śirastrikoṇe pūrvādi kāmeśvaryādika nyaset

sṛṇidvayaṃ tu nyānāsāgne dakṣiṇādyaṃ tu vinyaset

davādīni cakrāṇinyastavyāni varānane

cūḍādike ca nimnorddha śikhorddhakarṇapṛṣṭake

sarvasidhyādikaṃ ye ca prādakṣiṇyena vinyaset

nābhau tvaṣṭadalattuvaṃśe vāme ca pārśvake

vaṃśabhavāmāṃtarādinyanyedanya"ca catuṣṭayaṃ

mūlādhāre nyasenmudrā daśakaṃ sādhakottamaḥ

ūrdhvādho daśamudrāśca ūrddhvādho varjitaṃ puna

brahmaṇyādyaṣṭakaṃ tatra tadyāyāṃ tāsu pūrvavat

vāmajaṃghāṃ samārabhya vāmādi kramatopi ca

itthaṃ vinyasya deveśi svābhedena viciṃtayet

mūrdhni guhye ca hṛdaye netra tritaya eva ca

pṛṣṭhe jānuni nābhau ca vidyānyāsaṃ samācaret

śrīkaṃṭhādi ca vāgdevī rādhā **daye punaḥ

calatrayaiḥ samasta ca vidyāvīna trayānvitaṃ

vyāpakaṃ caiva nyasya khālī kṛtya prasyuṃnaḥ

ṣoḍhānyāsoṇimādiśca mūladevyādikaḥ priye

ṣoḍānyāsa samāyuktaṃ śrīcakranyāsamiṣṭadaṃ

tejomaya tanurbhūtvā jīvanmuktaḥ sukhī bhavet

siddhāṣṭaka manuprāpya trailoke viharatyasau

kṛtvā ṣoḍābhidhaṃ nyāsaṃ śrīcakranyāsamapyatha

tejomaya tanurbhūtvā śriyamaṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

kartānyāsa dvayasyāsya tadajñaṃ pitaraṃ tu vā

praṇamennāpi daivaṃ vā vinā svācāryamīśvaraṃ

siddhakṣetraṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ samaṃtā yojanatrayaṃ

tatra gaṃgāgayākāśīkurukṣetraṃ dhanaimisvaṃ

ājñā siddhikaraṃnyāsa dvayame tatsūdurlabhaṃ

ṣaṇmāsābhyā sa yogena vidhiviṣṇu śivātmakaḥ

na vaktavyamidaṃ nyāsa sa dvayaṃ sakalasiddhidaṃ

śrīviyāyā japo nityanitya samācāryayoṣaṇaṃ

hṛdanyāsadvayaṃ nityaṃ kṛtvā kṛtvāpi kāśuciḥ

cidrūpaṃ paramaṃdhāmaṃtraipurī kṛtā vigrahaḥ

nyāsa jātamidaṃ tubhyaṃ kathitaṃ girikanyake

śrīsuṃdaryā mahādevyā yajaṃ saṃpravakṣyate

uttarabhāge saubhāgyavidyodaye paṃcamodhyāyaḥ

dhyātvā devaṃ vījagadvātrīṃ śriyaṃ saubhāgyadevatā

maunena praviśedyāgamanuṃ parasyamīśvari

ādhāraśakti kamalāsanāya nama uccaret

tṛṇāsaneya śohādbhirdaurbhāgyaṃ dārukāsane

vicitrakaṃ valesiddhirmokṣa śrīrvyāghracarmaṇi

kuśāsane bhavedgānaṃ ratnapīṭheṣṭa siddhayaḥ

uccāryatā ratritayaṃ raktā vā daśataḥ pethara

uccāryahṛdayaścādbhūmau puṣpāṃjalīḥ kṣipet

mānasālaṃkṛto vāpi śuklāṃvaradharaḥ śuciḥ

paṃcaśakti catuḥśaṃbhurūpaṃ cakraṃ manoharaṃ

vasukoṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā daśāra dvitayaṃ tataḥ

tataḥ ṣoḍaśa patrāṇi likhedvattayutānyapi

samastayātakahara samastaphalasiddhidaṃ

mohanaṃ sarvajagatāṃ sarvatīrthaphalapradaṃ

nityānaṃdakaraṃ bhaktabhoigare vargāpavargadaṃ

sarvadharmamayaṃ sarvaratnavittaphalapradaṃ

bhūtapretapiśādi sarvabhīti nivārakaṃ

āyurārogpadaṃ svasti putrapautrābhivṛddhikaṃ

lokāyavādabhītighnaṃ mayamṛtyu vināśanaṃ

alakṣmīnāśanaṃ dhanyaṃ ciṃtāmarāyādi siddhidaṃ

vedavedāṃtaśāstraudya sārabhūtaṃ yaśaskaraṃ

ākarṣaṇakaraṃ sarvastaṃbhanaṃ jaṃbhanaṃ tathā

śāṃti vṛṣṭi dhanārogya maṃtrasiddhikaraṃ paraṃ

sarvasiddhikaraṃ śreyaskara manuptayaṃ

sarvajñānamayaṃ sarvavācāṃ siddhitvapradaṃ

sarvavratamayaṃ devi sarvāmṛtamayaṃ tathā

sarvonmādakaraṃ devi sarvāhlādakānarekaṃ

raudrāmicārako daṃḍa paramaṃtraugha bhakṣaṇaṃ

parasainyastaṃbhakaraṃ śastrastaṃbhanakārakaṃ

mahājvaraharaṃ devi mahāmṛtyuvināśanaṃ

mahālakṣmīmayaṃ devi mahāmāṃgalyasiddhidaṃ

itthame tasya cakrasya prabhāvo varṇito mayā

śrīcakrameta devoktaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇābhidhaṃ

śaktāśyakti vinirbhiya punarvahni purena vai

nāmādi cakrādhaḥ śaktiṃ vahninorddhvaṃ vibhedayet

ūrdhvaśaktimadho vahni madhyavahni vivarjitaṃ

tato madhyādi śaktyardhaṃ śaktivistārayedadhaḥ

vācatena maheśāni vahnicakrecaṇa bhedayet

madhyodhvarśati paryaṃtamādirā avadhipriye

tato vikāśayecchaktimādyāmapyūrddhamīśvara

vistārya vāhyaśaktiṃ tu sarvāṣastādvi bhedayet

tadvāhyatopi deveśi ṣoḍaśāraṃ tataḥ paraṃ

tataḥ sṛṣṭe mahācakraṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu hutāśanaḥ

evametanmahācakraṃ śriyo devyā udīritaṃ

agnīṣomātmakamidaṃ sarvavidyāmayaṃ priye

mātṛkāvarṇayustūjyamatha tadrahitaṃ tu vā

cakresmin sa viśeṣāstāsmādhakaṃ mānayaṃti hi

muktāmāṇikyaghaṭita samasthala virājitaṃ

ṣoḍaśāraṃ caṃdraviṃvarūpaṃ tu sakalālaya

aṣṭapatraṃ maheśāni japākusuma sannibhaṃ

etat trayaṃ maheśāni sṛṣṭicakraṃ sukhapradaṃ

dvitīyamapi padmapyaṃ japākusuma sānnibhaṃ

caturdaśāraṃ devevaśidādimī kusumaprabhaṃ

etat trayaṃ maheśāni sthiticakraṃ sthitipradaṃ

aṣṭapatraṃ varārohe vālārka kiraṇāruṇaṃ

upatsūrya sahasrābhaṃ vaṃdhūka kusumaprabhaṃ

trikoṇaṃ sarvasaṃbhūtikāraṇabhūtidaṃ sadā

sadāśivamayaṃ camranāyakaṃ parameśvari

paścimāmrāya saṃsevyaṃ trayāmuttarapūjitaṃ

cakrapatreṣu deveśi padādhvātu nigadyate

varṇādhvā mātṛkārūpī kathayāmita vā nadye

pārthivaṃ tanmayaṃ viddhi ṣoḍaśāṃskalāśrakaṃ

kādiśaṃtāḥ śakraparṇāḥ śakrakoṇeṣu saṃsthitāḥ

makārādi kṣakārāṃtān dvitīyepi daśārake

akathādi trikoṇāṃtaṃ hākṣayugmaṃ tu madhyagaṃ

ṣaṭtriṃśattatvabharituṃ cakraṃ mūlānusārataḥ

sa kālā bharavīyuktā mahātripurasuṃdarī

maṃtrādhvāyaṃ samākhyātau niścayena sadānaghe

merurūpaṃ mahīrūpaṃ dvidhā pūjārthamīṣyate

yadā suvarṇa lekhinyā likhitaṃ saṃprapūjayet

ayodhyānagaraṃ cakrarājaṃ nitya samarcayet

*śri māśāttayā jñeyā pūrvā śaiva nigadyate

dakṣiṇāśāmukho devi yathā cakraṃ samarcayet

uttarāśā mukhye devi yathācakraṃ samarcayet

īśānakoṇaṃ deveśi tadāgreyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

sarvāgamamayaṃ devi kathitaṃ vīravaṃdite

sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ viśvamarcidaṃ tasyānnaṃ saṃśayaḥ

abhyarcātha gṛhītvaitaccakraṃ sakalasiddhidaḥ

ajarāmaraṇo bhūtvā siddhiṃ prāprauti śāśvatīṃ

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi pūjāvidhi mataḥ paraṃ

pratiṣṭhāpyārcayeddhīmān sādhakaḥ śuddhamānasaḥ

amṛtāṃbho nidhiḥ pūvaṃ ratnadvīpa mataḥ paraṃ

saṃtānavāṭikā paścāddhari caṃdanavāṭikā

kadevavanavāṭī ca vāṭikā paṃcakīrtitāḥ

uccāryāḥ sakalārmatrā japahomārcanīdiṣudaṃ

padmarāga mahāratna prākaraśca tataḥ paraṃ

vaiḍūryaratna prākāra iṃdranīlābhidhastathā

vidrumākhya mahāratnaprākāro navamaṃ smṛtaḥ

atha mṛtākhyavāṭīsyāttata ānaṃdavāṭikā

caṃdrikoddhāramabhyarcya tataḥ śaileṃdra kanyake

ciṃtāmaṇi mahāratna gṛharājastataḥ paraṃ

dakṣiṇāmrāya rūpaṃ taddakṣiṇadvāramapyatha

uttarāmnāya rūpaṃ cottaradvāraṃ tatocayet

atha brahmamayaṃ madhva pādamekaṃ samarcayet

tato rudramayaṃ maṃca pādamekaṃ samarcayet

sadāśivamayamaṃ ca phalakaṃ ca tataḥ paraṃ

haṃtūla mahopādyadhānamabhyarcayedartha

mahāyavanikāṃ paścāddevī medirapūjakāḥ

bhāvayanmaṃdiraṃ devyāmatraitaiḥ samarcayet

datvābhitastatodīpāṃścakraṃ mūlena pūjayet

vādyagni jalasaṃyukta prāṇāyāmaistribhi priye

mūlavidyāṃ samuccāya prāṇānāyamya ca tridhā

aya sarpaṃtuye bhūtāye bhūtā bhuvi saṃsthitāḥ

iti maṃtreṇa savyānya pārśvāṣṇi pātena śailaje

devyahaṃ bhāvanāyuktaḥ saśarīre tataḥ priye

karaśuddhiṃ puraḥ kṛtvā kumārīmuccaredatha

ātmānaṃ rakṣkṣetimikṣipya hṛdayeṃñjaliṃ

devyātmāsa na śadaṃ ca caturthyaṃta namonvitaṃ

śriyaṃ māyāṃ kumārīṃ ca proccārya manunāmunā

uccārya bhuvaneśānī parāvījaṃ kumārikāṃ

trilokeśī kāmarājaṃ klamakṣaramataḥ paraṃ

uccāryānena maṃtrāṇāṃ tritayena ca sādhakuḥ

vinyasya mātṛkāṃ paścadviśinyādyavakuṃ tataḥ

ṣoḍhā cakrābhidhanyāsa dvitayaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

śuddhāṃbhasā vāmabhāge savṛtta caturasrakaṃ

pratiṣṭhāpya ṣaḍaṃgena samabhyarcya tataḥ paraṃ

tena saṃprokṣeyadarcā ityamātmona tathatha

kṛtvā tatra viśeṣārghyaṃ kṛtvāpyarvayecchrityāṃ

tvāmalena madhye ca vidyā ** trikoṇake

athavā bhava muccārya caturthyaṃta samāśvitaṃ

arghyapātrāyāraśakaṃ caturthī nati saṃyutaṃ

dhūmrārcirūṣmājvalinī jvālinī visphurliṃginī

vahnerdaśakalāṃ proktāyādi varṇasamudbhavāḥ

sūryamaṃḍala śabdaṃ ca caturthī sahitaṃ tataḥ

arghyapātrapadaṃ paścāccaturthī hṛdayanvitaṃ

dhūmrāmarīcirapi ca jvālinī rucirapyatha

kalā dvādaśa saṃproktā etāḥ mavituriṣṭadāḥ

parāṃ somapadaṃ paścānmaṃḍalāya paraṃ tataḥ

arghyā mṛtā yadā kaṃcanamaskāra samanvitaṃ

amṛtāmānadā pūṣā tuṣṭiṃtpuṣṭiratirdhṛtiḥ

pūrṇāpūrṇā mṛtākā sadāyinyaḥ ṣoḍaśeṣṭadāḥ

tatrābhyārcyā namaskāryā akathāyakṣarātmakaṃ

*kkāma śaktisahita koṇe tritayamupyatha

kṛtvā dhyātvā ṣaḍaṃgena samabhyarcya tataḥ paraṃ

caturnavati saṃkhyākān maṃtrān spṛṣṭvā japedatha

aṣṭatriṃśati saṃkhyākā uktāḥ parvata kanyake

sthitiśca siddhirityuktā daśabrahmakalā imāḥ

varadāndrādinī prīti dīrghā viṣṇukalā imāḥ

utkārī mṛtyurityuktā daśarudrakalā imāḥ

catvāra uktā etāśca kalāḥ sarvaphalapradāḥ

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyāśāṃtistathaiva ca

sūkṣmā tato sūkṣmāmṛtā jñānā tataḥ paraṃ

sadāśiva kalā etāḥ ṣoḍaśasthara saṃbhavāḥ

saḥśuciṣadityuktvā vasuraṃ tadekṣetyātha

taduroṇū sadatha prokta nṛṣadvarasadityatha

maṃtrāṃte ca pratadviṣṇusrapyaṃvakaṃ manustataḥ

mūlavidyātha saṃproktāścaturtavati maṃtrakāḥ

caturnavati maṃtraṃti pravadaṃtyibhi maṃtraṇaṃ

chaṃda sphuraṇāmātranidhehya kulavanāyike

amṛtatvaṃ nidhehyasmin vastuvitkinmarūpiṇī

bhūtvā parāmṛtākāraṃmayī citsphuraṇaṃ kuru

amṛte padamuccārya padamapya mṛtodbhāve

amṛtaṃ srāvaya yugaṃ vahni jñātayā samānvitaṃ

vāgbhavaṃ vadayugmaṃ ca padaṃ vāgvādinītyatha

kledaya dvitayaṃ paścāt mahākṣobhaṃ kuru dvayaṃ

anaṃtaṃ caṃdramavanaṃ manusvara samanvitaṃ

manusvara visargādhyamuccarediti paṃcamī

etābhissarvavidyābhirabhimaṃtryāmitaṃ śivā

tadviṃdubhirbrahmaraṃdhre guru tritayapādukāḥ

ātmanaḥ kuṃḍalinyāṃ ca tadviṃduṃ vodhasiddhidaṃ

iti kṛtvā viśeṣārghyaṃ vatsarevatsarepi vā

sarvajñaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥsyājjīvanmukto na saṃśayaḥ

śrīcakraṃ pūjayitvāśu sarvabhāgyabhidhirbhavat

yatkṛtvāpi śucinirtyarbhahā saubhāgyavān bhavet

dhyādhatvā saṃvinmayī devī mahātripurasuṃdarī

aṃtaḥ suṣumṇā kamalādi vinirbhedana kṣamāṃ

samastaloka dīpasya dīptibhutī sanātanī

jñānāṃ sāpuṭe nirgamayya līlā saṃkalpitā kṛtiṃ

trikhaṃḍamudrāsahito khāṃjalau kusumānvite

śrīmahātripuret yuktvā suṃdarīti padaṃ tataḥ

tato mūrtikalpaṣāmi nama ityamunā śive

vījatrayamidaṃ bhūyopyuccārya girikanyake

mahāpadmavanāṃtasthe kāraṇānaṃtavigrahe

iti maṃtreṇa paramāmicchā kalpitavigrahāṃ

catuḥṣaṣṭyupacārāṃśca kuryāddaivyai tataḥ paraṃ

yāmīti namasāyuktā upacāsmanūttamāḥ

śrīdevyai pādyamuccārya kalpayāmi namaḥ **

ityādi maṃtrān sakulānupacārābhidhānusmaret

atha maṃjanamaṃḍapaṇi pīṭhopaveśanaṃ

tata ullodakasnānaṃ kanaketi pada tataḥ

rthota vastraparītyuktvāmārjanaṃta tataḥ paraṃ

aruṇena dukūlenottarīyamathaṃ kalpayet

athālepana maṃḍapamaṇapīṭhopaveśanaṃ

atha śaṃkumadetyuktvā padaṃ mṛgamadetyatha

divyagaṃdha padaṃ sarvāgāṇāṃtace vilepanaṃ

mallikā mālatī jāti caṃpakāśoka śabdataḥ

punnāgatyatha kahlāramukhya savartuśabdataḥ

atha bhūṣaṇa śabdāṃte maṃḍapāte praveśanaṃ

pīṭhopavaiśarna paścādupacāra prakalpaye

atha sīmetā siṃdūraṃ tatastilaka ratnakaṃ

maṇikuṃdulayugmaṃ ca nāsābharaṇamatha

saṃtapta hemaciṃtākaṃvarakaṃ ca tataḥ paraṃ

ekāvalī tato devicchanna vīramataḥ

valayāvalimapyatra kalpayitvomikāvalī

kalpayet pādakaṭekaṃ ratnanūpuramapyatha

anyahasteṃkuśaṃ paścādapya pradrakṣu saṃbhavaṃ

śrīmanmāṇikya śabdāṃte pāduke kalpayedatha

devatābhissahetyuktvā mahācakrādhiṇehṛṇaṃ

sudhā savākhya caṣakaṃ tata ānamanīyakaṃ

paraṃ vilāsahāsaṃcamaṃgalā rātrika tataḥ

gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpadīpanaivedyāni ca kalpayet

samarpayitvā śrī devyai navamudrāḥ pradarśya ca

agnīśāsura vāyavya madhyādikṣu girīdraṃje

atha nityārcana vakṣye śṛṇuthaikāgnamānasā

vā ca sakalaśabdaṃ ca bhuvaneśī mataḥ paraṃ

eṣā kāmeśvarī vidyā sarvakāma samṛddhidā

ādau vidyāṃ samuccārya padaṃ kāmeśvrītyatha

vācaṃ bhagabhuge śabdaṃ bhaginīti bhagodari

bhagaguhye bhagetyuktvā yoginī ca bhagetyatha

bhagarūpetha nityādya klinne bhaga pada tataḥ

mehyānaya padaṃ paścādvarade pāmapyartha

kledayadrāvayet yuktvā śramodye padamapyatha

sarvasarvānathoccārya bhageśvari padaṃ tataḥ

parayektadomityathoccārya klinne sarvāṇi śabdataḥ

padaharapalaṃ māyāṃ nityaiṣā bhagamālinī

vahnijāyeti nityoktvā nityaklinnābhidhā parā

jrauṁ svāheti caturthyeṣābhe rudrāṃ sarvasiddhidā

padaṃ ca vahni vāsinyai namasānvitamapyatha

hrī klinne vā ca muccārya vījamaṃkuśanāmakaṃ

mahāvajreśvarī nityā ṣaṣṭhīyaṃ sarvadvidā

śivādūtyabhidhā nityā saptamī samuvīritā

kṣuśrī hūṃ kṣetrilokeśī padamastra mataḥ paraṃ

kumāryeva tataḥ proktā nityātha kulasuṃdarī

hasāyakatrāretyuktvādārṇa viṃdusmarānvitaṃ

nityābhidhe yamuddiṣṭānitthā sarvaśubhapradā

mukhavṛtta kāmarājaṃ vācyaglmāmityathoccaret

pre jrauṁ nīlapatākeyaṃ nityā sarvaphalapradā

viṃdvaṃ kitamiyaṃ nityā vijayāśu phalapradā

uktā samastaphaladā nittheyaṃ sarvamaṃgalā

jvālāmāliniśakāṃte devadeva pada tataḥ

jātavedasi śabdāṃte jvaladvitayamapyatha

raphasamakamuccārya jvālāmālinyathādrite

samastaiśvarya phaladā rogaśokanikṛṃtanā

klinnā paṃcadaśī nityāmā mahāsiddhipradā smṛtā

śrīvidyaiva mahāvidyā lalitā parameśvarī

nityā paṃcaśa prāktāyā etā girikanyake

ṣoḍaśīyā mahānityā mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī

pratipatyaurṇamāsyaṃta divaseṣva khileṣvapi

nityā paṃcadaśārcāṃta maṃtramuccārya yatnataḥ

nityā maṃtra japaṃ homamarcanaṃ yodite dine

nityā paṃcadaśātyarcya tithi nityā phalapradāṃ

mahadbhāgyama vāpnoti devānāmapi dullābhaṃ

janabhyacyātha vā nityaṃ śrīdevī lalitāṃ parāṃ

gururpāduḥ samabhyarcya śreyo rāśi mavāpnuyāt

gurupaṅkti manabhyarcya na vṛddhiṃ yāti bhūtale

śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmītyaṣṭārṇaṃ yojayestriye

pareśa parameśyaṃvāṃ gururūpāṃ purorcayat

paracaryānātha tato lopāmudrāṃ vāgastya saṃjñakau

sarvatrā naṃdanātha śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāni ca

ānaṃdanātha śabdo na lopāmudrā gurau śive

pati brahmāmukhāllabdhā vidyā salasiddhidāḥ

dinedinesya śreyāṃsi varddhate sarvasaṃpadaḥ

tāmabhyarcya naro nityaṃ śrīdhiyā sarvasiddhibhāk

jaganmāt dhiyābhyayā viśepeṇa mahotsave

divyaughamādāvabhyarcya siddhauyamatha pūjaptena

muktakeśīśvaraṃ dīpakalānāthamathoryayet

siddhauyamiti saṃpūjya mānavaughamathārcayet

tejodevamathābhyarcya manojaṃ ca tataḥ priye

paraṃ śrīrāmānaṃdanāthākhyamarcayejjagatāṃ guruṃ

viśeṣatārcayettredhā tataḥ svagurupādukāṃ

kṛtvālayogapūjāṃ vā pratyahaṃ sādhakottamaḥ

bhogāṃgākhyaṃ mahādevi bhogasvargāpavargadaṃ

vaśitva siddhiṃ prākāmya siddhiṃ bhuktimataḥ paraṃ

caturasre prāthamike pūjyā etā mama priye

kaumārī vaiṣṇavīmaṃ vā vārāhī ca tataḥ paraṃ

tṛtīye caturasretha mudrāṃ saṃkṣobhiṇīyapi

sarvavaśyābhidhāṃ mudrāṃ sarvonmādanikāṃ tathā

sarvatrikhaṃḍamudrāṃ ca pūjeyadiri kanyake

pūrvādi kramatobhyarcyā brāhmādyāḥ sarvamātaraḥ

etāḥ stritārī vījādyāḥ pādukāṃtāḥ prapūjayet

yoginyaḥ padamābhāṣya padaṃ trailokya mohanai

sāyudhāḥ padamābhāṣya padapaścāt saśaktayaḥ

vāraismapūjitāssaṃtu maṃtreṇānena suṃdari

tāratritayamuccārya karaśuddhikaramatuṃ

uccārya tripurācakreśvarya vā śrīpadaṃ tataḥ

sarvaṃ saṃkṣobhiṇī mudrāṃ vījapūrvāṃ pradarśayet

kalāsretha mahāpadme vakṣye pūjāvidhiṃ śṛṇu

pūjayet sakalānityāṃ kalā ṣoḍaśaśailaje

kāmākarṣiṇikā vudhyākarṣiṇī ca tataḥ paraṃ

sparśākarṣaṇikā rūpākarṣiṇī ca tataḥ paraṃ

cittākarṣaṇikā dhairyākarṣiṇī ca tataḥ paraṃ

vījākārṣeṇikācātmākarṣiṇī ca tataḥ paraṃ

etānityā kalāṃtāḥ syuḥ ṣoḍaśārādhi devatāḥ

etā guptapadāṃte ca yoginyaḥ padamuccaret

cakre samudrā ityādi śeṣaṃ pūrvavaduccaret

cakreśvaryai namaḥ śabdaṃ samuccārya tataḥ paraṃ

śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmītyuktvā cakreśvarīṃ yajet

sarvaviṇīmudrāṃ vījapūrvaṃ pradarśayet

tāratritayamuccārya kavargādyaṣṭakaṃ tataḥ

anaṃga kusumānaṃga mekhalā ca tataḥ paraṃ

anaṃgarekhācānaṃgaveginī yamalocane

devī śrīpādukā muktvā pūjayāmīti yojayet

sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇe cakre śiṣṭaṃ pūrvavaduccaret

cakreśvaryai namaḥ paścātpunastripurasuṃdarī

itthaṃ tṛtīyāvaraṇaiśvarī tripurasuṃdarī

devatā arthayet paścāt sarvasaubhāgyadāyake

sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī śaktissarvavidrāviṇī tathā

sarvasammohinī sarvastaṃbhinī ca tataḥ paraṃ

sarvaraṃjanikā sarvonmādinī ca tataḥ śive

sarvamaṃtramayīśakti sarvadvaṃdva kṣayaṃkarī

tritāraitāssaṃpradāya yogibhyaśca tataḥ paraṃ

śiṣṭaṃ pūrvavaduccārya cakre saubhāgyakecaye

vāsinīti padaṃ cakreśvarye śabdaṃ namonvitaṃ

cakreśvarī padaṃ paścādvasuvarṇaṃ samuccaret

sarvārthasādhake cakre devatārcā pravakṣyeta

sarvapriyaṃkarī devī sarvamaṃgalakāriṇī

sarvamṛtyupraśamanī sarvavighnanivāriṇī

ṇakārādi phakārāṃtā etā devīssamarcayet

śrīpādukā pūjayāmītyuktvā cakreśvarī yajet

sarvarakṣākare cakre devatā pūjayedatha

sarvajñānmayī devī sarvavyādhi vināśinī

sarvānaṃdamayī devī sarvarakṣā svarūpiṇī

tretāryātāni garbhādya yoginyaḥ padamapyatha

samaṣṭi pūjā kṛtvātha sādhya siddhāsanātparaṃ

uccārya tripuret yuktvā mālinī padamapyatha

uccārya pūjayeccakrarājñī tripuramālinī

sarvaṇyo hare cakre devatā arghayedatha

pūjāmaṃtra samāyuktaṃ namaḥ sthāne sayaccāyat

cakre śeṣamathoccārya tritārādyāssamarcayet

vīmathoyāryamudrāṃ kuryācca khecarī

drāṁ drīṁ klī ṣṇū mathoccāryasaḥ sarvapadamapyatha

atha tretārinādyaṃte sarvasaṃmohanīya ca

vaśīkaraṇaśabdaṃ ca caturthī hṛdayānvitaṃ

nama uktvā caturdikṣu vāṇādīniti pūrayet

mahāvajreśvarī paścānmahāśrībhagamālinī

mahāśrīsuṃdarī paṃścāt khaṃḍatritayapūrvikāṃ

yoginī navamī cakradevī ca navamī tathā

atha vaidava cakrasthā lalitā paradevatā

na maṃtra devatā bhedo vidye tatra samapriye

sarvasiddhipradecakre śeṣaṃ pūrvavaduccaret

uccārya tripurāvāṃ cakraiśvaryai nama uccaret

śrīpākā pūjayāmītyuktvā cakreśvarīṃ yajet

vaidaṃvetha mahācakre sāmrājī saṃvidaṃ yajet

suṃdari śrīmahāśabdaṃ tataśca parameśvarī

uktvābhyacyotratāryaiṣā parāpara padaṃ tataḥ

cakre samudrā śabdaṃ ca sa siddhissāyudhā padaṃ

padaṃ sadyarivārā sarvopacārairataḥ paraṃ

uktvātha śrīmahāśabdaṃ tripureti padātparaṃ

uktvātha śrīmahāśabdaṃ tripuretyatha suṃdarī

śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmītyarcayesaradevatāṃ

dhūpaṃ dīpanivedyātha navamudrāḥ pradarśayet

tarpayitvātha naivedya bhagavatyai samarpayet

hastā prakṣālane datvā tāṃvūlaṃ ca nivedayet

yoniṃ dhyātvā **dhenaṃ ciṃtayettaradhomasyaṃ

japet saubhāgya hṛdayaṃ bhogamokṣa phalapradaṃ

kalāpaṃcadaśārabdhaṃ namanīyaṃ paraṃ mahaḥ

aśeṣa viśvamedātmā pūrṇahṛtātmakaṃ śivaṃ

ekasmaimahatye tasmai namassakala cakṣuṣe

nānāvidha mahākārasānu bhavakāraṇaṃ

tattadrūpeṇayābhātitāṃ śrayehaṃvidho kalāṃ

yā tanustenaḍit prakhyātāṃ bhajebhavaśālinīṃ

ājñāyāṃ caṃdrasaṃkāśaṃ mahasta ca maheśvari

bhavāni bhavaśītyaitvāṃ bhāvayāmya mṛteśvari
