ityaṣva ṣaṣṭiṃ deveśi bhātitvapi cidātmani

uśrīlayasimātastva prakāśa vapuṣitvapi

prakāśavapuṣastva ca tatsarvaṃ nātiricyate

vāraṃvāraṃtvayā devi rūpādi madhusevyate

yogīnāṃ prīṣayatvāryatva pūjārthaṃ hi sarvadā

samārādhana pūrvarthaṃ smṛtaṃ sakalakāmadaṃ

saubhāgya hṛdayaṃ japtvā vāmabhāge tataḥ paraṃ

vāgmarva paścādvayakāṃte tataḥ paraṃ

tatra pātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya praṇavaṃ bhuvaneśvarī

* vahni bhāminī mityaṃ triruccārya tataḥ paraṃ

mūlavidyāṃ samuccārya tāratritayamapyatha

ārātrikamidaṃ dīpaṃ gṛhāṇā mama siddhaye

datvā puṣpāṃjaliṃ samyak tridhā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ

śrīvidyābhyarcya gaṃdhādyaistāṃvūlādyaissuvāsinī

kṣamasveti śriyaṃ devīmātmanyudvāsanettataḥ

śriyamaṣṭaguṇāṃ prāpya brahmarūpo bhavatyasau

śravaṇādapi yasyasyussiddhayoṣṭāṇimādayaḥ

māsapūjādhamā proktā pātakaudya vibhedinī

ṣaṇmāsamanvahaṃ bhaktyā cakrarājārcanā śubhaṃ

yaḥ kuryādvatsaranityāṃ navāvaraṇapūjanaṃ

vedaśāstrapurāṇādi vidyā api sudurlabhāḥ

dharmārtha kāmokṣāthi prakṣavārthā sudurlabhāḥ

ādau maṃdirapūjāsyāllayāṃgāryā tataḥ paraṃ

layāṃgā pūjā kṛtvā vā satataṃ sādhakottamaḥ

arghayan sarvasaubhāgyasaṃpannassiddhimātravet

vāhvahvā triśapte makātejayanyaṃ vadaśākṣarī

āṇimādyaṣṭa siddhīnāṃ nāyako jagatītale

cakrasihāsanaṃ devi deśe vā nagarepi vā

svagṛhe vā vane grāme jagatkṣobhaḥ prajāyate

pipīlikādinyāyena dūrādāyāṃtiyoṣitaḥ

taddarśanān mahādevi jāyaṃte sarvayoṣitaḥ

tasyāpi siddhirmahatī jāyate na cirādiha

dadāti sakalānarthānvatsaravrittayesmahi

sāṃgaṃ prapūjyate yatra dhanadhānyādi saṃkulā

dhiṣaṇādhiya sādṛśyaṃ vidyābhiścāsya sidhyati

putrapautrādi saṃpattiracirādasya sidhyati

bhavatyasya na saṃdeho viśvāsaṃ kurubhāmini

vījagarbhamavījaṃ vā yena mityaṃ prapūjyate

kiṃkarādavaniṣṭaṃti mūrtimatyo mama triye

gajāśvaratha saṃyuktā paśubhṛtya gaṇānvitā

tiṣṭhatye vā valā lakṣmīrdaivatairapi durlabhā

putrapautrābhivṛthyāsau kaśyapa pratimo bhavet

rājāno rājamāścattadbhṛtyāḥ sacivā api

nivasaṃti gṛhe yasya teṣāmapi naro gatā

svacchaṃdamaraṇaṃ tasya vaśyānāmapi jāyate

śrapacānāgṛhaṃ vāpita uktaṃ vadarīvanaṃ

cakrarājārcanaṃ samya kurvannanvahamādarāt

haima cakrārcanaṃ nityaṃ kṛtvā pratyahamādarāt

maṃḍala dvitayādvarddhaṃ rājānaṃ nagaraṃ tu vā

ṣaṇmāsaṃ pūjayedyastu varṇa cakranāyakaṃ

brahmaṣīnapi devaṣīnyatragānu surānapi

śrīcakra manvahaṃ haimaṃ sāṃśaṃ varṇaṃ prapūjayan

vaśīkaroti trailokyaṃ sa devā surarākṣasaṃ

raṃbhorvaśyādikā divyākṣarasopi manoharā

vaśībhūtā bhavaṃtyasyadāsīvannātra saṃśayaḥ

sarvavidyādhipatyaṃ ca jarāmṛtyu nivartanaṃ

muktāratna samṛddhiśca śreyo vṛddhirdine dine

atha vakṣye maheśāni śrīvidyāpūjanaṃ mahat

brahmapuṣpairmaheśāni sāṃgaṃ śrīcakramanvahaṃ

vimukto mahatīṃ lakṣmī dhanadhānyādi saṃkulāṃ

japāpuṣpaissamabhyarcya śrīkraṃsāṃgamanvahaṃ

vimukto nātra saṃdeho dhanavāna jāyate vudhaḥ

upapātakasaṃghāṃśca māsamātreṇa nāśayet

karavīrai samabhyarcya śrīcakraṃ sāṃgamanvahaṃ

vidhayājīva saṃkāśodhanena dhanadopamaḥ

śatapatrairmanoramyairmāsamadhaṃ samarcayan

saubhāgyamatulaṃ prāpya śriyaṃ prāpnotya caṃcalāṃ

māsamātreṇa muktosau mahāpāpopapātakaiḥ

adhiyassarvavidyānāṃ bhūtvā kāma samaprabhaḥ

vaṃdhūka kusumairnityaṃ māsamātraṃ samarjayan

vilvapatraissamabhyarcya māsamātraṃ niraṃtaraṃ

vilvapatraiśca jalajasahitaiḥ paripūjyan

āpuṣpaṃ mahṛdārogyaṃ samṛddhiṃ ca mahattarāṃ

mallikā kusumaiścakrarājamabhyarcyasādaraṃ

saṃprāpya vipulā lakṣmīṃ prāpnotyaṃte paraṃ padaṃ

śaitotvalairvā miśraiśca pratyahaṃ cakramarcayan

māsādvardhamavāpnoti śriyaṃ vidyāṃ ca durlabhāṃ

cakrarājaṃ samābhyarcya māsaṃ sāṃgaṃ prasannadhīḥ

tejasā ravisaṃkāśo bhūtvā pātaka varjitaḥ

jaṃvūpravālairabhyarcya cakrarājaṃ dine dine

avalāṃ śriya māsādya putrapautra sakhānvitaḥ

śamī dūrvāṃ kurairvāpi cakrarājaṃ samarcayan

saubhāgyamatulaṃ prāpya śriyamā protya caṃcalāṃ

vimuktaḥ sakalaiḥ pāpairlabhate kṣayasaṃpadaḥ

cakrarājaṃ samabhyarcya māsamekaṃ prasannadhīḥ

sugaṃdhi kusumairnityaṃ pallavairvā sugaṃdhibhiḥ

samasta pāparāśibhyo vimuktaḥ śriyamakṣayaṃ

ratnapūjāvidhānaṃ ca kathayāmita vā naye

nānāpuṣpaissugaṃdhaiśca karpūrakṣoda caṃdanaiḥ

dāsabhūtā karotyeva mahārogāṃścānāśāyet

muktāralaiśca racayet svarṇa puṣpāṇi suṃdari

ekaviṃśati rātreṇa paśya bhūyāna saṃśayaḥ

pravālaghaṭitaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiḥ śrīcakramarcayan

vaśyā bhavaṃti duṣṭāśca bhūpālā nātra saṃśayaḥ

vināśayati śakraṃśca vivudhānvaśamānayet

kusumaiḥ pūjayeccakraṃ trisaptāhāt sureśvari

vidyayā jīva sadṛśo bhavatyatra na saṃśayaḥ

ekaviṃśati rātreṇayorcayeccakranāyakaiṃ

vaśīkaroti trailokyaṃ sa devāsuramānavaṃ

trailokyaṃ staṃbhayannyeva trisamādānna saṃśayaḥ

dāsībhūtāstaruropāpināryo yauvanagarvitāḥ

saptāhādvairiṇo vaśyā rājānopi na saṃśayaḥ

vaiḍūryaghaṭitaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiścakraṃ samarcayan

sarvevaśyā bhavaṃtyeva pāpaudyaiśca vimucyate

nirmālya bhūtaiḥ kusumairucchiṣṭhaiḥ parameśvari

tadā devāsuranarāḥ pannagā rākṣasā api

janmakoṭi kṛtaiyā pairapi māsādvinucyate

daśāśvamaṃdhajatitaṃ phalamāpnonmanuttamaṃ

tairiva puṣpaiḥ pūjādi kartavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ

tatra tatra pradātavyaṃ na dātavyaṃ yathecchayo

koṭijanmakṛtaiḥ pāpairapi māsādimucyate

sugaṃdhi puṣpayuktairvāsujaṃ kṛpallavaistuvāḥ

cakrarāja samābhyarcya nimuktassarvapātakaiḥ

saṃprāpya śāśvatā puṣpaṃ putrapautrevatīṃ śriyaṃ

kārtike mārgaśīṣe vā māghe vā phālgunepi vā

saṃprārthya vahudhācāryaṃ śrīcakraṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

pūjitaṃ yena sauvarṇa śrīcakraṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

tadeva meruśikharaṃ kailāsasthāna mucyate

yasmindine sau gṛhṇāti svācārya karayaṃkajāt

ācāryapatnyai śrīvudhyā dadyādābharaṇa śubhaṃ

yathāśaktatā samabhyarcya svācāryamapi bhūṣaṇaiḥ

taṃ pālayaṃti sutavadyoginyo mātaropi ca

padmahastāḥ subhūṣaṇāḥ

arcayitvā śaṃkhapadmaunidhī ca dhṛta cāmarau

paścādbhāge svarṇaṃ daṃḍayuta chatradharaṃ yajet

arkamiṃduṃkujaṃ saimyaṃ jīvabhārgavamapyatha

arcayeddimājān dikṣu caturdaja vibhūṣitān

puṣpadaṃtaṃ sārvabhaumaṃ supratīkamiti kramāt

brahmaṇa mūrdhvādigbhāgeyattedviṣṇumadhopi ca

itthamāvaraṇopetāṃ mahālakṣmīṃ haripriyāṃ

saṃprāpnoti mahadbhāgyaṃ śāśvataṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

datvā siṃhāsanādīni samabhyarcyātha vidyayā

śrīdharaṃ ca hṛṣīkeśaṃ vaikuṃṭhaṃ viśvarūpakaṃ

patramūleṣu saṃpūjya dalamadhye tataḥ paraṃ

tadannetha ca yedvārṇān mahālakṣmyāḥ kramādamūn

poṣaṃ calaṃ ca tejaśca maheṃdrādidigīśvarān

anena vidhinarevī mahālakṣmī yajaṃtiye

yena kenāpi vidhinā pratyahaṃ sādhakottamaḥ

bhūtvā sakalavidyāmā māśrayo durlabhānapi

athavānyanmahadbhuptamarcanaṃ śṛṇu vallabhe

samastavedataṃtreṣu guptā tatvārthavedibhiḥ

tāṃtrivāraṃ sayuccārya lakṣmīmetāṃ samuccaran

śrīpatī samāvāhya dvādaśākṣaraviyayā

keśavādi caturviśannāmabhiḥ sādaraṃ vibhuṃ

samabhyarcya mahālakṣmīvratya vidyāmataḥ paraṃ

uccārya pūjayitvātha datvā naivedyamādarāt

jaśvāmedhaśa tasyāsau pratyahaṃ phalamaśrute

prāpnoti ca mahadbhāgyamāyurārogyamakṣayaṃ

avāpya śāśvataṃ dhāma vratyaṃtena saṃśayaḥ

prakāśiteyaṃ kalyāṇi na kutrāpi prakāśaya

aṣṭāviṃśākṣarī vidyāmapi guptāṃ ca śailaje

rājyalakṣmī mahāvidyāṃ yojayatyanvahaśuciḥ

sāṃga dakṣiṇayā bhuktaṃ pratyahaṃ bhavati dhruvaṃ

kṛtaṃ tena na saṃdeho bhū prādakṣiṇyamapyatha

śrīpīṭhamathavābhyarcya pratyekaṃ pratyahaṃ śuciḥ

smṛtvā prasannalakṣmīṃ ca śālāgrāma śilānvite

samabhyarcya viśuddhātmā pratyahaṃ sādhakottamaḥ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā smṛtvā vidyāṃ śrīhṛdayābhidhāṃ

mahādāna sahasrasyadājimedhaśa tasya

phalaṃ prāpya viśuddhātvā dhanadena samaḥ priyā

kaśya yena samobhūtvā kāṃtyā kaṃdarpasannibhaḥ

svacchaṃdamaraṇo bhūtvā kulasāhatprasaṃyuta

*satra lakṣmīvidyāyā māhātmyamadyanāsanaṃ

yatra tiṣṭhati sā lakṣmīstatraiva satataṃ sukhaṃ

nityaṃ dharmābhivṛddhiśca tasmāllakṣmī sadārcayet

śreyaskaramihāmutra kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi

kulasāhasrasahito labhate parame padaṃ

yasya vijñānataḥ sākṣātkartāhartā bhavet svarya

pūjyate tatra sakataṃvaśī kuryānna saṃśayaḥ

poṣitassakalāvaśyā jvalatkāmāgnipīḍitā

taddarśanātra saṃdeho jāyaṃte sarvayoṣitaḥ

japedavaśyaṃ śreyothī śrīvidyāṃ viśvamātṛkāṃ

sarvasiddhipradā nityaṃ sarvarājavaśaṃkarī

rudrākṣa mālikā mokṣa sarvakāma samṛddhidā

mohinī sarvajagatāṃ sarvasaṃpat samṛddhidā

putrajīvakamālātu vidyālakṣmīpradāsatāṃ

raktacaṃdanamālā tu sarvabhogamadāmatā

akṣamālāṃ samabhyarcya ca senānulepitāṃ

yoṣito bhrāmayaṃtyeva mano yadi suniścalaṃ

pātālatala nāgeṃdra kanyakāḥ kṣobhayaṃtitaṃ

tadā lakṣatrayaṃ kuryāt sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ

abhimānena sauṃdarya saubhāgyamadasaṃyutāḥ

japtena kṣatrayaṃ sadyo mahāpāpaudya bhedanaṃ

itthaṃ lakṣatrayaṃ japtvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ

pūruṣā yośitovaśyāścarācaragatāḥ priye

atīvasuṃrarīramyāṃ tanmadhye pratimāṃ parāṃ

ciṃtayedyāṃta tasmānu yojanānāṃ śatādapi

rājakanyātha vā patnī bhayalajjā vivarjitā

cakra madhyagato bhūtvā sādhakaściṃtayedyadi

sādhyamapya rūṇībhūtaṃ ciṃtayitvā japecchriyaṃ

sarvasauṃdarya subhagaḥ kāminī vallabho bhavet

dāsabhūtā karotyeva naranārī nṛpādikān

cakraṃ samarcayeyastu yasya nāma vidarbhitaṃ

cakra madhyagataṃ kurvannāma yasyāstu yoṣitaḥ

tadānate śīghraṃ yakṣiṇī rājakanyakāṃ

vidyādharī divyakanyāṣu kanyāṃ ripu striyaṃ

kāmavāṇaprabhinnāṃtaḥ karaṇāṃ divyasuṃdarī

saṃkṣobhayati bhūloka pātāla jaṭāyoṣitaḥ

athaṃ bhāgadvayaṃ devi caṃdanaṃmaranet sudhīḥ

jagattrayaṃ vaśīkayīnnaranārī kulānvitaṃ

dugdhaṃ ghṛtaṃ ruṇamagnaṃ vastraṃ karpūramapyatha

savaśyo jāyate devi nātra kāyā vivāraṇā

ākarṣaṇamidaṃ proktaṃ niścitaṃ lokamohanaṃ

cāruthaṃ gāraveṣāḍhyāṃ savarlikāraka śobhitāṃ

tadvāla kaṃṭhahṛnnābhi janmamaṃḍalayoṣitāṃ

sarvasaṃdhiṣu dehasya madanākṣaramālikhet

tadāśābhi mukho bhūtvā svayaṃ devīsvarūpavān

niyojya dahanāṃgāre caṃdrasūryakalātmake

ananya mānasāṃ premabhrāmyamāṇāṃ tadātmanā

mātṛkāṃ vilikheccakre vāhyasassakalāṃ priye

taddhārayedgaledakṣakoravāmetha vā priye

tathaiva mātṛkāyuktamanvahaṃ cakramarcayan

anena vidhinā devirocanāgara kuṃkumaiḥ

vidarbhitaṃ tvanāmnāpi yasminkasminnapi sthitaṃ

vaśīkuryān maheśāni pādākrāṃtaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

ekanekamavaṣṭabhya sādhyanāmākṣā priyaṃ

hema madhyagataṃ kṛtvā śikhāyāṃ vā bhujadvaye

eteṃdramapi deveśi dāsabhūtaṃ karoti hi

vyāghrāḥ kesariṇo duṣṭā api vaśyā bhavaṃti hi

madhye datuṣyathe vāpi caturdikṣu nidhāpayet

tathaiva sarvaduṣṭābhāṃpura sthānāṃ ca jāyeta

jvalaṃtī sarvarājeṃdramaṃḍitāṃ sāgarāṃ varāṃ

kaṭākṣākṣepamātreṇa tārpastasyavaśāḥ priye

dṛṣṭaivākarcayeddevi trailokyaṃ sa carācaraṃ

bhūtapretapiśācāśca jvarāṃścāturthikānapi

etatsiṃdūra subhagaṃ rātrau saṃpūjitaṃ priye

yadā dikṣu vidikṣvaivaṃ vidhiva kṛtamīśvari

bhūrjapatre samālikhya rocanāgaru kuṃkumaiḥ

nāmnā vidarbhitaṃ samyaṅmāṃsamātraṃ prapūjayan

athavādhārayet kaṃṭhe śikhāyāṃ vā bhujadvaye

arkakṣīreṇa sahitaṃ dhuttūrakarasaṃtathā

kastūrī camṃdrasaṃyuktamekīkṛtya tataḥ paraṃ

tasya vyāghari puṣpādhi jagata sarpa bhayādikaṃ

na jāyate mahādevi paramaṃtrābhicārataṃ

athavā madhyagāṃ devi trikoṇo bhayapārśvagāṃ

nidhāpayecca saptāhāṭṭāsavāt kiṃ karo bhavat

pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvāstaṃ bhayet paravāditaḥ

saṃvādādari saṃghosya mūko bhavati tatkṣaṇāt

cakraṃ vilikhya deveśi mahānīla rasena ca

nāma saṃyojya vidhivaddakṣiṇāśāmurayovudhaḥ

māhiṣyasya purīṣasya cassamākṛṣyaśaulaje

kṣiptvāra nāla madhyeri vidveṣaṇakaraṃ bhavet

laṃvamāne tadākāśe śaktraccāṭana kāravāṃ

vilikhya dhārayanmaṃtrī sarvavarṇānvaśenayet

anenaiva vidhānena sthāpayedvāri madhyarga

saubhāgyaṃ jāyate tasya yānī yena na saṃśayaḥ

samāhātho bhayet samyājvalamānāṃ viciṃtayet

mahāpāpopapāpauḥ yairapi māsādvivarjitaḥ

saṃprāpya dīrghamāyuṣyaṃ śriyaṃ cāṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

putrapautrādi saṃpattisahito divyabhogavān

rātritaṃ cakramabhyarcya rājyalakṣmīmacaṃcalāṃ

putrapautra samṛddhiṃ ca saṃprāpyāṃ ca paraṃ padaṃ

tāmracakraṃ samabhyarcya vimuktaḥ sarvapātakaiḥ

mukteha sakalānbhogānaṃte brahmapadaṃ labhet

śreyo rāśimiha prāpya dhanadena samaśriyā

cirāyurvipulā bhogānbhuktāṃte muktimāpnuyāt

pītadravyaissamabhyarcyastaṃbhayet sarvavādinaḥ

pūjayanvidhivadvidvān sarvalokaṃ vaśaṃ nayet

saṃpūjya vidhivadvidvān yoṣitāṃ vallabho bhavet

yasya nāmāṃkitaṃ tasya maṃtrahāniḥ prajāyeta

vidveṣaṇavyādhi śatrūccāṭanakārakaṃ

likhitaṃ pūjitaṃ nityaṃ mahāsiddhimadaṃ smṛtaṃ

sarvavaśyakaraṃ devi bhavatyatra na saṃśayaḥ

tailasthaṃ cakrarājaṃ tu śatruviddeṣakārakaṃ

yadaikāṃte ca dhurmārge siṃdūra satasā likhet

girāvekatarau tadvadavayan cakrarājakaṃ

śmaśani pūjayeccakraṃ mahābhūta dine vudhaḥ

labhate sadya evāsau cuṭikāṃ janapādukāḥ

mahāvivarasiddhiṃ ca yakṣiṇī siddhimapyatha

mahābhūta dine bhaumavārayukteyamānvite

japtvā ca triśataṃ paścāsādyaṃ saṃdhyāmupāsya ca

śrīcakraṃ sāgamudvṛtya patraiḥ puṣpaiḥ sugaṃdhibhiḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā gāyatryā purataḥ śuciḥ

navyavaraṇadevīnāṃ sakṛdevaghṛta haret

hutvā śrīvidyayā vahnau catuḥśrīmanubhistataḥ

japādibhistato hutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

sāṣṭāṃga daṃḍavannatvā vāso laṃkāraṇādibhiḥ

sarvasvamarpayeddravyamācārya caraṇadvayaḥ

samarpyacāryapatnyai ca dadyādākalpasaṃcayaṃ

khaḍgaṃ ca pādukā yugmadivyāṃ janamanuttamaṃ

kehasiddhi manuprāpya vā siddhiṃ mahatīmapi

paṃcaviṃśati varṣausau bhūtvā kaṃdarpasannibhaḥ

krīḍāte divyakanyābhirbhuṃjanbhogānyathepsitān

śrīcakrasadṛśaṃ pūjyaṃ nāsti lokatrayepi ca

vījatrayasya kathayasādhanaṃ me jagatpate

yettānuṣṭhitamātreṇa bhavādhauta nimajjati

sthūla sūkṣmavibhāgena trailokyetyattimātṛkā

tasyāṃ pariṇatāyāṃ tu na ka"cit paradṛṣyate

śaktaḥ syāt parameśāni śaktiyukto yadā bhavet

jñātenāpirmahaśani karmaśarma na kiṃcana

praviśya paramāgaṃtu sūkṣmākāra svarūpiṇī

vāmā śivā tathā jyeṣṭhā śṛṃgāṭikāratāṃgatā

evaṃ sā jparamāśaktirekaiva parameśvarī

jñānaśaktiḥ kriyāśaktiriricchāśaktitmikā priye

yadollasati śṛṃgāṭa pīṭhāt kuṭilarūpiṇī

tadudbhavāmṛtā svāda paramānaṃdanaṃditā

nirlakṣyaṃ nirguṇaṃ caiva kūlarūpa vivarjitaṃ

tena mārgeṇa saṃtuṣṭā punare kākinī satī

tatva traya vinirdiṣṭā varṇaśakti śivātmikā

kāmarāje kāmakalā kāmarūpa kriyātmikā

evaṃ devī tryakṣarā śrīrmahātripurasuṃdarī

saṃsmṛtā pāpaśamanī japtā mṛtyuvināśinī

hutāvidhaudya śamanī dhyātā sarvārdhasādhakī

śuklāṃvaraṃ parīdhānaḥ śubhracaṃdanacārcitaḥ

pūjayedvalaiḥ puṣpairbrahmacaryaratonaraḥ

saṃkalpya dhavale vāpi naivedyairmānasaiḥ śive

karpūradhvalāṃ śubhrapuṣpābharaṇaśobhitāṃ

muktāphalāmalyamaṇi japamālālasatkarāṃ

evaṃ dhyāyet maheśāni sarvavidyādherā bhavet

brahmajyotirmayīṃ dhyāyejjihvāgre dīparūpiṇīṃ

pāṣāṇena samo vāpi mūkaḥ paśu samopi vā

naṣṭacittotimeṃdo vā grāmyo mūrkhoti pātakī

jaḍomakaḥ paśu samopi vā raṣṭotidvarmedhāgataprajovinaṣṭadhīḥ

satyaṃḍitabhāpaṭoyajetā pratihata prabhaḥ

vātāhata samudrormimālātuyairupanyaset

pradaguphairmahākāvyakartā deveśi jāyate

jyotiḥ śāstretihyasādimīmāsā smṛti vākyavit

pātālaśāstravijñātamūta taṃtrāyartatvavit

mahāvyākaraṇodāra śabdasaṃkaramarmavit

nānāśāstrārtha śikṣādivettā bhuvanavistṛtaḥ

vāgmavasyasrayā devi māhātmyaṃ kathitaṃ tava

atha kāmavulāsaktaḥ sādhakaḥ parameśvaraḥ

raktavastrāvṛtassamyaṅmadhyakāma kalātmanā

mūlādi brahmaraṃdhrāṃta sphura dīpasvarūpiṇīṃ

ikṣuko daṃḍapuṣpeṣu varābhayalasatkarāṃ

ciṃtayat parameśāni kāmarājaṃ tato japet

rājāno vaśamāyāṃti pannagā rākṣasā surāḥ

manaściṃtitayopi tu dāsī ca vaśagā bhavet

vidyādharī kinnarī vā yakṣanāgāṃganātha vā

prajvalanmadanot*dahamaśraptamātasā

niveditātmasavasvāvaśagā devi jāyeta

cititāyāṣitāyonaumaṃkṣobhāti tatkṣaṇā

ākarṣayenna saṃdehāraṃ bhāvapi tilottamāṃ

tasyā mūrdhni smaredvījaṃ sravatpīyūṣavarṣṭaṇaṃ

nāḍī dvitayassarvatrailokyamapi mohayet

mohayaṃti jagatsarvaṃ prayogaṃ śṛṇuṣāvaṃti

vidarbhyādyena kāmenamatmathāṃtargataṃ kuru

makaradhvaja saṃjñaṃ va sarvame tadvarānane

trailokye mohanonāma yogoyaṃ parikīrtita

mūlādi brahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ śarīraciṃtayet parāṃ

hemaprabhā māsamānāṃ vidyutyuja samaprabhāṃ

jñānamudrāṃ ca dadhatī sākṣādamṛtarūpiṇī

etasyāḥ smaraṇāhevi nīlakaṃṭhatvamāgataṃ |

na teya samo maṃtrī viṣabhāra sahasranut

cāturthika jvarānuṣṭāna yasmārāṃśca nāśayet

dāhinā mamṛtākāraḥ kṣīradhāropamaḥ smṛtaḥ

duṣṭavyādhi grahānīkaśakinīrūpikāgaṇaiḥ

atha trikūṭa saṃpūrṇā mahātripurasuṃdari

samasta puṇyaphaladā sarvalokavaśaṃkarī

prātarjaptātha madhyāhne hṛtpadmasthāruṇa prabhā

mukhamaṃḍala madhyāsthāpayarāga maṇiprabhā

sidhyatyaṣṭaguṇaiśvaryaṃ saubhāgyaṃ ca mahattaraṃ

śriyo vidyānusaṃdhāne yasya svārya kṛpā bhavet

tipato yojayennityaṃ mahātripurasuṃdarīṃ

sarvāsāṃ sarvasaṃsthānāṃ yoginīnāṃ bhavet priyaḥ

paripūrṇaiva satataṃ japyate śrīryadi priye

catuḥṣaṣṭiryataḥ kodyoyoginīnāmahaujasāṃ

japtvā trikūṭīṃ saṃpūrṇāṃ mahātripurasuṃdarī

ṣoḍaśe divase vāpi kṣāmāṃgachana striyaṃ niśi

pāṇe sarvavidyānāṃ dhanāḍhyaṃ bhāgya saṃyutaṃ

naitayā sadṛśī kācidvidyā deveśi vidyate

trailokyamohanaṃrūpa prāptavān bhagavānhariḥ

samārādhyā bhavalloke sarvasaubhāgya suṃdaraḥ

japa stri saṃdhyametasyāssamastaphalasiddhaye

mahābhāgya nidhi bhūtvā prāpnotyaṃte paraṃ padaṃ

saṃprāpya sarvaśreyāṃsi pūjyate daivatairapi

caṃdrasūryāvapi jagatsṛṣṭi saṃhārakārakau

sādhakānāṃ hitāya tvaṃ va daśaṃkara suvrata

śravaṇādapi yasya syāt sādhako brahmarūpabhāka

aśvamedhaphalaṃ sāṃgaṃ prāpnotyanvahamakṣayaṃ

vājapeya phalaṃ prāpya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate

vimuktassavaryāyebhyo mahatī śriya makṣayāṃ

brahmalokeva satyaṃte daśa pūrvairdaśāvaraiḥ

mahāpāpopapāpaudyairapi muktaḥ sudāruṇaiḥ

mahatī mṛddhi māsādya putrapautrādi saṃyutāṃ

kulānāṃ śatumuddhanyavaikuṃṭheniva satyasau

aśvamedhaphalaṃ sāṃgaṃ manvahaṃ prāpya sādhakaḥ

muktāratnāri saṃpūrṇāṃ dhanadhānyaudya saṃkulāṃ

ārogyaṃ na hṛdā puṣpaṃ kāṃtiṃ kaṃdarpa sannibhāṃ

āyuḥ śrīkīrtisahitaḥ putrapautrādi saṃyutāṃ

sahasrakula saṃyukto modate yāme pade

kāmavatsubhagoloke vidyayādhiṣaṇaprabhaḥ

bhukteha sakalān bhogān vrajatyaṃte paraṃ padaṃ

sahasraṃ prajapennityamatha paṃcaśataṃ tu vā

vāpi nocet prāpnotyadhogati ******

paścimābhimukhellimaṃge vṛṣaśūnye śivāaye

cakrarājārddhabhaṃ yatra yatrācāryasya sannidhiḥ

ekādaśyāṃ māghamāse dvādaśyāṃ vā śuciḥ śive

ācāryaṃ sannidhīkṛtya cakrarājaṃ samarcayan

ekabhaktāśano devi gāyatrī vahuśo japan

sahasraṃ prajapennityaṃ śriyaṃ paṃcadaśākṣarī

savāsinī trayaṃ nityamekaikāṃ vāsuvāsinīṃ

māghamāsasya bhūyāṃ nirāhāro yata vrataḥ

cakrarājaṃ samābhyarcya sāgācaraṇamādarāt

laitāṃ śriyamārādhya jātavedasi sādhakaḥ

navāvaraṇā devīnāṃ ghṛtamekaikaśo hunet

hutvā paṃcadaśākṣaryā śriya ācārya sannidhau

******** tato japādibhi hunet

hutvā paṃcadaśākṣaryā śriya ācārya sannidhau

tato japādibhirhutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

cakrarājaṃ samabhyarcya sāṃgaṃ bhūyopi śailaje

ratnaviṃdu samāyukta ṣaṣṭi cihnuka hemajaṃ

pratyāmnāya suvarṇaṃ vā dūtvā vāso yugaṃ śubha

dadyāddhāmānanekāścaralaudyaṃ cākṣaye dhanaṃ

athavācārya pādābje varṣāśanamitaṃ dhanaṃ

varṣāśanamitaṃ dravyaṃ śataniṣkamudāhṛtaṃ

ekaviṃśati niṣkaṃ vā varṇa niṣkamathāpi vā

kṛtvaivaṃ niyamaṃ ghoraṃ puraścaraṇarmastiraṃ

ācāryayapatnyai sauvarṇa karṣamānamathāpi vā

dadyāt sūkṣmataraṃ vastraṃ kṣaumaṃ kāryāsakaṃ tu vā

śāśvatīṃ siddhimāpnoti śriyaṃ cāpi mahattarāṃ

kṛtvesyaṃ tatra vidyāyāḥ puraścaryā sahasrajaṃ

brahmahatyādi pāpau yopyasya naśyati tatkṣaṇāt

phalaṃ sāṃgamavāpnoti prabhāte sādhakastataḥ

cakrarājaṃ samabhyarcya savatsāṃgāṃ payasvinīṃ

suvāsinī paṃcadamaśaṣṭānnaistoṣayedvratī

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdva prakṣālita jalaṃ śuciḥ

tasminne vane svameśriyaṃ devīṃ prapaśyati

paśyatyasau śriyaṃ sākṣāccakṣurbhyāmeva sādhakaḥ

śriyaṃ śriyaṃ madārikāṃtāsmin dine svapne prapaśyati

akṣayā mahatī lakṣmīrdhanadhānyādi saṃkulā

vāci vāgdevatā tasya bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ

japtvāvarṇa sahasrāṇi śriyaḥ paṃcadaśākṣarī

mahātīṃ siddhimāsādya bhavatyevedaṃ sannibhaḥ

ācāryānujñayāvaṇa sāhasraṃ śriyamiṣṭadāṃ

dadhi madhvājya sahitodanena yanasodbhavaiḥ

sahasraṃ vidhayā hutvā raśmi vidyāpurassaraṃ

cakrarājaṃ samabhyarcya sāṃgāvaraṇamanvahaṃ

guḍodakena dadhyannaiḥ phalairapi manoharaiḥ

śrīcakra sannidhau paścādācāryaṃ daṃḍavannaset

alaṃkārairalaṃkṛtya vāso yugamaṃ samarpya ca

sannidhau paścādādhāryaṃ daṃḍavannaset

alaṃkārairalaṃ kṛtya vāso yugmaṃ samarpya ca

paṃcaniṣkamitaṃ vāpi pradadyād dakṣiṇādhanaṃ

vāsopi ca samarpyāthā taccheṣaṃ bhojayedvratī

mahadā puṣpamārogyaṃ śrīrapyaṣṭa guṇānvitā

kaṃdarpa samasaubhāgyaṃ vidyā vahuvidhāpi ca

siddhiṃ vinā na kāsyānāṃ siddhiruktā manīṣibhiḥ

yena kenāśupāyena pūrṇānugrahato guroḥ

dhyātvātha vā cakrarājamanaḥ pūjā samanvitaṃ

nigadeno pāṃśunā vāmana sā vā japet sadā

avyaktataḥ sphurānane upāṃśuḥ parikīrtitaḥ

jihvāyā japyate yena sopi mānasa ucyate

upāṃśu smaraṇenai tulyaṃ bhavati śailaje

mānasoccāraṇe naiva tulyamekena suṃdari

japyeta sa japaḥ prokto mānaso kṣaya siddhidaḥ

lakṣamātraṃ japedyastu cakrarājaṃ samarcayan

vārdhake yauvane vālye jāgrat svapnasuṣuptiṣu

upapātaka saṃyaiśca mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ

brahmahatyā surāyā nasvarṇasteyādikairapi

mucyate nātra saṃdeha koṭi janmakṛtairapi

japtvā lakṣatrayaṃ maṃtrī hayamedha sahasrajaṃ

caturlakṣaṃ japedyastu yakrarājaṃ samarcayan

jaḍopi sarvavidyā api bhāṃtyasya cakratahḥ

paṃcalakṣaṃ japedyastu niyataḥ śriyamiṣṭadāṃ

bhūtvā dhanadasaṃkāśaḥ śriyā bhavati saṃtataḥ

ajaraṇo bhūtvā prāpnotyā puṣpamakṣayo

lakṣa ṣaṭkaṃ japedyastu cakrarājaṃ samarcayan

sarvajñatva manuprāpya cirā puṣpamahattaraṃ

prāpnoti mahadaiśvaryaṃ muktāratnādi saṃkulaṃ

saptalakṣaṃ japedyastu śriyo vidyāṃ phalapradāṃ

saubhāgyaṃ paramaṃ labdhvā dhanadhānyādi saṃkulāṃ

aṣṭalakṣaṃ japedyastu mahātripurasuṃdarī

saṃprāpya kālamṛtyu ca vinirjityā jarāmaraḥ

ciṃtāmaṇigaṇākīrṇe kāmadhenu samanvite

krīḍate divyakanyābhiḥ kaṃdarpa iva suṃdaraḥ

adhiyassarvasiddhānāṃ bhūtvā viṃśati vārṣikaḥ

aṇimādi guśvaṇaiśvaryaṃ labdhā khecaratāmapi

varṇalakṣaṃ japedyastu śriyaḥ paṃcadaśākṣarīṃ

sarvajñaḥ subhago vāgmī sarvavidyāsu pāraga

paṃcaviṃśati varṣaśca śriyamaṣṭa guṇānvitāṃ

pūjiṭato yogamaṣṭāṃgaṃ labdhvā khecaratāmapi

parakāyaṃ praviśati jāyate divyabhogavān

śatayojanaparyaṃtamapi paśyati cakṣuṣā

viharatyeva gaṃdharvaissaṃstutopsarasāṃgaṇaiḥ

bhuktvā kalpaṃ mahābhogān brahma tādātmyaṃ siddhibhāk

tadārabhya dinaṃ nityamācāryānujñayā śive

adhaḥ śāyījitā mitro guruśuśrūṣaṇerataḥ

vidhivat saṃskṛte vahnau palāśa kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ

pūrvavadrāśmi vidyābhirbhūtvai kāmāhutiṃ śive

hutvā japādibhirhutvā kuryād brahmārpaṇāhuti

padmābhāve vilvapatraiḥ kevalena ghṛtena vā

athāvā varṣaniṣkaṃkhaṃ paṃcaniṣkamathāpi vā

vaṇa saṃkhyāssuvāsinyassaṃtoṣya bhojanādibhi

itthaṃ kṛtvā mahāsiddhiṃ puraścaryā śatodbhavāṃ

kaṃdarpa śatasaubhāgyaṃ śriyaṃ cāṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

yogamuṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ dhanadhānyākulāṃ śriyaṃ

pūjyatāṃ nṛpa sānnidhye daivatairapi pūjyatāṃ

vrajatyaṃte paraṃdhāma kulasāhasra saṃyutaḥ

ca setati vyāpinī lakṣmīrgajāścarathasaṃkulā

avalā jāyate tasya maṃdire nātra saṃśayaḥ

siddhatvaṃ vātha devatvaṃ labhatesmin kalevera

viśeṣahemi dravyāṇi pravakṣyāmi tataḥ paraṃ

yacchrūtvāpi na rodhīmānduritaudyairvimucyate

kuryāt samastavidyānāṃ sidhyathersādhakottamaḥ

tanmadhye navakoṣṭhānikatvā varṇān samālikhet

svarayugma krameṇaivakādicāṣṭa suyojayet

lakṣavarṇau śaṃbhukoṇe vilikhet kūrmasaṃjñikaṃ

mukhasya pārśvayoḥ pāṇīdvayaṃ jānī hi pārvati

tataḥ pādadvayaṃ viddhi tadaṃte pucchamīritaṃ

karasthāne mahākleśaḥ sarvakārya vināśakṛt

pucchetu dhanahāniśca jāyate nānyathā priye

dīpasthānaṃ purekṣetre grāmepi viṣayepi vā

kuryādanaṃta phaladaṃ viddhi parvata kanyake

kāmanāhauma karmāṇi vakṣyāmi phaladānyatha

caitramāse pūrṇimāyāṃ sahasra tritayaṃ hunet

bhavatyeva varārohe mahāvāgmī mahākaviḥ

sahasra tritayaṃ hutvā gaukṣīra trimadhusutaiḥ

vasorājakule vāpi viladvāre vanepi vā

saubhāgyena vilāsena sāmarthyenāpi suvrate

saṃmohayati bhūpānāṃ kanyā api manoharāḥ

laghukolasamaṃ hutvā sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

caṃpakaiḥ pāṭalairhutvā yata trimadhumiśritaiḥ

dhanadhānyā kulāṃ muktāralarāśi samākulāṃ

śrīkhaṃḍaṃ guguluṃ caṃdranāgaraṃ juhuyā yadi

rājanājeṃdradevānāṃpuraṃ *rvaśamānayet

ghṛta trimadhu sasmiśralākṣāṃ hutvā sahasraśaḥ

mabhutritathadvometa sahasra tritayaṃ śuciḥ

kṣīraṃ madhu ca dadhyājyaṃ pṛthagdyutvā sahasraśaḥ

kṣīreṇa madhutā hutvā pratyekaṃ trisahasrakaṃ

mahadāpuṣpamāpnoti sarvādhinyādhikartitaḥ

saubhāgyaṃ mahadā sādya dhanamakṣayamāpnuyāt

mahatīṃ śriyamāsādya vairiṇastaṃbhayatyasau

vimuktassarvarogaiśca svacchaṃdamaraṇo bhavet

trisahasraṃ viśuddhātmā dvitīya ivayakṣarāṭ

samāhaṃ sarvabhūyālā dāsabhūtā bhavati hi

ayaṃ dhanayavāpnoti vīchitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ

hutvā samāhamātreṇa krūrā bhūpanayopi ca

śvetātpalai strimadhvaktaiṃ śatādhika sahasrakaṃ

saṃprāpya sarvavidyānāṃ pārago bhavati dhruvaṃ

hutvā samāhamātreṇa kāṃtiṃ kaṃdarpa sannibhāṃ

kahlāra havanānmaṃtrī triśatādhika sahasrakaṃ

mānuluṃga phalairhutvā sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

nāraṃga phalahomena sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

kuṣmāṃḍa phalahomena sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

mānuluṃgaphalairhutvā sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

nāraṃgaphalahomena sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

kūṣmāṃḍaphalahomena sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

drākṣā phalaistrimadhvaktaissahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

kadalīphala homena lakṣamātreṇa bhūbhujaḥ

kharjūraphalaho na lakṣamātreṇabhūjaḥ

rikelayūlairhutvā lakṣaṃ trimadhumiśritaiḥ

pakvānna pūla homena lakṣamātreṇa suṃdari

panasasya phalai vījarahitai strimadhuplutaiḥ

vaśīkaroti bhūyālan kiṃkarā iva saṃtataṃ

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ pakṣamekaṃ niraṃtaraṃ

guggulasya ca hāmena ṣaṭhastreṇa suṃdari

kuṃkumadyata saṃyuktaṃ madhu tritayamiśritaṃ

vaśīkaroti trailokyaṃ yāvajīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

sahasratritayaṃ hutvā vairiṇo śamānayet

striyo vaśyā bhavaṃtyasya puruṣā api saṃtata

triśatādhika sāhasraṃdita tritayamīśvari

vācaspati saptaḥ sākṣādvidyayehāpi jāyate

ekaṃ na divase naiva vaśyāḥ sphurrājamaṃtriṇaḥ

sarvapāpa nivṛttiḥ syāddroga śāṃtiśca jāyeta

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ saptāha sādhakottamaḥ

ghṛta trimadhusaṃmiśra pāyasaṃ juhuyādyadi

trailokyaṃ svavaśīkṛtya śriyamaṣṭaguṇānvitāṃ

nānāvidhānnā homena māsi māsi śatrayaṃ

sopaskaraiśca kaṭukaissahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

apanetu maśakyaiśca matrauṣadhassāyanaiḥ

japāpuṣpaistrimadhuktaissaptāhaṃ juhuyādyadi

srvavidyānidhirbhūtvā kāmavatsuṃdaro bhavet

saubhāgyaṃ mahadāsāpavṛddhoṣi taruṇāyate

jaṃbūphalaistrimadhvaktaistrisahasraṃ śatādhikaṃ

dāsībhūtā bhavatyeva rājakanyā api dhruvaṃ

saptāhārdetya kanyāśca dāsībhūtā bhavaṃti hi

triśatādhika sāhasraṃ rājyalakṣmīrmahīyasī

ikṣu khaṃḍaiścaturaha śatādhika sahasrakaṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityamaṣṭāviṃśatidhāpivā

vidyāsiddhimavāpnoti śreyosi suvahūnyapi

samastābhīṣṭa saṃsiddhiṃ prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

puṣpaṃ magraṃ juhuyādūtyailaṃ kamalaṃ tu vā

śatasaṃkhyāstilā proktāstaṃḍulāśca tathaiva hi

culukārddhamatakṣīramaṃtravādarasammitaṃ

manaḥ prītikaraiḥ khaṃḍairjuhuyān manalocane

nārikelaṃ catuḥkhaṃḍamuktaṃ śāstreṣu suṃdāri

drākṣāphalaṃ samagrasyāṃ nāraṃgaṃ ca dvidhā smṛtaṃ

guṃjā samaṃ ca karpūraṃ kastūrī kuṃkumaṃ tathā

lākṣā guggula saṃkhyāśyādyadvastu sumanoharaṃ

etadā huti mānaṃte kathitaṃ sarvavighnahṛt

śriyaṃ saṃtoṣayennityaṃ japahomārcanādibhiḥ

ādau kuṃḍaṃ samutpādya caturasraṃ sulakṣaṇaṃ

sahasre hastamātraṃ syādvisahasre dvihastakaṃ

koṭi homeṣṭa hastaṃ ca kṛtvā kuṃḍasya lakṣaṇaṃ

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ staṃbhane proktaṃ māraṇe tadvadevahi

nānāsiddhikaraṃ proktaṃ padmakoṇaṃ maheśvari

māraṇe dakṣiṇa proktā paścimārvakṣyakarmaṇi

tathā muccāṭanaṃ syādvāyanve īśānye sarvasiddhidaṃ

kṛttikākarṣaṇe proktā maraṇī māraṇe tathā

vidveṣe śatatārāsyādahi vundhyaṃ tu śāṃtidaṃ

vudhe ca staṃbhanaṃ kāryaṃ guruvāre ca pauṣṭikaṃ

rājavṛttasya samidho hottavyāḥ staṃbhane śive

dāḍimī samidho home praśastā māraṇādike

vidveṣe caiva hotavyā unmatta samidhastathā

sarvakāryārthasiddhiśca sarvavighnanivāraṇaṃ

bhārgave vāsare vāpi navamyāṃ pūrṇimāsuvā

paṃcamavarṣamārabhya yāvadvādaśavatsaraṃ

kanyakāḥ pūjitā yena trailokyaṃ tena pūjitaṃ

kanyakā iti saṃproktā yāvadvādaśavatsaraṃ

suvāsinyopi saṃpūjyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ

saiva lakṣmīrjajammātā saiva gaurī sarasvatī

saiva sākṣānmahālakṣmīdhiyā pūjyā vibhūvaṇaiḥ

damapnāropaṇaṃ vakṣye sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca

tapobhagāṃyamadannomaya śailasute yadā

saṃtuṣṭa manasā tābhyāṃ varodato mayāptiye

tatsauramya samudbhūta saukhyamāsīt sulocane

damanena tuyo maṃtrī var'madhye sa pūjayet

itisyai varodato mayaiva sukhaṃdite

śrīśrīśapūjāniratairgaurīśaṃkara pūjakaiḥ

śrīcakrapūjānirataiḥ śālagrāmārcakairapi

maheṃdrā yaṣṭā dikpāla pūjanā saktamānasaiḥ

vatsare vatsare vaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ damanārcanaṃ

caitrādayastrayomāsāpakṣausitasitetarau

aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyā madhyāpi vā

svayamutyādyadamanaṃ samūlaṃ pūrvastu kāllarveyutaṃ

vāmataḥ sthāpayeddevi sarvatau bhadraje vuje

śriyaṃ kāmamataḥ paraṃ

pūrvavījñānicoddhārya praremātmanamuccaret

prītiṃ vade iti sthāne prīti maṃtroṣṭa varṇakaḥ

pūrvoktāyāṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi siṃhāsanopari

vadhvā stri śūlakuṃdrā ca bhrāmayeddamanopari

prathame divase kuryādadhivāsanamuttamaṃ

kāle mahāpūjāṃ kṛtvā yodhyā purābhidhe

kalaśānnavasaṃsthāpya hemaralādi pūritān

dakṣiṇe maṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā kuṃkumādyairvicipatritaṃ

tanmadhye pūjayet kāmaṃ taruṇaṃ cāruṇaprabhaṃ

ratiprīti viśobhāḍhyaṃ paṃcavāṇa dhanurdharaṃ

pūrvokti manunā samyagarcayot siddhi hetave

padmatāṃvūlahaste ca raktavastra virājite

vāmadakṣiṇayordhyātvā kāmasya tadanaṃtaraṃ

gauravarṇa vāmahaste sudhāpūrṇa ghaṭānvitaṃ

sāṃgaṃ kāmaṃ prapūjyātha mahātripurasuṃdarī

svarṇabhāra sahasraiśca divyasragvastrabhūṣaṇaiḥ

ācārya pūjyate yatra tatkarma sa phalaṃ smṛtaṃ

guṇe bhāve tatputraṃ tatpatnīṃ vā samarpayet

teṣāmabhāve tadbhātṛnatha vā saṃprapūjayet

śrīvidyayā hunedājyamatha triśatamādarāt

itthaṃ kṛtvā narastyamyagdamanāropaṇa vrataṃ

kṛtvā sāṃgamavāpnoti phalaṃ nātra vicāraṇā

anyathā madanāyaiva kṛtaṃ syādiri kanyake

bhakṣayennekṣu khaṃḍāni na bhavet strīṣu niṣṭhuraḥ

nānṛtaṃ kasya ciddhayādviśeṣeṇa guroḥ śive

daridro vā dhanāḍhyo vā mūrkho ca paṃḍitopi vā

kimatra vahunoktena parameśvara macyutaṃ

bhaktimānnāja vidyāyāṃ lakṣmyādīnapi sanmanūn

māhātmyate tacchrī devyā sāgare culukāṃvuvat

kiṃciduktaṃ mayā devi kiṃ punaḥ śrotumicchasi

caturlakṣmī sevyamānā śriyo vidyātvaparitā

śrīdevīlalitā sarvajagatkāraṇārūpiṇī

caturlakṣmī sevyamānā sākṣātkāmadudyāsmṛtā

triśaktiḥ sarvasāmrājyalakṣmīḥ paṃcaprakīrtitāḥ

śriyastrijagadaṃvāyā mūlavidyāgarīyasī

ekākṣarī mahāvidyāṃ lakṣmīmatha vadāmyahaṃ

viṃdunādayutaṃ lakṣmī vījame tadudāhṛtaṃ

samasta saṃpat sukhadā vidhātrā pūjitā purā

lakṣmī vījametadudāhṛtaṃ mārādhya lokānāmādhiyatyaṃ prayedire

śrīlakṣmīrdevatā proktā sarvasaṃyat samṛddhidā

dvitīyena tṛtīyena ṣasṭhenārṇena caimiti

ṣaḍaṃgāni nyasenmaṃtrī hacchiraśca śikhāṃ tataḥ

vaṣaḍhuṃ vauṣaḍaṃtaṃ ca puṭebhiryojayet kramāt

madhye puṣpaṃ vinikṣipye pīṭhaśaktīḥ samarcayet

vyuṣṭirutkṛṣṭi ṛddhaśca vasu dikṣu prapūjayet

dhyāyettataḥ śriyaṃ ramyāṃ sarvadeva namaskṛtāṃ

āsiṃcya mānāmamṛtairmuktāratna dravairapi

ratnaudya vahu makuṭā śuddha saumāṃgarāgiṇī

evaṃ dhyātvā yajeddevī padmayugmadharāṃ sadā

āvāhanādi mudrāśca krameṇaiva pradarśayet

iyaṃ tu viparītāsyāttadā vai sthāpanī bhavet

aṃtaraṃguṣṭhamuṣṭibhyāṃ tatheyaṃ tannirodhanī

aṃjaliṃ cārghyavat kṛtvā paramīkaraṇaṃ bhavet

madhyamānāmikā yugme sthāpayettu parasparaṃ

etāssādhāraṇamudrādarśayitvā japet priye

agnīśāsura vāyavya madhyadikṣvaṃgapūjanaṃ

vibhīṣikā mālikā ca śāṃkarī vasumālikā

iṃdrāgni yamarakṣorādvaruṇāni na saṃjñakān

brahmāviṣṇuśca lokeśāvūrddhādi kramato yajet

tapetyayasāsamyakkamalairājyatolitaiḥ

palamāna suvarṇena racitaṃ kamalaṃ kṣubhaṃ śubhaṃ

caturviṃśati sāhasramathavā sādhakottamaḥ

trisaharaṃ hunedagnaupamairgavyājyatolitaiḥ

vilvapatrairghṛtaplutaiḥ triśattādhika sāhasraṃ juhuyāt saṃskṛtenale

vatsarān mahatī lakṣmīṃ labhate vanasaṃkulāṃ

sahasrapaṃcakaṃ vāpi sahasra tritayaṃ tu vā

acalāṃ mahatīṃ lakṣmīṃ dhanavānyādi saṃkulāṃ

ekākṣaramikāṃmimāṃ lakṣmī japannavahamādarāt

śromicchasi

prasannalakṣmīvidyāyāḥ pārvatyai parameśvaraḥ

yadāha bhagavākto mahālakṣmīmanoḥ śubhaṃ

devadeva namastestu maheśa karuṇādhidhe

mahālakṣmī mahāvidyāvidhānaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

samyak pṛṣṭaṃ mahābhāge lokābhāmupakārakaṃ

purā nārāyaṇe noktaṃ janakāya mahātmane

ahamapyatra vakṣyāmi tubhyaṃ rājīvalocane

prasannalakṣmī māhātmyamadhunā kathayāmite

sparśādyaṃ brahmapūrvaṃ ca mahīṃ rudra samanvitāṃ

valānujaṃ tato vāyuṃ rudrasvara samanvitaṃ

mahālakṣmī padapaścāccaturthāyānvitaṃ

dakṣasiṃhāsaneśānī viṣṇunā pūjitā purā

ayodhyāpura vaikuṃṭha sarvasāmrājyanāyikā

prasannaśrīritikhyātā vedavetāṃtavedibhiḥ

upāsakānāṃ viprāṇāṃ māheṃdra śrīphalapradā

vedavedāṃgavedāṃta pūrvavāksiddhikāriṇī

sarvatīrthamayī sarvapātakaudyavidyātinī

yoginassatakathāśca vaśiṣṭāyā maharṣayaḥ

vasavoṣṭau mahābhāgāruhā ekāaśāpi ca

ārādhyāṣṭa uṇaiśvaryamavāpyādyāpi pārvati

alakāpurasāmrājyaṃ maheṃdrapurasaṃpadaṃ

alakāpurasāmrājyaṃ maheṃdrapurasaṃpadaṃ uyatātmanā

jñātu śaṃdhanyā mahālakṣmīrācāryānugrahaṃvinā

japet sayoginī vṛṃdairbhakṣyate māsaṣaṭkataḥ

aṇimāṇi guṇaiśvaryaṃ muktāralaudyamakṣayaṃ

mattānanekāmātaṃgānvājinopi vahnūn paśūn

samṛddhiṃ mahatī labdhvā vaikuṃṭheva satiṃ labhet

sāmrājyalakṣmīkāmena bhogamokṣārthinā sadā

acalaṃ mahadaiśvaryaṃ mahāsiddhima caṃcalāṃ

devāviyatyanirdraṇatiyaṃ hṛdayamena ca

tejasvitvaṃ sa vitrā ca sucāśutvamathedunā

agninā tāpaśaktitvamoṣadhīśatvamiṃdunā

lokaiśvaryarca saṃprāptamacalaṃ bhayavarjitaṃ

gaṇaiśvarya gaṇeśena naṃdinā śivatulyatā

yatra yatra mahadbhāgyaṃ prāptaṃ devarṣisiḥ purā

prasannalakṣmīmāhātmyaṃ brahmāpi jagatāṃpatiḥ

yadā urvāsasaḥ śāpācchakropya naranāyakaḥ

tadanugrahataḥ prāpya mahālakṣmīmimāṃ śive

anuttamaṃ mahadbhāgyaṃ prāptavāna marādhipaḥ

trayāṇāmapi lokānāṃ māvipatyamavān

hayaratnaṃ manovegamuccaiḥ śravasamānivamān

pakṣarākṣasarājatvaṃ prāpyālakāpurī śubhāṃ

prasannaśrīmahāvidyāṃ japtvā hutvā tataḥ paraṃ

alakāpurasāmrājyaṃ prāptvān girikanyake

prasannalakṣmīrvikhyātā samastāmīṣṭasiddhidā

mūrkhāya lubdhāyā mahālakṣmīriti smṛtā prasanṛtavādine

vedanidāparāyāpiyaṇa niṃdā parāya ca

maṃdabuddhiṃ jaḍaṃ mūrkhaṃ durācārarataṃ tathā

śrutyuktācāra rahitaṃ śiṣyatve parivarjayet

paradravya parastrīṣu parāṅmukhamataṃdritaṃ

śāṃtaṃ niṣkalmaṣaṃ śuddhaṃ haripūjā parāyaṇaṃ

dayādimāṃ mahālakṣmīvidyāṃ sakalasiddhidaṃ

āgatya brāhmaṇaṃ prāha prasannā śriyamiṣṭadāṃ

asūyakāyānṛjave jaḍāyānṛjavādine

rakṣaṇī yātvāyā nitya madatvā pāpakāriṇe

tadā maheṃdrasadṛśaṃ padaṃ dāsyamite dvija

tasmādeṣā mahālakṣmīnaṃ prakāśyāpi kutracit

japedimāṃ mahālakṣmīmācāryānugrahācchive

sarvasve nāpi kartavyā bhogamokṣa phalāptaye

pūjyamācārya pādābja dvayameva dine dine

cajyajetsvapi naraṃ patnīmātaraṃ ca sutānapi

sevyamācārya pāābja dvayameva dine dine

brahmānaṃda rasāvāpti maṇimādi guṇāṃ śriyaṃ

etadeva paraśreya ena detadeva paraṃstapaḥ

ayameva mahāyāga iyameva gatiḥ parā

yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ vā gatvācāryaṃ dvijottamaṃ

prasannakamalāvidyāṃ tadanugrahato japet

radyurdaśaratho rājā duṣṣaṃttobharatopi ca

rājyalakṣmīmimāṃ japtvā saṃsevyācāryamādarāt

śvetadvīpādhi yatvaṃ vā suvarṇagirisaṃpadaṃ

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyā api trailokyanāyakāḥ

nāstu śaktirmahaṣīṇāmasyā māhātmyavarṇane

prasannalakṣmīvidyeyaṃ nimivaṃśa samudbhavaiḥ

prasannalakṣmistadvaṃśe janakasyā bhavat sutā

janakā iti vikhyātā muktā jīvaṃta evate

iyameva mahāvāṇī sāvitrī ca sarasvatī

ataḥ paraṃ samudbhūta purāṇaṃ ca mahattaraṃ

etat samādhikā vidyāna dṛṣṭā kutracicchrutā

rājyalakṣmī na labdhāste bhogasvargāyavargadāṃ

iyaṃ kalpalatā sākṣānmataḥ saṃkalpasiddhidā

ye siddhā munayopīha divyabhoga samanvitaḥ

vācaḥ padāni catvāri parākhyaṃ prathamaṃ padaṃ

jānāratīśvara evaitatsarvasvatyāḥ padatrayaṃ

vaidavarīnāmakaṃ paścāccaturthaṃ vāmadaṃ vibhuḥ

maharṣayopi jānaṃti tapasā dagdha kilviṣāḥ

prasīddhameta devānna bhūtale girikanyake

pipīlikā sunāgeṣu sarveṣu ca mṛteṣu ca

vidyājālamidaṃ sarvaṃ nirgacchalyasya vaktrataḥ

adhikaṃ sadṛśaṃ vāpi vastu vidhātmakaṃ nāhi

upāsya dharmaṃ ca na bhogamokṣāna vā purutkṛṣṭapadaṃ cardova

anuṣṭhānavidhiṃ samyag yatkatvā siddhimāpnuyāt

gāyatraṃ chaṃda ityuktaṃ samasta jagadaṃvikā

mahālakṣmīrjaganmātādevatā kathitā śive

hṛdevaśaktiruddiṣṭā viniyogaruvabhīṣṭadaḥ

śikhā prasīdate naiva kavacaṃ tribhirakṣaraiḥ

netramastraṃ ca śeṣe vā śrīvījapuṭitaṃ pṛthak

hareḥ kāṃtāṃ kāṃkṣita pārijātalatikāṃ vaṃde sarojananāṃ

putrāga pāṭalāśoka caṃpakairūpaśobhitaṃ

samerumādhavī vilva devadārulavaṃgakaiḥ

mātuluṃgaiḥ karṇikāraiścaṃdanairvuṃjulairapi

sarvartu puṣṭyasahitairnamadbhiḥ suvirājita

sugaṃdhibhiśca kahlāraiḥ phullaiḥ kuvalayairapi

tānmadhye puline rasme mahāsurasita śobhite

caturdvārayuta divyahemaprākāra śobhitaṃ

divyaratna samāyukta kapāṭāṣṭaka śobhitaṃ

hemadaṃ uśilālavidvija paṃṅkti pariṣkṛtaṃ

divyasāhasra saṃśobhidīptastaṃbha virājitaṃ

vicitrāṃśuka saṃvarddhakāṃcanairapi koṭiśaḥ

mālatīkaṃtakījātī ca yakotpalakesaraiḥ

divyairanyaiḥ puṣpageṇeralaṃkṛta mahītalaṃ

caṃdanaṇarukarpūrairāmoditadigaṃtaraṃ

tanmaṃḍapasya madhyastha pārijātaṃ suradrumaṃ

tatrāsīnaṃ jagannāthaṃ kāla nīlāṃvuda prabhaṃ

padmarāga mahāratna vajramāṇikyaviṃdubhiḥ

kirīṭa hārakeyūra kaṭakādi vibhūṣitaṃ

śobhitaṃ pārijātādi puṣpamālya virājitaṃ

śaṃkhacakrā bhayavaradharaṃ yogini ṣevitaṃ

prācīnā nāmapi girāgocaramanūpamaṃ

nārāyaṇaṃ purānaṃda satyacidvanamacyutaṃ

dāḍimī kusumaprakhyāṃ japāṃ puṣpa samaprabhāṃ

dṛśaṃ jannūpuraṃ saṃśobhipādayaṃ kaṇamaṃḍitāṃ

sthalā raviṃda pratima padābjayugaśobhitā

virājita padāṃbhojāṃ bhaktārti haraṇo ghṛtāṃ

tanu mādhyāṃ pṛthu śroṇī nimnanābhi samanvitāṃ

viśālorusthalāṃ rājatkaustubheta virājitāṃ

ratnamuktāphaloyetta svarṇaciṃtākaśobhitāṃ

muktāvalī samāyuktāmekāvalikhirājitī

svarṇakeyūrasahitāṃ valayāvaliśobhitāṃ

prathamābharaṇoyeta kaṃvugrīva virājitāṃ

smitavaktrāṃ viśālākṣyaṃ pakvaviṃvaphalādharāṃ

vāṇī vilāsanilaparakta jihvāgraśobhitāṃ

taptasauvarṇatāṭaṃkṛvālī yugalaśobhitā

kāmako daṃḍasaudarvanirjitabhrūtatāyugāṃ

jātī caṃpaka punnāga puṣpatālya vibhūṣitāṃ

navamāṇikyasaṃvaddha kirīṭāvṛtamastakāṃ

vibhratīvaṃ supātraṃ ca muktāratnamayīṃ srajaṃ

akalaṃkaśarat pūrṇacaṃdraṃ viṃva samānanāṃ

vāṇī gaurīṃdrapatnyādi sevitāṃ pārśvayordvayoḥ

suvarṇasrāviṇā mūrdhni śvetachatreṇa śobhitāṃ

śakrādi maulimāṇikya virājita padāṃvujāṃ

devatāvṛṃda saṃsevyāṃ mahāsiddhaughavaṃditāṃ

trailokyākarṣaṇakarīṃ trailokya hlādakāriṇī

aṇimādi guṇādhārāṃ sarvasāmrājyasiddhidāṃ

divyacaṃdanaliptāṃgī divyakanyā pariṣṭutāṃ

trilocanī catuhasrī suprasannānanāṃvujāṃ

mahālakṣmīmahaṃ nitya bhajāmya khilasiddhidāṃ

yadvā mūlādhādi raṃdhrāṃtaṃ vālārkayuta sannibhāṃ

nirguṇāṃ saguṇāṃ vāpi mahālakṣmī haripriyāṃ

japtvā nityaṃ viśuddhātmā saṃpadāṃ māśreyā bhavet

mahālakṣmīṃ trayī rūpāṃ divyālaṃkāraśobhitāṃ

dhyātvā nārāyaṇadhiyādhyāyedācāryamacyutaṃ

samastābhīṣṭa phaladā bhavatyusya haripriyā

mahālakṣmīṃ vijānī himā kurudyātraṃ saṃśayaḥ

mahālakṣmīdhiyābhyarcyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcya bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ

tāṭaṃkābharaṇa dvaṃdvavālī yugalamuttama

aṃgulīyaka mukhyāni divyānyā bharaṇāni ca

datvā prāpnoti mahatī siddhiṃ cāmara durlabhāṃ

ācāryapatnyai śrīvudhyā pradatvā kalpasaṃnayaṃ

ācāryapatnyo vadvyaścet putriṇī tatrayā bhavet

anyā ācāryapatnyaśca vijñeyā sā tṛptannibhāḥ

vahudūrasthitā vāpi māyāssannidhau śive

ācāryaputrapatnyai vā pradadyāt sādhakottamaḥ

ācāryapatnīmabhyarcya purato bhūṣaṇādibhiḥ

itthaṃ dhyātvā mahālakṣmīṃ vidyāṃ śrīhṛdayābhidhāṃ

tṛśataṃ vā japaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ hutvā dine dine

samasta duritaudyāstena spṛśaṃti kadācana

tadgrahe paśuvṛddhiḥ syāt vanavṛddhirdine dine

muktā ratnaudya saṃkīrṇā dhanadhānya samākulā

acalā tadvahe lakṣmīstiṣṭhatyā caṃdratārakaṃ

śreyo rāśi samākīrṇā āyurārogya saṃyutāḥ

sa yatra vasane devi kṣetreyā pālayepi vā

ihāmutrāpi lakṣmī vā trirguṇopi guṇānvitāḥ

tadvaṃśyāvāmupi śive bhavenmuktissanātanī

sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ dhanyaṃ pūjanaṃ cātha vakṣyate

yatkṛtvā maṃda bhāgyamibhāgya vā tihajāyate

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dvitayaṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ

ācāryapādukā vidyāṃ daśadvādaśadhāpi vā

rātritaṃ śatitaṃ vā svacakṣurbhyāṃ dṛṣṭvā natvā ca sādaraṃ

ācāryavadanāṃ dṛṣṭvā pūrvamācārya pādayoḥ

snātvā saṃdhyāmupāsyātha prajaptvā vedamātaraṃ

yathā śaktyā prajaptvātha vidyāṃ śrīharayābhidhāṃ

prasannalakṣmīstotreṇa stutvā śrīhṛdayena vā

prasannalakṣmī hṛdayaṃ koratrāṣṭakamudīritaṃ

ārtharvaṇa mahadguhyaṃ lakṣmī hṛdayamiṣṭadaṃ

nityahomaṃ samāpyātha jvalitaṃ vedasi

yāgāmāraṃ praviśyātha smṛtvācārya padadvayaṃ

kṣīraṃ vā śarkarāmājyaṃ datvā naivedyamādarāt

tadardhe vā tadardha vā cihnuka vā tadardhakaṃ

rajitaṃ vā yathā śaktyā tāmraṃ tāṃvūlameva vā

udvāsyātha mahālakṣmī cakre saṃpūjayet sudhīḥ

yaduttaṃ tanmalla kramayodhyā nagarābhidhaṃ

samastaduritadhvasiptavapurāya phalapradaṃ

aṇimādi guṇā vāptikaraṃ trailokyamohanaṃ

putraprapautra tatpautrādyabhi vṛddhikaraṃ sadā

samastopa dravaharaṃ samastabhayanāśanaṃ

sarvāśubhaharaṃ puṃsāṃ samastādya vibhedanaṃ

apamṛtyu praśamanaṃ kāṃti medhā vivardhanaṃ

ārogyajananaṃ puṃsāṃ rogaśokanivāraṇaṃ

śrīcakraṃ tanmahadguptamarcayitvā dine dine

sarvavidyāmayaṃ yasmācchrīcakraṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

pradhya prapūjanaṃ vāpi kṛtvā saṃtatamādarāt

lakṣmīhṛdayavidyāṃ ca japtvā paṃcaśataṃ śuciḥ

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryaṃ dhanasaṃpattimakṣayāṃ

divyānapi mahābhogāni hāpyā sādyasādhakaḥ

ayodhyānagare tiṣṭhatyaṃte nātra vicāraṇā

śakrādyā aṣṭadikpālāstvaṃ ca lakṣmīssarasvatī

pūjitā api saṃtuṣṭā bhavatyaṃsya phalapradā

yena saṃpūjyate nityaṃ trailokyaṃ yena pūjitaṃ

pūjyate satataṃ yena sa sarvajño jagatpatiḥ

sāmrājyalakṣmīmathavā pūjayikta dine dine

lakṣmīnārāyaṇā vacasāṃgāvaṇa saṃyutau

bhavānīśaṃkaro yadvā sāṃgāvaraṇa saṃyutau

amahālakṣmīṃ samabhyarcya pratyekamathavā śive

atrāśakto mahālakṣmīṃ pīṭhenyatrātha vārcayet

trikoṇamatha ṣaṭkoṇaṃ dvādaśāramataḥ paraṃ

praṇavaṃ vilikhenmadhye sādhyakarma vidarbhiti

ṣaḍaṃgānyagni koṇādikoṇā ṣaṭke kramāllikhet

ṣoḍaśasvara saṃyuktaṃ kuryāt ṣoḍaśa patrakaṃ

dvidhā dvitīyamapi bhūgehaṃta tathaiva pariveṣṭhayet

uktame tanmahālakṣmyā yaṃtra duritabhedanaṃ

sarvaduḥkhapraśamanaṃ samastaśubhasiddhidaṃ

atra mūrti samanvitaṃ agrataḥ puṣpadhanvānaṃ rati devyā samanvitaṃ

utthāni pūrvamukteṣu sthāneṣudhidhiva yajet

gāyatrī devyuṣāvetirapi mahālakṣmīstriśaktyākhyaṃ jagatpate

sarvadyā mayaṃ prokta mayo dhyānagaṇabhidhaṃ

vadame kṛpayānātha yadinesti kṛpāmayi

mahītalesyānāmāni śṛṇuṣvā vahitā bhava

tathā sannati pīṭhasthā mahālakṣmīrharipriyā

pāṃduraṃge triśaktyākhyā lakṣmī viṭṭhala vallabhā

yoginyalaṃ purakhyātā yogasiddhipradāyinī

mīnākṣīti samākhyātā dakṣiṇe mathurāpure

śeṣā cale mahālakṣmīrvikhyātā girikanyake

laṃkāpurercitā nityaṃ śāṃkarī śaṃkarapriyā

maheṃdra parvate durgā mahiṣāsuramardinī

gaṃgāsāgaraṃ sargopa vikhyātā kamalālaya

nīlācale samākhyātā subhadrā bhuktidā

vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣīttattu mṛṣṭānna siddhidā

prayābhagemādhavī tāmrākhyātā nārāyaṇapriyā

rukmiṇī dvārakāyāṃ ca rāmamā mā haripriyā

yaśodāgokule proktā rādhāvṛṃdāvane vane

durgādevīti vikhyāviṃdhyādriśikheraśubhesī

gaṃgādvāre mahokṣetre sītā janakakanyakā

vikhyātā śrīpure sākṣān mahātripurasuṃdarī

mataṃgaparvatekhyātā śrīdevīmaṃtriṇī smṛtā

kurukṣetrepi vikhyātā pārvatī śaṃkarapriyā

śrīraṃgaparvate khyātā śrīdevī kamalālayā

tryaṃvaka girau khyātā rudrāṇī śaṃkarapriyā

cakrarājātmakānyāsāṃ pīṭhāni giri kanyake

etāni siddhakṣetrāṇi saṃtyanyānyapi bhūtale

prasannalakṣmī pīṭhāni sarvāṇyetāni suṃdari

śucirjiteṃdriyo nityaṃ guru śuśrūṣaṇerataḥ

divasatriteyanaiva mahatī siddhimāpnuyāt

tātitubhyaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇudyā vahitā śive

paraṃ anaṃtaśayana puṇyaṃ caṃdra prakāriṇī taṭaṃ

āhovalaṃ mahākṣetramalāpuramataḥ paraṃ

kanyākubja mahadgāma vedāraṇyamataḥ paraṃ

varāha śikharaṃ puṇyavedāri śikharaṃ tataḥ

ratnādri śikhaṃ puṇyaṃ lakṣmīśa kṣetramapyatha

dākṣāṇyābhidha kṣetraṃ brahmeśvarama mataḥ paraṃ

śrīkūrmapadamadyuktaṃ mārkaṃḍeya padābhidhaṃ

paṃcaprayogakṣetrāṇi kedārakṣetramapyatha

jaṃjṛmārgaṃ mahākṣetraṃ gaṃgāsāgarasaṃgamaḥ

naṃdigrāmābhidhakṣetra mahadatyaṃta pāvanaṃ

yatrāste bhagavān sākṣādyogarūḍhaḥ śriyaḥ patiḥ

pālayanna khilaṃ lokaṃ śiṣṭānāṃ hitamācaran

bhāgyodayena tatkṣetraṃ labhyāte sukṛtātmabhiḥ

sadya eva vimuktaḥ syān mahāpātakavānapi

vaikuṃṭhalokādāgahā siddhikarāṣvapi

tīrthāni saṃpravakṣyāmi siddhidāni mahītale

godāvarī kṛṣṇuveṇī kāverī saritāṃvarā

yoṣṇī ca tathā siṃdhunadī paramapāvatī

bhadrādri śikharasvāmi sānnidhyaṃ gautamī taṭaṃ

rāmahūdaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ rāmatīrtha manuttamaṃ

lakṣmīsarovaraṃ puṇyaṃ phalgunī tīrthamapyatha

maṃdākinā tadaṃ puṇyatīrthaṃ vāśiṣṭa saṃjñitaṃ

śivagaṃgā mahāpuṇyā dhanuṣkoṭirataḥ paraṃ

tulasīkānanaṃ puṇyaṃ vilvadrumavījaṃ paraṃ

kamalālayatīrtha ca sāvitrī tīrthamuttamaṃ

tīrthābhīmuttamaṃ śive sā lagnāma silācakrasānnidhye śrīdyayaṃ viduḥ

vilvavṛkṣavanaṃ puṇyaṃ mahāsiddhikaraṃ smṛtaṃ

darśanādapyathai teṣā bhavet siddhiśca śāśvatī

ekāhamapi jadhvā vā vidyāṃ śrīhṛdayābhidhāṃ

yada kṛtvā naro vidyā siddhiṃ na labhate kvacit

trilakṣadvaṃ pare proktaṃ cāturlakṣaṃ kalauyuge

kalauyuge sadāmartyāḥ parastrī dhanalolupāḥ

puroditeṣu pītīpyeṣvaṃpi mama priye

tulasīvana madhye vā vinvadrumavanepi vā

bhavānī maṃdire vāpi kamalalāyatanepi vā

ācāryaṃ sannidhīkṛtya puraścaraṇamāsmat

vadarīkānanaṃ proktaṃ tadevāśu phalapradaṃ

śātodāṃtojita krodho maunī saṃdhyā su saṃyataḥ

nāstikairvedavāhyairvani saṃbhāṣāpara kvacit

rajasvalāṃtyajādīnāma saṃbhāṣaṇa tatparaḥ

tapta kṛcchra paro cāpi paṃcagavyāśanopi vā

kaṃdamūla phalāśī vā havira annathāpi vā

mahālakṣmyā jape dvidyāṃ śuddho nirmalamānasaḥ

asadgoṣṭhīma saṃvaddhālāyaṃ paiśūnyameva ca

avadaśāstraniṣṭhāṃ ca varjayenmatimānnaraḥ

śṛṇu yānna kvacidyāga niṃdāṃ ca duritapradāṃ

samadaśī ca sarvatra mitrāritanayeṣu

cakrarājārcanaṃ nityaṃ kurvāṇo niyatavrataḥ

daśāṃśaṃ tarpayeddhīmānyayasekṣurasenadhā

taddaśāṃśaṃ hunedagnau kamalairghṛtamiśritaiḥ

suvāsinyaḥ śataṃ bhījyāstāvaṃto brāhmaṇājapi

niṣkasāhasramatha vā śataniṣkamathapi vā

rājyalakṣmyā avāpnoti dehāṃtepi paṇātiṃ

asādhyamapi devānāmapi dvīpāṃtaraṇyitaṃ

atrāśaktaḥ puraścaryāṃ kuryāllaghvīṃ yata vrataṃ

śaradādyāśvine māsi vrataṃ paramapāvanaṃ

sarvakalmaṣarogaghna sarvaduḥkhavibhedanaṃ

rājyalakṣmī mahāvidyā sidhyarthāninata vrataḥ

duḥkha pranāśanaṃ dhanye tuṣṭipuṣṭikaraṃ paraṃ

tadvidhā namatho vakṣye śṛṇudhvā vahitā śive

raviparvasamaṃ prakurāgamārtha viśāradāḥ

addhaudaya samaṃ prāhurvedavedātavedinaḥ

mahatīti samākhyātā mahāsiddhi phalapradā

sarvasiddhikarī proktā bhoga svargāpavargadā

kṛtvādau maṃgata snānamāryājñā purassaraṃ

gaṇādhipaṃ vāpi saṃkalpya prajapecchuciḥ

sahasraṃ prajapennityaṃ yadvāyaṃ ca śatādikaṃ

sālegrāmaśilācakramathavā girikanyake

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā nityaṃ śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā nataḥ paṃcadaśārṇayā

ekaikaśastahutvāstotramārtharvaṇaṃ śubhaṃ

navarātre yedekottara vṛddhikramoṇa

śrīśrīśāvarcayeddhīmānbhojayecca suvāsinīḥ

śrīśrīpatidhiyā yadvā bhojayenmithunaṃ śive

yadvācārya priyāṃ sākṣān mahālakṣmīdhiyā śive

aśakto maṃdabhāgyopiyācidhvā śūdrakānapi

ardhayitvā viśeṣeṇa tata ācāryamarcayet

mahānavamyāṃ prāptāyī prātarudvāda bhāgyataḥ

aṣṭāviṃśati hutvā gāyatryā jātavedasi

hutvā prasannalakṣmyātha sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

gavyājya saṃplutaṃ hutvā bhagavadraśmibhirghṛte

hutvā paṃcadaśākṣathīghṛtaṃ triśatamamādarāt

lakṣmyā prasannalakṣmyārtha triśaktyā ca tataḥ paraṃ

japādi homaṃ kṛtvātha prāyaścittāhutīstataḥ

atha paṃcaśataṃ lakṣmī lakṣmīśau kusumairyajet

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ praṇipatya samāhisaḥ

vajraviṃdu samāyuktaṃ kuṃḍala dvaya marpayet

svarṇa niṣka sahasraṃ vā śataniṣkamathāpi vā

sūkṣmaṃ vastrayugāṃ dadyāhṛhṇīyādāṣi śiṣaḥ śibhāḥ

kanakat na kacitākaṃ nāsābharaṇamapyatha

tāṭaṃkābharaṇaṃ vāpi ciṃtākaṃ haimajaṃ tu vā

sūkṣmaṃ vā sastato dadyān mahālakṣmīdhiyā tataḥ

yadvā paṃcadaśābhyarcāssuvāsinyona vāpi vā

dakṣiṇābhiścatāṃ kūlairbrāhmaṇānapi nāpayet

daridropi vrataṃ kṛtvā svācārya varaṇadvaye

dakṣiṇāmarpyaṣṭajīta vāgyataḥ

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāṃ navarātravrate śubhe

agniṣṭhoma sahasrasya vājapayeyakṣatasya ca

phalaṃ prāpya viśuddhātmā nārāyaṇasanvitāṃ

janmakoṭi amudbhūtairmahāpāpaiścadāruṇaiḥ

avāpya ca na dhānyaudya muktāratnasamākulāṃ

saṃtati vyāpinīṃ labdhvā sarvasaubhāgyasaṃyutaḥ

bhūtvā samṛddhiṃ mahatīṃ putrapautrādi saṃkulāṃ

bhuktvātha mānuyā divyān bhogānapi sudurlabhān

divyakanyā śatayutaṃ vimānaṃ kāmagaṃ śubhaṃ

navarātravratamida sarvasiddhikaraṃ śubhaṃ

saptamyāṃ ravisaṃkrāṃtirbhānuvāsara saṃyutā

arkasaṃkramaṇaṃ tatra brāhmekāse bhavedyadi

nakṣatraṃ bhagadaivatyaṃ bhavatyatra yadi priye

athacottara saṃkrāṃtissaptamyārmiduvāsare

vijayākhyaṃ mahāyogevaraṃtīmaṃ maharṣayaḥ

dhanussaṃkramaṇaṃ bhānoryayaṃ tadasamanvitaṃ

madhyāhne hṛṣasaṃkrāṃtiryadi sāvitra saṃyutā

samasta saṃpatsamavāpti hetuṃ samastapāpaudya vināśahetuṃ

pavitraṃ ciṃtāmaṇiṃ yogamudāharaṃti dharmārthakāmā divyamarthahetuṃ

yoga samāyuktaṃ tanmāsaṃ ca sakalaṃ śive

hṛṣasaṃkrāṃti paryaṃtaṃ prathametra pravakṣyate

tatrotthāya viśuddhātmā śrīśrīśadvādaśākṣarī

śālagrāma śilācakrecakrarāja samanvite

triśataṃ vā śataṃ nityamarcayedbrahmavidyayā

jaṃvū pravālairdūrvāgnaiḥ śamīpatrairathāpi vā

kṛsaraṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ sārdrakaṃ goghṛtaplutaṃ

vidhivat saṃkṛskṛte vahnau tadeva kṛsaraṃ śubhaṃ

juhuyādveda mātrādāvaṣṭā viśatidhā śive

kevalena ghṛtenātha lakṣmī hṛdaya vidyayā

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā catuḥ śrīmanubhistataḥ

japetyaṃ ca śataṃ nityaṃ japtvāṣṭākṣaramāditaḥ

gavyakoṭī sahasrāyuta homa puṇyaṃ dine dine sau labhate girīṃdraje

kṛtvetthaṃ makaraṃ bhānuryathā saṃkramate śive

triśataṃ juhuyādagnau ramā hṛdayavidyayā

ghṛtaṃ ca triśataṃ hutvā bhagavadraśmibhistataḥ

hutvaikadhā śriyo devyā vidyayāṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

***** hutvā śrīmaṣṭā viṃśatidhāpi vā

ūṣa saṃkramaṇe kālestātvācārya padadvayaṃ

ācārya sannidhāvagniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya tataḥ paraṃ

aṣṭā viṃśatidhā brahmavidyayāpi hunedghṛtaṃ

śrīmadaṣṭārṇayā paścāt triśataṃ juhuyād ghṛtaṃ

mahālakṣmī mathā vāhya jvaliṃte jātavedasi

prasannayā mahālakṣmyā sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

madhu tritaya saṃyuktairhutvācāryasya sannidhau

śriyā paṃcadaśākṣaryā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ ghṛtaṃ

triśakti lakṣmyā sāmrājya lakṣmyā ca ghṛtamīśvari

japādibhiḥ paścāt kṛtvā brahmārpaṇahuti

ācāryacaraṇāṃbhoja dvitayaṃ praṇipatya vai

kuṃḍaladvitayaṃ dadyāt karṇayugme tataḥ paraṃ

varṣāśanamitaṃ dravyaṃ dātavyaṃ caraṇadvaye

tadardhaṃ vārpayeddhīmān vittalobhaṃ varityajet

ārdrakaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ paṃcāḍhakamitaṃ śive

dhenudvayaṃ pradātavyaṃ vahukṣīraṃ saṃvatsakaṃ

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdve dadyāt sidhyarthamāśvari

kanatkunaka ciṃtākamarghyayedatha vā śive

datvā vastreṇa saṃmoṣya bhojayecca suvāsinīḥ

karayugmerpayeddhrīmān paladvaya vinirmitaṃ

atannāśaktotha raupyeṇa suśubhreṇa vinirmiti

suvāsinī śataṃ bhojyaṃ tadadhāṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

kūṣmāṃḍaṃ vahuvītāḍhyaṃ śrotriyāya dvijātaye

sthalaṃ sudīrgha kūṣmāṃḍaṃ tasminkāle mama priye

dakṣiṇāsahitaṃ bhūmau sadyaḥpyata karāśibhi

tāvat kalpa sahasrāṇi vaikuṃṭhe nivasatyasau

dhenudvādaśakaṃ dadyāttilarāśiṃ sudakṣiṇaṃ

yadyaddattaṃ mahāyoge tasminkāle maheśvari

itthaṃ kṛtvānna havanaṃ varṣa sāhasramādarāt

tatphalaṃ labhataṃ nātra saṃdehomīnalaudhane

uktenānena vidhinā vājimadhān sahasraśaḥ

phalamāpnoti tejasvī lakṣmīścāsya prasīdati

samarpyābharaṇaṃ haimaṃ sūkṣmavstra samanvitaṃ

itthaṃ kṛtvā puraścaryāmatra yogeti durlabhe

vimukto hayamadhānāṃ sahasrajanitaṃ phalaṃ

labdhvā nityaṃ viśuddhātmā mahadārogyāmakṣayaṃ

hastyaścarathasaṃkīrṇaṃ mahatīṃ śriyamakṣayāṃ

bhūtvā sidhyaṣṭakaṃ prāpya putrapautravatīṃ śriya

divyakatyā sahasreṇa sahitaṃ sarvabhogadaṃ

ajarāmaraṇo bhūtvā paṃcaviṃśati vārṣikaḥ

ṣaḍtriṃśadvatsarairyogarājoyaṃ labhyate śive

phalaṃ vaktuṃ satyaṃ paṃkaja locane

yacchrutvā sādhako bhūmau kṛtakṛtyo bhaviṣyati

ekādaśīdine somavāsareṇa samanvite

ati ṣaḍayute vāpi divā saṃdhyāstu vā niśi

alabhyaḥ kathito loke sarvakalyāṇasiddhidaḥ

nānena sadṛśo yaugaḥ kathyate paramarṣibhiḥ

japahomādikahṛṇāṃ mahālakṣmīḥ prasīdati

alabhyayoge saṃprāpte divā vā saṃdhyayorniśi

ācāryacaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ sāṣṭāṃgaṃ daṃḍavannamet

caturviśati saṃkhyākairupacāraissamarcayan

yājvaline saṃskṛte vahnau gurupādābja sannidhau

madhutritayasaṃmiśraiḥ śatādhika sahasrakaṃ

bhagavadvidya pāpaścā daṣṭāviṃśati varṇayā

ekaikaśastato hutvā śriyāyaḥ ca daśārṇayā

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā pratyekaṃ jātavedasi

prāyaścittāhutīḥ kṛtvā kuryādbrahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

ṣaṣṭi cihuka sauvarṇaṃ padmarāga samanvitaṃ

aṃkitaṃ svaguṇṭhe karṇayuge dātavyamādarāt

bhūṣitavyā prayatnena sūkṣmaṃ vā sassamarpya ca

paṃcāḍhakamitā vāpi caturāḍhakasasmitāḥ

śataniṣka suvarṇa vā tadardhyaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

datvettha dakṣiṇā dravyaṃ kuṃḍala dvitayānvitaṃ

dadyādavaśyaṃgiri medhanādhyaścet pravakṣate

pāritoṣika dānena saṃtoṣyācārya mādarāt

datvoktaṃ dakṣiṇādravyaṃ kuṃḍala dvitayānvitaṃ

paurāṇikaṃ vrataṃ yena kṛtaṃ tatra mama priye

tasmin dine nirāhāraḥ kṣīrāśī vā phalāśanaḥ

prātarutthitaḥ snātvā kṛtāhnikobhyarnya lakṣmīnārāyaṇau vibhū

yadvā dvādaśasaṃkhyākāmṛṣṭāśrairbhojayedvratī

ācāryā dāśiṣaḥ prāpya bhuṃjīyādvaṃdhūbhissaha

karoti viśuddhātmā sa siddho bhavati dhruvaṃ

kāyena manasā vācā jñānājñāna kṛtainapi

sāṃga dakṣiṇayāyukta puraścaryā śatodbhavaṃ

atrāvaśyaṃ pradātavyā śālagrāmaśilā śubhā

brahmāṃḍakoṭi dānasya phalaṃ prāpnoti śāśvataṃ

koṭī sahasra homasya phalamakṣaya maśnute

tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā vittalobhaṃ parityajet

niṣka dvādaśakaṃ vāpi datvācārya padāṃvuje

phalamāsādya śuddhātmā bhagavat kiṃ karo bhavet

ācārya karṇayugale datvā svarṇā calārpaṇāt

yatphalaṃ labhate bhūmau tatmāprotyakṣayaṃ phalaṃ

uddhṛtya prāpayatyāśau satyalokaṃ sanātanaṃ

śriyaṃ paśyati cakṣurbhyāṃ sākṣā bhagavadanvitī

gavāmayutadānasya samagraṃ phalamaśrute

kṛtvācāryasya sānnidhye śriyamaṣṭa guṇānvitāṃ

muktāratnādi saṃpūrṇā kaṃdarpasadṛśaḥ śriyā

samastasaṃpadādhāro bhūtvā putrādi saṃyutaṃ

ciramāyuṣya māsādya sarvabhoga samanvitaḥ

dehāṃte paramaṃ dhāma yogināmapi durlabhaṃ

lakṣmīnārāyaṇa samo yogo labhyo na vidyate

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyāsiddhikaraṃ śive

bhūyopi siddhidaṃ kṛtyaṃ yatkṛtvā siddhimma bhavet

vāsaṃtaṃ tava rātrākhyaṃ kṣatamasti mahatpuraṃ

putrapautrādi saṃpatti phaladaṃ japasiddhidaṃ

muktāratma suvarṇādi dravyarāśikaraṃ gṛhe

māsi caitrābhicai prāpte pratipadinamāditaḥ

ekaikadivasaṃ tatra mahodaya samaprabhaṃ

tāraṃ vāditi daivatyaṃ vārhasyatyamathāpi vā

tatphalaṃ na mayāvaktuṃ śakyate vedhasāpi ca

japahomādijanitaṃ phala tatra mahattaraṃ

śrīrāmajanmadivasaṃ yāvaṃdhyaṃ kurute bhuvi

śrīrāmajanmadivase yatkiṃcidapi kāṃcanaṃ

phalamāpya viśuddhātmā brahmasāyujya yaśrute

śrīrāmajanmadivase datvācārya padāṃvuje

śrīmadaṣṭārṇa maṃtreṇa rājyalakṣmyāpitadine

vidhānaṃ tatra vakṣyāmi vāsaṭena varānnake

navarātra yadā proktaṃ vāsaṃtaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ

snātvā tatra viśuddhātmā kṛta pūrvāhnika kriyaḥ

catuḥśataṃta vā triśataṃ jvalite jātavedasi

aṣṭā viṃśatidhā hutvā dvādaśa brahmavidyayā

tataḥ prasannalakṣmyāpi hunedaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ

japet paṃcaśataṃ śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manuṃ

saṃkalpārka sahasrāṇi japecchrī hṛdayaṃ śive

siṃhāṃsaneścarī paṃcadaśārṇāmadri kanyake

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nityamaṣṭā viṃśatidhāpi vā

japet paṃcādhikaṃ catvāriṃśadā vṛttimādarāt

mahālakṣmīdhiyā nityaṃ bhojayecca suvāsinī

tataḥ śrīrāmacaṃdrasya navamyāṃ prātarutthitaḥ

ācāryasannidhau paścāt saṃskṛte jātavedasi

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā śrīśrīśabdā daśārṇayā

mahālakṣmīmathā vāhya tasminnagrau trayīmayīṃ

tadā varaṇadevī nāmekaikāmāhuti hunet

yajñanāśayaṇāṃ kasthāṃ dhyātvānna niyatavrataḥ

kadalīphala saṃmiśraṃ kapilājya samanvitaṃ

ūṣatvā prasannalakṣmyātha gavyenājyena tatparaṃ

atha śriyo mahārājñā raśmividyāmirādaśat

aṣṭottaraśataṃ gavyenājyena juhuyādatha

hunedaṣṭottara śatamaṣṭā viṃśatidhā pivā

juhuyāt parvakāleya ekāmapyāhutiṃ ghṛtiṃ

puraścaraṇa homāṃte hutvāṣṭā viṃśadarṇayā

tasmādavaśyaṃ homāṃtaṃvyamaṣṭā viṃśati varṇayā

agniṃ pradakṣiṇī kṛtya namaskṛtya girīṃdraje

kuṃḍala dvitayaṃ haimaṃ ṣaṣṭi cihnuka nirmitaṃ

dadyādavaśyamācārya karṇayugmegni sanmidhau

jānakīsahitastasya puraścaryā śatodbhavāṃ

dukūla yugalaṃ vāpi kāryā samatha vāmadu

datvā dhanāḍhyo bhūyo vā svarṇasāhasramarpayet

tarāśaktopi ṣaṇinaṣkāṃnniniṣkaṃ vā samarpayet

ikṣu daṃḍagaṇopetaṃ nārikela phalānvitaṃ

daridro maṃdabhāgyopi bhikṣitvā sarvavarṇajān

kanyāṃ rūpavatī dadyācchīlaudārya guṇāvitāṃ

sūkṣmaṃ vā sassamarpyādau phalarāśi mataḥ paraṃ

dādāti yadi tanmūnaṃ na siddhimadhi gacchati

mahālakṣmīdhiyācāryapatnī mākalpasaṃcayaiḥ

śrīrāmajanmadivaseya itthaṃ samācaret

jānakīśa prasādena śriyaṃ maiṃdrīma caṃcalāṃ

putrapautra samṛddhiṃ ca kalyāṇāni dine dine

tasmindine nirāhāraḥ kṣīrāśī vā phalāśanaḥ

paredyuḥ śrīdhiyābhyarcyamṛṣṭarannaiḥ suvāsinīḥ

bhuṃjīta vaṃdhubhiḥ sārdhaṃ vipraśeṣānnamādarāt

karoti sa bhavedrāma caṃdrānu grahato bhuvi

putrapautrā śriyā yukto dīrghāpuṣpa samanvitaḥ

bhūtvā jātismaraḥ paścādgrāmaṃ daṣṭvā jagatati

samastasaṃpat sāmrājya hetubhūtaṃ girīṃdraje

vudhena bhṛguṇā vāpi sahitā vahi saṃyutā

samastādyau ghṛśamanī sarvapuṇya phalapradā

anayā sadṛśī nāsti tithissakalasiddhidā

atra jāgarti satataṃ lakṣmīśaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ

svadarśanotsukānṛṣṭvājanān paramadhārmikān

akṣayākhya tṛtīyāyāṃ yo māmatrā gajājanaḥ

avaśyaṃ mama sāyujyaṃ dāsyāmya saṃśayaḥ

śrīmadaṣṭākṣarī vidyāṃ japedye niyatavrataḥ

tasyākṣayā mahāsiddhimihadatvā maha āṃ

akṣayāṃ saṃpradāsyāni vaikuṃṭha mamasmannidhau

sunaṇīṃ ca hukumasya dadāsyasyā calāśriyaṃ

atrākṣayavine kṛtvā madanagrahato bhuvi

ityāha bhagavān yogī harinārāyaṇo vibhuḥ

tasmādavaśyaṃ vaiśākhe divase kṣayasaṃjñite

yadyakṣa ca mahāsiddhimicchatyatra narā bhuvi

etadvratasamaṃ nānyadvratanāstipālapradaṃ

akṣaye brahmasāyujya pradātṛtvena cākṣayaṃ

akṣayāyā tṛtīyāyāṃ prasannakamalāmanoḥ

brāhmekāle samutthāya brahmavidyāṃ prajapya ca

kṛta śaucastataḥ snātvā kṛtasaṃdhyā japakriyaḥ

nityahomaṃ samāpyātha śrīmadaṣṭākṣaraṃ manu

śuciḥ paṃcaśataṃ japtvā gurupādābja sannidhau

aṣṭāviṃśati bhāgavyamājya hutvā girīṃdraje

hutvā paṃcaśataṃ devi lakṣmīhṛdayavidyayā

ghṛtaplutairhunedagnau sahasraṃ triśatādhikaṃ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ gavyenājyena juhuyadatha

agniṃ prayakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskṛtya ca sādaraṃ

śrīśrīśa dvādaśākṣayapijet paṃcaśataṃ śive

dadhyannasahitaṃ devi naivedyaṃ ca samarpayet

datvā go dvitayaṃ vatsa vahukṣīra samanvitaṃ

triniṣkamathavā datvā gurupatnyai ramādhiyā

suvāsinīśca saṃbhojya siddhimāpnotyanuttamā

saṃtati vyāpinīṃ lakṣmīṃ dhanadhānyādisaṃkulāṃ

akṣayāmapi vāksiddhimakṣayāṃ putrasaṃtati

akṣayaṃ brahmasāyujyaṃ dehāṃtesya prayacchati

vaiśākhemāsi kartavyaṃ vratame tadanuttamaṃ

niṣkamekaṃ pradatvā svācārya caraṇadvaye

akṣayāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ kṛtvetthaṃ homamādarāt

ācāryapatnyai niṣkārvalakṣmī vudhyā samarpya ca

śrīmadhyannaṃ ghṛtamiśritaṃ

dadhyannameva juhuyācchuddhadhenughṛta ghṛtaṃ

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā tatoṣṭā viśaṃdarṇayā

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā śriyaḥ paṃcadaśārṇayā

pūrṇimāṃtamidaṃ kṛtvā pūrṇimādiva saṃtataḥ

samāpyāvaśyakaṃ karma saṃskṛte jātavedasi

dadhyannaṃ ghṛtasaṃmiśraṃ śrīmadaṣṭārṇavidyayā

dadhyannameva juhuyātyanepsamya phalānvita

bhagavadraśmividyābhirekaikāmāhutiṃ hunet

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā paścācchriyo raśmibhirapyatha

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā ghṛtenaiva girīṃdraje

brahmārpaṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā namaskṛtvātha pāvakaṃ

avaśyamapi dātavyaṃ kuṃḍaladvayamādarāt

muktālaṃkaraṇaṃ vāpi ratnālaṃkaraṇaṃ tu vā

palameka suvarṇaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

avaśyaṃgauḥ pradātavyā vahukṣmīrā savatsakā

vahūsthūlāni dīrghāṇi phalasaṃdyayutānyapi

kadalīphalasaṃdyaṃ ca dadyāt svagurave śive

dadhyannāpūya bhakṣyādyairbhojayakṣa suvāsinīḥ

arcāyedatha vā lakṣmī sannidhau vā samarpayet

tatpatnyai vā tatsutāyai tatpautrāyātha vārpayet

puraścaryā vratamidaṃ vaiśākhemāsi pārvati

siddhimāpnoti mahatīsaṃdyaḥ pratyayakāriṇī

dhanaiśca dhānyairmaṇimiśca saṃkulāṃ lakṣmīmavāpnoti ramāprasādāt

rathāśvamatto bhayadāti pūrṇāṃ rājyaśriyaṃ viṃdati vatseratra

āyuṣyamārogya dhanābhivṛddhiṃ vaṃśābhivṛddhiṃ ca maheṃdra tulyāṃ

kalevarāṃte haripār"vatī niraṃjanassāmyamupaiti divyaṃ

dhātuḥ padayāṃtyati bhogayuktā āruhyanaṃgirirājakanye

smṛtau vātha purāṇa saṃcaye saghoramānu grahadāyakena

kiṃ kiṃ varamiṣṭajātaṃ śriyeḥ prasādādirijeṃdrakanye

kṛtvā vratvaṃ prāpya mahārthasiddhiṃ caraṃ tilokeṣu vimuktavaṃdhāḥ

kṛtvā vaśyaṃ naraḥ sākṣān mahālakṣmī prapaśyati

brahmarṣayo vaśiṣṭādyāḥ śakrādyāścādegīśvrāḥ

mahālakṣmīprasādena lakṣmīśānugrahādapi

saṃprāpya bhagavatsāmye satataṃ sukhino bhavan

mahāpāpaudya dalanaṃ śatakratu phalapradaṃ

tatrārkadivasaṃ vādāvaṃte puṣkara saṃyutaṃ

koti tritayā tīrthāni sārdhakoṭi yutāni ca

dinadvādaśakaṃ tatra puṣkarastīrthanāyakaḥ

bhikṣvānnamapi datvā vā gautamyāṃ puṣkarāgame

brahmapno vā surāpāyīsteyī vā gurutalpakaḥ

viśvastaviṣadātāragarbhaḥ ghātī sahasraśaḥ

ahāraniyamaṃ kṛtvā yoge puṣkaranāmake

dinadvādaśakānmuktaḥ sarvapāpairbhavavyasau

tānugrahaṃ prāpya siddhimiṣṭāṃ vrajatyasau

datvā meru sahasrasya phalaṃ prāpnotyanuttamāṃ

koṭihoma phalanityaṃ prāpnoti girikanyake

godāvarī taṭe ramye nānyatra bhuvi pāvati

tatra vakṣyāmi girije śṛṇuṣva su samāhitā

godāvarītaṭaṃ ganvātamaskṛtyāthagautatamī

godāvadyatitaḥ snātvā kṛta pūrvāhnika kriyaḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā gāyatryājyaṃ dine dine

kṣīṇannaṃ madhusaṃmiśraṃ kapilājyena saṃplutaṃ

api paṃcaśataṃ hutvā śrīyuktāṣṭāṇayopyatha

ghṛtamekaikaśo hutvā suṃdarī raśmibhistata

aṣṭāviṃśatidhā hutvā prāyaścittāhutīrhudhveta

bhojayenmithuna dvaṃdvaṃ śriyaṃ paṃcaśataṃ japan

dānakramaṃ pravakṣyāmi dineṣu dvādaśāsvatha

pakva samyayutāṃ bhūmiṃ dvitīye divasepayet

sahasradakṣiṇāduktaṃ varṣadravyayutaṃ tu vā

dukūladyugalaṃ vāpi kāryā sa dvitayaṃ tu vā

khāsadvādaśakaṃ dhānyaṃ yadvā khārī catuṣṭayaṃ

saptame divase dadyādguḍaṃdakṣiṇayunvite

paladvādaśakaṃ raupyaṃ catuḥpalamitaṃ tu vā

paṃcācchakamitaṃ vāpi pradadyānnavame dine

ahastyaścaratha saṃpūrṇī navame divaserpyayet

daśame divase dadyādvāsona karaṇānvitaṃ

dvādaśe divase dadyāt suvarṇāni sahasraśaḥ

athavācārya pādābje dadyādekottaraṃ dhanaṃ

kuṃḍalādīnalaṃkārān tasminyoge samarpayat

ācāryāyāryayet svarṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ dvādaśe dine

tatrāpa śaktastasyārthavitalobhaṃ parityajet

ācāryāyāryayitvapi syādṛṇī pratyahaṃ śive

vastra prakṣālanādyaiśca pāda saṃvāhanādibhiḥ

śuśrūṣāṃ niyataḥ kuryādavaśyaṃ pratyahaṃ vudhaḥ

ācāryapatnīṃ vāsobhirbhūṣayeddivyabhūṣaṇaiḥ

itthaṃ puṣkarayogesmin kṛtvā godāvarī taṭe

sāṃgadakṣiṇayāyukta puraścaryā sahasrajaṃ

dhanadhānya gajāśvādi saṃpūrṇāṃ ratnasaṃkulī

prāpya śakradhaneśakradhaneśādi śriyaṃ tṛṇagaṇāyate

navarāga śiroratna dviguṇī kṛtarociṣaḥ

maṃdabuddhirjaḍo vāpi vidyā svaṣṭādaśasvapi

svacchaṃdamaraṇo bhūtvāyamaṃ tatkiṃ karāna pitṛṇī

piturmātuśca bhāryā yāni magraṃ nararakārṇave

vaṃśābhi vṛddhi mākalyaṃ prāpnoti śrīsamanvitāṃ

anugrahakarī lakṣmyānā parāsti śrutau smṛtau

bhūyopi saṃpravakṣyāmi śrotuṃtvaṃ niyatā bhava

amāpratipadossaṃdhau rāhugrasto raviryadi

pūrṇāpratipadossaṃdhaurāhuṇā ketunā api

bhānuvāsara saṃyuktarmiduvāra yutaṃ tu vā

prakṣaṃ sarvalokānāṃ dugrahaṇāṃ śubhaṃ

apamṛtyu praśamanaṃ vijayaśrī phalapradaṃ

yatkiṃcidapi japtaṃ vāhutaṃdattamathāpi vā

jātakaṃ mṛtakaṃ vātra sūtrakaṃ naiva vidyate

srākyanamācarītaṃ yena sa sadyaḥ pāparāśibhiḥ

gocarma sasmitā bhūmiryena tatra pradīyate

saptadvīpavatī pṛthvīṃ dhanādhānyādi saṃkulā

tadavaśyaṃ phalaṃ martyaḥ prāpnoti tu visādhakaḥ

hemādri sadṛśa svarṇadānasya phalamaśrute

gokoṭidānaṃ japuṇyaṃ prāpnot yatra na saṃśayaḥ

duttāstrayaste na bhavaṃ tilokāyaḥ kāṃcanaṃgāṃ ca mahīṃ pradadyāt

mahāphalaḥ kālo brahmavidbhirudīritaḥ

hutvā japtvātravācārya pādābja yugale śive

ācāryasiddhimāpnoti śrīprasādaṃ vrajatyapi

yacca yāvacca sarvasvaṃ ravīṃdu grahaṇe śubhe

pitaraṃ mātaraṃ vāpi samuddiśya mṛtānapi

mahāpāpaudya sahitā apite pitaraḥ śive

prayāṃti sadya evairate satyalokaṃ sanātanaṃ

sadyaḥ siddhikaraṃ noktaṃ tasmādatra pravakṣyate

dinatrayaṃ vā prayato brahmacārī phalāśanaḥ

yadvā ghṛtaplutairvilvapatraistila samanvitaiḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ rājyalakṣmyāpi juhuyādatha

durvalo rogapīḍitaḥ gurupādābjayugalaṃ praṇamya bhuvi daṃḍavat

utpalairjuhudyādagnau tilagavyājyamiśritaiḥ

atha prasannalakṣmyāpi prātādhika sahasrakaṃ

utpalānāma bhāvetu vilvapatrairghṛtaplutaiḥ

hunecchudgatilairevaghṛta trimadhumiśritaiḥ

lalitā raśmividyābhirekaikāmāhutiṃ ghṛtaṃ

prāyaścittāhutīḥ kṛtvā kuryādbrahmārpaṇāhutiṃ

śriyoṣṭā viṃśatīrhunet prāyaścittānnatvā tata ākalpa saṃcayaiḥ

samarpayedgrāmamekaṃ vahudhānyadhanānvitaṃ

apyosoyaskarāṃ dadyājajānaśvān paśūnapi

tulā hiraṇyagarbhādi dānānyapi samarpayet

kuṃḍala dvitayaṃ datvā guroḥ karṇayuga śiveḥ

ratnāsalaṃkaraṇaṃ yadvā rudrākṣābharaṇaṃ tu vā

gocarmasammitā bhūmirvahusasya samanvitā

tasyā abhāve tanmūlyaṃ vaṣārśanamitaṃ smṛtaṃ

vārānadyūmatha vā dadyādvittalobhaṃ parityajet

tanmūlyamathavā dadyājataḥ sākṣādramādhiyā

yugma dvādaśakaṃ bhojyaṃ maṣṭānnena tataḥ paraṃ

prāpnoti mahatīṃ siddhiṃ yogināmapi durlabhā

muktāratnagaṇākīrṇāmacalāṃ prāpnuyādasau

dāsabhūtā bhavatyasya prayacchaṃtya vivāṃchitaṃ

putrapautra prayautrādyaurbhogānbhuṅkte khilānapi

dīrghamāyuṣyamāsādya vimukto rogarāga saṃciyai

ajaraścāmaro bhūtvā bhagavat sadṛśā kṛtiḥ

āruhyabhagaṃ vaddhvānniva satyaṃ tena saṃśayaḥ

puraścaryāmimāṃ devi mahālakṣmīṃ haripriyāṃ

aṇimādi guṇā āpyajātiṃ smṛtvātha paurvikī

brahmeṃdrādyamarānnityaṃ paśya har' samanvitaḥ

saṃprāpya brahmasāyujyaṃ prāpnotyeva suniścitaṃ

mādyamāsādyamāvāsya bhānuvāsara saṃyutā

alabhya yoga uktoyaṃ labhyate vahuvatsaraiḥ

yogadvayamidaṃ prāhuralabhyaṃ bhuvi durlabhaṃ

samastaduḥkha śamanaṃ saṃsārārṇavatārakaṃ

snātvā mahoradhau nadyāṃ taḍādhe vā gṛhepi vā

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā vedamātrā ghṛtaṃ gavā

madhutritaya saṃyuktaṃ gavyājyena vimiśritaṃ

hutvātha bhagavadraśmi vidyābhirjuhuyādvataṃ

śriyaḥ paṃcadaśākṣaryā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ ghṛtaṃ

suṃdarī bhagavadvidyayāṣṭā viṃśatidhāpyatha

mahāvyāhṛtimirhutvā kṛtvā brahmārpaṇā hutiṃ

sādaraṃ praṇipatyātha svarṇālaṃkaraṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ

śatamānaṃ tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakaṃ

savatsāṃ sukuhukṣīrāṃ dadyādrāṃ ca payasvinīṃ

navavastra samāyuktaṃ bhūṣaṇādibhiranvitaṃ

suvāsinīśca virpāśca yathā śaktyātha toṣayet

mahātripurasuṃdaryāḥ saṃdaryāḥ saṃsiddhiṃ prāśūyātparāṃ

itthaṃ kuryāt puraścaryāṃ vidhinānena sādhakaḥ

avāpya mahatīṃ lakṣmīpuraścryā śatodbhavāṃ

aiśvaryā rogyasaṃtānaiḥ kaśyaṣena samaprabhaḥ

kulasāhasradvatya bhagavaddhāmni modate

śaktaścet sarvadānāni dadyādviprebhya ādarāt

tiladhenuṃ dudhidhenuṃ guḍadhenumathāpi vā

dhanāḍhyo lavaṇādyādi giridānamathā caret

suvarṇaṃ raṃjitaṃgāṃ ca lavaṇaṃ ca tilānyapi

dānānyetāni viprebhyaḥ śrotriyebhyaḥ samarpayet

suvarṇaracitaṃ pātraṃ dhanāḍhyaścet samarpya ca

sahasraṃ triśataṃ vāpi śataṃ vā dvādaśadhikaṃ

yatpuṇyāṃ samavāpnoti tadvaktuṃ naiva śakyate

sāṃgaṃ yāgadhanaṃ datvā kulakoṭi samanvitaḥ

brāhmemuhūrte utthāya śrīśrīśādvādaśākṣarī

raśmimālā manusmṛtya kṛtvā saṃdhyādikāḥ kriyāḥ

atha paṃcaśataṃ cāpi japtvā vā triśataṃ śive

bhṛguvarā vrataṃ kurvan cakrarājaṃ samarcayat

saṃprāpya ciramāyuṣyamārogyaṃ ca mahattaraṃ

bhūtvā bhūpāla sānnidhye mānya ācāryavat sadā

putrapautrādi sahito bhogān divyāṃśca mānuṣāt

yastu dvādaśa saṃkhyā kalakṣaṃ śrīhṛdayaṃ japet

ākāśagamanaṃ yogamaṣṭāṃgasahitaṃ śubhaṃ

ṛṣitvamatha siddhatvaṃ gaṃdharvatvaṃ ca yogitāṃ

anenaiva śarīreṇa kaṃdarpa śatasannibhaḥ

tejomaya tanurdivya kanyābhiḥ prārthitonvahaṃ

prasannalakṣmīvidyāyā dakṣiṇāmūrtināpurā

anyepi yogā atraiva pūrṇaśa samudīritāḥ

pratyahaṃ prajapan sākṣānnārāyaṇa ivāparaḥ

tadvaṃśe maṃdabhāgyo vā dāridro vā na jāyate

tadanvayeva satyeva satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ

triśakti lakṣmīvidyāyāstato vakṣyāmi sādhanaṃ

triśaktilakṣmīvidyāyāvidhānaṃ vada sāṃ sāṃprataṃ

yasyā vijñānataḥ sākṣānnārāyaṇamayaḥ pumān

ādyaṃ vījamiti proktaṃ vyoma vāmākṣi saṃyutaṃ

vyaṃjanāṣaṃ mahīyuktaṃ caturthasvara saṃyutaṃ

triśaktyākhyā mahālakṣmīriyaṃ vidyā samīritā

sarvapāpaudya saṃhartrī sarvapuṇya phalapradā

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyairapi nityamupāsitā

sāmrājyasiddhidā dharmavittakāma phalapradā

triśakti lakṣmyāṃ siddhāyāṃ nāsādhyaṃ trijarāt svapi

śrīdevyāḥ paścime bhāge mahāsiṃhāsaneśvari

sammohana ṛṣirvāsyāḥ kathito brahmavittamaiḥ

triśaktyākhyā mahālakṣmīrdevatā harivalābhā

kīakaṃ kāmarājaṃ syādviniyogastu siddhaye

sudhāṃbho nidhi madhyastharalaṃ dvīpe manohare

pārijātavanāṃtasthe sarvatassamalaṃkṛte

nārāyaṇāṃ kanilayāṃ vidyutkoṭi samaprabhāṃ

sphuradratnalasat svarṇakirīṭāvṛtamastakāṃ

vālī yugala tāṭaṃka svarṇa ciṃtākaṭaka hemakāṃcī guṇānvitāṃ

kolāṃkuśābja yugalapāśacāpāni vibhraṃtī

vījyamānāṃ surastrībhirmāṇikyā daśaryāṇibhiḥ

hastābhyāṃ sakhībhyāṃ suvarṇapāda kaṭaka hemakāṃcī guṇānvitāṃ

vālāṃkuśābja yugalapāśa cāpāni vibhratīṃ

vījyamānāṃ surastrībhirmāṇikyā darśayāṇibhiḥ

divyaratnamaya svarṇapeyaṃ hastābhirādarāt

saṃpūjita padāṃ nityaṃ bhaktatrāṇaṃ parāyaṇāṃ

trayīmayī mahaṃ vaṃdeśaraṇāgata vatsalāṃ

trisahasraṃ japedvidyāṃ sahasra dvitayaṃ tu vā

aṣṭā viṃśatidhā vāpi japtvā siddhimavāpnuyāt

dvirāvṛtyā trivījāni koṇa ṣaṭke kramāllikhet

caturasraṃ caturdvāra bhūṣitaṃ maṃḍalaṃ likhet

upacāraissamārādhya tatoṃgāvaraṇaṃ yajet

agrakoṇaṃ samārabhya mahālakṣmī hripriyāṃ

ṣaṭkoṇa pārśvayugale śaṃkhapapranidhī yajet

kaumārī vaiṣṇuvīṃ paścādvarāhamadanāmatha

maheṃdrādyāḥ samaparvāścaturasrāṣṭadikṣvatha

vījatraya mahāvidyāṃ japet sarvājamṛddhaye

cakrarāje samabhyarcya siddhimiṣṭāṃ vrajatyasau

sarvepicorairādhya śrīdevīcakranāyake

avaśyaṃ phalamāpnoti cakrarājasya sannidhau

avaśyaṃ mahatīṃ siddhi prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ

brahmaviṣṇu maheśādyāḥ śakrādyāścadigīśvarāḥ

sāṃgaṃ saṃpūjitātastuṣṭā bhavaṃti pratyahaṃ śive

sarvavidyāpīṭhamayaṃ śrīcakraṃ kathitaṃ vudhaiḥ

samasta devatācāryāḥ sāṃgaṃ sukṛtamaśrute

lakṣatrayaṃ japedvidyāṃ vijiteṃdriyamānasaḥ

daśāṃśaṃ kiṃśukairhutvā gavyājya trimadhulutaiḥ

makarastheravaumādye yadvārka dinamāditaḥ

ekādaśyāṃ hunedājya madhutritayamiśritai

athavā vilvapatraiśca pāyasenātha vā hunet

vidyāsiddhimavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇāṃ

aṇimādi guṇaiśvaryapradā pāpaudyabhejanī

nābhidaghnajale sthitvā sūryamaṃḍalamavyaṇaṃ

maṃḍalāṃtya dine sākṣān mahālakṣmīṃ prapaśyati

muktāralaughasahitāṃ saṃtati vyāpinīṃ śriyaṃ

tilairājyaplutairhutvā ṣadvahasraṃ yata vrataḥ

madhu tritapasammiśraṃ pāyasaṃ jvalitenale

sarvavidyā nidhirayaṃ sarvaiśvarya nidhirbhavet

manuṃ pāyasaṃ juhuyādagnai triśataṃ trimadhuplutaṃ

huta śiṣṭāśī tasyāṃ niśībharvadvratī

puraścaryā vratamidaṃ kṛtvā sādhaka puṃgavaḥ

sevyāte pratyarhasākṣāddha naralaughavarṣiḥ

sahasra tritayaṃ japtvā triśataṃ juhuyādvrataṃ

raktotpalairhunedagnau pūrṇimāyāṃ śatatrayaṃ

avāpya mahatīṃ lakṣmīṃ maheṃdra dhanadaprabhāṃ

avāpya sakalālokānnaranāru nṛpādikān

gaṃgāpravāhasadṛśīṃ vāgvibhūtimavāpyate

āśvine navarātre vā vāsaṃte vā samāhitaḥ

mahāmadhamyāṃ jvalite vahnau madhughṛtaplutaṃ

ācāryapatnīmācāryamabhyarcyā bharaṇīdibhiḥ

ācāryāyā vālmīki sadṛśo vidyābhirdhana saṃpadā

dhanadapratimo bhūtvā kāṃtyā madanasannibha

triśakti lakṣmīvidyāyāmeva bhaktiyutonvahaṃ

sahasra tritayaṃ jātvā sahasradvitayaṃ tu vā

triśatādhikāhasre japtvā yadvānvahaṃ śuciḥ

samastapāparāśibhyo vimukto bhāgya saṃpadāṃ

ādhārassarvavidyānāmapi pautra sukhānvitaḥ

cirāyustyakalānbhogān bhuktvā divyāṃśca mānuṣān

sarvārthasiddhidaṃ vakṣye samastaduritāyahaṃ

kṣamāvījaṃ śivavījaṃ ca vahni vāmākṣi maṃḍitaṃ

śrīruddheyaṃ mahāvidyā saptavarṇājvalocane

upāsakānāṃ sāmrājya śrīkarī bhuktimuktidā

mahāsārasvatakarī mahāsaubhāgyasiddhidā

dadyādrupāsakastheyaṃ bhūtale girikanyake

prajaptā sakalābhīṣṭapradogdhī kāmadhugyathā

dharmamarthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca kaivayaṃ cāṇimādikaṃ

ṛṣirnārāyaṇaḥ sākṣācchaṃdo gāyatramīritaṃ

mahālakṣmī jaganmātā devatānna samīritā

śriyā ṣaḍdīrghayutayā ṣaḍaṃgajanyā samācaret

saṃtāna pārijātā dikṣuradrumavanāvṛtte

navamāṇikya khacite mukta vidrumabhūṣite

aṃkevāme samāsīnāmattasī kusumaprabhāṃ

vālāditya śataprakhyāṃ nānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṃ

sanāna pārijātādi pravyamālya virājitāṃ

abhayaṃ ca prayacchaṃtīvarā niṣṭāśca vibhratī

cāmarādarśahastābhiḥ chatrahastābhirādarāt

śakrādi maulimāṇikya virājita padāṃvujāṃ

trayīmayīṃ mahālakṣmīṃbhaje siṃhāsaneśvarīṃ

dhyātvā saṃpūjya satataṃ cakrarāje manohare

tatrā śaktotha yaṃtresminnarcayet sādhakottamaḥ

catudvari samāyuktaṃ yaṃtraṃ sāmrājya siddhide

āvāhyopacaret sarvairupacārairmanoharaiḥ

trikoṇa madhye sāmrājya śriya mamyacya sādaraṃ

aṣṭadīrgha svarayutā brāhmyādyā aṣṭarmātaraḥ

iṃdrādīnatha dikpālān caturasrāṣṭa dikṣvapi

lakṣatrayaṃ puraścaryāt sāmrājya śrīphalāptaye

tadvaśāṃśaṃ hunedagnau kiṃjukairājyamiśritaiḥ

maṃtrasiddhimavāpnoti labhate vāṃchitaphalaṃ

japtvā palāśa kusumai rājya trimadhutolitaiḥ

samarthya dhenudaśākaṃ vidyāsiddhimavāpnuyāt

hutvā raktāṃvujai ratnau sārvabaumau bhaveddhruvaṃ

hutvā dvādaśa sāhasraṃ rājyalakṣmīmacaṃcalāṃ

sālibhirtudvato nityamaṣṭottara sahasrakaṃ

avāpya maṃḍale nāsau dhanadena samo bhavet

rājyaśriyamavāpnoti sarvavidyānivirbhavet

kaṃdarpa sadṛśaḥ kātyānaranārī nṛpādikān

kimatra vahunoktena savarmasmāda vāpnuyāt

śrotumicchasi kiṃbhūyo vadamaṣāṇavakṣubhe

raśmimālābhidhaṃ sūktaṃ śriyaḥ pravūhime prabho

yasmāḥ smaraṇa mātreṇa nityaṃ prātassamāhitaḥ

ādyā sakalavedānāṃ mātā sakalasiddhidā

sāvitrāṣṭākṣarī vidyā tṛtīyā kāṃtivarddhanī

tatasturīyagā patrī sāyujya phaladāyinī

gaṃdharvarājavidyāva vivāha phaladāyinī

tatastārā mahāvidyā jalāyacchamanī smṛtā

mahāgaṇeśavidyāva prayūhagaṇabhedinī

mṛtyuṃjaya daśārṇātha mṛtyubhīti nivāriṇī

matṛkākhyā mahāvidyā sarvajñatva phalapradā

saṃpatsarasvatīvidyā mahāsaṃyatpradāyinī

paraśaṃbhu mahāvidyā nityānaṃda sukhapradā

kumārikāvidyā samastepsitasiddhidā

aśvārūṭhā mahāvidyā sarvalokavaśaṃkarī

śrīdevyā mūlaviyāthā samastaphalabhogadā

vāgvādinī tato vidyā vācāṃ siddhikarī parā

śyāmalā pādukā paścāśyāmāvidyā phalapradā

vidyātha laghu vārāhī sarvavairinikṛṃtanī

tiraskaraṇa vārāhī tiraskaraṇakāriṇi

vidyātha rājavārtālī bhaktānu grahakāriṇī

mahatī pādukāpaścāccaturvarga phalapradā

rājaśyāmā mahāvidyā vighnadevī tataḥ paraṃ

raśmimāeti saṃproktā śrīdevyā sarvasiddhidā

sakṛddhedhā tridhā vāpi yonusmati sādhakaḥ

avāpya mahatīṃ lakṣmīṃ vāksiddhimatulāmapi

brahmasāyujyamāpnoti dehāṃte nātra saṃśayaḥ

sa mahābhāgyasīhitassamastābhīṣṭa bhāgbhavet

sakṛt dvedhā tridhā vāpismartavyā sarvasiddhidā

tatra taṃtreṣu munibhistattanmāhātmyamīritaṃ

śrīdevyāssarva rājñitvādraśmibhūtasmṛtā imāḥ

raśmimālā manusmṛtya sakṛdvādhi dine dine

jagattriya sāmrājñī śrīdevī parameśvarī

raśmimālā pradatāramācāryaṃ paramaṃ guru

śrīdevī raśmimālāyāḥ pradātaivapi tāṃvikā

sa eva vaṃdhussasakhā sa ācāryo mahānguruḥ

ityeva mavyā satataṃ raśmimālāpradaṃ guruṃ

itaḥ paraṃ girisute kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi

suguptamapi vededeṣupurāṇeṣva khileṣvapi

sāṃprataṃ praṣṭumicchāmi hṛdaye kiṃcidastime

yāgānuṣvāna hīnāśca viprāḥ sādhāraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ

tepi sādhāraṇāḥ proktā viprā vedavidopiva

saṃprāpya na hi varttaṃta cehu janmaśatairapi

tatrāpi raśmividyānīmālā sūkta mudīritaṃ

śrīdevyuyāmanā proktā raśmimālā samanvitaḥ

jaḍānāṃ madavuddhīnāṃ vidyāgrahaṇadhāraṇe

vedādhyayana hīnīścayāgānuṣṭhānavarjitāḥ

saṃsārasāgarottārahe tu bhūtaṃ sanātanaṃ

vedārtha vodhaphalaṃ sarvakratu phalapradaṃ

sāṃgānuṣṭhānajanita phaladaṃ smṛti mātrataḥ

ityukto girijā devyāśaṃkaro lokaśaṃkaraḥ

śṛṇuṣvā vahitā devi bhadre kalyāṇi suṃdari

kṛpayāte pravakṣyāmi rāmeṇa yadudīritaṃ

suramye maṃdire hemaratnaharya suśobhite

navaratnamaye svarṇa divyasiṃhāsane śubhe

kirīṭahārakeyūra kūṃḍalādi vibhūṣitaṃ

smitavaktraṃ vijaśālākṣavīrāsana samāśritaṃ

saumitri bharatādyaiśca saṃsevita padāṃvujaṃ

vaśiṣṭa vāmadevādyaissevitaṃ ca maharṣibhiḥ

līlā na dainarākṛti devaṃ trijagadrakṣaṇodyataṃ

sanakādyairmahāyogināyavairmānasaissutaiḥ

vedairmūrtidharaissākṣādvāgdevyā ca samanvitaḥ

māyāmānuṣadehatvāt pratyutthāyārghyamādade

dadau hiraṇyagarbhāya putrebhyopi yathāktamaṃ

saumitri bharatādyaiśca vidhātā lokanāyakaḥ

amūrtaṃ yatparadhāmanirguṇaṃ ca niraṃjanaṃ

vedāṃtavedyaṃ vimalaṃ paraṃbrahmeti kathyate

yadā pityātmakaṃ vahni caṃdrarūpaṃ sanātanaṃ

trayīmayaṃ trayīvedyaṃ kāraṇaṃ jagato bhavān

virāṭ svarāṭvasaṃmrāddhatvame varadyunaṃdana

purāṇa dharmaśāstrāṇitvanuñjāvirbhavasuta

tatvameva mahāvāhoṣiśvarūpaṃ parātparaṃ

tvaṃ pitā sarvajagatāṃ svatassarvamidaṃ tataṃ

tvanmukhyādagniriṃdraśca caṃdramā manaso bhavat

brāhmaṇāstvammukhājjātā vāhubhyāṃ kṣatriyāstathā

ahaṃ tvannābhi saṃbhūto rudromanyu sadbhavaḥ

vedā api ca vedāṃtā ahahaṃ rudropi devatāḥ

tavāyutāyuktaṃ śāṃśā māhātmyarmāya tatvataḥ

madyā śaṃkaraśakrādyairapi saṃprārthito bhavān

avatīrṇosi kauśalyāgarbhādaśārathānnṛpāt

rāmastvaṃ sarvalokānāmabhirāmosi bhūtale

ramaṃte satataṃ tasmādrāmosi brahmatūpadhṛt

satvaṃ dāśarathirbhūtvā koṭiśo rajanīcarān

ābhiṣiktosya yodhyāyāṃ śriyā devyā na yānvitaḥ

prasīda satataṃ mahyaṃ jānakīnāyaka prabho

brahmāṇamavravīdvātmaṃdhātāraṃ parameśvaraḥ

agnihotreṣutvatsuteṣu mahātmasu

kimarthamāgatositvaṃ putrairetairsyahātmabhiḥ

ittha bhagavatā brahmāpreritastapasāṃ nidhiḥ

eta siddhā mahābhāgāṃjita krodhā jiteṃdriyāḥ

devarṣayo nāradāyā vā lakhilyā api prabho

kratvanuṣṭhāna rahitā vadādhyayana varjitāḥ

tiṣṭaṃti vahavo viprā aśakto api karmasu

eteṣāṃ narakānmuktiḥ kalpāṃtepi na vidyate

avaśyamapirtavyā yāgāssaṃpūrṇadakṣiṇāḥ

abhyastavyo mahāyogo nityamaṣṭāṃga saṃyutaḥ

saṃsāratārako pāyo bhavatyetaissu karmabhiḥ

saṃsāratāraṇopāyaḥ kathaṃ narakamocanaṃ

ekana janmanā muktiruktā sarvaśrutiṣvapi

nyāsajālaṃ tathā pūjāsatra vākulyamastyapi

dhrmārtha kāmamokṣādiyumarthāḥ sakalā api

guruśuśrūṣayā vidyā smaraṇādapi kevalaṃ

saptakoṭi mahāmaṃtra puraścaryā phalaṃ tathā

rājavidyāṃ mama vrūhi matsutebhyaśca sādaraṃ

tasmāra sarvasiddhānāmadhiyaṃ kapilaṃ vibhu

yogābhyāsarataṃ śāṃtaṃ jagatryā hitaiṣiṇaṃ

tatrāgatastathaivāsau siddhayogi gaṇāvṛtaḥ

taṃdṛṣṭā vismitassarve sahasosthāya vāyatāḥ

jānukīyatinā tatra pādyārghyairap pūjitaḥ

jāhūtosmitvayā sākṣānnārāyaṇa raghūttama

kiṃ vaktavyaṃ mayā svāminnājñāpaya jagatpate

uvāca svā"a saṃbhūtaṃ kapilaṃ kardamātmanaṃ

sarvayogavidācāryassarvalokahiterataḥ

pitāmahena sahitā atri bhṛtvādayopyamī

ahaṃ mānuṣa yonistho rājāsanagatohaṃ

nārāyaṇosi vidyānāṃ hetu stvamasivrata

matsannidhau bhavānebhyaḥ kathaya svagururbhavān

visyasya kiṃcisrovā ca rāmaṃ dāśarathiṃ vibhuṃ

ādi madhyāṃtarahi tassatyānaṃda cidātmakaḥ

arūpo viśvarūpastvaṃ puruṣaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ

brahmaviṣṇu maheśānāmapyācāryo bhavānasi

dharmasaṃsthāpanārthyā paraghoranvaya saṃbhavaḥ

devatā ṛṣayassiddhāvayaṃ brahmādayopyamī

tvadaśaṃ saṃbhavatvena sarvajñosmi sanātanaḥ

etebhyo brahmaputrebhyo rahasyaṃ kathayāmyahaṃ

saṃtuṣṭā vismitā bhūtvā praṇemuḥ kapilaṃ vibhuṃ

saṃsārataraṇoyāya ajāyāsādbhavennavā

yadi sidhyatyanāyā sā tadupāyaṃvada svataḥ

sanakādīn mahābhāgānuvāca kapilaḥ prabhuḥ

yūyaṃ sakalavidyānāṃ pāragā dīrghadarśinaḥ

bhajadyamenaṃ saṃsāratāraka brahmasaṃjñitaṃ

asya niśvasitaṃ vedāssa rahasyāssanātanāḥ

etat kṛṭākṣaleśena viśvaṃ sarvamudetyapi

guhāsu sarvabhūtānāṃ niviṣṭaḥ parameśvaraḥ

vedānadhītya sakalā niṣṭāyāgan sa dakṣiṇān

etatkaikarya sidhyartaṃ prārthayaṃti mahaṣvapiḥ

yoginaḥ prārthayaṃtyetatsāyujyaṃ sarvāsaiddhikhyaṃ

mānuṣākṛti māyannaṃtadetaddrāmanāmakaṃ

ijyoyamakhilairyāgaissaṃpūrṇavaradakṣiṇaiḥ

ayaṃ vedamayo maṃtramūrtiryajñamayo vibhuḥ

iyaṃ sīmā mahābhāgācicchaktiḥ śrīrudīritā

etadaṃśāṃśa saṃbhūto brahmāyaṃ viśvasargakṛt

yajvāno dakṣiṇā yuktā vedādhyayana tatpurāḥ

adhītya sakalācedāniṣṭāpi sakalānkratūn

brahma tādātmya saṃsiddhi rācāryānugrahaṃ vinā

rāmābhidhaṃ mahat dhāma samupāsya vicakṣaṇāḥ

puṇyaṃ pāpamiti proktaṃ dvividhaṃ karmabhūtale

vedasyādhyayanaṃ puṇyaṃ vedānāmarthaciṃtanaṃ

saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇā yuktaṃ yāgakarma phalapradaṃ

japaḥ vaṃśakaravidyāyā vedamāturjayonvahaṃ

śrīvidyāyā jayo nityaṃ brahmavidyā japonvahaṃ

śrīmaṣṭārṇavidyāyānyāsa raśmi samanvitaṃ

kṣīṇe samastayāyaudy vahujanmabhirjite

dharmeṣvevācalā vuddhirjāyate dvijapuṃgavāḥ

tataḥ śivatvaṃ nārāyaṇāṃ yāsti ratobhyupaiti tataḥ

paraṃ śāśvatamacyutatvaṃ

tatsaṃyogīrti vikhyātāḥ paṃcapātakinastvamī

viśvasta viṣadātā ca vālaghrogarbhapātakaḥ

ekādaśasyurmadyāni tālajādīni bhūtale

ācāryapatnī tatputrī svamātā svabhaginyapi

etāmātaradratyuktāstatsaṃgāduruttalyaṃgaḥ

vidyācauryaṃ dukūlānāṃ cauryaṃsteyamudīritaṃ

ācāryaniṃdā śravaṇaṃ svayamācārya dvaḍhyaṇaṃ

brahmahatyā samānisyuḥ pātakāni dvijottamāḥ

ākalyaṃ narake duḥkhameteṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ phale

tatkṛtvaiva vimuktaḥ syāt pāpebhyo nānyathā dvijā

tadaiva paramaṃ jñānamutyatsyati kalevare

utpatsyati mahahānamapavargapradāyakaṃ

pituḥ putrasya saṃvādaṃ pāpe kalvedvijṛrṣabhāḥ

kṛto yanayanaṃ śāṃtaṃ sumatiṃ jaḍarūpiṇaṃ

guruśuśrūṣaṇa vyagro bhikṣānnakṛta bhojanaḥ

iṣṭamutpādayāpatyamāśrayethā vanaṃ tataḥ

evamāṣṭyasitahya yatra gatvā na śocati

pitāputrasu vahuśaḥ prāhaprītyā punaḥ punaḥ

saṃcodya māno vahuśaḥ prahasyedamathāvravīt

tathaivānya niśāstrāṇi śilpāni vividhāni

nirvedāḥ paritoyāścaye ca vṛdhnudayetathā

mātaro vahudhā hṛṣāḥ pitaro vahavastathā

vādhavāhavaḥ prāptāḥ pitaraśca pṛthagvidhāḥ

garbhaduḥkhānyanekāni vālatve yovane tathā

brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ caiva yoniṣu

tathaiva rājabhṛtyānāṃ rājñāmāhavaśālināṃ

bhṛtyatāṃdātatāṃ cāpi gatosmi vahuśo nṛṇāṃ

hṛtaṃ mayāhṛtaṃ cānyairhatavānūdyātitastathā

pitṛmātṛsuta bhrātṛ kalatrādi kṛtena ca

itthayaṃ sāracakresmin bhramatānāta saṃkaṭe

vijñāte yatra sarvoyamṛgyayuḥ sāmasaṃjñitaḥ

tasmādutyanna vodhesya vedaiḥ kiṃ me prayojane

guṇaiśca varjitaṃ brahman tatmākṣyāmi paraṃ padaṃ

vijñātānya mṛgagrāhī saṃgapāśa śatākulā

trayī dharmamadharmākhyaṃ kiṃ pākaphalasannibhaḥ

pitā prāha mahābhāgaṃ svasutaṃ hṛṣṭamānasaḥ

kena teja utā pūrvamidānīṃ ca prabuddhatā

yatte jñāṃtiro bhūtamāvirbhāvamupāgataṃ

yaścā hṛmā samanyasmin janmanyasmāt yasmāt yasra tat

ātmaniṣṭhā vicāreṣu yaniṣṭhāṃ parāṃgataḥ

satsaṃyogāt satsvabhāvā dvicāraṇā gati vodhanāt

ācāryatī ca saṃprāptaḥ śiṣyasaṃdehahṛttamaḥ

athāyāto prahāyogī sākṣācchiṣyo vivasvataḥ

tadṛṣṭvā tithimāyāṃtaṃ yājñavalkyaṃ mahāmati

brahmasāyujya sidhyarthaṃ yācāṃ ca kṛtavānahaṃ
